Presence — the listing lifecycle proposal

-

-

-

Product Edit Studio · proposal for your approval · 13 September 2026

# Presence

A listing object for a studio that does not have one, and the repair programme that has to land underneath it.

Approve the state model, but approve it as a repair programme with a UI at the end of it, not a UI with repairs attached. The audit's 163 confirmed findings are one absence — the studio has no listing object — and Presence supplies it: a coordinate record at (product × channel × market × account × alias) carrying a declared intent and an observed fact with a mandatory as-of, never merged, plus typed `reach`/`reversal` on the action registry and a server-computed plan bound to a `preflightToken`. The sequencing is forced by one fact I re-verified in the tree: the delist path today escalates (the Amazon `unpublish` branch logs and then deletes unconditionally, channel-delist.service.ts:151-166; Shopify duck-types its way to `deleteProduct`, :186-203), reads an eBay ownership refusal as a completed removal (:227,:231,:290-293), parks retryable failures with no `holdUntil` (:313-343) so the 60s cron re-routes them through a `dispatchSync` that has no lifecycle branch (outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623) into the ordinary update path where they finish green — and the endpoint that drives the destructive half carries no authorisation at all (amazon-flat-file.routes.ts: 29 route registrations, 0 occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate, against a global rbacHook that defaults to shadow). Wiring a Presence button over that ships every one of those defects with an operator's name on it. So: Wave 0 (guards, honest copy, measurements) starts today in three parallel lanes; Wave 1 repairs the delist path and is the gate on everything after it; Wave 2 lands additive schema, the read-only presence API and the DS type contract; Wave 3 ships the surface with local-reach verbs only; Wave 4 is the first time the studio may write to a live marketplace and should be its own approval; Wave 5 carries the irreversible and identity work. Two things the Owner must want in advance: most coordinates will read "not checked" on day one where they read a confident green "Live" today (that is the change, not a regression), and the pre-push tree is not green now — the grid-kit ratchet is RED at 34 against a baseline of 32 from another lane's importers, check-ds-dts-fresh is RED, check-grid-swatch-contrast crashes on every run so its silence proves nothing, and 6 studio tests fail in 2 files.

Built from the audit of 163 verified findings. Three competing state models were written and scored by nine judges; the winner was synthesised with the others grafted in. Seventeen specifications follow, each rewritten by an adversarial reviewer who checked every named component against the tree. Nothing here is built.

## Before anything else

Ten things the plan puts ahead of every design decision. Most are live risks now, not after Presence ships.

- AUTHORISATION on the four unguarded destructive surfaces, as explicit in-file preHandlers that bind regardless of NEXUS_RBAC_MODE (rbac-hook.ts:30-32 defaults to shadow): POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove (amazon-flat-file.routes.ts — measured 29 route registrations, 0 auth references, looping a caller-supplied target array into a hard delete that also fans out to child products via parentId, amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:47-65); POST /api/products/bulk-hard-delete (products-catalog.routes.ts:1773 — the file's only preHandler is at :1603 on a different route); routes/etsy.ts (6 app.post registrations, 0 auth; POST /etsy/sync/inventory/to-etsy reaches a live PATCH on a real Etsy listing); PUT /api/products/:id/matrix/channel-listing/:listingId (matrix.routes.ts:133-149 writes a caller-supplied externalListingId with `where:{id:listingId}` — no product scoping at all); and listing-recovery.routes.ts, which says at :18-19 that it has no guard while executeRecovery permanently deletes and renames Product.sku globally. Securing the operator path while the endpoint stays open secures the wrong half.
- NO ADAPTER MAY ESCALATE. Re-verified: channel-delist.service.ts:151-166 is not a fallback — the `action === 'unpublish'` branch logs a warning and then calls deleteListingsItem unconditionally, so every Amazon unpublish is a permanent delete today. :186-203 duck-types `typeof svcAny.updateProduct === 'function'` and falls through to deleteProduct if a rename ever lands. Both must refuse, not escalate. Shopify's delist must stop returning the double-wrapped green lie and return an explicit non-retryable failure instead.
- OUTCOME HONESTY. Split ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS (channel-delist.service.ts:223-239) into ENDED_CONFIRMED and COULD_NOT_ASK — `/item cannot be accessed/` (:227), `/invalid item/` (:231), `/item not found/` (:230) and the not-found arm of :229 are ownership or reachability refusals recorded today as completed removals at :290-293. A transport failure is UNKNOWN, never SUCCESS and never FAILURE. applyDelistResultToQueue must set holdUntil on a retryable park, and dispatchSync must REFUSE lifecycle syncTypes outright rather than falling into syncToAmazon/syncToShopify/syncToWoocommerce update paths (this refusal is also the whole of the WooCommerce containment measure).
- FIVE-LEVEL NARROWING, PRE-READ AND WRITE TOGETHER — and only after the NULL-distribution measurement, because both ChannelListing unique indexes are declared NULLS NOT DISTINCT in raw SQL precisely because every row today carries a NULL alias (schema.prisma:1770-1786). Narrowing a write without its pre-read is strictly worse than today. Sites: ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:636-639 + :645-647; amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:52-55 + :59-65; amazon-market-offer.service.ts loadRow :65-76 (the reference path withdraw-offer is modelled on, currently a three-level findFirst); sync-control.routes.ts:895-898; marketplaces.routes.ts:318 AND :418 (hardcoded aliasKey:''); flat-file.service.ts:3198-3204; recovery.service.ts:97-104 + :334-348; ebay-label-guard.service.ts:148-156, whose six-hourly identity backfill can re-attach an ItemID to a deliberately ended coordinate on the wrong account.
- ONE SHARED assertPushAllowed(listingId), extracted from outbound-sync.service.ts:903-910 and applied to every push path — pushAmazonPrice above all (pricing-outbound.service.ts:140-149 selects only {id, platformAttributes} on an unscoped findFirst, then patchListingPrice issues op:'replace' on /attributes/purchasable_offer, the very instance the close removed with op:'delete', and stamps syncStatus:'IN_SYNC'), plus syncToShopify and syncToWoocommerce (measured: zero syncPaused checks in either), syncNativeShopifyOffer, and Shopify's content path (`status` and `salesChannels` are replayable sheet cells, so a presence end is undone by the next /synchronize and reported as success).
- THE AMAZON GATE IS INVERTED FOR THE ONE IRREVERSIBLE CALL. deleteListingsItem reads process.env.AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE directly (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1143) while every other write calls getAmazonPublishMode(): with the master flag NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH off and AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE=live, the permanent delete still goes out. And closeMarketOffers persists offerClosedAt unconditionally (:178-194) while patchPurchasableOffer returns {success:true, dryRun:true} under the default gated mode — so presence would render WITHDRAWN over a live selling offer. No lifecycle DB write on a dryRun result, in any adapter.
- THE CASCADE'S SHAPE AND ITS COUNT. Enqueue delist rows with productId:null and channelListingId:null (both already nullable at schema.prisma:6824,:6827 — no migration), coordinate on the row's own columns plus payload, holdUntil +5 minutes with a POST /delist-cascade/cancel that makes the grace window real; the worker reads targetChannel from the queue row it just loaded, not from job.data; Amazon delist resolves the seller SKU (Offer.sku, else Product.sku) and never passes externalListingId, which flat-file-pull.service.ts:267 populates with the ASIN; eBay resolves the connection that OWNS the ItemID; report channelCascadeDispatched read back AFTER commit and channelSkipped naming every listing that stays live because no adapter exists.
- ONE LIVENESS VOCABULARY, TWO PREDICATES. Move isLiveOnChannel UNCHANGED into packages/shared as identityHeld — it is pinned as intended by listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:15-31 against a measured family of 20 non-ACTIVE eBay rows holding real ItemIDs — and add sellingRisk beside it. packages/shared resolves subpaths through dist, so this needs an exports entry and a build, or every import fails at runtime and not at typecheck.
- THE CALLER MUST STOP ARMING OVER A FAILED PREFLIGHT. BulkActionBar.tsx renders preflightError as an amber note (:1205-1209) and arms on `typed.trim().toUpperCase() === 'DELETE'` (:1128) — so a 500 on the preflight does not block a permanent delete, and the literal word 'delete' in lowercase arms it. Both must be fixed before any check is added to that endpoint, or adding checks makes deletes measurably less safe.
- THE MEASUREMENTS, all read-only, all blocking a later wave: (M1) WHICH DATABASE is apps/api/.env on — on 09-11 it pointed at a local Docker pg and only Product.version (54 vs 51) separated it from Neon; (M2) Offer row count on Amazon coordinates on BOTH databases, having first established whether RLS is on, because a zero taken without nexus.workspace_id set is a vacuous green — this decides whether offerScope is inert; (M3) NULL distribution of channelConnectionId and aliasKey per coordinate per channel, which decides whether the five-level narrowing is a fix or a silent no-op; (M4) 30-day count of AMAZON STATUS_UPDATE rows landing SUCCESS through the empty-patch arm (outbound-sync.service.ts:1063-1074 → :750-752), which is the blast radius of making that honest; (M5) the eBay account-level out-of-stock preference, read in Seller Hub, which decides whether the shipped 'deactivate — the safe hide' column has been ENDING listings retroactively; (M6) whether any Etsy listing_id is already persisted, which decides whether the hard-delete Etsy trap is armed today or armed by our own ingest; (M7) re-run the _versionHistory count (0 of 977 on 09-01) against the right database, which grades the platformAttributes clobber as live or latent.

## What is actually true right now

Three agents ran the repository's own instruments, read all 104 studio tests and mined the legacy tree before any design started. They corrected the audit in places, and they corrected the critics.

#### ground:measure — executing the repo's own instruments against the studio, and computing WCAG ratios from token source
Measured

- `check-grid-kit-ratchet --check` is RED (dsDataGrid 33 vs baseline 32). Sole offender is the untracked studio file `apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3`, mounted by `channel-ops/ErrorsSyncTab.tsx:48,55`. Zero audit findings mention it. It is a live pre-push blocker owned by the studio.

It is the audit's `sc:state-honesty` probe already written and running, and no finding cites it. Notable rows: `sheet/channel/AliasBandCell.tsx:132` (`alias?.listingStatus ?? 'NOT LISTED'` — absence printed as an assertion), `PresentationTab.tsx:136` (`|| 'Not published'`, so an empty-string external id also reads as not published), `drawer/panes/ComparePane.tsx:246`, `variants/channel/ChannelProjection.tsx:411`.
- The studio's own vitest suite is RED: 104 files → 102 pass / 2 fail; 1574 tests → 1568 pass / 6 fail.
- `drawer/layers.vitest.test.ts:55` fails with `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot`. `apps/api/src/services/pim/attribute-resolver.ts:25` added `'channelSnapshot' // a following legacy listing value; drift, never an operator pin` (mtime today 09:28, modified in working tree); `_studio/drawer/types.ts:353-378` `toLayer` does not map it and the comment at `types.ts:319-320` still recites the old eight-member union. The repo's own test is asserting the exact gap the critics describe: a provenance source meaning 'what is live on the channel now' with nowhere in the studio's model to attach.
- `sheet/master/masterWrite.vitest.test.ts` has 5 failures on write routing (`:71` expected fetch 2× got 1×; `:330`; `:337`). `masterWrite.ts` mtime today 15:42; its test mtime Sep 11 04:53 — producer changed without consumer.
- `check-ds-dts-fresh --check` is RED: 51 of 243 committed DS `.d.ts` are stale, including `grid/actions/registry.d.ts`, `grid/NexusGrid.d.ts`, `components/DataGrid.d.ts`, `components/Modal.d.ts`, `components/Tabs.d.ts`, `components/Drawer.d.ts`.
- `scripts/check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs` CRASHES on every run: it requires a literal `.dark {` block, but `tokens.css:450` declares `.dark, .dark body:has(.nds-theme-responsive), .dark body .nds-theme-responsive {`. `tokens.css` is unmodified in the working tree, so the gate is dead on the committed tree too. Its RED convicts nothing.
- `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` prints 'All 24 pairs pass AA' but hardcodes RGB triples (:33,:46) for the LEGACY `--text-*` / `--surface-*` vocabulary. `_studio/` uses zero of those tokens (positive control: 36 studio files reference `--nds-`). It structurally cannot see `--nds-danger` used as text. Its dark `surface-card` [15,23,42] is already out of sync with `globals.css:98` (`.theme-console` = 17 20 34).
- Browser-gate exit-code asymmetry, read from source: `check-layout-v2.mjs:432`, `check-editor-open.mjs:275` and `check-control-census.mjs:432` exit 2 with 'NOT MEASURED' when no server is up, but `check-grid-chrome.mjs:41` exits 0 — the one gate that returns a clean code while measuring nothing.
- `check-writability` exits 2 with DRIFT and deliberately refuses to print a number ('Reporting a number here would assert a rule the source no longer matches') — an honest abstention, not a pass.
- `check-route-prisma-ratchet --check` is RED (API side): `apps/api/src/routes/brand-story.routes.ts` 32 → 33.
- Grounds verified: `--nds-grid-bg: var(--nds-surface)` is re-declared in BOTH `:root` (tokens.css:237) and the dark block (:525), so light = #ffffff and dark = #18263b — the grounds the audit assumed are correct.
- Every audit contrast number reproduces to two decimals under independent computation: danger 3.91/3.89, warning 3.76/4.05, danger-text 7.36/7.14, warning-text 8.87/8.30, focus glow 1.17/1.13, waiting 1.10/1.08, saving 1.14/1.10, pending 1.17/1.16.
- NEW: `--nds-success` #15a34a is 3.30:1 on the light cell (4.62 dark) at `grid.css:508` (.nds-cell-stock-ok) — the WORST member of the status-token-as-text family, worse than danger or warning. A fix scoped to danger+warning leaves it behind.
- NEW: `.nds-cell-is-waiting` (grid.css:572) and `.nds-cell-is-unknown` (grid.css:573) have byte-identical backgrounds (both `color-mix(in srgb, var(--nds-warning) 8%, transparent)`) — separation exactly 1.000. `unknown`'s 1px inset ring is the only thing distinguishing two states the file insists are different, which constrains any fix that gives `waiting` a ring.
- `MenuItemDef` (Menu.tsx:8-50) is exactly {id, label, icon, disabled, onSelect, href, target, title, description, separator}. No tone/severity/danger/variant and no grouping key beyond `separator: boolean`. This is the measured basis of the CH.1 tension: `SheetToolbar.tsx:104`'s `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` cannot express severity or grouping.
- `.claude/DS-GAPS.md:330-348` ALREADY files 'Menu cannot express a destructive item, or explain a disabled one' (2026-08-26). Its live half verified: zero `.nds-menu` danger/destructive rules in `design-system/styles/*.css` (positive control: 20 `.nds-menu` rules in components.css). Its other half is now STALE — it recites MenuItemDef as six fields; `title` and `description` have since landed. An append-only gap log can convict what has already been fixed.
- `menuAdapters.tsx` puts the refusal reason in a hover-only slot in BOTH adapters — `title: reason ?? undefined` at :86 (DS Menu) and AG's `tooltip: reason ?? undefined` at :165 — and NEITHER sets `description`, the slot `Menu.tsx:29-40` documents as mandatory for this exact case (rulings #133/#231: 'an instruction may never live only on a disabled control'). Only two studio call sites set `description` on a menu item (`variants/family/FamilyBand.tsx:59`, `variants/channel/ChannelProjection.tsx:404,411`).
- `aria-hidden` on provenance marks is a DOCUMENTED decision, not an oversight: `design-system/grid/renderers/provenanceMark.tsx:36-42` says it avoids 'a second focusable thing in every cell: a 100-column sheet must not add 100 tab stops per row'. But the stated reason does not support the mechanism — `aria-hidden` suppresses ANNOUNCEMENT, it does not create or remove tab stops (a non-focusable `<span>` is not a tab stop), and the `title` at :92 that the comment relies on as the explanation is itself inert under `aria-hidden`.
- `styles.tabStrip` and `styles.mergedRow` have ZERO references anywhere in apps/web/src (positive control on the same module: styles.shell 6, styles.body 8, styles.bands 2, styles.skelHeader 1). So `studio.module.css:69-80` and `:235-282` are DEAD — including the band-height block whose comment at :232-234 cites `check-token-resolution` as its guarantor. The tokens resolve; the rules consuming them are unreachable.
- Skeleton chrome measured exactly from source: `loading.tsx:19,24,30` + `studio.module.css:191-203` = 48 + 44 + 34 = 126px content + three 1px border-bottoms = 129px. The LIVE-frame side (89px vs 90px) cannot be settled from the cited studio CSS because those selectors are the dead ones; `StudioFrame.tsx:43-48` renders only shell/bands/body/tabBody and band heights come from DS patterns.css. Two audit findings file this one defect with arithmetic that disagrees by 1px, and neither is measured.
- `globals.css` is 332 lines, not 3 — it carries the whole legacy `--text-*`/`--surface-*` vocabulary plus a THIRD theme scope, `.theme-console` (:97-100), which neither `check-contrast` nor `check-dark-pin-parity` covers. The A11Y-1 sub-claim that it contains zero `:focus-visible` rules is CONFIRMED (0 matches for 'focus' at all; positive control: 13 for 'button').
- `_studio/sheet/StructuredAttributeEditor.tsx:24` uses an inline `style={{ width: 'min(620px, 85vw)', maxHeight: '65vh' }}` rather than the DS `editorBox()` at `design-system/grid/editors/sheet.ts:86` — one instance of the audit's '620px' claim confirmed; I did not enumerate the other three, so the 'four editors' set claim is not verified by me.
- The audit's exact strings for the toolbar padding conflict (`padding: 0 6px` and `padding: 14px 16px`) do not occur in any studio stylesheet — every match in apps/web/src is in `marketing/ads/*.css`. That finding's cited mechanism is not reproducible at those literals.
- METHOD WARNING for other lanes: `timeout` does not exist on macOS. Wrapping a gate in `timeout 600 node scripts/…` makes every run report EXIT=0 from 'command not found' — a vacuous green that looks exactly like a clean gate sweep.
Contradicts or narrows
- The DS menu adapter puts a disabled verb's reason in a hover-only tooltip, against the DS's own written rule — while the lane's hand-rolled adapter does it correctly
- Every provenance mark in the sheet is `aria-hidden`, so "inherited / pinned / AI-drafted / formula refused" reaches assistive tech as nothing — while every neighbouring cell mark in the same file is exposed
- The route skeleton draws three bands totalling 129px against the real frame's two bands totalling 89px, so the sheet jumps 40px upward on every studio load — in a file whose comment promises it does not
- The route skeleton draws 129px of chrome where the live frame draws 90px — the page jumps 39px the moment data lands, which is precisely what the skeleton's own comment says it exists to prevent
- Four studio popup cell editors bypass the DS editorBox() sizing contract and hardcode widths up to 620px
- The sheet toolbar band has two competing padding rules at identical specificity — `0 6px` and `14px 16px` — and which one applies is decided by stylesheet import order, not by a decision

▸ Full report
## 0. Scope and honesty notes
The three `ground-*.json` files the brief names do **not exist** — `find /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-awais-nexus-commerce/ -name 'ground-*'` returns nothing, and the session scratchpad holds only `audit-findings.json`, `audit-critics.json`, `audit-maps.json`, `audit-probes.json` and the HTML report. So this is a first measurement, not a cross-check of one.
Two things I did **not** do. (a) `check-layout-v2.mjs`, `check-control-census.mjs`, `check-editor-open.mjs` and `check-grid-chrome.mjs` all `import { chromium } from '@playwright/test'` and drive `http://localhost:3000` (layout-v2:93,227; census:53,59; editor-open:64,70; grid-chrome:25,27). Running them is driving a browser against a dev server wired to the production API, which the brief forbids. **Every pixel, clipping and geometry claim in the audit therefore remains unmeasured**, and I say so rather than paper over it. (b) I inadvertently ran `check-listing-table.mjs` and `check-channel-table.mjs`, which open a Postgres connection; both are read-only `SELECT`s, but per MEMORY's *WHICH DATABASE* pointer I cannot say which DB answered, so I lean on nothing they printed.
One trap I fell into and caught: my first gate batch wrapped each run in `timeout 600`, which does not exist on macOS. Every gate reported `EXIT=0` from a `command not found`. That is a textbook vacuous green; all results below are from the re-run without it.
## 1. What the executable instruments actually say
**Typecheck: GREEN.** `npx tsc --noEmit -p apps/web/tsconfig.json --incremental false` → exit 0, **zero** diagnostics. The uncommitted tree compiles.
**Green gates** (all `--check` where supported): `ds-conformance-guard` (no section above baseline); `check-token-resolution` (107 stylesheets, 383 defined / 344 consumed, 0 unresolvable, 12 runtime-written abstentions); `check-raw-primitives-ratchet` (4340 vs baseline 4692); `check-ds-fork-drift` (152 shared / 7 differing / 145 identical, no new drift); `check-css-parse`; `check-dark-pin-parity` (374 `:root` tokens, 105 pinned, 0 missing, 0 drifted); `check-dark-alias-scope`; `check-silent-disabled` (21 vs 27); `check-button-vocabulary` (53 vs 243); `check-help-cursor` (0 vs 0); the hex/radius/DS-shadow CSS ratchets; `check-alias-form`, `check-link-targets`, `check-grid-modules`, `check-ag-grid-import-boundary`, `check-context-boundary`, `check-global-exposure`, `check-variants-sheet-parity`, `check-grid-option-identity`, `check-shell-pin-fresh`, `check-ds-gaps-append-only`.
**Six instruments are RED or broken. Three are the studio's own.**
1. **`check-grid-kit-ratchet --check` → exit 1.** `DS DataGrid (<table>)` is **33 against a baseline of 32**. I re-derived the file set with the gate's own predicate: the single new importer is the **untracked** studio file `apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3` — `import { Card, DataGrid, type Column } from '@/design-system/components'`, mounted unconditionally by `channel-ops/ErrorsSyncTab.tsx:48,55`. This is a live pre-push blocker created by the studio, on a retiring grid kit, and **the 180-finding roster never mentions it** (`ReadinessPanel` 0 hits, `grid-kit` 0 hits).
2. **`check-wire-null-defaults --check` → exit 1: 28 against a baseline of 17, and all 28 offenders are inside `_studio/`.** This gate is the audit's `sc:state-honesty` probe, already written, already running, already red, and cited by **zero** findings. Its most consequential rows are lifecycle-shaped: `sheet/channel/AliasBandCell.tsx:132` renders `alias?.listingStatus ?? 'NOT LISTED'` — an *absent* status is printed as the *assertion* "NOT LISTED"; `PresentationTab.tsx:136` renders `row?.listing?.externalListingId || 'Not published'` (note `||`, so an empty-string external id also becomes "Not published"); `drawer/panes/ComparePane.tsx:246` invents the reason `'No listing on this coordinate.'`; `variants/channel/ChannelProjection.tsx:411` invents `'Listing aliases are not available for this family.'`. The gate's own closing line is the design brief: *"Each of these invents a value the wire declined to give… a plausible default looks like data."*
3. **`_studio/` vitest is RED.** `npx vitest run "src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio"`: **104 files, 102 pass / 2 fail; 1574 tests, 1568 pass / 6 fail.**
   - `drawer/layers.vitest.test.ts:55` — *"every attribute-resolver ValueSource maps to a known layer"* fails with `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot`. `apps/api/src/services/pim/attribute-resolver.ts:25` gained `| 'channelSnapshot' // a following legacy listing value; drift, never an operator pin` (file mtime today 09:28, `M` in the working tree); the drawer's `toLayer` in `_studio/drawer/types.ts:353-378` does not map it, and the comment at `types.ts:319-320` still recites the old eight-member union. The test's docstring states the consequence exactly: the field renders "Unrecognised", *"or a maintainer folds it into `master` and the drawer starts claiming a channel override is the master's own value."* **This is the critics' structural verdict caught by the repo's own instrument**: the API has minted a provenance source that means *what is live on the channel right now*, and the studio's layer vocabulary has nowhere to put it.
   - `sheet/master/masterWrite.vitest.test.ts` — 5 failures on write **routing** (`:71` expected fetch twice, got once; `:330`, `:337`). `masterWrite.ts` was written today at 15:42, its test last touched Sep 11 04:53 — a producer changed without its consumer, exactly the *producer + consumer land together* rule.
4. **`check-ds-dts-fresh --check` → exit 1: 51 of 243 committed DS declarations are stale**, including `grid/actions/registry.d.ts`, `grid/NexusGrid.d.ts`, `components/DataGrid.d.ts`, `components/Menu`-adjacent (`Modal`, `Tabs`, `Drawer`, `Listbox`). An implementer reading `registry.d.ts` is reading a contract the source no longer matches.
5. **`check-writability` → exit 2 (DRIFT).** It refuses to report a number because *"products.routes.ts: 'the registry lookup uses that marketplace' no longer matches source"*. An honest abstention, not a pass.
6. **`check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs` CRASHES** — `Error: … tokens.css has no block matching /\.dark \{([\s\S]*?)\n\}/`. `tokens.css:450` declares `.dark, .dark body:has(.nds-theme-responsive), .dark body .nds-theme-responsive {`; a bare `.dark {` does not exist (`/usr/bin/grep -n '^\.dark {'` rc=1). `tokens.css` is **unmodified in the working tree**, so this gate has been dead on the committed tree too. Its RED convicts nothing.
7. Not studio, but red: `check-route-prisma-ratchet --check` → exit 1, `apps/api/src/routes/brand-story.routes.ts: 32 → 33`.
## 2. The green that could never have gone red
`scripts/check-contrast.mjs` prints **"All 24 pairs pass AA"**. It is vacuous for this programme on two counts. It **hardcodes RGB triples** in the script (`LIGHT`/`DARK` at :33,:46) rather than reading CSS, and those triples name the *legacy* vocabulary — `text-secondary`, `surface-card`, `surface-canvas` — which lives in `globals.css`/`shared-shell.css`. `_studio/` uses **zero** of those tokens (positive control: 36 studio files reference `--nds-`). It cannot see `--nds-danger` used as body text. Its dark `surface-card` triple `[15,23,42]` is already out of sync with `globals.css:98`'s `.theme-console` value `17 20 34`.
Exit-code asymmetry among the browser gates, read from source: `layout-v2:432`, `editor-open:275` and `control-census:432` exit **2** with an explicit "NOT MEASURED" on a missing server, but **`check-grid-chrome.mjs:41` exits 0** — the one gate that reports a clean code while measuring nothing.
## 3. Contrast, computed independently in both themes
I parsed `tokens.css` + `tokens-global.css` into `:root` and `.dark` maps, resolved `var()` chains, composited alphas and `color-mix()` myself, and confirmed the grounds: `--nds-grid-bg: var(--nds-surface)` is re-declared in both scopes (`tokens.css:237` and `:525`), so light = `#ffffff`, dark = `#18263b` — the grounds the audit assumed are correct.
**Every audit number reproduces to two decimals.** `--nds-danger` #e5484d → **3.91** / **3.89**; `--nds-warning` #b87503 → **3.76** / **4.05**; replacements `--nds-danger-text` #9c2f2a → **7.36**, dark #ef9c93 → **7.14**; `--nds-warning-text` #6d3f10 → **8.87**, dark `--nds-warning-strong` #f0b46a → **8.30**. Focus glow `rgb(31 111 222 / 0.12)` composites to **#e4eefb = 1.17:1** light and **#192f4f = 1.13:1** dark; on `.nds-btn.primary` the focus border-colour change is **1.00:1** (identical value). `waiting` = warning 8% → **#f9f4eb 1.10:1** light, **#252c37 1.08:1** dark; `saving` = info 10% → **1.14 / 1.10**; `pending` = warning 14% → **1.17 / 1.16**.
Three things the audit did not state that my run adds. **`--nds-success` #15a34a is the worst of the family at 3.30:1 light** (4.62 dark) — `.nds-cell-stock-ok` at `grid.css:508`; a fix scoped to danger+warning leaves the lowest-contrast member behind. **`waiting` and `unknown` have byte-identical backgrounds** (`grid.css:572` and `:573`, both `color-mix(in srgb, var(--nds-warning) 8%, transparent)`) — separation exactly **1.000**, so `unknown`'s 1px ring is the *only* thing distinguishing two states the file insists are different. And `.dark` overrides neither `--nds-danger` nor `--nds-warning`, which my map confirms by inheritance.
## 4. Set claims re-derived with positive controls
- **`--nds-gutter-content` has zero consumers.** Defined twice (`tokens.css:226`, `tokens-global.css:227`); `var(--nds-gutter-content` → 0. Positive control: `var(--nds-` occurs **11,985** times across **305 distinct** tokens in `apps/web/src`; the only gutter-shaped consumer anywhere is `var(--nds-grid-sheet-gutter`. Confirmed.
- **`GridAction.danger` has no production consumer.** `/usr/bin/grep -rn danger design-system/grid/actions/` returns only the declaration (`registry.ts:183-184`) and its stale mirror (`registry.d.ts:188-189`); `menuAdapters.tsx` contains **0** occurrences (positive control: 17 for "action"). `MenuItemDef` (`Menu.tsx:8-50`) is `{id, label, icon, disabled, onSelect, href, target, title, description, separator}` — no `tone`, `severity`, `danger`, `variant` or group key; the only grouping primitive is `separator: boolean`. **This is the measured basis of the CH.1 tension**: `SheetToolbar.tsx:104`'s `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` genuinely cannot express severity or grouping.
- **`.claude/DS-GAPS.md:330-348` already files this DS gap** ("`Menu` cannot express a destructive item, or explain a disabled one") and I verified its live half: zero `.nds-menu` danger/destructive rules in `design-system/styles/*.css` (positive control: 20 `.nds-menu` rules in `components.css`). Its *other* half is now stale — it recites `MenuItemDef` as six fields, and `title`/`description` have since landed.
- **`.nds-addvar-field`**: exactly 4 studio files (`sheet/AttributeShapeInput.tsx`, `sheet/master/AddVariationDialog.tsx`, `sheet/master/ClassificationDialog.tsx`, `sheet/ImpactProtectorsInput.tsx`). Confirmed.
- **Dead studio CSS.** `styles.tabStrip` and `styles.mergedRow` have **0** references anywhere in `apps/web/src` (positive control on the same module: `styles.shell` 6, `styles.body` 8, `styles.bands` 2, `styles.skelHeader` 1). So `studio.module.css:69-80` and `:235-282` — including the band-height block whose comment at `:232-234` invokes `check-token-resolution` as its guarantor — render nothing. The tokens resolve; the rules consuming them are unreachable. That is a green aimed one layer away from the claim.
- **Skeleton arithmetic.** From source, `loading.tsx:19,24,30` + `studio.module.css:191-203`: 48 + 44 + 34 = 126px content plus three 1px `border-bottom` = **exactly 129px**. Confirmed. The *live* side cannot be settled from the cited studio CSS, because the rules cited are the dead ones; `StudioFrame.tsx:43-48` renders only `shell/bands/body/tabBody`, so band heights come from DS `patterns.css`. A browser settles it; I did not run one.

#### ground:tests — the 104 vitest files inside apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/
Measured
- The studio suite is NOT green. Reproducible hermetic baseline (NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL and PES3_API both pinned to a closed loopback port): Test Files 2 failed | 102 passed; Tests 6 failed | 1555 passed | 13 skipped (1574).
- 5 of the 6 failures are STALE tests, not a regression: sheet/master/masterWrite.vitest.test.ts (mtime Sep 11 04:53) asserts a two-route write that the LX.8 change deleted from masterWrite.ts (mtime Sep 12 15:42). The replacement contract IS tested and passes, in sheet/content-write.vitest.test.ts (mtime Sep 12 16:46), and the API supports it (bulk-edit.service.ts:57-59, :554-571).
- The 6th failure is a LIVE defect: apps/api/src/services/pim/attribute-resolver.ts:25 added ValueSource 'channelSnapshot'; sheet/channel/types.ts:169 took it, but drawer/types.ts LAYER_OF (:352-376) did not, so toLayer() returns 'unknown' and the drawer renders a drifted channel value as 'Unrecognised'. drawer/types.ts is clean at HEAD with an mtime 7 hours EARLIER than the producer.
- apps/api/src/services/products/bulk-edit.service.ts:563 calls contentAddress(change.contentAddress, …) for EVERY change unconditionally, before the isLocalizableContent branch, and :571 refuses the WHOLE batch if any change lacks one. packages/shared/content-language.ts:11-13 throws on undefined. Every /api/products/bulk caller that does not send a per-change contentAddress is now refused wholesale — including POST /products/:id/restore (products.routes.ts:835), which forwards an optional body-level field.
- GridAction.danger is INERT, not merely under-rendered: menuAdapters.tsx has 0 occurrences of 'danger' (positive control: 5 for 'label'); both DS test files set danger:true on a fixture and assert nothing about it; the only danger tone is ActionConfirm.tsx:94, keyed on needsTyping (impact.level === 'type-to-confirm'), never on action.danger.
- The DS gap is precisely located: MenuItemDef (design-system/components/Menu.tsx:8-45) has no danger, no separator and no group field, and registry.vitest.test.ts:88 pins declaration order. A separator precedent already exists in one adapter (menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:93).
- Lifecycle vocabulary is absent from all 104 studio tests: 'wave-1' 0 hits, 'delist' 0, 'irreversible' 0, 'variationExcluded' 0; 'offerActive' 2 (label fixtures only); 'relist' 1 (a server sentence in a fixture). Positive controls in the same sweep — 'preview', 'archive', 'isPublished', 'Excluded' — all hit.
- Test coverage is inverted relative to audit risk. Zero test reference: StudioBar.tsx (19 finding-citations), drawer/panes/ListingsPane.tsx (11), StudioTabHost.tsx (10), PublishMenu.tsx (7), StudioSubheader.tsx (7), studio-data.ts (7), lastMarket.ts (3). ChannelSheet.tsx (33 citations) and MasterSheet.tsx (23) have no component test — apparent hits are matches on the type name ChannelSheetRow.
- The liveness primitive the design needs already exists and is tested — isLiveOnChannel/classifyListings in sheet/master/listingRisk.ts, 'the external id decides, never the status', measured against a real family with 20 non-ACTIVE eBay rows carrying real ItemIDs — and has exactly ONE consumer repo-wide, familyActions.ts:452.
- The REACH vocabulary also already exists and is tested — channel-ops/syncQueue.ts modeForChannel/gateNote, four honest states, explicitly refusing to default to 'live', already returning a distinct sentence for WooCommerce — and is consulted by no verb.
- The 'held with a sentence, never a silent disable' pattern is shipped and server-supplied: heldReason (16 hits in apps/api/src) and locked.reason on GET /products/:id/studio/projection (product-studio.routes.ts:189), which 400s without an explicit channel and market (:180-186, ruling #573) — the server already refuses an ambient coordinate on the surface most like a listing.
- scopes.vitest.test.ts pins WooCommerce as a live channel in three assertions (:103, :295, :313); its fixture is the real marketplaces table read 2026-09-01, predating the 2026-09-12 Woo ruling. The same file's 'Relationships stays removed' test WAS updated for the 2026-09-12 ruling — so the suite tracked one Owner ruling of that date and not the other.
- defaultMarket is a five-way tie broken alphabetically to 'DE', asserted as 'a deterministic answer, not a meaningful one' (scopes.vitest.test.ts:124-129); the observed DE-then-PL deep-link instability was investigated and defaultMarket EXONERATED (:161-176) — that instability remains unattributed.
- The vitest config doc block is stale: it claims 'zero .tsx, zero React imports', but the config now carries oxc: { jsx: { runtime: 'automatic' } } and 4 studio tests (13 web-wide) render via react-dom/server. Component contract tests are possible today without adding jsdom.
- live-channel-scope.vitest.test.ts:206 is `if (CHANNEL !== 'AMAZON') return` — a bare return, not ctx.skip() — so on the default CHANNEL='EBAY' it passes having asserted nothing, contradicting the doctrine at :97-99 in the same file.
- critic:coverage's set claim 'zero roster findings cite a test path' is false: findings #35, #38 and #80 each cite a .vitest.test.ts path in evidence.
Contradicts or narrows
- "Retire a colourway permanently" is a dead end: Delete child refuses on any external id, and no studio verb can ever clear one
- Unticking a live variant says "Excluded" but the size stays on sale — the write never leaves the database
- 'Delete' means 'recycle bin, restorable' on /products and /products/next, and 'permanent, no bin, no undo' in the studio — and the studio asks for less confirmation than for its reversible verbs
- No reversible per-market eBay end exists anywhere, and the eBay primitive that would give one (quantity 0 / out-of-stock) is implemented but never exposed

▸ Full report
## 1. Is the tree green? No — and the reproducible baseline is 6 failures in 2 files
I ran the suite read-only with the API base pinned to a closed loopback port (`NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:1`) so nothing could fall back to production — `apps/web/src/lib/backend-url.ts:12-14` defaults to the prod Railway host when that var is unset. Every URL in the 104 files is a stub; the one file that issues real requests, `sheet/master/masterWrite.deadport.vitest.test.ts`, binds and releases a port itself and asserts loopback in all 5 of its 5 `it` blocks before writing.
Reproducible baseline (`PES3_API` also pinned dead): **Test Files 2 failed | 102 passed; Tests 6 failed | 1555 passed | 13 skipped (1574)**.
My first run reported 1568 passed / 0 skipped because a local API was listening on :8091 (`lsof` confirmed pid 9111). Exactly one file depends on it — `sheet/channel/live-channel-scope.vitest.test.ts:27` — and I verified its skip path is honest, not a vacuous green: pointed at a dead port it reports "13 skipped", pointed at the live API it reports "13 passed". One caveat: `live-channel-scope.vitest.test.ts:206` is `if (CHANNEL !== 'AMAZON') return` — a bare return, not `ctx.skip()` — so on the default `CHANNEL='EBAY'` that test passes having asserted nothing, contradicting the doctrine written three lines above it at :97-99. 1 of those 13 "passes" is empty.
**The 6 failures are two different things, and only one is a defect.**
(a) `sheet/master/masterWrite.vitest.test.ts` — 5 failures. These are **stale tests, not a regression.** The uncommitted working tree deleted the localized write route from `masterWrite.ts` (the `/api/products/:id/global` PATCH; `git diff` shows −44 lines) and now sends every cell through `/api/products/bulk` with a per-change `contentAddress`. The API supports this: `apps/api/src/services/products/bulk-edit.service.ts:57-59` declares `contentAddress`/`contentVersion` per change, and :554-571 routes localizable content to `applyContentBulk`. The authoritative test for the new contract is `sheet/content-write.vitest.test.ts`, which **passes** and asserts the single-bulk path for both `commitMasterRow` and `commitChannelRow`. mtimes settle the order: the failing test Sep 11 04:53, `masterWrite.ts` Sep 12 15:42, `content-write.vitest.test.ts` Sep 12 16:46. LX.8 landed producer, consumer and a new test, and left the old test behind.
(b) `drawer/layers.vitest.test.ts` — 1 failure, and this **is a live defect.** `apps/api/src/services/pim/attribute-resolver.ts:25` gained `ValueSource = 'channelSnapshot'` ("a following legacy listing value; drift, never an operator pin"). The channel sheet's mirror took it (`sheet/channel/types.ts:169`, mtime Sep 12 15:05, modified in the tree). The **drawer's did not**: `LAYER_OF` at `drawer/types.ts:352-376` has no entry, so `toLayer('channelSnapshot')` returns `'unknown'` (`drawer/types.ts:378-381`) and the drawer renders a drifted channel value as "Unrecognised". `drawer/types.ts` is clean at HEAD, mtime Sep 12 02:22 — seven hours *before* the producer changed. Producer + consumer landed together in one of two consumers. The parity test caught it and is red now.
Caveat on all of the above: `bulk-edit.service.ts` was modified at 23:29 tonight. This is a snapshot of a moving tree.
## 2. What the tests assert as INTENDED — the decisions a fix must not silently overturn
- **`delete-variant` refuses on any external id.** `sheet/master/listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:11-31` pins "the external id decides, never the status": a `DRAFT`, `ENDED` or null-status row holding a real ItemID is LIVE, and `isPublished:false` does not make it safe. `familyActions.vitest.test.ts:270-276` calls this "THE case this whole preflight exists for", measured on a family where all 20 non-ACTIVE eBay rows carried real ItemIDs. The refusal is a safety decision, not a dead end.
- **Ruling #118: `run` applies the snapshot `preflight` produced, never a re-read.** Asserted five times in `familyActions.vitest.test.ts` (:301-311, :331-337, :234-241). This is the mechanism a lifecycle verb needs to freeze an ambient coordinate — it exists.
- **A preflight that could not look must REFUSE, not soften.** `familyActions.vitest.test.ts:299-306`; `runAction.vitest.test.ts:51,60`.
- **`level:'none'` + `unavailable` is the correct refusal shape**, and `validateImpact` returns `[]` for it (`familyActions.vitest.test.ts:277`). `registry.ts` rejects `unavailable` with any other level.
- **The drawer must NOT reimplement the confirm sequence** (`drawer/recordActions.vitest.test.ts:69-83`) and **must stay below the overlay** so registry confirms portal above it (`:92-127`, `--nds-z-rail < --nds-z-overlay < --nds-z-drawer`).
- **`defaultMarket` is a five-way tie broken alphabetically to 'DE'** — `scopes.vitest.test.ts:124-129` calls it "a deterministic answer, not a meaningful one". Designed arbitrariness. Also: a deep link resolving DE-then-PL was investigated and `defaultMarket` **exonerated** (:161-176, PL is single-channel and unreachable). That instability is still unattributed.
- **WooCommerce is pinned as a live channel in 3 assertions** (`scopes.vitest.test.ts:103, 295, 313`). Any Woo removal breaks them.
- **`sheet/channel/rows.vitest.test.ts:105-109` asserts NO quantity/stock total exists in the module** via a runtime self-guard, `assertNoQuantitySummation`.
- **"Excluded" is a spec'd state name** (`variants/family/projections.vitest.test.ts:30`, §3.3) meaning "connected channel, no listing row" — a fact about the local record.
## 3. Primitives the design needs that already exist, tested, in the wrong place
- **Liveness.** `classifyListings` / `isLiveOnChannel` (`sheet/master/listingRisk.ts`) has exactly **one** consumer repo-wide: `familyActions.ts:452`. Not on the channel sheet, not in the drawer, not in the ScopeBar meter.
- **Reach.** `channel-ops/syncQueue.ts` has a tested four-state publish-gate vocabulary — `live` (silence), `gated`, `dry-run`, unrecognised-mode (warn), undescribed-channel (distinct warn) — which explicitly refuses to default to `live` (`syncQueue.vitest.test.ts:152-193`). `modeForChannel(r,'WOOCOMMERCE')` already returns null and `gateNote(null,'WooCommerce')` already says "not described by this server response". It is confined to the Errors & Sync tab and consulted by no verb.
- **Held-with-a-sentence.** `variants/channel/projection.vitest.test.ts:452-472` shows the shipped pattern: `split.creatable:false` + `heldReason`, `locked.reason: 'Item 257584954808 is live with Colore and Taglia. Adding or removing a specific relists it…'`, `order.writableHere:false`. Server-supplied (`heldReason`: 16 hits in `apps/api/src`), backed by a real endpoint — `GET /products/:id/studio/projection` (`product-studio.routes.ts:189`), which **400s without an explicit channel and market** (`:180-186`, ruling #573). The server already refuses an ambient coordinate on the surface most like a listing.
## 4. Where the tests are silent — a set claim with a positive control
Grepping all 104 files: **`wave-1` 0 hits, `delist` 0, `irreversible` 0, `variationExcluded` 0**. `offerActive` 2 hits, both fixture fields for a label resolver. `relist` 1 hit — a server sentence in a fixture. Positive controls in the same sweep: `preview`, `archive`, `isPublished`, `Excluded` all hit. **The lane's own test suite contains no listing-lifecycle assertions**, which corroborates the design critic by omission.
Coverage is inverted relative to risk. Files with zero test reference: `StudioBar.tsx` (19 finding-citations), `drawer/panes/ListingsPane.tsx` (11), `StudioTabHost.tsx` (10), `PublishMenu.tsx` (7), `StudioSubheader.tsx` (7), `studio-data.ts` (7), `lastMarket.ts` (3). `ChannelSheet.tsx` (33 citations, the most-cited file in the audit) and `MasterSheet.tsx` (23) have **no component test** — the six apparent hits are matches on the type name `ChannelSheetRow`. Meanwhile `readiness.ts`, cited by one finding, has 13 test references.
The vitest config's own doc block is stale: it says "all 89 are pure-logic `.ts` files — zero `.tsx`, zero React imports", but the config now carries `oxc: { jsx: { runtime: 'automatic' } }` and 4 studio tests render via `react-dom/server`. **Component contract testing is available today without adding jsdom** — confirmation copy can be gated.
## 5. Fixture provenance
- `scopes.vitest.test.ts` — **real**, the actual `GET /api/marketplaces/grouped` table read 2026-09-01. Dated before the 09-12 Woo ruling.
- `import/fixture.ts` — **hybrid, self-declared**: real prod ids (read-only counts 2026-09-02), invented values, `isFixture` on the payload and a banner on screen. Proves the parse path.
- `variants/channel/projection.vitest.test.ts` — hand-authored `ProjectionPage` matching the VP.2 contract, with `INVENTED` markers at :375 and :442. Proves parse and copy, not the writer's path.
- `live-channel-scope.vitest.test.ts` — the only writer-produced fixture: a real `/studio/sheet` payload from a live API.
- `content-write.vitest.test.ts`, `familyActions.vitest.test.ts`, `masterWrite.vitest.test.ts` — hand-built literals. The CAS-source assertion (Product v4 vs listing v19) is proven against a literal, not a server read.
## 6. Two corrections to the briefing's own premises
**`GridAction.danger` is not "one severity bit" — it is an inert field.** `registry.ts:183` promises "Destructive verbs are grouped apart and rendered with danger tone by every adapter." `menuAdapters.tsx` contains **0 occurrences** of `danger` (positive control: 5 for `label`). Both `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:16` and `registry.vitest.test.ts:34` set `danger: true` on a fixture and assert nothing about it. The only danger tone in the stack is `ActionConfirm.tsx:94`, keyed on `needsTyping` — i.e. on `impact.level === 'type-to-confirm'`, not on `danger`. The working severity channel is `ActionImpact.level` plus `findings[].severity`; `danger` is a comment asserting a property the code never had.
**The DS gap behind the CH.1 tension is real and precisely located.** `MenuItemDef` (`design-system/components/Menu.tsx:8-45`) has `id/label/icon/disabled/onSelect/href/target/title/description` — no danger, no separator, no group. `registry.vitest.test.ts:88` additionally pins declaration order ("a menu does not reshuffle between renders"). A separator mechanism does already exist in one adapter (`menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:93`, lane verbs above AG clipboard entries separated by a rule) — that is the smallest precedent for an amendment, and it is the Owner's call, not mine.
**Finally, a correction to critic:coverage.** Its set claim "zero roster findings cite a test path" is false: #35 cites `drawer/listingUrl.vitest.test.ts:31`, #38 cites `variants/channel/projection.vitest.test.ts:462`, #80 cites `flat-file-ufx6-gpsr.vitest.test.ts:316-317`. Its larger point — that no probe *read* the suite — stands.

#### ground:legacy — the legacy edit tree, the recover page and the studio restore family as a capability source
Measured
- The legacy edit page is the LIVE route, not dead code: edit/page.tsx:14-37 renders ProductEditClient, which imports 16 tabs + amazon-cockpit + ebay-cockpit at :56-72; edit/studio/page.tsx:1-8 says the studio is mounted beside it until the Owner swaps them.
- A shipped 'Listing Hub' exists at MasterDataTab.tsx:915-1435 (MarketAvailabilityCard, :999; rendered :629): one row per channel×marketplace from GET /api/products/:id/all-listings (:1038), with listingStatus/offerActive/title/hasDescription/price/lastSyncedAt (:917-927).
- That hub has a liveness vocabulary the studio lacks: STATUS_META maps ACTIVE→Live, DRAFT→Draft, INACTIVE→Paused, ENDED→Ended, ERROR→Error (MasterDataTab.tsx:934-940), rendered ALONGSIDE a separate 3-point readiness dot (:967-978) and a relative last-synced age (:983-997).
- Plural lifecycle intent is already expressible on the legacy page: pauseNonIT (:1170-1192) renders a 'Pause non-IT' button (:1251-1255); setAll gives Activate all / Pause all (:1149, :1256-1261); multi-select + selection action bar gives Activate / Pause / Publish over an arbitrary subset (:1069-1105, :1220-1227, :1269-1299).
- Every hub write is optimistic with an explicit rollback on failure and a per-coordinate toast (:1088/:1098, :1128/:1142, :1153/:1164, :1177/:1187), all against PATCH /api/products/:id/offer-availability.
- The studio has per-listing snapshot, manual checkpoint and restore-to-draft ROUTES on its own namespace — product-studio.routes.ts:900 (GET snapshots), :912 (POST checkpoint), :930-948 (POST restore, sets isPublished=false, auto-snapshots current state first, returns undoSnapshotId) — backed by services/pim/listing-snapshot.service.ts:178,269,309.
- Set claim (/usr/bin/grep over apps/web/src): ZERO web consumers of those listing-snapshot routes. Positive control: 'restore-points' returns 6 hits in the same sweep. Set claim over audit-findings.json: zero of 180 findings cite them.
- /products/[id]/recover is a working per-listing lifecycle console: a picker over real ChannelListing rows (RecoverClient.tsx:44-52, rendered :262-303), five actions (:29-35), and a consequence preview carrying reviewsPreserved/asinPreserved/skuPreserved/skuCooldownRisk/blockers[]/warnings[]/before-after identifiers/ETA (:71-84), debounced per tuple (:139-188), with execution refused while blockers exist (:193).
- eBay has a per-listing version history with reason grading: VersionHistoryDrawer.tsx:1-13 lists the last 10 snapshots (auto / manual / pre-publish / pre-restore) over POST /api/ebay/cockpit/snapshot (:98) and /snapshot/restore (:120); restore is non-destructive because the API snapshots current state as 'pre-restore' first.
- ebay-cockpit/publish/PublishDrawer.tsx:18-22,142 offers 'Restore pre-publish snapshot' as the FAILURE-path escape hatch, and ApplyToSiblingsModal.tsx:14-19,298 snapshots every target as 'pre-template-apply' so a bulk write is undoable per target.
- WorkflowTab.tsx:3-21 is a full stage gate (DRAFT→REVIEW→APPROVED→PUBLISHED) with SLA, move-stage-with-comment, per-stage thread, transition history, Detach behind useConfirm, and assignees over POST/DELETE /api/products/:id/workflow/assignments (:185, :215). The studio has no workflow surface (navigation.tsx:12-19) and 0 audit findings mention it.
- Date-based lifecycle scheduling exists on the legacy page: PricingTab.tsx:14-20 lists ScheduledProductChange rows (status flips + price moves) and cancels PENDING ones via /api/products/scheduled-changes/:id/cancel (:153, :221).
- Per-coordinate lifecycle-adjacent verbs the studio never calls: POST /products/:id/listings/:ch/:mp/publish (ChannelListingTab.tsx:1065; route marketplaces.routes.ts:990-1001), /replicate (:794; route :663-684), PATCH /products/:id/auto-publish-content (:150; route marketplaces.routes.ts:344), GET /api/ebay/pull-listing (:237; route ebay.routes.ts:349).
- PublishReviewModal.tsx:1-16 is a preflight-then-publish flow over POST /publish-preflight (marketplaces.routes.ts:1161): per-coordinate ready/blocked with reasons, the RESOLVED title/price that would be sent, the EFFECTIVE per-channel action (amazon-live / amazon-dry-run / amazon-unconfigured / mark-active), per-row progress and Retry-failed. Zero audit findings mention publish-preflight.
- SuppressionCard.tsx:3-24 lists active Amazon suppressions with per-reason 'fix in <card>' jump links and a Mark-resolved write (PATCH /api/listings/amazon/suppressions/:id). The studio surfaces suppression strings but has no resolve verb.
- The studio's restore family SUPERSEDES SnapshotModal rather than lagging it: useRestorePoints.ts:6-24 replaced the free datetime-local with server-recorded instants, shows non-restorable points with a reason instead of hiding them, and routes formula points via restoreVia rather than parsing the action name. Do not port SnapshotModal.tsx back.
- MatrixTab.tsx:568-618,650-662 has a cross-surface undo/redo stack (⌘Z / ⌘⇧Z) spanning master and channel-pricing writes. The DS grid only has AG's cell-edit stack (GridSheet.tsx:109-110, undoRedoCellEditing true, limit 200), which is not the same guarantee.
- ImagePublishHistory.tsx:13-15 merges AmazonImageFeedJob + ChannelImagePublishJob into one job feed with a Retry that cancels the original and re-queues (POST /api/image-publish-jobs/:jobId/retry). The studio has no job-retry surface.
- /api/listings/bulk-action already REFUSES lifecycle: listings-syndication.routes.ts:3335-3339 returns 409 CHANNEL_WORKFLOW_REQUIRED for publish/unpublish with 'A local status flag cannot confirm a marketplace change.' Valid actions are resync, set-price, follow-master, unfollow-master, set-pricing-rule (:3343), and jobs are created with isRollbackable:false (:3388).
- Alias archive is deliberately unwired on the client, in writing: useChannelSheet.ts:442-444 — 'Archiving (DELETE) is deliberately NOT wired here: it takes a live listing's local record out of the sheet, so it asks first, separately.' The server side is an ARCHIVE-not-delete decision documented at product-studio.routes.ts:985-989.
- The studio's ONLY per-coordinate lifecycle verb is 'offer-toggle' (sheet/channel/channelActions.ts:163), and the file's own header (:16-22) states it 'PREFLIGHT truthfully and refuse[s] the outward-facing half'; the other two registry verbs are 'broadcast-to-listings' (:309) and 'open-record' (:381).
Contradicts or narrows
- critic:coverage — 'the legacy edit/tabs/ tree … dismissed because _studio/ does not import it'. NARROWED: the tree is not merely un-imported, it is the LIVE route. edit/page.tsx:14-37 renders ProductEditClient, which imports 16 tabs plus both cockpits (:56-72), and edit/studio/page.tsx:1-8 states the studio is mounted beside it and the old page 'stays the live route until the Owner swaps them'. These capabilities are in production today, so the swap is a capability-loss event, not a migration of dead code.
- critic:design — 'There is no Listings surface — the listing exists only as a field of a variant row, so lifecycle has nowhere to attach'. CORRECTED in scope: true of _studio/, false of the product edit page as a whole. MasterDataTab.tsx:915,999,1229 is a shipped 'Listing Hub' — one row per (channel, marketplace) coordinate from GET /api/products/:id/all-listings (:1038), carrying listingStatus/offerActive/price/lastSyncedAt (:917-927), with a Live/Draft/Paused/Ended/Error vocabulary (:934-940) separate from readiness (:967-978). The noun the Owner is asking for has a working precedent to copy, not to invent.
- critic:design — 'The scope is single-valued … so plural intents that dominate real lifecycle work ("off everywhere except DE") cannot be stated at all'. NARROWED to the studio: the legacy hub states exactly that intent today — pauseNonIT (MasterDataTab.tsx:1170-1192) renders as a 'Pause non-IT' button (:1251-1255), alongside Activate all / Pause all (:1149,:1256-1261) and a multi-select selection bar (:1069-1105,:1269-1299).
- critic:coverage — 'the restore/recover family … is the nearest existing "put it back"'. EXTENDED: the nearest existing 'put it back' for a LISTING is not that family at all. POST /products/:id/listings/:listingId/snapshots/:snapshotId/restore (product-studio.routes.ts:930-948, service listing-snapshot.service.ts:178,269,309) restores to draft with an undoSnapshotId and touches no marketplace — and /usr/bin/grep over apps/web/src finds ZERO consumers (positive control: 'restore-points' = 6 hits). Zero of the 180 audit findings mention it. The mechanism is built and routed; only the surface is missing.
- The audit roster's coverage of the legacy tree is thinner than its 27 'tabs/' mentions suggest. /usr/bin/grep over audit-findings.json returns 0 for: 'Listing Hub', 'MarketAvailabilityCard', 'pauseNonIT', 'WorkflowTab', 'SnapshotModal', 'VersionHistoryDrawer', 'ApplyToSiblingsModal', 'ImagePublishHistory', 'MatrixTab', 'publish-preflight', and the listing-snapshot routes. Approval workflow, per-listing version history, bulk-apply undo-per-target, and preflight-then-publish-with-progress were never enumerated as capabilities at all.
- Phase-0 ground artefacts are NOT on disk. find over the scratchpad for 'ground*' returns nothing; tsc-web.txt is 0 bytes and tests-index.txt is a findings index, not a test inventory. No claim in this report depends on a test assertion or on a typecheck result, and 'the uncommitted tree is green' remains unmeasured.

▸ Full report
## 0. Two housekeeping facts first
**The Phase-0 ground files are not on disk.** `find /private/tmp/claude-501/…/scratchpad -name 'ground*'` returns nothing. What is there is `tests-index.txt` (360 lines — a *findings* index, not a test inventory) and `tsc-web.txt`, which is **0 bytes**. So the "typecheck ran" and "104 vitest files were read" inputs I was told to build on are not available to me; I did not rely on them, and no claim below rests on a test's assertion. Anyone downstream should treat "the tree is green" as unmeasured.
**The legacy page is not legacy yet — it is the live route.** `edit/page.tsx:14-37` renders `ProductEditClient`; `edit/studio/page.tsx:1-8` states in its own header that the studio "is mounted BESIDE the old one… `/products/[id]/edit` still renders `ProductEditClient`… and stays the live route until the Owner swaps them." `ProductEditClient.tsx` imports 16 tabs plus both cockpits (`:56-72`). Everything below is therefore not archaeology: it is capability that **operators have today** and that disappears at the swap.
## 1. The single biggest finding: the Listings surface the design critic says has no precedent **already exists** — on the page the studio replaces
`MasterDataTab.tsx:915` opens a section literally headed `── MA.1 / OL.A.1 Listing Hub (Market Availability + per-coordinate state) ──`. `MarketAvailabilityCard` (`:999`, rendered at `:629`) is the object the critic says the studio lacks:
- **One row per real coordinate**, fed by `GET /api/products/:id/all-listings` (`:1038`; route at `marketplaces.routes.ts:256-258`). Row type `MarketRow` (`:917-927`) carries `listingId`, `listingStatus`, `offerActive`, `title`, `hasDescription`, `price`, `lastSyncedAt`.
- **A liveness vocabulary orthogonal to readiness**: `STATUS_META` (`:934-940`) maps ACTIVE→"Live", DRAFT→"Draft", INACTIVE→"Paused", ENDED→"Ended", ERROR→"Error", *and separately* a readiness dot (`readinessFor`, `:967-978`) and a relative "synced" age (`relTime`, `:983-997`). That is exactly consequence (b) the critic says the studio has no axis for — the old page has both meters side by side.
- **Plural intent, stated directly.** `pauseNonIT` (`:1170-1192`) renders as a button labelled **"Pause non-IT"** (`:1251-1255`). That is the critic's "off everywhere except DE" — already shipped, hard-coded to IT. Plus `setAll(true/false)` → "Activate all"/"Pause all" (`:1149`, `:1256-1261`), and multi-select (`:1069-1077`, select-all at `:1220-1227`) driving `setSelectedAvailability` (`:1081-1105`) with an "N selected" action bar (`:1269-1299`).
- **Optimistic write + rollback + honest toast** on every one of those paths (`:1088/:1098`, `:1128/:1142`, `:1153/:1164`, `:1177/:1187`), all against the same `PATCH /api/products/:id/offer-availability`.
- **Drill-in** to a per-channel cockpit (`COCKPIT_CHANNELS`, `:930`), a **cross-channel propagation drawer** (`CrossChannelMatrix`, `:1421`), a **Publish Review** modal over the selection (`:1424-1430`), and a link out to catalogue-wide automation rules (`:1244-1251`).
The studio's counterpart is `drawer/panes/ListingsPane.tsx`, which is one listing (`:11-16`: "🔴 ONE listing, not a map"), read-only by decision (`:5-9`), inside a per-row drawer. The studio's only per-coordinate write is `offer-toggle` in `sheet/channel/channelActions.ts:163`, single-coordinate, and its own header (`:16-22`) says it "refuse[s] the outward-facing half". **The studio does not have the Listing Hub; the page it replaces does.**
The audit never saw it: `/usr/bin/grep` over `audit-findings.json` returns **0** for "Listing Hub", "MarketAvailabilityCard", "pauseNonIT" and "Pause non-IT" (positive control: "tabs/" 27, "ebay-cockpit" 20).
## 2. "Put it back": a built, routed, **unwired** listing snapshot/restore
`RestoreMode.tsx:13-19` says master restore "is a DIFFERENT mechanism from the listing snapshot/restore of PES.5 §12", which "writes captured values onto a `ChannelListing` and sets `isPublished = false`; it has an `undoSnapshotId`". That mechanism is real and it is on the **studio's own** route namespace:
- `GET /products/:id/listings/:listingId/snapshots` — `product-studio.routes.ts:900`
- `POST …/snapshots` — operator checkpoint, `:912`
- `POST …/snapshots/:snapshotId/restore` — restore-to-draft, auto-snapshots current state first, returns `undoSnapshotId`, "the marketplace is NOT touched" — `:930-948`
- service: `apps/api/src/services/pim/listing-snapshot.service.ts:178,269,309`
**Set claim, `/usr/bin/grep` over `apps/web/src`: zero consumers.** Positive control: `restore-points` returns 6 hits in the same sweep. And zero of the 180 audit findings mention these routes. This is the "put it back" half of the Owner's sentence, already paid for, with no surface.
Its working precedent is on the legacy tree: eBay's `VersionHistoryDrawer.tsx:1-13` lists the last 10 snapshots with reason (`auto`/`manual`/`pre-publish`/`pre-restore`) and a Restore button, over `POST /api/ebay/cockpit/snapshot` (`:98`) and `/snapshot/restore` (`:120`); `PublishDrawer.tsx:18-22,142` offers "Restore pre-publish snapshot" as the failure-path escape hatch; `ApplyToSiblingsModal.tsx:14-19,298` snapshots every target as `pre-template-apply` so a bulk write is undoable per target. **0 audit findings mention any of these three.**
## 3. `/products/[id]/recover` — a working per-listing lifecycle console
`recover/page.tsx` + `RecoverClient.tsx` (572 lines) is a linear flow whose shape is what §1 and §2 need: **a picker over real `ChannelListing` rows** (`RecoverClient.tsx:44-52` — id, channel, marketplace, `externalListingId`, `listingStatus`; rendered `:262-303`), five actions (`REPUBLISH_IN_PLACE`, `DELETE_RELIST_SAME`, `SAME_ASIN_NEW_SKU`, `NEW_ASIN_SAME_SKU`, `FULL_RESET`, `:29-35,82-88`), a **consequence preview before the verb runs** — `reviewsPreserved` / `asinPreserved` / `skuPreserved` / `skuCooldownRisk` / `blockers[]` / `warnings[]` / before-and-after identifiers / ETA (`:71-84`) — debounced per (listing, action, newSku) tuple (`:139-188`), execution **blocked while `blockers.length > 0`** (`:193`), and a 20-event history strip. This is the REACH + REVERSIBILITY model the GridAction type lacks, already expressed as data. It is not in the studio: the studio's API sweep shows no `/recover` call.
## 4. Whole capability classes with no studio tab at all
Studio tabs are fixed at `_studio/navigation.tsx:12-19`: variants, variation-order, presentation, sheet(Information), images(Media), analytics, activity, errors, shopify-family, shopify-metafields. Against the 16 legacy tabs:
| Capability | Legacy home | Studio replacement |
|---|---|---|
| **Approval workflow / stage gate** — attach workflow, move stage with comment, per-stage thread, transition history, **assignees** (`POST/DELETE /workflow/assignments`, `:185,215`), Detach behind confirm | `WorkflowTab.tsx:3-21` | **none** (0 audit findings mention it) |
| **Date-based lifecycle scheduling** — `ScheduledProductChange` status flips + price moves, list PENDING/APPLIED/CANCELLED, **cancel** (`PricingTab.tsx:14-20,153,221`) | PricingTab | **none** — confirms audit "no date-based lifecycle scheduling at all" |
| **Per-coordinate publish** — `POST /products/:id/listings/:ch/:mp/publish` (`ChannelListingTab.tsx:1065`; route `marketplaces.routes.ts:990-1001`) and **`/replicate`** (`:794`; route `:663-684`) | ChannelListingTab | **none** |
| **Multi-coordinate preflight → publish with per-row progress and Retry-failed** over `POST /publish-preflight` (`marketplaces.routes.ts:1161`) | `PublishReviewModal.tsx:1-16` | **none** (0 findings mention `publish-preflight`) |
| **Pull live state from the channel** — `GET /api/ebay/pull-listing` (`ChannelListingTab.tsx:237`) | ChannelListingTab | **none** |
| **Suppression management** — list active suppressions, per-reason "fix in <card>" jump, **Mark resolved** (`PATCH /api/listings/amazon/suppressions/:id`) | `SuppressionCard.tsx:3-24` | studio has `suppress` strings but no resolve verb |
| **Time-travel snapshot + restore with per-field coverage grading** (`/state?at=` + `/restore`) | `SnapshotModal.tsx:1-14` | **superseded and improved** — `useRestorePoints.ts:6-24` replaces the free `datetime-local` with real recorded instants and refuses to invent them. Keep the studio's. |
| **Cell-level undo/redo (⌘Z) across master and channel cells** | `MatrixTab.tsx:568-618,650-662` | partial — `GridSheet.tsx:109-110` sets `undoRedoCellEditing:true, limit 200`, but that is AG's cell-edit stack, not a cross-surface write history |
| Relations, Compliance/certificates, per-locale translation completeness, SEO, tier prices, image publish jobs + **retry** (`ImagePublishHistory.tsx:13-15`) | 6 tabs | **none**; Relationships was removed by Owner ruling 2026-09-12 (`navigation.tsx:9-11`) |
## 5. One containment note
`/api/listings/bulk-action` (`listings-syndication.routes.ts:3312-3346`) is the catalogue-wide bulk writer, and it **already refuses lifecycle**: `publish`/`unpublish` return `409 CHANNEL_WORKFLOW_REQUIRED` with the message "A local status flag cannot confirm a marketplace change." Valid actions are `resync`, `set-price`, `follow-master`, `unfollow-master`, `set-pricing-rule`, and `isRollbackable: false` (`:3388`). That refusal is the correct precedent for every verb designed in §1: **the server already says a local flag is not a marketplace fact.** Any Listings surface must inherit that sentence rather than re-invent a local toggle.

## The spine

Presence — the listing state model for the Product Edit Studio

#### What it is
The studio has no listing object, so lifecycle has nowhere to attach. Presence adds one: a coordinate record at (product × channel × market × account × alias), carrying two never-merged axes — a declared **intent** and an observed **fact** with a mandatory as-of — plus a derived verdict. Verbs gain typed `reach` and `reversal{verb, fidelity}` on `GridAction`/`ActionImpact`, so the reversal sentence is rendered from the type instead of authored. Plural intents ("off everywhere except DE", "stop selling now") are stated as a server-computed plan bound to a `preflightToken` that enumerates every five-level target and its version — the structural answer to an ambient coordinate. Nine upstream defects are preconditions, not follow-ups: a new button over them ships them with an operator's name on it.
Schema
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntent String? — the declared intent: NONE|DRAFT|LIVE|HELD|WITHDRAWN|ENDED|DISCONTINUED|RELEASED. Nullable and NEVER backfilled: null means 'never stated' and the read derives a provisional intent from the legacy columns, carried on the wire WITH its provenance so no surface can present it as an operator decision.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntentAt / presenceIntentBy / presenceIntentReason
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceEffectiveFrom DateTime? / presenceUntil DateTime? — the time axis. Wave 1 stores and DISPLAYS them; nothing executes them (see openQuestions on the scheduler).
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.channelFact String? / channelFactAt DateTime? / channelFactVia String? / channelFactDetail Json? — the observation and its as-of, deliberately distinct from lastSyncedAt, whose own header (studio-sheet.service.ts:1416-1420) warns it records when a sync last RAN. Null = UNKNOWN. NO BACKFILL: rows written before these columns existed must derive UNKNOWN, never DRAFT. This is explicitly the arm that broke design #2 — the eBay end path (ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1961,1984) already wrote {listingStatus:'ENDED', offerActive:false, externalListingId:null}, and a naive derivation reads those as 'never published'.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.endedAt / endedBy / endedReason — 'ended by us, deliberately, then', distinct from listingStatus happening to say ENDED.
- ADDITIVE — new model ListingIdentity (the tombstone AND the registry — it is READ, which is what makes RELEASED mean something). Fields: workspaceId String @default(dbgenerated("NULLIF(current_setting('nexus.workspace_id', true), '')")) [412 of 432 models carry this; design #3 omitted it]; id; productId String (plain string, NO Prisma relation — the AuditLog property at products-catalog.routes.ts:1902-1908 that lets it survive the purge); sku; channel; marketplace; channelConnectionId String?; aliasKey String @default(""); externalListingId; externalParentId; fulfillmentMethod String?; offerSku String?; firstSeenAt; lastSeenAt; retiredAt DateTime?; retiredReason; retiredBy; doNotRecreate Boolean @default(false); lastSnapshotId String?. @@unique([workspaceId, channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, aliasKey, externalListingId]). READ BY: every create/publish path (46 non-test channelListing.create/upsert sites in apps/api/src) — a RELEASED or doNotRecreate identity refuses recreation at that coordinate without an explicit operator override.
- NOT PROPOSED — a new ListingPresenceEvent table. ProductEvent already exists, is already FK-free by design (schema.prisma:15475-15478: 'plain string FKs (no Prisma relation) so we never block deletes on the parent'), already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing', is already emitted (bullmq-sync.worker.ts, listings-syndication.routes.ts, amazon-flat-file.routes.ts) and already read (audit-log.routes.ts). Presence emits there with aggregateId = ChannelListing.id and the full coordinate + external id in `data`. A third store would re-create the confirmed defect that lifecycle history is unreachable because Activity reads the event store while lifecycle writes the audit log. EbayListingIndex (schema.prisma:14078-14118, @@unique([workspaceId, marketplace, itemId]), with endedAt and relistedFromItemId, never deleted) already answers 'what ItemID did we retire' for eBay — so no proposal here may claim a new table is 'the only thing that can'.
- NOT PROPOSED, and not needed — ChannelListingSnapshot.channelListingId → nullable + SetNull (BREAKING; the column is non-null at schema.prisma:1895 and both readers key on it). The release precondition (a confirmed-gone fact before any purge) removes the need for it.
- NOT PROPOSED, and not needed — OutboundSyncQueue FKs Cascade → SetNull (BREAKING, model-wide, breaks the ordinary push path). Both columns are ALREADY nullable (schema.prisma:6824,6827), so the delist rows can simply be enqueued with productId:null and channelListingId:null, coordinate in the row's own columns plus payload. That is a route change with no migration and it is the whole of PD-1/ER-1.
- NOT DECLARED — ChannelListing.variationExcluded. family-projection.service.ts:389 cites migration `20260911a_vp2_variation_excluded`; I checked and it DOES NOT EXIST in packages/database/prisma/migrations (only 20260911020000_information_formula_destination, 20260911_category_taxonomies and five 20260912_lx* folders). The raw-SQL helpers stay. Declaring a column the database may not have is an outage for every concurrent session, per the reasoning already written at :387-396.
- SEQUENCING (binding, not optional) — apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma. MEMORY records that apps/api/.env pointed at a local Docker pg on 09-11 and that only Product.version (54 vs 51) separated the two; discriminate, never infer.
API
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence — every real coordinate for the product: intent (with provenance when derived), fact, as-of, verdict, gate mode, fan-out set, and per-verb availability WITH its reach and reversal, answered by the server so a preflight is never a client guess. No coordinate required (read-only, no target).
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/one?channel=&market=&accountId=&aliasKey= — one coordinate, 400 on any missing dimension, reusing requireCoordinate's shape verbatim (product-studio.routes.ts:180-187, ruling #573 'A projection is one channel on one market') widened to require account and alias.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/verify { coordinates[], reason } — READS the channel, writes only the channelFact* columns. NOT a fourth live-read seam: it extends the existing operator-triggered read path (ChannelTruthPanel + POST /live-channel-images/refresh, product-images-crud.routes.ts) with a listing-existence field, and reuses getListingsItem / getItemListingStatus (ebay-trading-api.service.ts:316), both of which already exist. Verdict is four-valued and 'could-not-ask' is load-bearing. Enqueued automatically after every lifecycle write, so a lane verifies its claim instead of asserting it.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/plan { verb, targets[], options } — the server-side preflight. Returns an ActionImpact-shaped plan with per-coordinate consequences, reach, reversal{verb,fidelity}, gate mode, server-computed fan-out (named coordinates, never a count), refusals each NAMING the verb that resolves them, the operational-preflight result for any local-destructive verb, plus a planId and a short-TTL preflightToken binding the enumerated five-level target set and each target's version. No writes.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/apply { planId, preflightToken, confirmPhrase? } — applies exactly that plan. Refuses a missing, expired, or set-mismatched token; 409s per target on version drift. Per-target results in the shipped MarketOfferResult shape (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:46-55: updated/skippedFba/unchanged/failed/results[]/error/remaining) with NO aggregate success. THIS IS THE ONLY WRITER — plan/apply must not become the fourth ChannelListing removal path.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/history — ProductEvent for the coordinate, including retired identities read from ListingIdentity.
- PATCH /api/products/:id/aliases/:aliasId { status: 'ACTIVE' | 'ARCHIVED' } — one new body field plus a restoreAlias writer beside archiveAlias (listing-alias.service.ts:194-197), so archive stops being permanent. Refused while the alias holds identity with a non-terminal intent.
- AUTHORISATION, on every route above and as a wave-1 precondition on the existing ones — an explicit in-file preHandler naming the required permission. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (rbac-hook.ts:9-13,31: 'resolve + LOG what would be denied, but allow the request'), and apps/api/src/routes/amazon-flat-file.routes.ts contains ZERO occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate over a route that hard-deletes ChannelListing rows from a caller-supplied target array (:1806-1819). Securing the operator path with a typed ASIN while leaving the endpoint open secures the wrong half.
- PRECONDITION P1 — enqueue the delist rows with productId:null and channelListingId:null (both already nullable), coordinate in the row's own columns plus payload, and report channelCascadeDispatched read back AFTER commit rather than the pre-commit create count.
- PRECONDITION P2 — the worker must read targetChannel from the queue row it just loaded, not from job.data (bullmq-sync.worker.ts:88,146 vs outbound-enqueue.ts:52-58).
- PRECONDITION P3 — Amazon delist must resolve the seller SKU (Offer.sku, or product.sku) and never pass externalListingId, which flat-file-pull.service.ts:267 populates with the ASIN.
- PRECONDITION P4 — eBay delist must resolve the connection that OWNS the ItemID, not tryResolveConnection({channel:'EBAY', primary:true}) (channel-delist.service.ts:263), and must stop classifying 'item cannot be accessed' / 'invalid item' as success (:227,231,290-293).
- PRECONDITION P5 (the one that makes WITHDRAWN honest) — pushAmazonPrice must select and honour offerClosedAt/presenceIntent. Today it selects only {id, platformAttributes} (pricing-outbound.service.ts:140-149) and patchListingPrice issues op:'replace' on /attributes/purchasable_offer (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:687-699) — the very instance close removed with op:'delete' — so a reprice silently resurrects a closed offer and stamps syncStatus:'IN_SYNC'. The refusal exists one path away at outbound-sync.service.ts:904-910 and this path inherits none of it. Extract it as one shared assertPushAllowed().
- PRECONDITION P6 — applyDelistResultToQueue must set holdUntil on a retryable park, and dispatchSync must REFUSE lifecycle syncTypes outright rather than routing them into syncToAmazon/syncToWoocommerce update paths (outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623 has no lifecycle branch; the BullMQ worker at :141-158 does).
- PRECONDITION P7 — no adapter may escalate: remove the Amazon UNPUBLISH→DELETE fallback (channel-delist.service.ts:155-158; patchListingsItem exists in the client and is not wired) and the duck-typed Shopify updateProduct→deleteProduct fallback (:192-203). Refuse instead.
- PRECONDITION P8 — narrow both removal services to the full five-level coordinate, INCLUDING the pre-read (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:636-639 and :645-647; amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:52-55 and :60-62). Narrowing the delete alone is strictly worse than today. Same for amazon-market-offer.service.ts:66-68, sync-control.routes.ts:895-898 and marketplaces.routes.ts:317's hardcoded aliasKey:''.
- PRECONDITION P9 — move isLiveOnChannel (unchanged, as identityHeld) and the new sellingRisk into packages/shared and repoint products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682 and :1857-1860 at them.
- REQUIRED BEFORE ANY relist PROMISE — exercise restoreToDraft against a captured request and read the BODY, not the status.
