The route ships at product-studio.routes.ts:938-946 with ZERO web callers (/usr/bin/grep over apps/web/src returns nothing), and SNAPSHOT_FIELDS deliberately excludes externalListingId (listing-snapshot.service.ts:70-83). 'Recoverable with loss' is a promise this path keeps.
- ETSY — PublishReadiness (channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:317-322) describes amazon, ebay and shopify only, and modeForChannel returns null for Etsy AND WooCommerce (:323-332). Either it gains an etsy branch, or wave 1 states plainly that Etsy presence is READ-ONLY. It must not be silently caught by the WooCommerce containment rule.

▸ The full state model
# Presence — a listing state model for the Product Edit Studio
## 0. Method note, and three corrections to the inputs
I re-derived every claim below from the working tree with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed`. Three inputs are wrong and the design must not inherit them.
**(a) The eBay hold EXISTS and ships.** `ebay-columns.ts:157` defines a per-row `Action` enum `['', 'deactivate', 'end', 'skip']` whose `deactivate` is documented as "set the live offer to quantity 0 (keeps the ItemID — the safe hide) … to this market only"; the server half runs at `ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1874-1901` (zeroes `<market>_qty`, calls `pushOffersOnly`, reports "deactivated — quantity 0 on the live offer (ItemID kept)"). A second, channel-agnostic reversible hold ships as `ZERO_PIN` at `sync-control.routes.ts:934-960`, reversed by `FOLLOW`. Any model that greys out eBay's hold is lying about a shipped capability. **Shopify's hold also exists** — `channel-delist.service.ts:192-196` writes `{product:{id, status:'draft'}}`.
**(b) The cited migration does not exist.** `family-projection.service.ts:389` names `20260911a_vp2_variation_excluded`; `ls packages/database/prisma/migrations | /usr/bin/grep -i "2026091\|vp2"` returns only `20260911020000_information_formula_destination`, `20260911_category_taxonomies` and five `20260912_lx*` folders. The comment is a claim, not a measurement. `variationExcluded` stays raw-SQL and is not declared here.
**(c) The market fallback is `defaultMarket`, not localStorage — with a one-tick window that is worse.** `contracts.tsx:649` falls back to `defaultMarket(baseOptions)` (`scopes.ts:174-183`, sorted by channel count then alphabetically). `readLastMarket()` is real but runs in an effect at `contracts.tsx:888-893` that *corrects the URL a tick later*. So the coordinate on first paint and the coordinate one tick later can differ, silently. That is the targeting hazard, and it is sharper than "it reads localStorage".
## 1. The object
Presence is one record per **coordinate**: `(product, channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, aliasKey)` — the exact compound key `ChannelListing` already carries twice (`schema.prisma:1785-1786`). It is not new storage: `useWorkspaceDestination.ts:6-14` already resolves `{…, aliasKey, listing:{id, productId, aliasKey, version}}` and `StudioTabHost.tsx:66-75` uses it only as a mount gate.
**Fulfilment is a level BELOW the coordinate, not a sixth dimension.** `Offer` is unique on `(workspaceId, channelListingId, fulfillmentMethod)` with its own `sku`, `price`, `quantity`, `isActive` (`schema.prisma:2287-2321`, comment at :2295: "Different SKU per fulfillment method"). So a verb that touches an offer takes `offerScope: 'all' | 'FBM' | 'FBA'`; the coordinate stays five-level. This makes "withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA" statable and answers "which seller SKU does release use" — `Offer.sku` for the named scope. `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63` refuses on any FBA signal; that refusal survives as the default for `offerScope:'all'`, and is no longer the only behaviour.
**Where it lives:** the first item of each existing channel group in the nav drawer, above `Listing information` (`StudioSubheader.tsx:38`), plus one product-wide roll-up under THIS PRODUCT. It is a different noun from the sheet — the sheet is content (cells), presence is state (identity, fact, fate) — so D9 holds. `ListingsPane` stays singular and read-only; its own header says why (`ListingsPane.tsx:12-16`) and nothing here overturns it.
## 2. Two axes, never merged
`ScopeBarReadiness` (`ScopeBar.tsx:36-52`) measures *can I publish*. Presence is orthogonal. **ScopeBar is not changed** — it has nine consumers outside the studio and a second axis there is a shared-component change nobody has approved.
**INTENT** — declared, durable, per coordinate, with `at / by / reason / effectiveFrom / until`:
| intent | means | identity | reversal |
|---|---|---|---|
| `NONE` | no row | — | create |
| `DRAFT` | row exists, never sent | none | free |
| `LIVE` | intended to be selling | held | — |
| `HELD` | we send nothing; the channel keeps serving what it last received | held | `resume`, exact |
| `WITHDRAWN` | the offer is off on the channel; listing, identity and reviews stand | held | `restore-offer`, revalidated |
| `ENDED` | ended on the channel; identity and content kept locally | **kept** | `relist`, lossy |
| `DISCONTINUED` | ended, and never recreate | kept | none (operator may downgrade to `ENDED`) |
| `RELEASED` | identity deliberately surrendered | moved to `ListingIdentity` | none |
`DISCONTINUED` is the supplier-drops-the-line terminal the roster had no word for. It sits between `ENDED` (which invites relist) and `RELEASED` (which surrenders the ASIN/ItemID).
**FACT** — what the channel last said. `UNKNOWN | SELLING | NOT_SELLING | SUPPRESSED | ABSENT | REFUSED`, with `observedAt` and `observedVia ∈ {channel-read, write-ack, inbound-report, operator-attest}`. **`UNKNOWN` is the default, is never backfilled, and never renders as agreement** — the doctrine already shipped one tab away at `ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:3-13` ("'not checked' is not 'no drift'"). `REFUSED` (the channel declined to answer *us*) is split from `ABSENT` (it answered and has no such identity) because `channel-delist.service.ts:227,231` folds `/item cannot be accessed/` and `/invalid item/` into `ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS` and returns `{success:true}` at :290-293 — an ownership refusal recorded today as a completed removal.
**VERDICT** — derived on read, never stored: `agrees | pending | diverged | unknown | unreachable`. A fact older than the last intent change with nothing in flight degrades to `unknown`, not to green.
This replaces the current liveness test at its root. Both row builders compute `listing && listing.externalListingId && listing.isPublished → 'live'` (`studio-sheet.service.ts:1483-1487`, `sheet-rows.service.ts:271`) — a claim about our record printed as `readiness.ts:53` "Live · Already published on this channel". Neither reads `offerClosedAt`, `listingStatus` or `syncPaused`.
## 3. Liveness: two predicates, two names, never one
The survival rule ("never null `externalListingId`") and the liveness rule ("the id decides") collide unless they are separated. They are:
- `identityHeld(row)` = `externalListingId` present. **This is `isLiveOnChannel` unchanged** (`listingRisk.ts:47-49`), because `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:15-31` pins exactly this as intended ("a DRAFT row holding a real ItemID is LIVE", "isPublished:false does not make a real listing safe") against a measured family of 20 non-ACTIVE eBay rows with real ItemIDs. It is not redefined and not widened.
- `sellingRisk(row)` = `identityHeld` **AND** intent ∉ {`ENDED`,`DISCONTINUED`,`RELEASED`} **OR** fact = `SELLING`. This is the new one, and it is what a delete confirmation and the hard-delete preflight use.
Both move to `packages/shared` so `products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682` and `:1857-1860` — which today use `listingStatus IN (ACTIVE,INACTIVE) AND externalListingId NOT NULL`, an AND that excludes precisely those 20 rows — read the same definition as the studio.
## 4. Verbs — reach × reversibility, in the type system
`GridAction` carries one bit, `danger?: boolean` (`registry.ts:183-184`), whose own doc says destructive verbs are "grouped apart and rendered with danger tone by every adapter". Measured: `/usr/bin/grep -c danger menuAdapters.tsx` = **0** against a positive control of 5 for `label`; `actionMenuItems` maps to `{id,label,title,disabled,onSelect}` only (`menuAdapters.tsx:76-89`); `ActionConfirm.tsx:80,94` keys its danger tone on `needsTyping`. The bit is not weak, it is inert.
Add `reach: 'local' | 'local-destructive' | 'channel'` to **`GridAction`** (it gates availability and grouping before any preflight runs) and `reversal: { verb: string; fidelity: 'exact' | 'lossy' | 'none' }` to **`ActionImpact`** (it reaches the confirmation renderer). `validateImpact(impact)` receives only the impact (`registry.ts:264-273`) and therefore cannot see an action-level field — so a new `validateAction(action)` sits beside it and refuses a `reach:'channel'` verb whose preflight returns no `reversal`. **The reversal sentence is rendered from the field; a lane may not author it.** Every lie in the roster is an authored sentence.
| verb | reach | Amazon | eBay | Shopify | Etsy | reversal |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `hold` / `resume` | local | `syncPaused` | `syncPaused` | `syncPaused` | `syncPaused` | `resume`, exact |
| `withdraw-offer` / `restore-offer` | channel | `purchasable_offer` `op:'delete'` per market (`amazon-market-offer.service.ts:165-172`) — FBA refused unless `offerScope:'FBM'` | qty 0, ItemID kept (`ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1874-1901`) | `status:'draft'` (`channel-delist.service.ts:192-196`) | **disabled — Nexus holds a read-only Etsy client** (`etsy/read-client.ts:5`); `marketplaces/etsy.service.ts` PATCHes price, quantity and variation quantity only | `restore-offer`, **exact only after revalidation** |
| `end` | channel | `patchListingsItem` availability DISCONTINUED — **not** `deleteListingsItem` | `withdraw_by_inventory_item_group` / offer DELETE, **id kept** | unpublish | disabled | `relist`, lossy |
| `relist` | channel | ~30-day same-SKU cooldown (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1119-1122`) | SellSimilar mints a NEW ItemID | republish | disabled | `end`, lossy |
| `discontinue` / `undiscontinue` | local | intent only, blocks create | " | " | " | exact |
| `release` | channel + local | `deleteListingsItem` on `Offer.sku` | end + surrender ItemID | delete | disabled | **none** |
| `exclude-child` / `include-child` | local | `variationExcluded` | " | " | " | exact |
| `remove-variation` | channel | child offer close | `ReviseFixedPriceItem Delete=true` | variant delete | disabled | lossy |
| `verify` | channel, **read-only** | `getListingsItem` | `getItemListingStatus` | product read | listing read | n/a |
Two invariants: **an adapter may never perform a more destructive act than the one it was asked for** (violated twice today — `channel-delist.service.ts:155-158` "Amazon UNPUBLISH falling back to DELETE", and the duck-typed `typeof svcAny.updateProduct === 'function'` → `deleteProduct` at :192-203); and **a verb may not promise a reach the gate will not deliver** — under `gated`/`dry-run`/`sandbox`/unrecognised the impact downgrades to `reach:'local'` and carries `gateNote()` verbatim (`channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:347-373`).
## 5. Intent is a LOCK, not a label
This is the flaw that sank the winner. `presenceIntent` must be read, layer by layer, or the next cron undoes the safety stop:
| layer | today | required |
|---|---|---|
| enqueue | `syncPaused` checked | intent ∈ {HELD,WITHDRAWN,ENDED,DISCONTINUED,RELEASED} refuses enqueue |
| dispatch | `syncPaused` + `offerClosedAt` checked at `outbound-sync.service.ts:904-910` | same test, same helper |
| direct push | **`pricing-outbound.service.ts:140-149` selects only `{id, platformAttributes}`** and `patchListingPrice` issues `op:'replace'` on `/attributes/purchasable_offer` (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:687-699`) — the instance close deleted with `op:'delete'`. Replace-on-absent **recreates it**, then stamps `syncStatus:'IN_SYNC'` (:196-200) while `offerClosedAt` stays set | one shared `assertPushAllowed(listingId)` on every push path |
| create / re-activate | `confirmReconRow` upserts `listingStatus:'ACTIVE'` unconditionally (`listing-reconciliation.service.ts:503-536`) with `aliasKey:''` hardcoded; 46 non-test `channelListing.create/upsert` sites exist | consult `ListingIdentity`; `RELEASED`/`DISCONTINUED` refuses recreate without an explicit override |
A gate script asserts no writer sets two of `{isPublished, offerActive, syncPaused, presenceIntent}` in one statement. **Per-channel input truth table**, because `offerActive` is not uniform: it is consumed on Amazon only, and only at the next flat-file submit, where `flat-file.service.ts:3415-3420` injects `skip_offer`. On eBay, Shopify and Etsy it is written and read by nothing.
## 6. Composition, plurality and targeting
**Enumerate, never widen.** Every presence write names all five levels or fails closed. Today `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:645-647` and `amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:60-62` `deleteMany` on `(productId, channel, marketplace)`, destroying every account and every alias in the market; `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:66-68` and `sync-control.routes.ts:895-898` load with the same blind filter; `marketplaces.routes.ts:317` hardcodes `aliasKey:''`. Narrowing the delete without also narrowing the pre-read (`:636-639` / `:52-55`) is strictly worse than today.
**A roll-up is a distribution, not a state.** "Amazon: 3 selling, 1 ended, 1 unknown." A set's confidence is its weakest member's and its as-of its oldest member's; one `UNKNOWN` makes the set `UNKNOWN`; a set is never "mostly live". The weakest member gates whether a verb is offered, never the label.
**Plural intents are a PLAN.** `POST /presence/plan` takes a verb plus a target set and returns per-coordinate consequences, per-target `expectedVersion` and a short-TTL **`preflightToken`** binding the enumerated five-level set. `apply` refuses a token that is missing, expired, or whose set differs from the body's, and 409s per target on version drift. This is ruling #118's mechanism (asserted five times in `familyActions.vitest.test.ts:301-311,331-337`) made server-side, and it is the only thing that makes "off everywhere except DE", "off eBay entirely, keep Amazon" and **`stop-sale`** ("stop selling everywhere, keep everything, record why") statable against a single-valued scope (`contracts.tsx:954-957`, a 3-tuple with no alias dimension). The **selection lives on the presence grid**, which has its own rows and its own `BulkActionBar` — not on the sheet, so CH.1's toolbar is untouched.
**Fan-out is per verb × channel × market, computed server-side, itemised as named coordinates.** Correcting the winner: the Amazon EU shared-quantity pool (`amazon-eu-quantity-guard.ts:24-26`, nine markets, the 302-zero incident) is **not** a fan-out for `withdraw-offer` — that file's own header at :18-20 says "Per-market suppression is NOT a quantity operation — it is a Seller Central offer-close". It **is** a fan-out for any quantity-based hold, which is why `ZERO_PIN` on Amazon must stay behind the guard. A confirmation that cries wolf on the one safe per-market verb trains operators to click through the one that is not.
## 7. Making the irreversible step hard
`release` requires, re-checked server-side: intent = `ENDED` or `DISCONTINUED` (the ladder is walked); a fact of `ABSENT` or `NOT_SELLING` from a real `verify` inside a freshness window — never inferred; the **operational preflight** (`products-catalog.routes.ts:1666-1760` — open orders, bundles, FBA units, whose only caller today is `BulkActionBar.tsx:1104`) run and clear, on this and on every `reach:'local-destructive'` verb; a `ListingIdentity` tombstone written in the same transaction; `confirmPhrase` = **the real external id**, which `registry.ts:117` already demands ("A SKU, never 'DELETE'") and which the only shipped permanent-delete UI violates (`BulkActionBar.tsx:1129` arms on the literal word DELETE); and `holdUntil = +5 minutes` on the dispatched rows (`schema.prisma:6858`, the field added for exactly this).
**A transport failure is an UNKNOWN outcome** — never SUCCESS, never FAILURE. Today `applyDelistResultToQueue` (`channel-delist.service.ts:313-343`) parks a retryable failure PENDING with no `holdUntil`; the 60s cron re-routes it through `dispatchSync` (`outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623`, which has no lifecycle branch at all) into the ordinary update path and it finishes green at `:748-752`.
## 8. Waves
**Wave 1 (approval sought now):** the nine preconditions; additive columns; the read + `verify` + history; presence rendered honestly with two marks and an as-of; the DS type additions; `hold`/`resume`, `exclude`/`include`, `discontinue` — every verb whose reach is local. No channel-reaching verb ships.
**Wave 2:** `withdraw-offer`/`restore-offer`, `end`/`relist`, the plan/apply pair, the presence grid and its selection.
**Wave 3:** `release`, `remove-variation`, alias restore (inert until PES.5-ii drops the legacy indexes — `listing-alias.service.ts:98` still raises `AliasCreationBlockedError`).
The tree is not green: `layers.vitest.test.ts:55` is red on `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot` (`attribute-resolver.ts:25` defines it, `drawer/types.ts:352-376` has no entry) and `masterWrite.vitest.test.ts` has 5 failures. Wave 1 includes mapping `channelSnapshot` — presence is already leaking into the provenance axis before presence exists.
## 9. Honest cost
An honest model makes the studio look emptier. Most coordinates will read `UNKNOWN` on day one, where today they read a confident green "Live". That is the change, not a regression, and the Owner has to want it before it ships.

## The sequence

Waves are numbered because the dependencies are real. The rule that forces the order: the delist path today escalates an unpublish into a permanent delete, reads an ownership refusal as a completed removal, and parks failures where a cron reroutes them into the ordinary update path to finish green. A button over that ships every one of those defects with an operator's name on it.
0

### Wave 0 — Measure, guard, and stop lying (starts today; three lanes in parallel)
Starts immediately. Nothing blocks it.

Nothing here depends on the state model and two of the three items are live risks today, not after Presence ships. The unguarded routes are reachable now; the lying copy is on screen now; and four of the measurements gate work in Wave 1, so they must be started before Wave 1 is scheduled, not during it. Lane boundaries are clean: W0-SEC touches only route files and the auth manifest, W0-COPY touches only strings plus two one-line lane fixes, W0-MEAS writes nothing.

Lanes Lane W0-SEC (apps/api routes + lib/auth) · Lane W0-COPY (apps/web _studio strings, en.json/it.json, _components/BulkActionBar.tsx) · Lane W0-MEAS (read-only, no repo writes)

- W0-SEC: in-file preHandlers on POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove, POST /api/products/bulk-hard-delete, all six routes/etsy.ts posts, PUT /api/products/:id/matrix/channel-listing/:listingId and the three listing-recovery routes. Each names its permission explicitly and throws 403 regardless of NEXUS_RBAC_MODE. Add the matching entries to apps/api/src/lib/auth/permissions-manifest.ts ordered BEFORE the /api/products prefix rule at :439 (first-match-wins has already produced one unreachable rule here), and extend permissions-manifest-order.vitest.test.ts.
- W0-SEC: resolve the actor. userIdFor() returns the literal 'default-user' (products-catalog.routes.ts:82-84) and the literal appears at ten non-test sites; every guard added above should stamp the request's real user, or the register stays forged while the door is locked.
- W0-COPY: the honest-copy pass over the five lying surfaces — the /products hard-delete modal's three radio bodies (which contradict channel-delist.service.ts:150-166), readiness.ts:53's 'Live · Already published on this channel' and :56's unlisted string, ListingsPane.tsx:103's raw listingStatus, AliasBandCell.tsx:132's 'NOT LISTED' default (two lines, not one — bandTitle's `status.toLowerCase()` throws on null), and family-projection's two divergent excluded-variant reasons via one exported excludedReason() called from BOTH :551-552 and :1220.
- W0-COPY, load-bearing and not cosmetic: set `level:'none'` wherever `unavailable` is set in channelActions.ts (:224, :339). Today registry.ts:270-272 plus runAction.ts:70-73 replace the lane's honest refusal with the DS's internal string 'preflight failed but still asked for a confirmation'. Without this every honest refusal string written in this pass is unreachable code.
- W0-COPY: BulkActionBar must not arm while preflightError is set, and confirmPhrase comparison becomes exact and case-sensitive (:1128-1129 currently uppercases, so 'delete' arms the app's most destructive verb).
- W0-GATES (inside W0-SEC's lane to avoid a third writer in scripts/): repair scripts/check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs, which crashes on every run because it expects a bare `.dark {` block and tokens.css:450 declares a compound selector. It has therefore never convicted anything and its silence is not a pass. Capture the route-prisma census and the grid-kit ratchet attribution as this programme's recorded baseline (ratchet is RED at 34 against baseline 32; the studio owns exactly one importer, channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3, and clearing it yields 33 — still red on another lane's work).
- W0-MEAS: M1 WHICH DATABASE; M2 Offer coverage on Amazon coordinates (both DBs, RLS established first); M3 NULL distribution of channelConnectionId/aliasKey per coordinate per channel; M4 30-day count of green empty-patch STATUS_UPDATE rows; M5 the eBay out-of-stock preference in Seller Hub; M6 whether any Etsy listing_id is persisted; M7 re-run the _versionHistory count. Each reported with the connection string it was taken against — discriminate, never infer.1

### Wave 1 — Repair the delist path (the gate on everything after it)
Blocked by W0-SEC (guards must precede any change that widens what a destructive endpoint attempts) and W0-MEAS M3 (the five-level narrowing is a silent no-op against NULL-bearing rows) and M1 (any count quoted in this wave must name its database).

These are preconditions, not follow-ups: a new button over them ships the defect with an operator's name on it. Internal order matters as much as the wave boundary. No-escalation and outcome-honesty land FIRST, because the corrected liveness predicate (which widens what the cascade attempts by a measured ~20 rows on one eBay family alone) must not reach an adapter that still escalates Amazon UNPUBLISH to DELETE. Lane split is by FILE, not by channel, because channel-delist.service.ts carries all four adapters and cannot have three writers. W1-SHARED authors assertPushAllowed as a stub with tests before W1-QUEUE and W1-GATE consume it, so the helper is not written twice.

Lanes Lane W1-QUEUE owns channel-delist.service.ts, outbound-sync.service.ts, bullmq-sync.worker.ts, outbound-enqueue.ts and the cascade block of products-catalog.routes.ts · Lane W1-NARROW owns the removal and offer services (ebay-flat-file-delete, amazon-flat-file-remove, amazon-market-offer, sync-control.routes, marketplaces.routes, recovery.service, ebay-label-guard) · Lane W1-GATE owns amazon-sp-api.client.ts, the publish-gate service, pricing-outbound.service.ts and flat-file.service.ts's skip_offer path · Lane W1-SHARED owns packages/shared and authors assertPushAllowed first

- Step 1 — no escalation: delete the Amazon UNPUBLISH→DELETE fall-through (channel-delist.service.ts:151-166) and refuse instead until patchListingsItem exists in the client; delete the duck-typed Shopify updateProduct→deleteProduct path (:186-203); make delistShopify return {success:false, retryable:false} for both actions rather than the double-wrapped 2xx. A visible FAILED row beats a green lie. Expect and announce that the delist dashboard goes visibly red on day one.
- Step 2 — honest outcomes: split ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS into ENDED_CONFIRMED and COULD_NOT_ASK (the latter → outcome UNKNOWN, fact REFUSED); transport failure is UNKNOWN; applyDelistResultToQueue sets holdUntil on a retryable park; dispatchSync refuses lifecycle syncTypes outright (the WooCommerce containment measure in full); new errorCodes on OutboundSyncQueue.errorCode each with operator copy, because a code nobody renders is a different silence, not less of it.
- Step 3 — targeting: Amazon delist resolves the seller SKU and never passes the ASIN-bearing externalListingId (the producer side must join product:{select:{sku:true}} and carry sellerSku on the payload, because by drain time the Product row is gone); eBay resolves the connection that OWNS the ItemID rather than tryResolveConnection({channel:'EBAY',primary:true}); the worker reads targetChannel from the queue row it loaded; the eBay site-id resolution moves OUT of the auth try/catch and returns EBAY_UNKNOWN_MARKET, and siteIdForMarket gains a bidirectional GB/UK fold (it is called from reviseInventoryStatus, getItemListingStatus, getItemQuantities, reviseInventoryStatusBatch and tryRemoveVariationFromListing, so the defect is every Trading call, not just the delist).
- Step 4 — five-level narrowing, pre-read and write in the same edit, at every site listed in doFirst, with one shared coordinate-narrowing helper so the three lanes that touch this class of defect cannot diverge. Gated on M3.
- Step 5 — the push lock: one assertPushAllowed(listingId) on every push path, plus wiring closedMarketSet, which today has ZERO callers while its own comment claims 'used by every Amazon write stack so nothing can silently resurrect a closed offer'. Every claim of the form 'the guard already exists' in this programme needs its callers counted, not its comment read.
- Step 6 — the gate: deleteListingsItem calls getAmazonPublishMode() instead of reading process.env directly; no presence or lifecycle DB write on a dryRun result anywhere; closeMarketOffers also writes offerActive:false so the next flat-file submit does not clear skip_offer and re-open the buy box.
- Step 7 — cascade shape: nulled FKs plus coordinate on payload, holdUntil +5min, POST /api/products/delist-cascade/cancel (the worker already honours CANCELLED, so without this route the five minutes is a delay with nothing to undo to), channelCascadeDispatched read back after commit, channelSkipped naming Etsy coordinates that will stay live.
- Step 8 — packages/shared: identityHeld (isLiveOnChannel byte-identical, its vitest passing unchanged) plus sellingRisk, with the exports-map entry and a build; repoint products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682 and :1857-1860. NOTE: actually WIDENING the preflight/cascade predicate (PD-2) is an Owner decision and lands only after steps 1-6, never before.
- Step 9, conditional on M4 and the Owner's answer — the empty-patch arm: outbound-sync.service.ts:1063-1074 returns success with no dryRun field and :750-752 keys the green line on dryRun alone, so a STATUS_UPDATE that sent Amazon nothing lands SUCCESS. Until this is honest, nothing can distinguish 'confirmed stopped' from 'nothing was sent'.2

### Wave 2 — Additive schema, the read-only presence API, and the DS contract
Blocked by Wave 1 steps 1-6 (the schema is honest only if the writers are), plus W0-MEAS M1 for the both-databases sequencing rule.

Everything here is additive or read-only, so it can land while the tree is still red elsewhere, and it is the substrate three later waves need. The four lanes have disjoint file sets, which is what makes them safely concurrent against one uncommitted tree — the only shared seam is the wire type, so W2-READ publishes the response shape first and W2-WIRE imports the unions from the DS renderer rather than restating them (RowReadinessState is restated in four places today, which is a set claim that goes stale). W2-DS owns .d.ts regeneration exclusively: 52 of 243 committed declarations are stale right now, including the two account components the picker is told to reuse.

Lanes Lane W2-SCHEMA (packages/database, migration folders staged OUTSIDE prisma/migrations until apply time per hub ruling #12) · Lane W2-READ (apps/api read paths and new GET routes; must not write) · Lane W2-DS (apps/web/src/design-system/** only, including every .d.ts regeneration) · Lane W2-WIRE (apps/web _studio types and loaders)

- SCHEMA, all additive, nullable and NEVER backfilled, applied to BOTH databases and verified before being declared in schema.prisma: ChannelListing.presenceIntent + presenceIntentAt/By/Reason; presenceEffectiveFrom/presenceUntil (stored and displayed, executed by nothing — the surface must say the date is a note, not an alarm); channelFact/channelFactAt/channelFactVia/channelFactDetail; endedAt/endedBy/endedReason; saleStopId. Null means 'never stated' or UNKNOWN, and the read derives a provisional intent from the legacy columns carried on the wire WITH its provenance. No backfill is the arm that must not be optimised away: the eBay end path already writes {listingStatus:'ENDED', offerActive:false, externalListingId:null} and a naive derivation reads that as 'never published'.
- SCHEMA: the new ListingIdentity model — the tombstone AND the registry, because it is READ, which is what makes RELEASED mean anything. workspaceId with the dbgenerated default 412 of 432 models carry; productId as a plain string with NO Prisma relation so it survives the purge; channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, aliasKey, externalListingId, externalParentId, fulfillmentMethod, offerSku, retiredAt/Reason/By, doNotRecreate, retiredSnapshot Json?, supersededByExternalListingId. Read by every ChannelListing create/upsert path (46 non-test sites). Be explicit about what it does NOT close: it cannot stop the Amazon flat-file row loader, which never touches a ChannelListing.
- SCHEMA: NOT proposed — a new presence event table (ProductEvent is already FK-free by design and already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing'), an Amazon-specific table, an Etsy table, or nullable ChannelListingSnapshot.channelListingId. NOT declared — ChannelListing.variationExcluded: family-projection.service.ts:389 cites migration 20260911a_vp2_variation_excluded and I re-checked the migrations directory, which holds only 20260911020000_information_formula_destination, 20260911_category_taxonomies and five 20260912_lx* folders. A comment is a claim, not a measurement.
- PROJECTION, no migration: offerClosedAt/offerClosedBy/offerCloseReason and syncPaused into LISTING_SELECT and onto the wire (zero occurrences of offerClosedAt in all of apps/web/src today, which is why a deliberately closed market renders as selling); Product.deletedAt; Marketplace.isParticipating/participationStatus; connection health as a tri-state so a FAILED read stops rendering as 'not connected'; a sources block on the sync-queue read so the console stops making a measurement-shaped claim about three tables it never queries.
- READ API: GET /studio/presence (every real coordinate plus every Opening, with intent+provenance, fact+as-of+via, derived verdict, gate mode, and per-verb availability WITH reach and reversal answered by the server so a preflight is never a client guess); GET /studio/presence/one (400 on any missing dimension, requireCoordinate's shape widened to require account and alias); GET /studio/presence/history over ProductEvent; GET /studio/presence/amazon-posture; POST /studio/presence/verify — READ ONLY, writes only the channelFact* columns, four-valued with 'could-not-ask' load-bearing, with a per-call coordinate cap and an hourly ceiling because Amazon reports run a serial queue and a roll-up saying '1 not checked' invites 'check them all'.
- READ API: extract the five queries inside GET /products/hard-delete-preflight into operational-impact.service.ts and add POST /api/products/operational-impact (coordinate-shaped body, read-only, one query per check partitioned in memory — only ChannelListing has the composite index). The two NEW checks (stock holds, ads) must NOT inherit the endpoint's all-or-nothing 500 semantics; they surface as status:'unavailable' in a `checks` key, or adding checks makes deletes less safe.
- DS CONTRACT: `reach: 'local'|'local-destructive'|'channel'` on GridAction (it gates availability and grouping before any preflight runs); `reversal:{verb,fidelity}`, `subject`, `acknowledge`, `asOf`, `refusal`, `handoffs` and an optional read-only `review` on ActionImpact; `findings[].severity` gains 'unknown' plus a `blocking` flag and an `asOf`; a sibling validateAction(action) because validateImpact receives only the impact and structurally cannot see an action-level field. The reversal sentence is RENDERED from the type; a lane may not author it. Label this honestly: the audit REFUTED 'ActionImpact cannot express reversibility' as a defect — this is a contract enhancement whose case is that a guard can then enforce what is currently prose.
- DS MENU: `tone?: Tone` on MenuItemDef and one danger rule with the CORRECT selector — `.nds-menu button[data-tone="danger"], .nds-menu a[role="menuitem"][data-tone="danger"]`, not `.nds-menu-item[data-tone]`, which matches nothing because Menu emits only .nds-menu-item-text/-name/-desc. Map GridAction.danger in BOTH adapters (measured 0 occurrences of `danger` in menuAdapters.tsx against a control of 5 for `label`) and move a disabled verb's reason from `title` (hover-only, and Menu's keyboard navigation skips disabled items) to `description`, keeping title supplementary. Append the correction to .claude/DS-GAPS.md rather than editing it — the file is append-only under its own gate.
- DS RENDERERS: design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts as a pure sibling of readiness.ts (intent/fact/verdict tables, no converter to the readiness vocabularies), a PresenceMark built on Pill and Tag — NOT Badge, which is the SP/SB/SD advertising mark and carries no tone — and NOT SourceIndicator, whose ValueSourceKind union is the cell-value provenance axis. An AsOf component that renders 'not checked' rather than an em dash. Move ActionConfirm.tsx to components/ (it is reached today only through grid/index.ts while two studio surfaces already call useActionConfirm with hand-built literals), replacing its four uses of the grid-only .nds-cell-strong class.
- DS A11Y, and these are preconditions for any confirmation dialog, not follow-ups: .nds-btn:focus-visible currently paints nothing on .primary and .danger because the later .danger rule wins the border-color and the focus-ring token is 12% alpha — add a real outline; pair an [aria-disabled="true"] selector beside every :disabled rule in components.css and patterns.css before any component trades the attribute away, or a held menu item ships looking exactly like a live one; repoint .nds-grid-confirm-error and -warn from --nds-danger/--nds-warning (3.91/3.76:1 as body text) to the -text tier.
- STUDIO WIRE: map ValueSource 'channelSnapshot' (attribute-resolver.ts:25 defines it, drawer/types.ts has no entry, layers.vitest.test.ts:55 is RED) — presence is already leaking into the provenance axis before presence exists. Swap channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3 off components/DataGrid to the grid/datagrid shim (34 → 33; the remaining red is another lane's and must be attributed, not absorbed).3

### Wave 3 — The presence surface, local-reach verbs only
Blocked by Wave 2 in full, and Owner decisions D2 (D9 / the tab id) and D22 (vocabulary and it.json).

This is the first wave with a new UI, and it ships nothing that reaches a marketplace: every verb here writes a local flag. That is deliberate — it lets the honest model run in production for a while with the studio unable to cause external harm, so the day the channel verbs land the only new variable is the channel call itself. W3-VOCAB must land in the same write as its wire fields, or operators get a new vocabulary describing the same three lies. The surface's home is the D9 decision and the whole plural half of the model depends on it.

Lanes Lane W3-SURFACE (apps/web _studio presence surface + nav) · Lane W3-VOCAB (copy landing with the wire fields — producer and consumer in ONE write) · Lane W3-API (the local-verb writers in apps/api)

- The presence surface: rows are five-level coordinates, two never-merged marks plus a mandatory as-of, verdict derived on read and never stored, a roll-up rendered as a distribution ('Amazon: 3 selling, 1 ended, 1 not checked') whose confidence is its weakest member's and whose as-of is its oldest member's — a set is never 'mostly live'. Mounted as the first item of each existing per-channel nav group above 'Listing information', plus one product-wide roll-up. It must render an EmptyState naming the missing dimension when it has no coordinate, never a coordinate-less grid, or it reintroduces the ambient-targeting hazard the whole design exists to kill.
- Local verbs only, each with typed reach 'local': hold/resume (syncPaused, now honoured on every push path after Wave 1), exclude-child/include-child, discontinue/undiscontinue (intent only, blocks create), and verify (a channel READ). Alias archive/restore ships behind PATCH /aliases/:aliasId with a real restoreAlias writer beside archiveAlias — verified there is no restore writer anywhere today — and stays inert until PES.5-ii drops the legacy indexes.
- INTENT IS A LOCK, NOT A LABEL, and this is the flaw that sinks a state model: enqueue refuses a terminal intent, dispatch tests the same helper, every direct push path consults assertPushAllowed, and every create/re-activate path consults ListingIdentity so a RELEASED or doNotRecreate identity refuses recreation without an explicit override. A gate asserts no writer sets two of {isPublished, offerActive, syncPaused, presenceIntent} in one statement, with the Amazon skip_offer pairing declared as the one sanctioned exception.
- The vocabulary landing: retire 'Live' for 'Listed' across the descriptor, the drawer, the footer and the confirmations, with one pure meta module and a scoped, ratcheted parity gate — scoped by key prefix and by _studio/**, excluding the flat-file enum where 'deactivate' is a data value an operator types, because a gate's RED is a claim too.
- Presence rendered in the toolbar descriptor and the drawer pane, NOT on the alias band. Putting marks there reverses a recorded 2026-09-05 decision written into the file it changed (AliasBandCell.tsx:141-143) and is geometrically unsafe, because bandWidth's derivation takes the variant row, so a third pill on the level-0 band cannot widen the column — it compresses the alias label, which is the #742 failure that file records.
- Ship live, not dark, and say the cost out loud on the surface itself: most coordinates read 'not checked' with an as-of of never. The honest model makes the studio look emptier than the lie it replaces.4

### Wave 4 — Plan/apply and the reversible channel verbs (its own Owner approval)
Blocked by Wave 3 in production, plus Owner decisions D6 (PD-2 widening), D8 (FBA), D11 (eBay hold semantics), D13 (Shopify lock), D14 (removing the preview-only refusal) and D19 (the token mechanism).

This is the moment the studio can write to a live marketplace. channelActions.ts:249-251 and :258-261 refuse every channel-reaching verb today as preview-only, and this wave deletes that refusal — which should be approved deliberately, after Wave 3 has run in production with local verbs only, not inherited as a consequence of approving the model. The three channel adapters are genuinely independent files and make clean concurrent lanes; W4-PLAN publishes the plan contract first because all three implement against it. Plural intents ('off everywhere except DE', 'off eBay entirely, keep Amazon') become statable here and only here, and only as a server-computed plan — that is the structural answer to an ambient coordinate.

Lanes Lane W4-PLAN (the plan/apply pair and the preflightToken) · Lane W4-AMZ (amazon-presence.adapter.ts and its four resolvers) · Lane W4-EBAY (ebay-presence-adapter + ebay-presence-guards) · Lane W4-SHOP (services/shopify/presence.service.ts on the per-account GraphQL client) · Lane W4-UI (the plural COLLECT → PLAN → CONFIRM → APPLY flow)

- POST /studio/presence/plan and /apply. plan is server-side and writes nothing; apply refuses a missing, expired or set-mismatched preflightToken and 409s per target on version drift. Per-target results in the shipped MarketOfferResult shape with NO aggregate success field, so the UI cannot render a green summary it was not given. apply is THE ONLY WRITER, enforced by a gate — plan/apply must not become the fourth ChannelListing removal path beside the two flat-file deleteMany services and the delist cascade.
- Fan-out computed server-side and itemised as NAMED coordinates, never a count — and computed per verb: the Amazon EU shared-quantity pool is NOT a fan-out for a per-market offer close (that file's own header says per-market suppression is a Seller Central offer-close, not a quantity operation) but IS one for any quantity-based hold and for reopen, which forces followMasterQuantity:true and enqueues an immediate QUANTITY_UPDATE. A confirmation that cries wolf on the one safe verb trains operators to click through the one that is not.
- Amazon: withdraw-offer/restore-offer via purchasable_offer op:'delete'/restore with a revalidated price (never replaying last season's figure with no margin or floor check), four resolvers (seller SKU, marketplace id from Marketplace.marketplaceId deleting both literal maps and all eight `?? IT` defaults, connection, ASIN ownership as a pure function whose EMPTY result is 'unverifiable', never 'rejected'), and honest silence where the client has no method: there is no patchListingsItem, so Amazon has a reversible offer close and a permanent delete and nothing between them until one is written.
- eBay: the reversible hold already ships (the Action column's `deactivate` zeroes the live offer keeping the ItemID, plus channel-agnostic ZERO_PIN) — presence must delegate to it, not rebuild it, and must not grey it out as missing. The shared-listing guards are extracted WITH an exclusion set: Guard 2's correctness depends on the target rows already being deleted, so extracted verbatim into a plan it fails SHUT on every coordinate and reports 'shared' — a green-looking refusal nobody would notice for a week. ENDED stops nulling externalListingId, paired in the SAME wave with sellingRisk and the delete-confirmation copy.
- Shopify: every verb through the per-account GraphQL client, never ShopifyService (env-credentialed, REST 2024-01, no readback); ARCHIVED is the reversible takedown and publication removal the narrower one; restore-offer must pass a FRESH read as `value` and the captured set as `nextValue` or it 409s every time; availability gated on the connection actually holding read_publications/write_publications, or the verb throws and reads as a Shopify outage.
- Etsy stays READ-ONLY and says so in those words, for a reason distinct from WooCommerce's: Nexus holds a read-only Etsy client. It must not be silently swept into the containment rule.
- The relist half: a revalidation gate whose PASS is earned (unknown never rolls up to pass; a market-wide pass on a five-level target renders warn), the diff rendered read-only in the History pane as a mode, and any edited value landing locally through the ordinary mutator BEFORE the channel call — otherwise the next follow-master push overwrites the channel with the pre-relist value. Exercise restoreToDraft against a captured request and read the BODY, not the status, before any 'recoverable with loss' sentence ships: the route has zero web callers and SNAPSHOT_FIELDS deliberately excludes externalListingId.
- PD-2: widen the liveness predicate at BOTH the preflight and the cascade enqueue in one write, now that the adapters are honest. Show operators both numbers ('11 hold a marketplace id; Nexus will try to reach 7') rather than one number that silently grew.
- Delete the preview-only refusal in channelActions.ts and runAction's short-circuit. Record it as its own approval in the claims ledger.5

### Wave 5 — Irreversible verbs, stop-sale, identity moves, Etsy
Blocked by Wave 4, plus Owner decisions D9 (Etsy), D16 (stop-sale), D17 (identity moves) and the release-ladder half of D4 (RBAC).

Everything here is either permanent, cross-cutting, or arms a trap that Wave 1's honesty work has only just contained. They are grouped last not because they matter least but because each one is only safe on top of the full stack: release needs the tombstone, the operational preflight and a verified-gone fact; stop-sale needs assertPushAllowed and the empty-patch fix; identity moves need five-level narrowing plus a rehearsal on a fixture ASIN; and the Etsy adapter's first act — persisting a listing_id — is what arms the hard-delete Etsy defect, so the preflight partition must land before the ingest, not after.

Lanes Lane W5-RELEASE · Lane W5-STOPSALE · Lane W5-IDENTITY · Lane W5-ETSY (each owns a disjoint service set; W5-ETSY's enum change must be a single write touching schema, dispatchChannelDelist and dispatchSync together)

- release: the ladder walked and re-checked server-side — intent ENDED or DISCONTINUED, a fact of ABSENT or NOT_SELLING from a real verify inside a freshness window (never inferred), the operational preflight run and clear, a ListingIdentity tombstone written in the same transaction, confirmPhrase = the real external id (a SKU, never the word DELETE), and holdUntil +5 minutes with the cancel route live. Plus remove-variation.
- stop-sale: one verb that stops selling on every coordinate while destroying nothing, with a first-class cause. SaleStop with a control snapshot (without it 'resume, exact' is a false promise for any pinned row), a partial unique index expressed in raw SQL with COALESCE because Postgres treats NULL causeRefs as distinct, a recall quarantine term in the published-quantity function (15 call sites, required field so no path silently opts out — the gate must reject a literal `quarantined: 0`), and 'LIFECYCLE' added to the existing default-on scheduled-changes cron rather than a new one. It must never route through the master status cascade, which skips every ENDED and ERROR listing.
- identity moves: RELAUNCH-SKU, MOVE-ACCOUNT and ATTRIBUTE on the presence grid, with a per-target strategy table where an unset row blocks the confirm. MOVE-ACCOUNT's source will normally be an INACTIVE grant, because the active-account unique index forbids two active grants sharing an externalAccountId — so the candidate query must read the full connection population, and eBay is cosmetic until MAP.4 lands and the primary-connection dispatch is fixed. RELAUNCH-SKU's recreate carries fidelity 'none' until a fixture-ASIN rehearsal proves a second seller SKU can be created: the delete puts the old SKU into a ~30-day cooldown, so a failed create leaves the coordinate dark with no fallback.
- Etsy: the preflight partition FIRST (cascadeCovered vs cascadeUnsupported from one exported constant), then in ONE write the SyncChannel ETSY enum value, the dispatchChannelDelist Etsy branch and the dispatchSync refusal — applying the enum alone converts today's loud Prisma rejection into three retries and a dead letter per row, per bulk edit. Then BUILD the ingest (it does not exist: etsy-sync.service.ts queries Product.etsyListingId, a column that is not on Product, through `(prisma as any)`, and throws at runtime). Retire or fix the two legacy write methods that PATCH price and quantity to an endpoint whose vendored update schema contains neither.
- Fulfilment lanes: the offer level as a bounded pair inside the coordinate, with the durable axis journalled to ProductEvent rather than trusted to Offer, which cascades on ChannelListing delete. Conditional on M2 showing Offer is actually populated — if it is near zero, offerScope is inert and must not appear in any confirmation.6

### Parallel lane P — sheet toolbar and accessibility (independent of Presence)
Blocked by Wave 2's DS lane for the MenuItemDef and registry type additions; otherwise nothing.

Neither depends on the state model and both touch files no Presence lane owns, so they can run alongside Waves 1-3 and give the programme visible progress while the backend repair is invisible. The one hard coupling is MenuItemDef's tone, which Wave 2's DS lane owns — P-TOOLBAR must consume it, not author it. The a11y lane is on the critical path for exactly one thing: no confirmation dialog for a destructive verb should ship while its Confirm button has no perceptible keyboard focus, so P-A11Y's focus fix must precede Wave 4.

Lanes Lane P-TOOLBAR (apps/web _studio/sheet + design-system/grid/toolbars) · Lane P-A11Y (design-system tokens, stylesheets and scripts/)

- The six-zone toolbar with a deterministic collapse ladder driven by the bar's own inline size through one DS hook that SUBSUMES the studio's existing, zero-consumer useContainerTier so ratified thresholds are unchanged and hysteresis is added; a chip count contract where the producer declares the unit and the renderer never re-derives it; a typed status slot that makes CH.1's 'never a control' a type error rather than a comment (it is violated in shipped code today — ChannelSheet passes SchemaStatus, which renders a Button).
- Two corrections the reviewed toolbar work must not inherit: the DS already ships the menu-selection mechanism (MenuProps.selectedId with a live style rule), so no hand-rolled tick; and ScopeBar has three call sites, not thirteen, so fixing its silent chip hiding is now cheap — but it is a separate item, not a smuggled one.
- Resolve the unrecorded ToolbarButton fork: two different components, both exported from DS barrels, one of which sets title on a possibly-disabled button. Pick the primitives implementation, re-export, delete the copy.
- A11y: the six confirmed defects fixed at the AAA bar, with the corrections that matter — do NOT drop the `disabled` attribute from Menu without pairing every :disabled rule with [aria-disabled="true"] first (four rules in components.css, two in patterns.css), or a held item ships indistinguishable from a live one, reintroducing a defect the DS already fixed once; do NOT sort danger verbs last, which would split the four surfaces ruling #110 says render one declaration identically; AG's icon takes Element|string, so the React node that works in the DS adapter renders nothing in the right-click menu, silently.
- Two new gates, each with a positive control in the same run and each seeded red before the fix: check-focus-visible.mjs and check-token-role.mjs. Widen and tighten check-silent-disabled in two separate writes with a re-measured baseline between them, because doing both at once makes the resulting count uninterpretable.

## Decisions only you can make

24 of them, each with the options and what I would choose. Several ask whether a standing ruling of yours should be amended. None of those has been treated as settled.
D1

#### CH.1 — does it stand as written, and if lifecycle is ever to be reachable from the sheet, does the overflow gain a typed tone?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) CH.1 stands unamended and lifecycle lives only on the presence surface — no change to SheetToolbar at all. (b) CH.1's rule stands (no new buttons) and the overflow gains `tone?: 'default'|'danger'` on MenuItemDef plus a separator group, so a destructive item is apart structurally as well as visually. (c) Neither: severity stays unrendered in menus.

##### Recommendation
(b), but note that no proposal in this programme NEEDS it — every lifecycle verb lives on the presence surface and the sheet toolbar is untouched. The amendment is worth making anyway because the DS half is currently inert: MenuItemDef carries no tone, both adapters drop GridAction.danger (measured 0 hits against a control of 5 for `label`), and there is no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet, so 'End the listing on Amazon IT' would render byte-identical to 'Reload'. Two smaller facts belong in the same ruling: SheetToolbar.tsx:254 force-disables every overflow item during a pending write with no reason shown, and CH.1's `trailing` rule is already violated in shipped code. This is the Owner's ruling; it is not presented as settled.
D2

#### D9 — is a per-coordinate presence surface a NEW OBJECT or a SECOND WAY IN? And if it is a new object, does it get a `listings` tab id, and does it appear under each channel group, under THIS PRODUCT, or both?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) New object, granted a tab id, mounted in each per-channel nav group above 'Listing information' plus one product-wide roll-up. (b) New object but no tab: presence lives only inside the existing drawer and toolbar descriptor. (c) A second way in — refused, and only the per-coordinate verbs survive.

##### Recommendation
(a). The sheet is content (cells); presence is state (identity, fact, fate). Zero overlap in rows and in written fields. But the precedent cuts against this and it belongs on the record: StudioSubheader.tsx:34-37 already refuses a per-channel Variants item on exactly these grounds, and the Owner removed the `relationships` tab on 2026-09-12. If the Owner rules (c), the entire plural half of the model — the plan/apply pair, 'off everywhere except DE', stop-sale — is unbuildable, and Wave 4 shrinks to per-coordinate verbs only. This is the single decision with the largest effect on the shape of the programme.
D3

#### The legacy Listing Hub: MasterDataTab ships a working per-coordinate hub today with a Live/Draft/Paused/Ended/Error vocabulary already separate from readiness and a shipped plural verb ('Pause non-IT'). The studio swap is therefore a capability-LOSS event unless presence lands first.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Hold the swap until Wave 4 completes. (b) Keep the legacy hub mounted alongside the presence surface until Wave 4, then retire it. (c) Swap now and accept the loss.

##### Recommendation
(b). Keeping it mounted costs nothing and means Wave 3 can ship the honest read without removing a capability operators use. Retire it in the same write that lands the plural plan, so the hub's one plural verb has a successor on the day it disappears.
D4

#### RBAC: every permission guard this programme adds is a log line while rbacHook defaults to shadow. Is the flip to NEXUS_RBAC_MODE=enforce part of this work, and is `release` gated on it? Related: userIdFor() returns the literal 'default-user'.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) In-file preHandlers that throw 403 regardless of the mode, shipped in Wave 0; the flip is a separate ops decision. (b) Gate the irreversible verbs on the flip. (c) Flip now as a precondition of Wave 0.

##### Recommendation
(a) for the guards plus resolve the actor in the same wave, and (b) for `release` specifically. The flip has blast radius across every route in the API and should not be smuggled in under a studio programme — but an irreversible verb whose audit trail says 'default-user' is not an audit trail, so the actor fix is not optional even if the flip waits.
D5

#### The untouchable flat-file rule — does it cover the ROUTES and SERVICES, or only the editors? This programme needs to change ebay-flat-file.routes.ts's end/deactivate branches, amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts, ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts, a read-filter in GET /amazon/flat-file/rows, and a Product.categoryAttributes.amazonFileExcluded stamp mirroring what eBay already does.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) The rule covers the EDITORS; server-side routes and services are in scope. (b) The rule covers everything under the flat-file name; presence delegates by calling those routes and never edits them, and drops any fix it cannot make from outside. (c) Case-by-case, per file, on request.

##### Recommendation
(a), ratified explicitly. Under (b) three things become unbuildable: the five-level narrowing of the two removal services (P8), the eBay reversible hold (its only writer lives inside ebay-flat-file.routes.ts), and closing the third Amazon resurrection path — the flat-file row loader ignores listing scope entirely, so nothing there reads a ChannelListing and ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate cannot reach it. A lane should not be deciding this boundary on its own.
D6

#### PD-2 — widen the liveness predicate from `listingStatus IN (ACTIVE,INACTIVE) AND externalListingId NOT NULL` to identityHeld, at BOTH the hard-delete preflight and the delist cascade enqueue. It widens what the warning shows AND what the destructive action attempts, by a measured ~20 rows on one eBay family alone. And the operator-visible count grows.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Approve as one write across both sites, sequenced after Wave 1 steps 1-6, showing operators both numbers ('11 hold a marketplace id; Nexus will try to reach 7'). (b) Widen the preview only and state on screen that the preview and the action disagree by design. (c) Leave both as they are.

##### Recommendation
(a). (b) is the producer/consumer split this codebase has already been burned by — the preview would promise a delist the transaction never enqueues. The sequencing is the whole safety argument: widening before the adapters stop escalating sends UNPUBLISH/DELETE for ~20 DRAFT/ENDED rows holding real ItemIDs through a path that turns Amazon unpublish into a permanent delete.
D7

#### The empty-patch arm: a STATUS_UPDATE that sends Amazon nothing currently lands SUCCESS (outbound-sync.service.ts:1063-1074 returns success with no dryRun field; the status writer at :750-752 keys the green line on dryRun alone). Making it honest turns every such row red.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Measure the 30-day count first, then ship as an announced ops change. (b) Ship immediately — a green line that means nothing is worse than a red one. (c) Leave it and have presence treat 'SKIPPED-as-SUCCESS' as UNKNOWN at the read.

##### Recommendation
(a). The fix is right and (c) is a plaster over a producer defect, but nobody has measured how many rows go red on the first morning, and the first symptom of an honesty fix should not be a dashboard nobody can read.
D8

#### FBA — two proposals give two different answers to one question. amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63 refuses close/reopen on ANY FBA signal, fail-closed, on the Owner's stated rule that Amazon owns FBA logistics end to end.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Fail-closed stands for every offer on an FBA product; presence emits a named Seller Central handoff instead ('Nexus cannot stop this FBA offer; close it in Seller Central'). (b) Add offerScope:'FBM' so 'withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA' becomes expressible on a mixed coordinate. (c) (a) now, (b) later if the measurement supports it.

##### Recommendation
(c), and the measurement is a precondition: Offer coverage on Amazon coordinates is UNMEASURED, all three Offer writers are broken against the schema (they write leadTime/minPrice/maxPrice/costPrice, none of which exist, behind `prisma as any`), and if the table is near-empty then offerScope is inert and a confirmation offering it is a promise nothing can keep. Also unify the two inconsistent isFbaCoordinate predicates that currently guard one verb, or a coordinate can be 'FBA, refuse' on one path and 'FBM, push' on the other — and the push path is the dangerous one.
D9

#### Etsy — read-only, or a write client? Related: when does SyncChannel gain ETSY, which host is correct, and what happens to the two legacy write methods, one of which is reachable from an unauthenticated route today?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) READ-ONLY through Waves 1-4, stated in those words and never swept into the WooCommerce containment rule; build the ingest, the enum and both dispatch branches in one write at Wave 5; guard the route and delete the two broken write methods now. (b) Build a write client in Wave 4 alongside the other channels. (c) Etsy stays read-only indefinitely.

##### Recommendation
(a). Etsy is an in-scope channel with an absent gate, not an out-of-scope one — modeForChannel returns null for it, which the code itself calls the most dangerous possible default to guess, and Nexus holds a read-only client. Three things must be said plainly: the ingest does not exist (it queries a Product.etsyListingId column that is not on Product, through `prisma as any`, and throws at runtime — this is 'build one', not 'rewrite one'); the enum is additive in Prisma and NOT additive in behaviour, because today an Etsy row loudly rejects the bulk enqueue batch and the moment ETSY joins the union it succeeds into `default: throw`; and there is no sandbox and no scope introspection, so the first Etsy write is a production experiment on a real listing.
D10

#### WooCommerce containment — confirm the scope. Out of scope as a channel (Owner, 2026-09-12), but the cron is syncing today.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Containment only: dispatchSync refuses lifecycle syncTypes rather than routing them into the Woo update path, syncToWoocommerce gains assertPushAllowed (it has zero syncPaused checks today, so a hold an operator believes is channel-wide does not gate it at all), and Woo presence renders read-only with the existing honest gate sentence. (b) Nothing at all. (c) Treat Woo as in scope.

##### Recommendation
(a), confirmed as containment and not channel work. Without it an UNPUBLISH_LISTING row falls through to `let newQty = payload?.quantity ?? 0`, sets inventory to zero and reports SUCCESS.
D11

#### eBay hold semantics: has the shipped 'deactivate — the safe hide (ItemID kept)' column actually been hiding listings, or ending them? Nexus has never read the account-level out-of-stock preference. And should the recommended 'Unpublish' default route to the reversible hold that already ships?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Measure the preference in Seller Hub before Wave 4 designs a hold on top of it; meanwhile 'unpublish' on eBay REFUSES rather than ending. (b) Measure, and if the preference is off, correct how the shipped column is described TODAY as well as prospectively. (c) Route 'unpublish' to the shipped qty-0 hold now, making the recommended default honest for the first time.

##### Recommendation
(a) plus (b). (c) is attractive and is a behaviour change on a shipped path, not a bug fix, so it belongs in Wave 4 as an explicit named verb rather than as a quiet re-route. The retroactive half of (b) matters: if the preference is off, a label Nexus has been showing for months has been wrong about what it did.
D12

#### eBay relist and the ads coupling: there is no relist in this codebase (RelistFixedPriceItem has zero occurrences repo-wide) — the only 'put it back' mints a new ItemID. And writing endedAt into EbayListingIndex would flip that listing's ads to STALE.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Say plainly that an eBay end is permanent for that ItemID, offer 'publish again as a new listing' with the lineage recorded, and have presence write only ListingIdentity, leaving the ads index to its own discovery sweep. (b) Build a relist and let presence update EbayListingIndex.endedAt, accepting that a lifecycle verb stales the ads. (c) Build the relist; leave the ads index alone.

##### Recommendation
(a). EbayListingIndex declares itself an ads-side index never written by the flat-file paths, has no account column, and its endedAt is driven by a sweep with a truncation guard that deliberately refuses end-flips on a partial read — a lifecycle write there is a second irreversible act hidden inside one confirmation. Separately: adopt the GB/UK bidirectional normaliser only; reconciling the stored values (ChannelListing holds 'GB', SharedListingMembership and EbayListingIndex hold 'UK') is its own approved data change.
D13

#### Shopify: `status` and `salesChannels` are replayable sheet cells, so a presence `end` (ARCHIVED) is undone by the next manual /synchronize, verified, and reported as success. How does intent become a lock on the content path? Related: the Markets axis, and the bin cascade.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Presence WRITES its intent into the stored status/salesChannels cells so the sheet and presence cannot contradict each other, with assertPushAllowed as the guard. (b) REFUSE the presence verb while a conflicting cell is stored. (c) Extend assertPushAllowed to make synchronizeContent refuse on a non-LIVE intent, leaving two stored values that silently disagree.

##### Recommendation
(a) with (c) as the guard. This is the correction that sinks the Shopify slice if missed — without it presence is a label, not a lock, on the one channel that looked safe. It changes what the sheet's status cell MEANS, which is why it is the Owner's. Two riders: state in one sentence that this connection is one webstore and per-market availability is not modelled (do NOT seed Marketplace rows per Shopify market — nine server paths pin the literal 'GLOBAL' and seeding would flip defaultMarket for every channel at once); and the /products bin cascade should downgrade to `end` for Shopify and say so, because it can never satisfy the release ladder.
D14

#### Amazon `end`: there is no patchListingsItem in the SP-API client (verified — only a comment). Should Wave 4/5 write it, and is removing the studio's preview-only refusal its own approval?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Wave 4 states plainly that Amazon has a reversible offer close and a permanent delete and nothing between them; Wave 5 writes patchListingsItem if wanted. (b) Write patchListingsItem as part of Wave 4. (c) Ship an Amazon 'end' that maps to the offer close and call it ended.

##### Recommendation
(a), and removing the preview-only refusal (channelActions.ts:249-251 and :258-261) is its own Owner approval, taken after Wave 3 has run in production with local verbs only. (c) is a lie by naming. Also settle whether the channel verbs ship gated: everything Amazon-writing is currently gated by default, and whether the flag is being flipped for this programme changes every confirmation's copy.
D15

#### Scheduling: intent carries effectiveFrom/until. Wave 1 stores and displays them and nothing executes them.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) No executor; the surface says in words that the date is a note, not an alarm. (b) Build an executor on the existing default-on scheduled-changes cron by adding a LIFECYCLE kind (a code change, not a migration — `kind` is a free String whose own comment invites extension). (c) Build a dedicated presence scheduler.

##### Recommendation
(a) for Waves 1-4, (b) in Wave 5 if the Owner wants it. A declared intent nothing enforces is precisely the failure the intent-as-a-lock section exists to prevent, so the surface must be explicit. Note the precedent: the studio's one scheduling surface is behind a hardcoded false, its cron is off in prod, and ScheduledImagePublish has zero rows ever — a scheduler built and not armed is the shape this codebase already has.
D16

#### Stop-sale and recall: does opening a LotRecall fire a stop-sale automatically, is the verb product-scoped or cross-product, does lifting a stop auto-resume selling, and which flag is authoritative for 'never recreate'?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
Automatic vs proposed; product-scoped now with cross-product filed separately vs a /products programme first; two-step lift vs one-click 'lift and resume'; Product.doNotRecreate vs ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate vs both behind one helper.

##### Recommendation
Proposed, not automatic — openRecall is reached from an inventory route, and an inventory action that silently reaches four marketplaces is exactly the endpoint-safety-is-not-interaction-safety failure; the 5-minute holdUntil is the undo window once the operator has approved. Product-scoped now (a recall arrives on a LOT and one lot maps to one product; the supplier-notice and category-withdrawal cases belong to a /products programme under D9). Two-step lift, because after a recall the pool has moved. And ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate is authoritative at the coordinate with Product.lifecycleState as the product-level intent, read by ONE shared helper — two flags for one rule at two grains is how a discontinued listing comes back.
D17

#### Identity moves (RELAUNCH-SKU, MOVE-ACCOUNT, ATTRIBUTE) and identity merge — in scope, and when?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Ratify all three as Wave 5, with identity MERGE and account consolidation explicitly out of scope. (b) All four in Wave 5. (c) File the whole identity axis as a separate programme.

##### Recommendation
(a). The scenarios critic named identity change as an entire missing axis and it is real — channelConnectionId is in both ChannelListing unique keys and no verb in this model changes it, so agency handover, entity change and relaunching under a new SKU keeping the ASIN have no writer anywhere. Merge is a set operation on identity rather than a state transition and folding it in would double the model. Two riders: eBay MOVE-ACCOUNT is cosmetic until MAP.4 lands AND the primary-connection dispatch is fixed, and 'sku' should be removed from writerAcceptsField so the rename is reachable only through the identity plan rather than being held back by a display-only `editable:false` flag.
D18

#### Restore and recovery: where does the relist review live, can the diff be edited inside the confirmation, does an edited value land locally before or after the channel call, and what happens to the unguarded /recover page?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
Relist review as a third mode in History vs its own surface; edit-then-replan vs widening AskToConfirm to return a result object; local write before the channel call vs conditional on its success; /recover guarded and folded into History vs taken offline now.

##### Recommendation
History as a mode (an Owner decision of 2026-09-01 says restore is a mode there specifically so there is no second home for the word restore — though History would then carry three mechanisms and that is worth the Owner's eye). Edit-then-replan: AskToConfirm returns a boolean and widening that seam changes the DS's nine safety rules, which exist because a five-listing delete once became one click. Local write FIRST through the ordinary mutator, because a second write path is a second provenance story and because otherwise the next follow-master push overwrites the channel. /recover guarded in Wave 0 and folded into History in Wave 5 — it currently has no auth by its own admission while it permanently deletes, nulls externalListingId across every account and alias in a market, and renames Product.sku globally.
D19

#### Confirmation mechanics: how does the preflightToken bind facts it may not cache, and does a plural typed confirm violate the one-coordinate-at-a-time ruling?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
Stateless HMAC over the plan digest with an expiry vs a persisted plan row; plural typed confirm allowed vs refused with the per-coordinate verb offered instead.

##### Recommendation
Stateless HMAC with a short TTL — the report is explicitly never persisted and never cached, and an in-memory token does not survive a second API instance. Refuse the plural typed confirm and offer the per-coordinate verb, per the existing ruling; if a future plan wants 'stop selling on nine markets' behind one phrase, that ruling moves first. Fix the literal 'DELETE' gate now regardless (case-insensitive today, and the DS's own rule says a SKU, never the word DELETE).
D20

#### The tree is not green. The grid-kit ratchet is RED at 34 against a baseline of 32 and stays red at 33 after the studio clears its only importer; check-ds-dts-fresh is RED with 52 of 243 declarations stale; check-grid-swatch-contrast crashes on every run; 6 studio tests fail in 2 files (layers on unmapped channelSnapshot, plus 5 in masterWrite). 'Never bypass pre-push' binds.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Wave 1 lands with a stated, attributed red on the ratchet while the owning lane clears its importers; this programme fixes channelSkipped/channelSnapshot and the swatch gate, and treats masterWrite as its owning lane's. (b) Hold everything until another lane clears. (c) Re-freeze the baseline with a written reason.

##### Recommendation
(a), with the census captured in Wave 0 as this programme's recorded baseline so a green run later can distinguish 'my change is clean' from 'the pre-existing reds moved'. The swatch gate must be repaired or retired in Wave 0 — it has crashed on every run, so its silence has never been a pass and its RED would convict nothing.
D21

#### DS semantics that presence and the a11y pass both depend on: what makes a menu item 'held but explainable' rather than dead, where does a disabled control's reason live, should --nds-success-text be raised, and how verbose should provenance marks be?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
held discriminator = `description != null` vs an explicit `held?: boolean` vs an unconditional attribute swap; reason in `title` vs `description` vs both; success-text raised now vs deferred vs excepted in writing; ProvenanceMark as role=img+aria-label vs a visually-hidden text node.

##### Recommendation
`description != null` as the discriminator (smallest blast radius across 14 Menu consumers, and it makes the reason the thing that earns keyboard reachability); BOTH title and description in the same change so the existing adapter test stays green while the doctrine is honoured; raise --nds-success-text or write the exception into the token file with its reason (it misses 7:1 on four declared grounds and .nds-cell-stock-ok lands on two of them in every grid); and role=img now with a browser check afterwards, since this run is forbidden a browser and the alternative is unmeasurable from source.
D22

#### Copy and vocabulary edges: the three Italian keys nobody here can translate; how far the ban on 'Live'/'Deactivate'/'Archive' extends; the 'Unpublish (recommended)' default on a destructive radio; and whether a refusal may name a legacy control in prose.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
it.json — hold for a translator / ship English at the Italian keys marked for translation / delete the three keys so the runtime falls back to English visibly. Ban list — studio only vs extended to listings.bulk.* and products.hardDelete.*. The radio default — string fix only vs also changing the default to 'none'. Refusals — may name a legacy control in prose vs strictly terminal.

##### Recommendation
Delete the three Italian keys (visible English fallback beats an untouched Italian lie, and 'hold for a translator' blocks a whole honest pass on three strings). Keep the gate scoped to the studio and listing/lifecycle key prefixes, ratcheted rather than zeroed, excluding the flat-file enum where 'deactivate' is a data value an operator types. Fix the string now and change the default to 'none' in the same wave as PD-2, not before — it is a behaviour change on a shipped destructive control. And allow a refusal to NAME a legacy control in prose without a hyperlink: the strict reading buys only that the studio does not endorse the page operators will find anyway.
D23

#### Is the sheet-toolbar redesign part of this programme, and if so which of its sub-questions does the Owner want to settle now — the unit vocabulary, merging Export/Import into one Transfer control, the 6px vs 16px gutter, and whether width-driven collapse is inside CH.1?

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
(a) Run it as a parallel lane with only the honest-scope subset (units, typed status slot, portalled views prompt, padding specificity). (b) Run the full six-zone ladder. (c) Defer entirely until Presence ships.

##### Recommendation
(a). The audit's own verdict REFUTED the measured-overflow finding that justified the full ladder, so the ladder is prophylaxis and every width number in it is unmeasured against CSS the tree no longer has. Take the two confirmed fixes and the typed status slot now; hold the ladder, the Transfer merge (it removes a control CH.1 named) and the gutter (an open UXLD question that must not be resolved quietly inside a width fix) for a separate ruling. The units fix cannot land without ratifying a change to a test that currently pins '3 columns' — that is a design decision, not a bug.
D24

#### Fulfilment authority: ChannelListing.fulfillmentMethod says fulfilment is exclusive per coordinate; Offer says a coordinate can hold FBA and FBM at once. Nothing reconciles them. Related: the master sheet's editable Fulfilment cell, and three broken Offer writers.

▸ Options and my recommendation

##### Options
FCF.1 authoritative vs Offer authoritative vs FCF.1 becomes a derived cache; master Fulfilment cell made read-only vs relabelled 'Fulfilment (product default)'; the three Offer writers retired vs repaired.

##### Recommendation
Relabel the cell rather than removing a capability, and fix the live defect behind it first: amazon-mapper reads `product.fulfillmentChannel || 'FBA'` into a live Amazon variation payload while bulk-edit lets that cell clear the field to null, so an unset fulfilment silently asserts FBA on an outbound push. Leave FCF.1 unchanged in Wave 1 — making it a derived cache is the Owner's ruling, not a design's. Retire the matrix.routes Offer writers (no web caller, though the route is registered, so retiring is a capability removal and needs the nod) and repair the inbound-sync one, which sits on the SP-API catalog ingest path.

## The specifications

Seventeen, each written then rewritten by an adversarial reviewer who opened every file the design named.

Channel adapters

#### Presence on Shopify — the adapter, corrected against the working tree

Shopify has two write surfaces; every presence verb must use the per-account GraphQL 2026-07 client and none may use the env-credentialed REST 2024-01 service. ARCHIVED is the reversible takedown (keeps ids, handle, translations, metafields, `nexus.family_id`) and publication removal is the narrower one, so WITHDRAWN and ENDED stay distinguishable by observation. Ten preconditions must land first. The reviewed proposal missed the decisive one: the studio's own sheet stores `status` and `salesChannels` as replayable cells, so a presence `end` is silently reversed by the next content sync. `restore-offer` as written always 409s. Disclosure already accepts `open`. Corrections are listed first.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- Disclosure has no `tone`. CORRECTED from the reviewed proposal: `open` is NOT missing — DisclosureProps extends Omit<DetailsHTMLAttributes<HTMLDetailsElement>,'children'> (Disclosure.tsx:4) and {...rest} lands on <details> (:13), so `open` already works. ADD `tone?: Tone` to DisclosureProps plus a `.nds-disclosure.warning/.danger` rule in design-system/styles/components.css, and optionally `defaultOpen?: boolean` (React's `open` is effectively controlled and fights the user's own toggle). Never a page-local <details> in the studio.
- MenuItemDef has no tone, severity or group key; menuAdapters.tsx contains 0 occurrences of `danger` against a positive control of 5 for `label`, and there is no `.nds-menu` danger rule in any design-system/styles stylesheet. `Archive on Shopify` and `Delete from Shopify` render byte-identical.
