FILED at .claude/DS-GAPS.md:331-350 — but that entry is HALF STALE: its item 2 ('No per-item title') is no longer true, Menu.tsx:24 has carried `title` and :26-42 `description`. Only item 1 stands. Do not hand-roll a red item; the smallest amendment is the Owner's ruling.
- GridAction has no `reach` and ActionImpact no `reversal{verb,fidelity}`. The Shopify adapter is a CONSUMER of the spine's addition, not its author, but cannot render an honest reversal sentence without it — and Shopify is where the generic table is wrong twice (`end` is exact here, not lossy; `relist` from ARCHIVED keeps every id).
- SummaryTable carries no per-row tone and no per-cell alignment (SummaryTable.tsx:3-7). A 'publication removed' row therefore cannot be toned by the table itself; the tone must ride a Pill inside the cell. Checked the DS first: no better fit exists and no new component is proposed. If the Owner wants toned rows, that is a DS addition to SummaryTable, not a page-local table.
- The DS contradicts itself on where a disabled verb's reason lives: actionMenuItems puts it on `title` (a hover-only tooltip, menuAdapters.tsx:85) while MenuItemDef.description's own doc says rulings #133/#231 forbid exactly that. Presence disables verbs constantly (no publication scope, gated publish mode, wrong intent), so this decides whether operators can read the refusals. Not mine to settle — openQuestions.

##### API
- PRECONDITION SHOP-P1 (wave 1, two lines) — delistShopify (channel-delist.service.ts:180-208) returns { success:false, retryable:false, error } for BOTH actions instead of sending the double-wrapped body. Note both current failure arms: an unset SHOPIFY_SHOP_NAME/SHOPIFY_ACCESS_TOKEN makes the constructor throw into a retryable loop (shopify.service.ts:77-81), while creds present produce the green lie at :196. A visible FAILED row beats either. Ship this first.
- PRECONDITION SHOP-P2 (wave 1) — content-sync.service.ts:139/:141/:143-144 must persist listingStatus and the channelFact* columns it already computed at :134; channel-sheet-projection.ts:63 must stop stamping the literal 'ARCHIVED' onto listingStatus and put it in channelFactDetail; and both hard-delete filters (products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682 preflight, :1855-1859 enqueue) must consult the shared sellingRisk(). Producer and consumer in ONE write; assert the DB row, not the mapping object; flip the remote status as the positive control.
- PRECONDITION SHOP-P3 (wave 1) — products-catalog.routes.ts:1863-1870 must select channelConnectionId and aliasKey and carry them in the queue row's payload. The adapter REFUSES a job with no connection id rather than falling back to env credentials; a fallback is how a two-store workspace archives the wrong store's product.
- PRECONDITION SHOP-P4 (wave 1, containment) — the legacy Shopify tail of dispatchSync must REFUSE lifecycle syncTypes. Verified: dispatchSync (outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623) routes by targetChannel with NO syncType branch, unlike the BullMQ worker (bullmq-sync.worker.ts:141-158) which does have the delist branch; syncToShopify (:1862) branches only on CONTENT_UPDATE and PRICE_UPDATE, so an UNPUBLISH_LISTING row on a listing without platformAttributes.nexusFamilyId falls through to `let newQty = payload?.quantity ?? 0` (:2058), sets inventory to 0 and reports SUCCESS. The native path already refuses unknown syncTypes (offer-sync.service.ts:37); the legacy path inherits none of it. Same refusal covers WooCommerce.
- PRECONDITION SHOP-P5 (wave 1) — the studio's offer-toggle preflight says 'Buyers stop seeing this offer on this channel and market' (channelActions.ts:228-230; the label is at :163). On Shopify that is false — offerActive is read by nothing there. On a SHOPIFY scope the verb must be replaced by hold/resume on syncPaused (which IS honoured, offer-sync.service.ts:11) or state its true local-only consequence. No test pins that sentence: channelActions.vitest.test.ts asserts only offerActive shapes (:120-122).
- PRECONDITION SHOP-P6 (wave 1, NEW — the one the reviewed proposal missed) — presence intent must be a LOCK on the Shopify content path. `status` and `salesChannels` are in nativeFieldKeys (packages/shared/shopify-information.ts:135); listingInformationDraft emits a NativeEdit for any STORED cell of such a field (listing-information-plan.ts:93-103); synchronizeContent applies every one (content-sync.service.ts:112). So a presence `end` is un-archived by the next manual /synchronize, verified, and reported as success. Required: extend the spine's shared assertPushAllowed(listingId) to previewContentSync/synchronizeContent AND to syncNativeShopifyOffer (which today reads only syncPaused/syncLocked/isPublished, offer-sync.service.ts:11); and either write the presence intent into the stored status/salesChannels cells or refuse the verb while a conflicting cell is stored. The confirmActive prompt at content-sync.service.ts:70 must also name the intent ('this listing was deliberately ended on <date> by <who>'), not just 'a live or archived product'.
- NEW — every Shopify presence verb executes through shopifyAdmin(accountId) (services/shopify/admin-client.ts). No presence verb may construct ShopifyService: it is account-blind by construction (env credentials, marketplaces/shopify.service.ts:74-76), pinned to REST 2024-01 (:79) and performs no readback (:136-145). Verified set of construction sites: channel-delist.service.ts:186, stock.routes.ts:2716, marketplace.service.ts:62.
- NEW — services/shopify/presence.service.ts, one function per verb, each reusing a shipped primitive rather than issuing a raw mutation. withdraw-offer / restore-offer → applyInformationPublications (information-publications.ts:20-35: reads, refuses on drift with 409, applies, re-verifies with 502). CRITICAL: `edit.value` is what we believe Shopify currently IS, so restore-offer must pass a FRESH read as value and the captured set as nextValue — replaying the captured set as value 409s every time. end / relist → applyNativeEdit({field:'status'}) (information-gateway.ts:133-155, same read/409/write/re-read/502 discipline, and idempotent because :135 short-circuits when current already equals nextValue). verify → product{id status handle} + readInformationPublications. release → the four-part capture, then productDelete, and only after the ladder.
- NEW — per-verb availability must consult the connection's scopes. schema.publications is attached only when the connection holds read_publications/write_publications (linked-products-gateway.ts:92-93). Without it, withdraw-offer and restore-offer return disabled('This Shopify connection cannot read or change sales channels — reconnect it with publication access'), and verify maps the resulting throw to REFUSED, never NOT_SELLING or ABSENT.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence — Shopify contributes one coordinate per (GLOBAL × connection × alias), with fact, as-of, verdict, the resolved publication set BY NAME, the gate mode from getShopifyPublishMode(), and per-verb availability with reach and reversal. verify works while publishing is gated: admin-client.ts:13-15 gates only strings matching /^\s*mutation\b/.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/plan — for Shopify returns resolved publication NAMES (never gids), the exact status transition, and a per-target expectedVersion drawn from ChannelListing.version (the same optimistic key content-sync.service.ts:73 already uses). No writes.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/apply — refuses a missing, expired or set-mismatched preflightToken; 409s per target on version drift; per-target results, no aggregate success. THE ONLY WRITER; it must not become a second Shopify removal path beside the queue.
- RBAC — the /synchronize preHandler (routes/images/shopify-linked-products.routes.ts:41-52) gates only products.images.edit and inventory.adjust. Add: a nativeEdit with field 'status' moving off the current value, or field 'salesChannels' removing a publication, requires products.publish. These in-file guards throw 403 regardless of NEXUS_RBAC_MODE, which defaults to shadow (rbac-hook.ts:31) — that is precisely why the guard belongs in the file and not only in the manifest. Every presence route carries an explicit in-file preHandler naming its permission.
- NOT PROPOSED — seeding one Marketplace row per Shopify market. NINE server paths pin the literal 'GLOBAL' (channel-sheet.service.ts:22,28,77,78,136,148; content-workspace.service.ts:20 and :103; content-sync.service.ts:141) plus the seed row at packages/database/scripts/seed-marketplaces.ts:40. Seeding markets first makes the switcher lie and flips defaultMarket (scopes.ts:174-175) for every channel at once. Wave 1 states the limit in one sentence instead.

##### Schema
- AMENDMENT TO AN UNRATIFIED MODEL, not an additive change to a shipped one — ListingIdentity.retiredSnapshot Json?. ListingIdentity is the spine's new model and has not been approved; this adds one column to it. Holds the Shopify pre-destruction capture: handle, seo{title,description}, tags, vendor, productType, templateSuffix, category, publishedAt; per variant the id, sku and inventoryItem id; all metafields including nexus.family_id; the publication set with schedules; translations per published locale. It cannot live in ChannelListingSnapshot (channelListingId is non-null with onDelete: Cascade, schema.prisma:1895-1896 — it dies with the Product) and must not live in ProductEvent.data (that model's own comment at schema.prisma:15483 reads 'Delta payload: only the fields that changed, never a full snapshot').
- NO SCHEMA CHANGE — ChannelListing.listingStatus. The column exists at schema.prisma:1617 with vocabulary DRAFT|ACTIVE|INACTIVE|ENDED|ERROR. The defect is that the Shopify writer never sets it. Write ACTIVE when Shopify says ACTIVE, INACTIVE for DRAFT or ARCHIVED — deliberately NOT a literal ARCHIVED. CORRECTION to the reviewed proposal: channel-sheet-projection.ts:63 currently stamps the literal 'ARCHIVED' onto listingStatus ON THE WIRE (drawer/types.ts:307 carries it), so persisting INACTIVE without moving that literal into channelFactDetail leaves one field name carrying two vocabularies. Both halves must land together.
- WRITES THE SPINE'S ADDITIVE COLUMNS, ADDS NONE OF ITS OWN — ChannelListing.channelFact / channelFactAt / channelFactVia / channelFactDetail. Shopify populates them from every verified write ('write-ack') and every verify ('channel-read'). channelFactDetail carries { shopifyStatus, publications: [{id,name,publishDate}] } so no reader re-derives. NO BACKFILL: a Shopify row written before these columns existed derives UNKNOWN, never DRAFT — content-sync.service.ts:139 writes isPublished:false for both 'never synced' and 'synced to a DRAFT product', which is the exact eBay-end arm that broke an earlier design.
- NO CHANGE, AND NONE NEEDED — OutboundSyncQueue. Verified at schema.prisma:6824-6828: productId and channelListingId are BOTH nullable AND both onDelete: Cascade. The Shopify adapter reads channelConnectionId and aliasKey from the queue row's payload, which needs only a wider select at products-catalog.routes.ts:1863-1870, not a migration.
- SEQUENCING (binding) — apply the ListingIdentity migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring it in schema.prisma. MEMORY records apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11, with only Product.version (54 vs 51) separating the two. Discriminate, never infer. And per the ratified migration-staging rule, the folder stays out of packages/database/prisma/migrations until it is meant to apply — a parked folder is applied by the next sibling's migrate deploy.

##### Open for you
- SHOP-P6 SCOPE — the second writer. Should presence WRITE its intent into the stored `status`/`salesChannels` sheet cells (one declared value per Shopify field, so the sheet and presence cannot contradict each other), or REFUSE the verb while a conflicting cell is stored, or extend assertPushAllowed to make synchronizeContent refuse on a non-LIVE intent? The first is honest and touches the sheet; the second is safest and blocks operators; the third leaves two stored values that silently disagree. I recommend the first with the third as the guard, but this changes what the sheet's status cell MEANS and that is the Owner's.
- DISCLOSURE TONE — ratify the corrected DS gap. `open` already works; only `tone` is missing. Is adding `tone?: Tone` plus a stylesheet rule to Disclosure acceptable as a DS change, or should a destructive Shopify plan use a Banner-wrapped always-expanded block instead and leave Disclosure alone?
- THE DS'S OWN CONTRADICTION on where a disabled verb's reason lives — `title` (tooltip, what actionMenuItems ships) versus `description` (a second line, what Menu.tsx's doc says rulings #133/#231 require). Presence disables verbs constantly, so this decides whether refusals are readable. Which is the rule?
- CH.1 — the tension, stated, not resolved. This proposal does not need CH.1 to bend: every Shopify verb lives on the presence surface and the sheet toolbar is untouched. But under CH.1 as written, if lifecycle is ever reachable from the sheet, 'End the listing on Shopify' renders byte-identical to 'Reload' (0 danger hits in menuAdapters against a control of 5; no .nds-menu danger rule). Does CH.1 stand as written, with lifecycle living only on the presence surface?
- D9 — is a presence surface a NEW OBJECT or a SECOND WAY IN? Evidence for the former, which the reviewed proposal did not cite: D9's own reasoning at StudioHeader.tsx:87-97 already contemplates that "'List on…' is a new coordinate plus this menu" — it treats coordinate creation as legitimate and only forbids re-reaching the sheet. Presence is state, not content. The Owner rules.
- THE BIN CASCADE ON SHOPIFY. channelAction:'delete' from the /products bin can never satisfy the release ladder (verified ABSENT fact, operational preflight, confirmPhrase = the real Shopify product id, holdUntil +5min). Should it downgrade to `end` and say so, or be refused outright for SHOPIFY?
- MARKETS. Wave 1 states in one sentence that this connection is one webstore and per-market availability is not modelled. Wave 2 gives the buildable partial (publicationScope). Is that acceptable, or does the Owner want the full Markets axis — catalogs, price lists, market localizations, all currently zero-hit in this repo — scoped as its own programme?
- ETSY. modeForChannel returns null for Etsy AND WooCommerce (syncQueue.ts:323-332). Wave 1 states plainly that Etsy presence is READ-ONLY (Nexus holds a read-only Etsy client). Confirm that Etsy is stated, not silently swept into the WooCommerce containment rule.
- WOOCOMMERCE CONTAINMENT — confirm the scope. Out of scope as a channel. The one containment measure here: the legacy Shopify tail of dispatchSync must refuse lifecycle syncTypes rather than route them into the quantity path, and the same refusal covers Woo. Confirming this is containment, not channel work.

##### Risks
- THE ONE THAT SINKS IT IF MISSED (C1). The studio's sheet stores `status` and `salesChannels` as replayable cells (nativeFieldKeys, packages/shared/shopify-information.ts:135), listingInformationDraft turns any stored cell into a NativeEdit (listing-information-plan.ts:93-103), and synchronizeContent applies every one (content-sync.service.ts:112). A presence `end` (ARCHIVED) is therefore UNDONE by the next manual /synchronize, verified and reported as success. SHOP-P6 is not optional; without it presence is a label, not a lock, on the one channel the reviewed proposal called safe.
- syncNativeShopifyOffer gates on syncPaused/syncLocked/isPublished and nothing else (offer-sync.service.ts:11). A WITHDRAWN coordinate keeps status ACTIVE so isPublished stays true, and PRICE_UPDATE and QUANTITY_UPDATE keep pushing to it. Covered by SHOP-P6; if P6 ships only on the content path, this arm stays open.
- restore-offer 409s on every run unless `value` is a fresh read taken inside the apply (information-publications.ts:29). Building it from the captured set is the natural mistake and it fails closed, so it would be caught — but only after an operator has been told the reversal is exact.
- A `relist` from ARCHIVED replays the variant price stored at capture time with no margin, floor or promo-window revalidation — the Shopify twin of the reopen-replays-last-season's-price finding the scenarios critic raised against Amazon's reopenMarketOffers.
- withdraw-offer and restore-offer silently do not exist on a connection without read_publications/write_publications (linked-products-gateway.ts:92-93). If availability is not gated on the scope, the verb is offered, throws inside the adapter, and the operator sees a failure that reads as a Shopify outage.
- Presence writes INACTIVE to listingStatus while channel-sheet-projection.ts:63 stamps the literal ARCHIVED onto the same field on the wire. Two vocabularies on one field name; whichever reader wins, one of them is wrong. Fixed in §5, but it is a cross-lane edit and the projection belongs to the Shopify sheet lane.
- The wave-1 containment turns a silent green into a visible FAILED queue row. That is correct and it is also a user-visible regression in the delist dashboard on day one; the Owner should expect it rather than discover it.
- A transport or shape failure on the legacy adapter is an UNKNOWN outcome, not a known one (C7). Neither arm of delistShopify — the constructor throw or the double-wrapped 2xx — tells us what Shopify did. Nothing built on top may read either as a fact.
- The proposal depends on ListingIdentity, which the spine has not had ratified. If the Owner rejects that model, the Shopify release capture has nowhere legal to live and `release` cannot ship at all — ChannelListingSnapshot dies with the Product and ProductEvent forbids full snapshots.

##### The specification
# Presence on Shopify — adapter specification (corrected)
## CORRECTIONS
Every item was re-derived with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed`. No dev server, no API call, no browser.
**C1 — A defect the proposal reintroduces, and the largest one. Presence has a SECOND WRITER on Shopify, and it is inside the studio.** `nativeFieldKeys` (`packages/shared/shopify-information.ts:135`) contains both `status` and `salesChannels`. `listingInformationDraft` turns any **stored** channel cell for such a field into a `NativeEdit` (`listing-information-plan.ts:93-103`, gated only on `stored.state === 'stored'`), and `synchronizeContent` applies every one through `applyNativeEdit` (`content-sync.service.ts:112`). So presence `end` writes ARCHIVED, the stored sheet cell still says ACTIVE, and the next content sync emits `{field:'status', value:'ARCHIVED', nextValue:'ACTIVE'}` and **un-archives a deliberately ended listing** — then verifies it and reports success. `withdraw-offer` has the identical hole through `salesChannels`. The automatic path is partly protected (`offer-sync.service.ts:18` refuses `CONTENT_UPDATE` unless `remote.status === 'ACTIVE'`); the manual `/synchronize` is not — `publishContent` asks only for `confirmActive` (`content-publisher.ts:196`) and the prompt at `content-sync.service.ts:70` says "a live or archived product", never "this listing was deliberately ended". New precondition **SHOP-P6**.
**C2 — `restore-offer` as specified always 409s.** `applyInformationPublications` reads `edit.value` as *what we believe Shopify currently is* and throws 409 on any divergence (`information-publications.ts:29`). Replaying the captured set as `value` guarantees divergence, because withdrawing is what created it. Corrected in §2.
**C3 — Disclosure already accepts `open`.** `DisclosureProps extends Omit<DetailsHTMLAttributes<HTMLDetailsElement>,'children'>` (`Disclosure.tsx:4`) and `{...rest}` spreads onto `<details>` (`:13`). The gap is `tone` plus a `.nds-disclosure` tone rule, and a React-idiomatic `defaultOpen` (React's `open` fights the user's own toggle) — one real prop and one ergonomic one, not two missing ones.
**C4 — `MenuItemDef` already carries `title` and `description`** (`Menu.tsx:24,:26-42`). `.claude/DS-GAPS.md:331-350` is **half stale**: its item 2 ("No per-item `title`") is no longer true. Item 1 stands, re-measured: `danger` = 0 in `menuAdapters.tsx` against a control of 5 for `label`; no `.nds-menu` danger rule in `design-system/styles/`. Note the DS contradicts itself — `actionMenuItems` puts a disabled verb's reason on `title` (a tooltip, `menuAdapters.tsx:85`) while `description`'s own doc says rulings #133/#231 forbid exactly that. Not mine to settle; it is in openQuestions.
**C5 — `channel-sheet-projection.ts:63` does more than was claimed.** It sets `listingStatus: providerStatus` as well as `isPublished:false`, and `drawer/types.ts:307` carries `listingStatus?: string` — so the **wire** model already distinguishes ARCHIVED from DRAFT after a live read. Only the **column** stays `DRAFT`. Writing `INACTIVE` to the column while the projection stamps the literal `ARCHIVED` onto the same field name gives one field two vocabularies. Corrected in §5.
**C6 — the GLOBAL pin is nine sites, not seven.** `channel-sheet.service.ts:22,28,77,78,136,148`; `content-workspace.service.ts:20` **and `:103`**; `content-sync.service.ts:141`. Plus the seed row (`packages/database/scripts/seed-marketplaces.ts:40`). The conclusion is unchanged and better supported.
**C7 — `delistShopify` fails two ways, and only one was named.** `new ShopifyService()` throws when the env vars are unset (`shopify.service.ts:77-81`), and the outer catch turns that into `{success:false, retryable:true}` — a retry loop. The green lie is the other arm: creds present, double-wrapped PUT, `makeRequest` returns on any 2xx, `{success:true}` at `:196`. A shape failure is an UNKNOWN outcome, not a known one.
**C8 — `applyNativeEdit`'s status branch is `information-gateway.ts:133-155`, not 140-151**, and it **short-circuits to a no-op when current already equals `nextValue`** (`:135`). That makes `end` idempotent, which the proposal did not claim and should.
**C9 — `ShopifyLinkedWorkspace.tsx:354` is not "the identical raw block".** It is a `<section>` in a `Modal`, always expanded — same raw `<pre>` value, different container. The extraction is right; the identity claim is not.
**C10 — `channelActions.ts` cites.** The `offer-toggle` label is `:163-183`; the sentence "Buyers stop seeing this offer on this channel and market" is the **preflight consequence at `:228-230`**. `channelActions.vitest.test.ts` pins only `offerActive` shapes (`:120-122`) — no test asserts that sentence, so SHOP-P5 changes nothing a test defends.
**C11 — `SummaryTable` is narrower than assumed**: `{label, columns: readonly string[], rows: {id, cells: readonly ReactNode[]}[]}` (`SummaryTable.tsx:3-7`). No row tone, no cell alignment. Tone must ride a `Pill` inside a cell.
Re-confirmed unchanged: the three-site `new ShopifyService` set; `shopifyAdmin`'s mutation-only gate (`admin-client.ts:13-15`); zero hits for `contextualPricing|priceList|marketWebPresence|marketLocalizations|catalogs\(|MarketCatalog` against a control of 7; `listingStatus` absent from `content-sync.service.ts:139/:141/:143-144`; both hard-delete filters at `products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682` and `:1855-1859`; no `channelConnectionId`/`aliasKey` in the select at `:1863-1870`; `ChannelListingSnapshot.channelListingId` non-null + Cascade (`schema.prisma:1895-1896`); `ProductEvent` "never a full snapshot" (`:15483`); `OutboundSyncQueue` both FKs nullable **and** Cascade (`:6824-6828`); CH.1 verbatim (`SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20`); D9 (`StudioHeader.tsx:87-97`); `modeForChannel` null for Etsy and Woo (`syncQueue.ts:323-332`); `restoreToDraft` zero web callers.
## 1. Two write surfaces; one is disqualified
The legacy `ShopifyService` is REST `2024-01` (`shopify.service.ts:79`), credentialed from `process.env` in its constructor (`:74-76`), and `makeRequest` throws only on `!response.ok` — no `userErrors`, no readback (`:136-145`). It is **account-blind by construction**, so it cannot address a five-level coordinate. Three call sites: `channel-delist.service.ts:186`, `stock.routes.ts:2716`, `marketplace.service.ts:62`.
Every presence verb uses `shopifyAdmin(accountId)`: GraphQL `2026-07`, per-`ChannelConnection` token, `assertWritable` + `getShopifyPublishMode() !== 'live'` refusal on mutations, a rate/circuit gate, `assertShopifyResult` for `userErrors` (`admin-client.ts:6-33`). Because the gate is keyed on `/^\s*mutation\b/`, **`verify` works while publishing is gated** — the surface can be honest about a store it may not write to.
## 2. Shopify's lattice, and the verb table
Two orthogonal facts, both already read: **ProductStatus** `ACTIVE|DRAFT|ARCHIVED` (`information-gateway.ts:21-23`) and **publication membership** via `resourcePublicationsV2(onlyPublished:false)` (`information-publications.ts:9-16`).
ARCHIVED is the reversible takedown: it keeps the product id, handle, SEO, variant and inventory-item ids, translations and every metafield **including `nexus.family_id`**, which is the identity `readRemoteProduct` matches on (`content-publisher.ts:33-37`). It reverses in one already-implemented mutation with read-first, 409-on-drift, write, re-read, 502-on-unconfirmed (`information-gateway.ts:133-155`). DRAFT is disqualified because the studio has spent it: `productSet` forces `status:'DRAFT'` on create (`content-publisher.ts:222`) and `publishContent` gates on `remote.status !== 'DRAFT'` (`:196`).
`publicationScope: 'all' | readonly publicationId[]` is Shopify's sub-coordinate; the coordinate stays five-level.
| verb | reach | Shopify act | reversal |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hold`/`resume` | local | `syncPaused` only — honoured at `offer-sync.service.ts:11` **and, after P6, on the content path too** | `resume`, exact |
| `withdraw-offer` | channel | `publishableUnpublish` for the scope; status untouched | `restore-offer`, exact after revalidation |
| `restore-offer` | channel | `publishablePublish`; **`value` = a fresh read taken inside the apply, `nextValue` = the captured set** (C2) | `withdraw-offer`, exact |
| `end` | channel | `applyNativeEdit({field:'status', nextValue:'ARCHIVED'})`; idempotent by `:135` | `relist`, **exact on Shopify** |
| `relist` | channel | status→ACTIVE, re-read publications, re-apply captured set, **then revalidate price against current floor/margin** | `end`, exact |
| `discontinue` | local | intent + `doNotRecreate`; no Shopify write | exact |
| `release` | channel+local | `productDelete` after the §4 capture and the ladder | **none** |
| `remove-variation` | channel | not designed here — `mapRemoteVariants` refuses implicit removals (`content-publisher.ts:187`) | lossy |
| `verify` | read-only | one query; works while gated | n/a |
Two corrections to the spine's generic table, which the adapter owns: `end` is **exact** on Shopify, not lossy; and WITHDRAWN and ENDED are observationally distinguishable *because* they sit on different axes. Had both mapped to ARCHIVED, only intent could tell them apart and the fact axis would be vacuous here.
**Availability, not just rendering (new).** `schema.publications` is attached only when the connection holds `read_publications`/`write_publications` (`linked-products-gateway.ts:92-93`). Without it, `withdraw-offer` and `restore-offer` must return `disabled('This Shopify connection cannot read or change sales channels — reconnect it with publication access')`, and `verify` must map the resulting throw to `REFUSED`, never `NOT_SELLING`.
## 3. The fact axis
One query per coordinate: `product(id){id status handle}` plus `readInformationPublications`. `null` → `ABSENT`; `ACTIVE` + ≥1 published → `SELLING`; `ACTIVE` + 0 published → `NOT_SELLING` (`reason:'withdrawn from every sales channel'`); `DRAFT` or `ARCHIVED` → `NOT_SELLING` with the verbatim status in `channelFactDetail`; an auth/scope/reauth throw → `REFUSED`; anything else → `UNKNOWN`. **Shopify emits no `SUPPRESSED`** — an out-of-stock `DENY` variant on a published ACTIVE product is `SELLING` with a finding. Say it; do not invent it.
## 4. SHOP-1, SHOP-5: containment, and the capture
`delistShopify` calls `svcAny.updateProduct(productId, {product:{id,status:'draft'}})` (`channel-delist.service.ts:193-195`) while `updateProduct` itself wraps (`shopify.service.ts:390-392`), emitting `{"product":{"product":{…}}}`. The `svcAny` cast at `:191` is what passes `tsc`; the method exists, so the delete fallback is unreachable for `unpublish`.
**Wave 1 containment:** return `{success:false, retryable:false, error:'Shopify lifecycle is not dispatched from this adapter. Use the presence verbs.'}` for both actions. **Wave 2:** delete the function, route `case 'SHOPIFY'` to `shopifyPresence.apply(job)`, assert the deletion with a zero-caller test carrying a positive control.
`productDelete` releases the handle and destroys the numeric id, variant and inventory-item ids, translations, every metafield including `nexus.family_id`, and every publication. Capture, composed from four selections that already exist, inside the adapter immediately before the mutation: `productNativeSelection` (`information-gateway.ts:14`), `PRODUCT_SELECTION` (`content-publisher.ts:27`), `readInformationPublications`, and `translations(locale:)` per published `shopLocale`. It lands on `ListingIdentity.retiredSnapshot` — not `ChannelListingSnapshot` (non-null Cascade, dies with the Product), not `ProductEvent.data` (its own comment forbids full snapshots), not `SNAPSHOT_FIELDS` (local columns, excludes `externalListingId`).
What the capture cannot restore, and the confirmation must name: app-owned reviews, order line links, the handle if taken since (`information-gateway.ts:141-144` already 409s), and Shopify's `publishedAt` history. Relist reasserts the handle as a **separate** `applyNativeEdit({field:'handle'})` with `redirectNewHandle:true` (`:145`), never by guessing `productSet` accepts one. After a whole-product purge there is no `:id`, so the replay verb belongs on a listing-recovery surface keyed on the surviving `ListingIdentity`. The bin cascade's `channelAction:'delete'` cannot satisfy the release ladder, so on Shopify it downgrades to `end` or is refused — the Owner's call.
## 5. SHOP-2: the DRAFT that hides every studio listing
`content-sync.service.ts:134` computes `isPublished` and `:139/:141/:143-144` never write `listingStatus`, so the row keeps `@default("DRAFT")` (`schema.prisma:1617`) and both hard-delete filters skip it — invisible to the operator's warning **and** to the cascade, while `platformAttributes` (product id, variant ids, inventory item ids) dies to `onDelete: Cascade`.
Producer and consumer in one write. Producer: extend `:139` and `:143-144` with `listingStatus: verifiedProduct.status === 'ACTIVE' ? 'ACTIVE' : 'INACTIVE'` plus `channelFact`, `channelFactAt`, `channelFactVia:'write-ack'`, `channelFactDetail:{shopifyStatus, publications}`. `INACTIVE`, not a literal `ARCHIVED`, because the column's documented vocabulary (`:1617`) lacks it. **And, per C5, `channel-sheet-projection.ts:63` must stop stamping the literal `ARCHIVED` onto `listingStatus`** and put it in the fact pair instead — otherwise the column and the wire disagree on the same field name the moment presence reads either. Consumer: both filters consult the shared `sellingRisk()` the spine's P9 extracts. Test the **database row** after a verified sync, and flip the remote status as the positive control — a zero-change round trip cannot test this write.
## 6. SHOP-3: Markets
Zero hits for the whole Markets vocabulary against a control of 7. `ShopifyStoreSchema` has `publications` and `locales` and no markets node; `information-translations.ts:35,63,75` deliberately discards market-scoped content. **Do not seed market rows in wave 1** — nine paths pin `'GLOBAL'` (C6), and seeding first converts a visible gap into an invisible one and flips `defaultMarket` (`scopes.ts:174-175`) for every channel at once. Wave 1 scopes Shopify presence at `(product × SHOPIFY × GLOBAL × connection × alias)` and says in one sentence that this connection is one webstore. Wave 2 delivers the buildable partial: `withdraw-offer` with `publicationScope`, since a market catalog surfaces as a publication.
## 7. SHOP-6: the review that gates the real write
`ShopifySheetReview.tsx:71-74` renders every `nativeEdit` through one untoned collapsed `Disclosure` whose body is `<pre>{change.value ?? 'Not set'}</pre>`; `informationValueLabel` is applied only to the metafield branch. `salesChannels` reaches the screen as raw gids; `status` as the raw enum. The `/synchronize` preHandler gates only `products.images.edit` and `inventory.adjust` (`shopify-linked-products.routes.ts:41-52`) — **nothing gates status or publications**.
1. Extract **one** `NativeEditReview` consumed by both `ShopifySheetReview.tsx:71` and `ShopifyLinkedWorkspace.tsx:354`. Shared means exactly the same.
2. `salesChannels` → resolve each id through `schema.publications`, guarded `?? []`; an unresolvable gid renders as "a publication this connection can no longer see". Render as DS `SummaryTable`, columns Sales channel / Now / After, with the tone carried by a `Pill` in the cell (C11).
3. `status` → `Pill`: ACTIVE `success`, DRAFT `neutral`, ARCHIVED `warning`, everything else — `UNLISTED` (`shopify-information.ts:214`) and the tolerated current value at `ShopifyNativeEditor.tsx:44` — `neutral`. Never an exhaustive switch.
4. A plan removing a publication or moving status off ACTIVE renders **expanded** inside `Banner tone="warning"` (`plan.warnings` already has a Banner renderer at `ShopifySheetReview.tsx:70`), and the primary button routes through the registry confirm at `ConfirmLevel 'confirm'`.
5. The consequence sentence is authored **server-side** beside the existing warnings at `linked-products.service.ts:192-199`, so the automation path gets it too.
6. Same preHandler: a `status` edit moving off the current value, or a `salesChannels` edit removing a publication, requires `products.publish`. These in-file guards throw 403 regardless of `NEXUS_RBAC_MODE` shadow — that is why they belong here and not only in the manifest.
## 8. Waves
**Wave 1:** SHOP-P1..P6; the fact columns written and displayed; `verify`; `hold`/`resume`/`discontinue`; the shared `NativeEditReview`; the DS `tone` additions. No channel-reaching state change ships. **Wave 2:** `withdraw-offer`/`restore-offer`, `end`/`relist`, plan/apply, the presence grid. **Wave 3:** `release`, `remove-variation`.
## 9. What I could not establish
Whether Shopify preserves publication memberships across ARCHIVE→ACTIVE (not answerable here; the design does not depend on it — `relist` re-reads and re-applies, and `applyInformationPublications` 502s on any unconfirmed member). Which arm the double-wrapped PUT takes at Shopify's end (code-certain that no arm sets `status=draft` and the success arm has no readback). The live FK state of `OutboundSyncQueue` (both arms are defects; P1 settles it). Whether any store returns `UNLISTED`. Whether `productSet` accepts `handle`. Every pixel claim — the four browser gates drive `localhost:3000` against the production API and were not run.

#### The Etsy adapter: presence for a channel with no queue, no sandbox and no webhooks (corrected)

Etsy is a first-class studio scope with a full editable sheet and — contrary to the draft — not quite zero write path: one unauthenticated route pushes quantity to a live Etsy listing today. I re-derived every claim. Fifteen corrections, four of them material: Product.etsyListingId does not exist, so the Etsy ingest is dead code rather than mis-targeted; the listingUrl fix as drafted would not work and its test pins the current answer deliberately; POST /etsy/sync/inventory/to-etsy and PUT /matrix/channel-listing/:listingId are both route-unguarded live/identity writers; and a seventh Etsy-touching CONFIRMED finding was omitted. The coordinate, the fact vocabulary from the vendored OpenAPI snapshot, the verbs with typed reach and reversal, and the enum's non-additive blast radius all survive.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-50) carries no tone, severity or group key, and both adapters drop GridAction.danger (menuAdapters.tsx: 0 'danger' against a control of 5 for 'label'). There is no .nds-menu danger rule in design-system/styles/components.css (measured: 0 danger rules against 20 .nds-menu rules). Under CH.1 as written an Etsy 'Delete the listing on Etsy…' item renders byte-identical to 'Reload'. CORRECTION to the draft: the DS-GAPS.md filing at :330-348 recites MenuItemDef as '{id,label,icon,disabled,onSelect,separator}' and claims 'no per-item title' — BOTH are now stale; Menu.tsx has carried `title` and a `description` second line since. Proposed DS addition: an optional typed `tone` plus a group key on MenuItemDef, honoured by both adapters and by a .nds-menu danger rule. This amends CH.1's RENDERING, not its rule — it adds no button. Owner's ruling.
- GridAction has one severity bit, `danger?: boolean` (registry.ts:184), and it is inert. Proposed DS addition: `reach: 'local' | 'local-destructive' | 'channel'` on GridAction — it gates availability and grouping before any preflight runs.
- ActionImpact has no reversal field, so 'you can undo this' is prose a lane authors. Proposed DS addition: `reversal: { verb: string; fidelity: 'exact' | 'lossy' | 'none' }`, rendered by ActionConfirm from the type.
- validateImpact(impact) receives only the impact (registry.ts:264-273) and cannot check an action-level field. Proposed DS addition: `validateAction(action)` beside it, refusing a reach:'channel' verb whose preflight returns no reversal.
- No DS way to render a per-channel capability absence. Proposed: NOT a new component — a typed capability record in the studio plus DS Banner. Checked design-system/components for banner/callout/alert/notice; Banner.tsx is the one tokenized callout and a second would be the copy the shared-components rule forbids.
- apps/web/src/design-system/grid/actions/registry.d.ts is stale. MEASURED THIS RUN (correction: the draft said 51): `node scripts/check-ds-dts-fresh.mjs --check` exits 1 with '52 of 243 committed declaration(s) are stale', and grid/actions/registry.d.ts is one of them. Regenerate before anyone builds against these types.

##### API
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/verify — the Etsy arm. Reads GET /shops/{shop_id}/listings/{listing_id} through etsyReader (services/etsy/read-client.ts:6-23) on the coordinate's own CX connection; writes ONLY the channelFact* columns. Maps state per §3. 401/403 → REFUSED (where an ungranted listings_w first becomes visible, since spec.ts:137 says scopes cannot be introspected). 404 → ABSENT. 429/timeout/network → NO WRITE. CORRECTION vs the draft: etsyReader's own config throws — non-ETSY connection (read-client.ts:8) and missing/invalid shop identity (:10-11) — must record UNKNOWN with 'Nexus could not ask', NEVER REFUSED; 'the channel declined us' and 'we never asked' are different facts. state_timestamp and ending_timestamp go in channelFactDetail; channelFactAt is our read time.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/plan — the Etsy arm returns, per coordinate: reach, reversal{verb,fidelity}, the resolved listing_id, and a daily-quota check. Etsy's model is daily_quota with x-remaining-today / x-limit-per-day (cx/connectors/etsy/spec.ts:212-227): a plan exceeding the remaining quota is REFUSED at plan time, named, not attempted. Etsy fan-out is always 1 — GLOBAL is the only Etsy market (marketplaces.routes.ts:78) — so an Etsy row in a plural plan must never be padded with a fan-out warning.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/apply — the ONLY Etsy writer. Wave-2 verbs: withdraw-offer, restore-offer, stop-renewing, resume-renewing. Each is one PATCH /shops/{shop_id}/listings/{listing_id} carrying exactly one field: {state:'inactive'} / {state:'active'} / {should_auto_renew:false} / {should_auto_renew:true}. The vendored update schema's state enum is exactly ['active','inactive'] (etsy-listing-schema.ts:109) and should_auto_renew is in the update block (:93) — both established. CORRECTION: stop-renewing may set presenceEffectiveFrom ONLY when a verify inside the freshness window has stored channelFactDetail.ending_timestamp; ending_timestamp is in the read block only (:15), never the update block, so without that gate the declared end date is a guess. Per-target results in the MarketOfferResult shape with no aggregate success.
- NEW — services/etsy/write-client.ts, built the way read-client.ts is: CX connection, getAccessToken(accountId), x-api-key = `${app.clientId}:${app.clientSecret}` (the CORRECT header — marketplaces/etsy.service.ts:274 sends the access token there), base host to be settled by the open question (read-client.ts:16 / spec.ts:16 vs etsy.service.ts:240). It exposes only the four one-field PATCHes above and must not be reachable from any legacy env-credential path.
- ETSY BRANCH in dispatchChannelDelist (channel-delist.service.ts:105-124) — landing WITH the enum value. Until a delete adapter exists it returns { success:false, retryable:false, error:'Nexus cannot end an Etsy listing yet. The listing is still live on Etsy and Nexus has kept its Etsy id.' } — an operator sentence in the WooCommerce branch's shape (:112-117), so the gap becomes a SKIPPED queue row with a reason instead of the default branch's `Unknown channel: ETSY` (:118-123, corrected from the draft's :121-125).
- ETSY BRANCH in dispatchSync (outbound-sync.service.ts:617-623) — same write. It must REFUSE outright, not fall through to `default: throw`. Without it, adding ETSY to the enum converts bulk-action.service.ts:2952's unfiltered cast from a loud Prisma createMany rejection (outbound-enqueue.ts:73-78) into three retries and a dead letter per row.
- GET /api/products/hard-delete-preflight — partition channelListings into cascadeCovered and cascadeUnsupported, both from ONE exported ROUTABLE_SYNC_CHANNELS, never a second hand-written array. POST /products/bulk-hard-delete returns channelCascadeSkipped beside channelCascadeEnqueued. WAVE 1, BEFORE any Etsy listing_id is persisted — the adapter is what arms this trap.
- BUILD (not rewrite) the Etsy ingest. CORRECTION: the draft called this a rewrite of etsy-sync.service.ts:142-176. That code is DEAD — it queries Product.etsyListingId, which does not exist (schema.prisma:1370 is ProductVariation's), through `(prisma as any)`, and throws at runtime; etsy-sync.job.ts:84 does the same. Build an ingest that upserts ChannelListing on (workspaceId, productId, 'ETSY', 'GLOBAL', channelConnectionId, aliasKey) with externalListingId = listing_id, listingStatus mapped from state, channelFact* with observedVia 'channel-read', and the verbatim Etsy payload into platformAttributes. It must never write Product.name / basePrice / totalStock / status, and it must run on the CX connection (etsyReader), not the env-var legacy client. Presence has no fact source until this lands.
- AUTHORISATION — an explicit in-file preHandler naming the required permission on every presence route, and as WAVE-1 PRECONDITIONS on two existing surfaces. (a) routes/matrix.routes.ts: 0 occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate against a control of 6 fastify. registrations; PUT /api/products/:id/matrix/channel-listing/:listingId (:133-149) writes a caller-supplied externalListingId with `where: { id: listingId }` — CORRECTION to the draft: it is not scoped by the :id product param at all, so it reaches ANY ChannelListing in the workspace. (b) routes/etsy.ts: 0 preHandler against 6 app.post registrations, and POST /etsy/sync/inventory/to-etsy (:88-89) reaches marketplaces/etsy.service.ts:423's live PATCH to an Etsy listing in the legacy workspace. This is the stronger of the two — it reaches Etsy, not a local column. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (apps/api/src/lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:30-32; path corrected from the draft's lib/rbac-hook.ts).
- RETIRE OR FIX — marketplaces/etsy.service.ts's three write methods. updateListingPrice (:398) and updateListingQuantity (:423) PATCH `price` and `quantity` to updateListing; I grepped the vendored update block (lines 88-115) for both, rc=1, against a positive control of 1 for `title`. Etsy v3 holds price and quantity on the inventory endpoint. These calls are dead or wrong, one of them is reachable unauthenticated, and neither state should survive into a channel with a presence model.
- STUDIO — isStoreChannel() in _studio/scopes.ts, seeded from sheet-columns.service.ts:908 (`GLOBAL_CHANNELS = ['SHOPIFY','WOOCOMMERCE','ETSY']`, consumed :946), replacing the four literals at StudioBar.tsx:35, contracts.tsx:670-676 and SchemaStatus.tsx:18. This closes the seventh Etsy-touching CONFIRMED finding, which the draft omitted; presence needs 'is this a GLOBAL single-store channel' defined once rather than as four hardcoded set claims.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE (Prisma) BUT NOT ADDITIVE IN BEHAVIOUR — enum SyncChannel gains ETSY (schema.prisma:57-66). Must NOT be applied alone. Order, binding: (1) filter bulk-action.service.ts:2937-2952; (2) in ONE write: the enum value + the ETSY branch in dispatchChannelDelist (channel-delist.service.ts:105-124) + the ETSY refusal branch in dispatchSync (outbound-sync.service.ts:617-623); (3) export ROUTABLE_SYNC_CHANNELS and gate all four hardcoded copies against it — products-catalog.routes.ts:1875, stock-import.service.ts:626, stock-movement.service.ts:757, and the bulk-action cast.
- ADDITIVE — the Presence columns on ChannelListing already specified by the state model (presenceIntent, presenceIntentAt/By/Reason, presenceEffectiveFrom/Until, channelFact, channelFactAt, channelFactVia, channelFactDetail, endedAt/By/Reason). Etsy adds no column of its own. Etsy's state_timestamp, ending_timestamp, num_favorers, should_auto_renew and url belong in channelFactDetail Json? and platformAttributes.
- ADDITIVE — model ListingIdentity as specified by the state model. For Etsy, externalListingId holds the Etsy listing_id and externalParentId is always null. The tombstone is what makes `release` mean something on a channel whose delete is genuinely permanent.
- NOT PROPOSED — no Etsy-specific table. ProductEvent (schema.prisma:15475-15478) is FK-free by design and already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing'; Etsy presence history emits there.
- CORRECTED — the draft proposed deprecating then dropping `Product.etsyListingId`. THAT COLUMN DOES NOT EXIST. /usr/bin/grep -n etsyListingId packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma returns exactly one line, :1370, inside model ProductVariation (opens :1308). NOTHING TO DEPRECATE, NOTHING TO DROP, NO BREAKING CHANGE HERE. The consequence is the opposite of the draft's: etsy-sync.service.ts:143 and etsy-sync.job.ts:84 query Product on a field it lacks, through `(prisma as any)`, and throw at runtime — the Etsy ingest and its inventory job are DEAD CODE, not merely mis-targeted. Wave 1 BUILDS an ingest; it does not rewrite one.
- OPEN, NOT PROPOSED — ProductVariation.etsyListingId / etsySku (schema.prisma:1370-1371). These are the real per-variant Etsy ids in the schema, siblings of amazonAsin / ebayVariationId / shopifyVariantId. Presence puts the external id on the coordinate instead. Dropping them is BREAKING and cross-channel in shape; I could not run the query that would show whether rows depend on them. Filed as an open question, not proposed.
- SEQUENCING (binding) — apply the SyncChannel migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring it in schema.prisma. MEMORY records apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11 with only Product.version (54 vs 51) separating the two. An enum value present in the Prisma client and absent in the database turns every Etsy enqueue into a runtime rejection — the same silent-storm shape as applying it too early.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, recited verbatim at SheetToolbar.tsx:14-20) says a scope's verbs are items of the one ⋯ overflow and never a button. The prop is `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` (SheetToolbar.tsx:104) and MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-50) has no tone, severity or group; both adapters drop GridAction.danger (0 hits, control 5); there is no .nds-menu danger rule (0 against 20 .nds-menu rules). THIS PROPOSAL DOES NOT NEED CH.1 TO BEND — every Etsy lifecycle verb lives on the presence surface and the sheet toolbar is untouched. The smallest amendment, if the Owner wants lifecycle reachable from the sheet at all, keeps the rule (no new buttons) and asks only that the overflow gain a typed tone plus a separator group; `separator` already exists. Does CH.1 stand as written, with Etsy lifecycle living only on the presence surface?
- ETSY LISTING FEES. Does reactivating an inactive Etsy listing, or renewing an expired one, incur a listing fee or restart the listing clock? I cannot establish this from the repo, and it decides whether `restore-offer` renders fidelity 'exact' or 'lossy'. Until answered it renders 'lossy' with an explicit 'Nexus cannot confirm the fee consequence'.
- THE HOST. api.etsy.com/v3/application (read-client.ts:16, spec.ts:16) or openapi.etsy.com/v3 (marketplaces/etsy.service.ts:240)? One is wrong and the first write has no sandbox to fail into.
- THE LEGACY ETSY SERVICE — RETIRE OR FIX? marketplaces/etsy.service.ts's updateListingPrice (:398) and updateListingQuantity (:423) PATCH `price` and `quantity` to updateListing; the vendored update block (lines 88-115) contains neither (rc=1, control 1 for `title`). They are dead or wrong. One of them is reachable from an unauthenticated POST today (routes/etsy.ts:88-89). Delete both methods and the route, or guard the route and fix the methods onto the inventory endpoint?
- RBAC ENFORCE. Every guard this proposal adds is a log line while rbacHook defaults to shadow (lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:30-32). Two Etsy-adjacent routes are unguarded and one reaches a live listing. Does the Owner want the Etsy adapter gated on flipping NEXUS_RBAC_MODE=enforce (blast radius: every route in the API), or shipped behind in-file guards that work regardless?
- ETSY PRESENCE READ-ONLY, OR A GATE? PublishReadiness (channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:317-322) describes amazon, ebay and shopify only, and modeForChannel returns null for Etsy AND WooCommerce (:323-332), documented as 'the most dangerous possible default' to guess. Either the response gains an etsy branch, or wave 1 states plainly that Etsy presence is READ-ONLY. Etsy must not be silently swept up by the WooCommerce containment rule — it is an in-scope channel with an absent gate, not an out-of-scope one.
- PRODUCTVARIATION.ETSYLISTINGID. schema.prisma:1370-1371 holds per-variant etsyListingId and etsySku alongside amazonAsin, ebayVariationId, shopifyVariantId. Presence puts the external id on the coordinate (ChannelListing / ListingIdentity). Does the Owner want these variant columns deprecated as part of the Etsy work, or left as a separate cross-channel cleanup? Dropping them is BREAKING and I could not run the query that would show whether rows depend on them.
- D9 — the Banner and the presence surface. The Etsy Banner extends the existing sheet body (no new page), which I read as within D9 (StudioHeader.tsx:87-97). But the presence surface itself is the state model's question, not mine, and if the Owner reads it as a second way in, only the per-coordinate Etsy verbs survive and the plural plan does not.
- WHETHER ANY ETSY LISTING_ID IS ALREADY PERSISTED. matrix.routes.ts:149 can have written one at any time, from any caller, with no product scoping and no auth. I have no database access. A single SELECT settles whether ETSY-1 is armed today rather than armed by wave 1, and it should be run before the preflight partition is scheduled.

##### Risks
- THE ENUM IS THE SHARPEST RISK AND IT IS NOT ADDITIVE IN BEHAVIOUR. bulk-action.service.ts:2937-2952 casts every ChannelListing.channel into the four-member union with no filter, and enqueueOutboundRowsInstant createMany's the rows (outbound-enqueue.ts:73-78). Today an Etsy row rejects the batch loudly. The moment ETSY joins SyncChannel that line starts succeeding into dispatchSync's `default: throw` (outbound-sync.service.ts:617-623): three retries and a dead letter per row, per bulk edit. Applying the pre-approved additive migration ALONE converts a loud failure into a silent storm. Filter first; land the enum, the delist branch and the dispatch branch in ONE write.
- MY OWN ADAPTER ARMS THE HARD-DELETE DEFECT. ETSY-1 is currently unreachable only because no Etsy row carries an externalListingId (verified: /usr/bin/grep -rn externalListingId apps/api/src | grep -i etsy → rc=1). The first thing the adapter does is persist a listing_id. If the preflight partition does not land BEFORE the ingest, the purge path silently drops live Etsy listings and the audit's own critical finding ships with an operator's name on it.
- TWO UNGUARDED WRITE SURFACES ARE LIVE TODAY, NOT AFTER THIS SHIPS. routes/etsy.ts has 0 preHandler over 6 app.post registrations, and POST /etsy/sync/inventory/to-etsy (:88-89) reaches a live PATCH to an Etsy listing in the legacy workspace. matrix.routes.ts has 0 preHandler over 6 registrations and its PUT (:133-149) writes a caller-supplied externalListingId with `where: { id: listingId }` — no product scoping, any listing in the workspace. The global rbacHook defaults to shadow (lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:30-32), so a new in-file guard is the only thing that works.
- THE INGEST IS DEAD CODE, WHICH CHANGES THE COST ESTIMATE. Product has no etsyListingId; etsy-sync.service.ts:143 and etsy-sync.job.ts:84 query it through `(prisma as any)` and throw at runtime. Wave 1 is not 'rewrite an ingest', it is 'build one'. The real per-coordinate ids already in the schema are ProductVariation.etsyListingId / etsySku (schema.prisma:1370-1371) — a variant-level id where the coordinate model wants a listing-level one. Anyone estimating from the draft will under-scope this.
- TWO HOSTS, ONE OF THEM WRONG. read-client.ts:16 and spec.ts:16 use https://api.etsy.com/v3/application; marketplaces/etsy.service.ts:240 uses https://openapi.etsy.com/v3. A write client built on the wrong one fails on a live listing with no sandbox to catch it (spec.ts:230).
- NO SANDBOX AND NO SCOPE INTROSPECTION MEANS THE FIRST WRITE IS A PRODUCTION EXPERIMENT. spec.ts:230 and :137. The rehearsed withdraw→restore pair mitigates but does not remove this: if listings_w was never granted, the first attempt fails ON a real listing, and 'insufficient_scope' is only observable at that moment.
- A DAILY QUOTA IS NOT A RATE LIMIT. spec.ts:212-227 parses x-remaining-today / x-limit-per-day, model 'daily_quota'. A plural plan that exhausts it does not slow down, it stops for the calendar day, and a half-applied plan across coordinates is the worst state to leave presence in. Plan-time refusal is mandatory, not advisory.
- THE HONEST MODEL MAKES THE ETSY SCOPE LOOK EMPTY. Every Etsy verdict reads 'unknown — never checked' until the ingest lands, and the Listing status group is re-labelled 'Not imported yet'. That is the change, not a regression, and the Owner has to want it before it ships.
- STOP-RENEWING CAN PRESENT A GUESSED DATE. ending_timestamp exists only in the read block (etsy-listing-schema.ts:15). Without the verify gate in C10, the surface shows an operator a specific end date Nexus never observed.

##### The specification
## CORRECTIONS
**C1. `Product.etsyListingId` does not exist. The Etsy ingest is dead code, not mis-targeted.** `/usr/bin/grep -n etsyListingId packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma` returns exactly one line — `:1370`, inside `model ProductVariation` (which opens at `:1308`; the preceding `^model` is `RepricingDecision` at `:1274`). `awk '/^model Product \{/,/^\}/'` over the schema, grepped for `etsy`, returns rc=1. So `etsy-sync.service.ts:143` (`(prisma as any).product.findFirst({ where: { etsyListingId } })`) and `etsy-sync.job.ts:84` (`product.findMany({ where: { etsyListingId: { not: null } } })`) both query a field `Product` does not have; the `as any` is what lets them compile. They throw at runtime. The draft's schema item — "deprecate `Product.etsyListingId` in wave 1, schedule the BREAKING drop later" — proposes retiring a column that was never there, and its wave-1 "rewrite the ingest" understates the state: there is nothing to rewrite *from*. Replaced below.
**C2. The `listingUrl` fix as drafted would not work, and its test is a deliberate pin.** The draft says "derive both from one table". That fails: `if (!tld) return null` at `listingUrl.ts:48` fires before the `switch` for market `GLOBAL`, and Etsy needs no TLD at all — the market code is irrelevant. The fix is to hoist `case 'ETSY'` **above** the TLD lookup. Second, `listingUrl.vitest.test.ts:29-33` asserts `ETSY→null` under the title "returns null for channels not addressable from a marketplace code", grouped with SHOPIFY and WOOCOMMERCE. That is an intended behaviour, and the draft called it "the wrong answer" without saying so. It should change because the premise is false for Etsy alone: `listings-syndication.routes.ts:115-116` (not `:113`) already returns `https://www.etsy.com/listing/${externalId}` from the id by itself.
**C3. "Zero write path" is over-stated, and the one that exists is unauthenticated.** `routes/etsy.ts:88-89` registers `POST /etsy/sync/inventory/to-etsy`, which reaches `EtsyService.updateListingQuantity` (`marketplaces/etsy.service.ts:423`) — a live `PATCH /shops/{shop_id}/listings/{listing_id}`. `/usr/bin/grep -c "preHandler|requirePermission" apps/api/src/routes/etsy.ts` = **0**, against 6 `app.post` registrations. In the legacy workspace with the five env vars set (`utils/config.ts:106-112`), that is an unauthenticated POST that writes to a live Etsy listing.
**C4. `matrix.routes.ts` is worse than the draft states.** `updateChannelListing` uses `where: { id: listingId }` (`:140-141`) — it never scopes by the `:id` product param. It writes a caller-supplied `externalListingId` onto *any* ChannelListing in the workspace, not merely any channel. 0 `preHandler` against 6 `fastify.` registrations, confirmed.
**C5. RBAC hook path.** It is `apps/api/src/lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts`, not `lib/rbac-hook.ts`. Shadow default documented `:8-12`; `mode()` at `:30-32`.
**C6. The honest-disclosure inventory was incomplete — the sentence is hidden in two places, not one.** Besides `SaveIndicator.tsx:36-37` (rendered inside a `title=` attribute at `:46`), `SchemaStatus.tsx:42` carries "Saving changes here updates Nexus; it does not send or publish an Etsy listing" **inside a Modal**, behind a "Requirements" button (`:32`). A modal an operator must open is no more a disclosure than a tooltip.
**C7. A seventh Etsy-touching CONFIRMED finding was omitted.** `ch:woocommerce`, "content-language control gated on a hardcoded `['SHOPIFY','ETSY']`". Its Etsy half is squarely in scope and presence depends on it: `StudioBar.tsx:35`, `contracts.tsx:670-676`, `sheet-columns.service.ts:908` (`GLOBAL_CHANNELS = ['SHOPIFY','WOOCOMMERCE','ETSY']`, consumed `:946`), `SchemaStatus.tsx:18`. Four literals for one concept. Adopted below as `isStoreChannel()` — a set claim derived once, not WooCommerce channel work.
**C8. d.ts staleness measured this run:** `node scripts/check-ds-dts-fresh.mjs --check` exits 1 with "**52** of 243", not 51. `grid/actions/registry.d.ts` is in the list (grep count 1), so that half stands.
**C9. Line cites.** `channel-delist.service.ts` `default` branch is `:118-123`, not `:121-125` (the WooCommerce branch at `:112-117` is right). CH.1's recital is `SheetToolbar.tsx:14-20`. `crossChannel.ts` `mechanismFor` is `:40-50`. `read-client.ts` is `:6-23` and the base URL `:16` — both confirmed. `etsy.ts:123` for the state helpText is **correct** (I mis-counted it as `:125` on first read and confirmed `:123` by grep — recording the confirmation, not a change).
**C10. `stop-renewing` cannot set the ENDED date in wave 1 as drafted.** `ending_timestamp` appears only in the *listing* (read) block (`etsy-listing-schema.ts:15`) — never in `update`. It reaches Nexus only through a `verify`. So `stop-renewing` must be **gated on a fresh verify that populated `channelFactDetail.ending_timestamp`**; without one, `presenceEffectiveFrom` is a guess presented as a date.
**C11. `release` must be shown as unspecifiable in the verb table, not merely deferred.** §7 says `deleteListing` is in no repo artefact; the table nonetheless gave it a mechanism and a reach. Same treatment as `renew`.
**C12. `etsyReader` has two config refusals that must not read as `REFUSED`.** It throws on a non-ETSY connection (`read-client.ts:8`) and on a missing/invalid shop identity (`:10-11`). Recording those as fact `REFUSED` says "Etsy declined us" when the truth is "Nexus never asked". They record `UNKNOWN` with a "could not ask" detail.
**C13. The enum's allow-list census is four copies, not three.** Confirmed this run: `products-catalog.routes.ts:1875` `SUPPORTED`, `stock-import.service.ts:626` `VALID_SYNC_TARGETS`, `stock-movement.service.ts:757` `validTargets` — plus the unfiltered cast at `bulk-action.service.ts:2952`.
**C14. The dead-letter claim holds, and the mechanism is `createMany`.** `enqueueOutboundRowsInstant` createMany's the rows (`outbound-enqueue.ts:73-78`), so today one Etsy row rejects the whole batch loudly.
**C15. The mapping of Etsy `draft` needed a verdict rule, which the draft omitted.** Kept as fact `NOT_SELLING`, with the derived verdict against intent `LIVE` stated explicitly as `diverged`.
---
## 0. Method
Re-derived from the working tree with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed`. No API called, no server started, nothing written. The three `ground-*.json` files are absent from the scratchpad (re-confirmed by `ls`). Six `ch:etsy` findings plus one `ch:woocommerce` finding touch Etsy; all seven are CONFIRMED and I opened the cited lines for all seven.
`apps/api/src/services/pim/channel-specs/etsy-listing-schema.ts:1-4` carries the repo's own vendored Etsy OpenAPI snapshot (`source: etsy.com/openapi/...`, `verifiedAt: "2026-09-10"`). That is the authority, not memory of Etsy's docs.
## 1. What exists
**Read:** `services/etsy/read-client.ts:6-23`. GETs only, `https://api.etsy.com/v3/application` (`:16`), CX token, `x-api-key: ${clientId}:${clientSecret}`.
**Connection:** `cx/connectors/etsy/spec.ts`. `ETSY_REQUIRED_SCOPES` (`:19-32`) already includes `listings_r`, `listings_w`, `listings_d` — no new consent grant. Three facts dominate: `sandbox: { available: false }` (`:230`), `webhooks: { subscriptionApi: false, lifecycleTopics: [] }` (`:228`), and `:137` "Etsy's identity endpoints do not introspect OAuth scopes" — a token's write scope is unverifiable except by attempting a write.
**Write, legacy:** `marketplaces/etsy.service.ts` — three methods (`:398`, `:423`, `:448`), gated by `requireLegacyCredentials('Etsy')` (`:263` → `lib/workspace-legacy-credentials.ts:4-8`, 409 outside the legacy workspace) and five env vars. Two defects: `baseUrl = https://openapi.etsy.com/v3` (`:240`) against the modern paths' `api.etsy.com/v3/application` — two hosts, both cannot be right; and `'x-api-key': this.accessToken` (`:274`), the bearer in the API-key header. And, decisively: `price` and `quantity` are **not** in the vendored `update` block. I grepped lines 88-115 for both, rc=1, against a positive control of 1 for `title`. Two of three shipped write methods target fields the repo's own snapshot says `updateListing` does not accept. Per C3, one of them is reachable by an unauthenticated POST.
**Ingest:** dead (C1). `etsy-sync.service.ts:142-176` writes `Product.name/basePrice/totalStock/status`, never a ChannelListing — through a `where` clause `Product` cannot satisfy.
**Queue:** `SyncChannel` (`schema.prisma:57-66`) has no ETSY. **Delist:** 0 ETSY occurrences in `channel-delist.service.ts`; the `default` (`:118-123`) returns `Unknown channel: ${targetChannel}`. **Hard delete:** counted at `products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682`, dropped at `:1875`.
Nothing Etsy runs unattended: `jobs/etsy-sync.job.ts` has one "cron" hit (an import), and `CRON_REGISTRY['etsy-sync']` (`cron-registry.ts:235`) fires only from `sync-logs.routes.ts:512`. That claim survives; the "zero write path" one does not.
## 2. Corrections to the audit's own reading
**ETSY-2 is half wrong, as drafted.** `state` is read-only by construction (`channel-specs/etsy.ts:84`, `writable = … && key !== 'state'`; `editable:false` applied `:105`) and both help texts are honest (`:123`, `:55`). The defect is that the copy points at channel actions that exist nowhere, and the "Listing status" group (`etsy.ts:4`) is permanently empty because the ingest is dead. The sheet is honest about the save and dishonest about the source.
**ETSY-1 is armed, not currently reachable — and the adapter arms it.** The preflight needs `listingStatus IN (ACTIVE,INACTIVE)` **and** `externalListingId NOT NULL`; every Etsy row the studio creates is `'DRAFT'` with no id (`channel-value-write.ts:16`; `marketplaces.routes.ts:316`). `/usr/bin/grep -rn externalListingId apps/api/src | grep -i etsy` returns rc=1 — no Etsy-specific writer. The one channel-agnostic hole is `matrix.routes.ts:149` (see C4). So ETSY-1 is a **wave-1 precondition**, not a follow-up.
## 3. The coordinate
`(product, ETSY, GLOBAL, channelConnectionId, aliasKey)`. `GLOBAL` is fixed — `marketplaces.routes.ts:78` is the only Etsy market row. No fulfilment split, so `offerScope` is always `'all'`.
**FACT, from `etsy-listing-schema.ts:12** (`active | inactive | sold_out | draft | expired`): `active`→`SELLING`; `inactive`→`NOT_SELLING` (seller-withdrawn); `sold_out`→`NOT_SELLING` (quantity exhausted; renewal decides return); `draft`→`NOT_SELLING` (never published); `expired`→`NOT_SELLING` (clock ran out); HTTP 404→`ABSENT`; 401/403→`REFUSED` (incl. `insufficient_scope`); 429/timeout/network→**no write**, the previous fact and its as-of stand; `etsyReader` config throws (`read-client.ts:8,10-11`)→`UNKNOWN` with "Nexus could not ask", **never `REFUSED`** (C12).
**Verdict rules, stated (C15):** intent `LIVE` + fact `NOT_SELLING` (any detail) → `diverged`, never `agrees`. Intent `WITHDRAWN` + fact `NOT_SELLING/inactive` → `agrees`. Intent `LIVE` + fact `NOT_SELLING/draft` → `diverged`, with the detail naming that Etsy has never published it. Any fact older than the last intent change with nothing in flight → `unknown`.
`state_timestamp` (`:19`) and `ending_timestamp` (`:15`) go into `channelFactDetail`. **`channelFactAt` is our read time, never Etsy's timestamp.**
`etsy.service.ts:19` documents a stale v2 vocabulary (`deactivated`, `alchemy`). Use the vendored, dated set; delete the comment.
## 4. Verbs
`reach` on `GridAction` (`registry.ts:184` is where `danger?: boolean` sits), `reversal { verb, fidelity }` on `ActionImpact`, `validateAction` beside `validateImpact` (`:264`).
| verb | reach | mechanism | reversal |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hold`/`resume` | local | `syncPaused`, no channel call | `resume`, exact |
| `withdraw-offer` | channel | `updateListing { state:'inactive' }` | `restore-offer`, **lossy** pending the fee question |
| `restore-offer` | channel | `updateListing { state:'active' }` | `withdraw-offer`, exact |
| `stop-renewing`/`resume-renewing` | channel | `updateListing { should_auto_renew:false\|true }` | exact until `ending_timestamp` |
| `end`, `relist` | — | not offered; the writable state enum has two members | — |
| `renew` | — | **not specifiable — §7** | — |
| `release` | — | **not specifiable — §7** | — |
| `verify` | channel, read-only | `getListing` via `etsyReader` | n/a |
| `discontinue`, `exclude-child` | local | intent only | exact |
`withdraw-offer` is grounded exactly: the vendored `update` block's `state` enum is `["active","inactive"]` (`etsy-listing-schema.ts:109`) and `should_auto_renew` is in the block (`:93`). Consequences the repo can prove: `listing_id`, `url` and `num_favorers` kept (`:23-24`).
**`end` is deliberately absent.** There is no rung between inactive and delete. Aliasing `end` onto `withdraw-offer` would make one cross-channel plural plan mean two different things on two channels. `stop-renewing` is the honest Etsy shape of that intent — and per C10 it may only declare `presenceEffectiveFrom` when a fresh `verify` has stored `ending_timestamp`; otherwise it declares intent `ENDED` with **no date** and says why.
**`sold-out` is a fact, never a verb.** Quantity lives on `updateListingInventory`, which no repo client calls, so **`ZERO_PIN` is refused on Etsy** with that reason rather than silently doing nothing.
## 5. The enum and its blast radius
Additive in Prisma, **not additive in behaviour**. Four copies of "routable", confirmed this run (C13): `products-catalog.routes.ts:1875`; `bulk-action.service.ts:2937-2952`, **unfiltered** — it selects every ChannelListing for the product and casts `l.channel` into the four-member union, and `enqueueOutboundRowsInstant` `createMany`s the rows (`outbound-enqueue.ts:73-78`), so today an Etsy row rejects the batch loudly and the moment ETSY joins the enum the same line starts succeeding into `dispatchSync`'s `default: throw` (`outbound-sync.service.ts:617-623`) — three retries and a dead letter per row; `stock-import.service.ts:626` and `stock-movement.service.ts:757`, which keep skipping Etsy silently.
**Sequencing, binding:** filter `bulk-action.service.ts` **first**; land the enum value, the `ETSY` branch in `dispatchChannelDelist` (`:105-124`) and the `ETSY` branch in `dispatchSync` in **one write**; export one `ROUTABLE_SYNC_CHANNELS` and gate every copy against it.
The ETSY delist branch, until the adapter exists, returns a typed non-retryable unsupported result in the WooCommerce branch's shape (`:112-117`) with an operator sentence — "Nexus cannot end an Etsy listing yet. It is still live on Etsy. Its Etsy id has been kept." The gap becomes a SKIPPED queue row with a reason instead of an invisible array filter.
## 6. The honest interim
One typed record, generalising the precedent at `images/plan/crossChannel.ts:40-50` (`mechanismFor` → `'unsupported'`, "Named as unsupported rather than silently omitted"):
```
channelCapability('ETSY') = { read:'operator-triggered', create:'absent',
  update:'absent', lifecycle:'absent', verify:'absent',
  reason:'Nexus can read an Etsy shop. It cannot yet create, update or end an Etsy listing.' }
```
One definition drives the banner, `PublishMenu`, the wizard, the presence verdict and every disabled reason. Seven changes, no new components:
1. **DS `Banner tone="info"`**, persistent, top of the Etsy sheet body. It replaces the `title=` tooltip at `SaveIndicator.tsx:46`; the modal line at `SchemaStatus.tsx:42` is kept but reworded so it is no longer the only statement (C6).
2. **Disable `offer-toggle` on the Etsy scope** with a specific reason. It is offered today (`channelActions.ts:404`; `ChannelScopeChannel` includes `'ETSY'` at `sheet/channel/types.ts:149`) and its confirmation says "Buyers stop seeing this offer on this channel and market" (`channelActions.ts:229-230`) — false twice on Etsy: nothing reaches Etsy, and `offerActive` suppresses nothing unless `NEXUS_RESPECT_OFFER_ACTIVE === '1'` (`outbound-sync.service.ts:653`), which is opt-in and off.
3. **Every Etsy verdict is `unknown` — "Never checked"**, never `agrees`, until the ingest lands.
4. **Re-word the "Listing status" group**: `readOnlyReason` "Reported by Etsy" → "Not imported yet."
5. **Fix `listingUrl` for Etsy** by hoisting `case 'ETSY'` above the TLD lookup (C2), amending the comment at `:59-60` to name Shopify and WooCommerce only, and changing `listingUrl.vitest.test.ts:29-33` — naming that the test currently pins `null` as intended, and that the intent is right for the other two channels and wrong for Etsy alone.
6. **Publish and the wizard** state the absence from the record, not from a literal.
7. **`isStoreChannel()` in `scopes.ts`** (C7), seeded from `sheet-columns.service.ts:908`, replacing the four literals at `StudioBar.tsx:35`, `contracts.tsx:670-676` and `SchemaStatus.tsx:18`. Presence needs "is this a GLOBAL single-store channel" defined once.
## 7. What I could not establish
- **`renew` has no basis here.** `/usr/bin/grep -rn "renew"` over `marketplaces/etsy.service.ts`, `services/etsy/` and `routes/etsy.ts` returns one hit: the `should_auto_renew` declaration. The vendored snapshot carries property schemas only, no operation list. Do not hand-write the path — extend the generator, re-run it, then wire. Renew costs money and cannot be undone.
- **`deleteListing`**: same position. The scope is requested; the operation is in no repo artefact. Hence C11.
- **Whether any Etsy `ChannelListing` row holds a real `listing_id`.** No database access.
- **Etsy's listing-fee behaviour** on reactivate and renew. Until answered, `restore-offer` renders fidelity `lossy` with "reactivation may restart Etsy's listing clock; Nexus cannot confirm the fee consequence".
- **Which of the two hosts is right** (`api.etsy.com/v3/application` vs `openapi.etsy.com/v3`). Establish before the first write.
- **Nothing was measured in a browser.**
## 8. Waves
**Wave 1:** the capability record and the seven interim changes; ETSY-1's partition, landed *before* any writer stores a `listing_id`; the RBAC preconditions (C3, C4) — these two are the sharpest items in the proposal and neither is Etsy-specific work anyone else is doing; the ingest **built** (not rewritten) onto ChannelListing with the fact columns, on the CX connection; `verify`; `listingUrl`; `hold`/`resume`, `discontinue`, `exclude-child`. No channel-reaching write.
**Wave 2:** the enum with its four consumers in one write; `withdraw-offer`/`restore-offer`; `stop-renewing`/`resume-renewing` (gated per C10); plan/apply with a daily-quota check (`spec.ts:212-227`).
**Wave 3:** `renew` and `release`, both gated on the operation list being generated first.
Because Etsy has no sandbox (`spec.ts:230`), the first write on any connection is on a live listing — the condition `channelActions.ts:15-21` already names for eBay. So the first write per connection is `withdraw-offer` on an operator-chosen listing followed by `restore-offer`, run as one rehearsed pair. Because scope cannot be introspected (`spec.ts:137`), an `insufficient_scope` refusal records fact `REFUSED` and leaves intent untouched.

#### Presence on eBay — the channel adapter, corrected

An adversarial pass over the eBay presence adapter spec. Seventeen corrections: `Badge` is the ads-program badge and cannot carry an intent; deriving the connection from `{itemId}` breaks the Inventory lane because that form reads `SharedListingMembership` only; shared-listing Guard 2 is only correct AFTER the target rows are deleted, so extracting it "verbatim" into a plan would fail shut on every coordinate; `EbayListingIndex` is an ads read-model with a truncation circuit-breaker, not a lifecycle store; the GB defect hits every Trading call, not just `delistEbay`; the reconcile job is a capped sample, not a daily census; the fact-stamp must join on ItemID (joining on SKU manufactures a REFUTED defect); eBay offers have no PATCH; and keeping `externalListingId` flips a test-pinned classifier, so `sellingRisk` must ship in the same wave. Two CONFIRMED findings the spec ignored are named.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- Badge is NOT a general badge and cannot carry a presence intent. BadgeProps is {program: AdProgram, className?, children} rendering `nds-badge ${program}` (primitives/Badge.tsx:6-13) — the SP/SB/SD ads-program badge. CORRECTED USE: intent = Tag (tone?: TagTone), fact = Pill (tone + dot). No DS change needed for this; the proposal's claim was the gap.
- Menu cannot express a destructive item or a severity. MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-50) has no tone, no severity, no group key; menuAdapters.tsx contains 0 occurrences of 'danger' against a positive control of 5 for 'label'; components.css has 20 .nds-menu rules and no danger rule among them. 'End the listing on eBay IT' renders byte-identical to 'Open record'. ADD an optional `tone?: 'default'|'danger'` on MenuItemDef plus a .nds-menu-item.danger rule in design-system/styles/components.css, and map GridAction.danger onto it in both adapters. Already filed at .claude/DS-GAPS.md:330-348 — whose recital of MenuItemDef is stale (title and description have since landed, and Menu.tsx documents both).
- GridAction has no REACH. `danger?: boolean` (registry.ts:183-184) cannot distinguish 'writes a local flag' from 'ends a live eBay listing'. Its own doc claims destructive verbs are 'grouped apart and rendered with danger tone by every adapter' — measured false. ADD `reach: 'local'|'local-destructive'|'channel'` to GridAction.
- ActionImpact has no REVERSIBILITY. ADD `reversal: {verb: string; fidelity: 'exact'|'lossy'|'none'}` and render the sentence in ActionConfirm.tsx from the field so no lane authors it. ActionConfirm today keys its only severity signal on requiresTypedConfirm (Button variant={needsTyping ? 'danger' : 'primary'}), so a channel-reaching verb with a plain confirm renders as a primary button.
- validateImpact(impact) receives only the impact (registry.ts:264-273) and cannot see an action-level reach. ADD a sibling validateAction(action) refusing a reach:'channel' verb whose preflight returned no reversal.
- No DS component states a two-axis truth (declared intent vs observed fact with a mandatory as-of and a freshness degradation). ADD design-system/components/PresenceMark.tsx composing Tag (intent) + Pill (fact, with dot) + KeyValue-style as-of, whose DEFAULT rendering is UNKNOWN and which can never render green without an observedAt. The doctrine ships one tab away as page-local code (ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:3-13, "'not checked' is not 'no drift'").
- No DS confirmation exists for a verb raised OUTSIDE a grid. ActionConfirm is reached through the grid action registry; the per-alias row menu on the presence surface must go through the same registry rather than hand-rolling a dialog. If the presence surface ever needs a non-grid verb, the gap is real and must be filed rather than hand-rolled.

##### API
- NEW SERVICE apps/api/src/services/ebay-presence-adapter.service.ts — replaces delistEbay (channel-delist.service.ts:243-306). plan(coordinate, verb, options) and apply(plan, preflightToken). Honours the verb (no more `_action` at :245); resolves the lane (ACTIVE SharedListingMembership ⇒ Trading, else Inventory) and refuses cross-lane; resolves the site id BEFORE the auth block; returns per-coordinate MarketOfferResult-shaped results with no aggregate boolean.
- CORRECTED (C2) — connection resolution order: `{ listingId: channelListing.id }` FIRST (reads ChannelListing.channelConnectionId), `{ itemId, marketplace }` only as a Trading-lane cross-check, and REFUSE when neither yields an attributed account. The spec's 'derive from {itemId}' alone would break every Inventory-lane delist: connection-resolver.service.ts:212-224 resolves that form through sharedListingMembership.findFirst and throws NoConnectionError when there is no membership row — and, when the membership has no channelConnectionId, falls back to resolveDeclared('EBAY') anyway, which is the drift the change was meant to remove.
- NEW SERVICE apps/api/src/services/ebay-presence-guards.ts — CORRECTED (C3): the five guards extracted with an EXCLUSION SET, not verbatim. survivingReferences(itemIds, { excludingListingIds }) over ChannelListing.externalListingId + ACTIVE SharedListingMembership.itemId.
