Guard 2's correctness depends on the target rows already being deleted (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:695-703 says so); from a plan, nothing is deleted, so without the exclusion the guard skips EVERY ItemID and reports 'shared'. Guards: (1) parentId DB-truth variation-child (:685); (2) surviving-reference, exclusion-aware (:695-723); (3) whole-listing delist only for an explicit listing-level target (:356-360); (4) own-ItemIDs-only for a family end (:565-576); (5) per-listing marketplace, never the target's (:576-580).
- NEW SERVICE apps/api/src/services/ebay-presence-hold.service.ts — CORRECTED (C9): Inventory lane is GET-merge-PUT (read the live offer, overwrite availableQuantity, strip read-only fields, PUT the merged body) — eBay Inventory updateOffer is a FULL PUT and there is no PATCH; whether the GET body round-trips is a sandbox precondition. It deliberately does not reuse pushOffersOnly, which refuses a priceless row (ebay-variation-push.service.ts:2238-2241), reconciles and REPLACES policy ids (:2168-2185) and rewrites the whole offer body (:2260-2278). Trading lane: reviseInventoryStatusBatch, ≤4 SKUs/call (ebay-trading-api.service.ts:403, REVISE_INVENTORY_STATUS_MAX_ENTRIES at :51).
- CHANGE ebay-trading-api.service.ts:10-22 — add exported ebaySiteMarket(code) folding GB→UK and ebayListingRegion(code) folding UK→GB; siteIdForMarket normalises through the first. WIDER THAN THE SPEC CLAIMED (C6): siteIdForMarket is also called inside reviseInventoryStatus (:301), getItemListingStatus (:321), getItemQuantities (:395), reviseInventoryStatusBatch (:408) and tryRemoveVariationFromListing (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:189), each from its own ctx.market — so verify and the Trading hold throw on 'GB' exactly as the delist does.
- CHANGE channel-delist.service.ts:274-282 — site-id resolution moves OUT of the auth try/catch and returns EBAY_UNKNOWN_MARKET (non-retryable, distinct from EBAY_DELIST_AUTH_ERROR). applyDelistResultToQueue:332-343 marks a non-retryable failure FAILED + isDead on the FIRST attempt, so the misclassification is terminal.
- CHANGE channel-delist.service.ts:223-239 — split ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS into ENDED_CONFIRMED (already ended / already closed / auction already closed / item has already been deleted) and COULD_NOT_ASK (/item cannot be accessed/i :227, /invalid item/i :231, /item not found/i :230, and /listing (is )?not (…|found)/i :229 which also folds a not-found). COULD_NOT_ASK returns outcome UNKNOWN with fact REFUSED — never success (:296). A transport failure is likewise UNKNOWN.
- CHANGE ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1956-1960 and :1979-1983 — the end path sets isPublished:false and does NOT null externalListingId. /usr/bin/grep -c isPublished on that file returns 0 today, and isPublished is the only lifecycle gate in the worker (bullmq-sync.worker.ts:177-190). PAIRED, SAME WAVE (C10): sellingRisk plus the delete-confirmation copy, because listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:17 pins isLiveOnChannel(listingStatus 'ENDED') === true and today only the nulled id keeps ended rows out of that warning.
- CHANGE ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1849 — the deactivate filter must apply the SAME Trading-lane discrimination the end branch applies at :1940-1947, instead of filtering only `_shared !== true`. Today a Trading-lane row asked to deactivate reaches pushOffersOnly and dies with 'No existing offer … run Full Publish first' (ebay-variation-push.service.ts:2255-2257).
- CHANGE ebay-flat-file.routes.ts — every eBay write resolves its base URL via getEbayApiBaseForMode(getEbayPublishMode()). The file imports getEbayPublishMode (:54) but checks only mode==='gated' (:1513-1516, :3300-3302) and then uses the module constant EBAY_API_BASE (:77); getEbayApiBaseForMode has 0 callers there and returns '' for gated/dry-run precisely so a fetch fails loudly. channel-delist.service.ts, ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts and ebay-trading-api.service.ts consult the gate nowhere (rc=1; controls: 3 hits in outbound-sync.service.ts, 3 in ebay-flat-file.routes.ts), while callTradingApi gates on its own NEXUS_EBAY_REAL_API (:234-246) and picks its endpoint from EBAY_SANDBOX (:224-228) — so an End can reach live eBay while every ordinary push is refused.
- CHANGE products-catalog.routes.ts:1852-1899 — the cascade stops enqueueing one unguarded, undeduplicated row per ChannelListing. It calls the guard module WITH its own target ids as the exclusion set (C3), dedupes by (externalListingId, marketplace), and enqueues only survivors. A 21-row shared family currently enqueues 21 End jobs for one ItemID; the first ends it and the other 20 return success via the already-ended matcher.
- CHANGE — narrow to the full five-level coordinate, BOTH the pre-read and the write in every case: ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:636-639 + :645-647; flat-file/listing-content-write.service.ts:112-120 (findFirst on {productId, channel, OR:[{marketplace},{region}]}, no account, no alias — CORRECTED PATH, C5); and NEWLY ADDED (C15) the deactivate post-write channelListing.updateMany({productId:{in}, channel:'EBAY', region},{quantity:0}) at ~:1905-1910, the end parent write over {product:{OR:[{id},{parentId}]}, channel:'EBAY', region} at :1956-1960 which reaches every child, the Trading membership pre-check {sku, marketplace, status:'ACTIVE'} at :1940-1946 with no channelConnectionId, and sync-control.routes.ts:895-898 (ZERO_PIN/PAUSE/RESUME row resolution). Narrowing a write without its pre-read is strictly worse than today. Before any of it: measure the actual NULL distribution of channelConnectionId and aliasKey, or the narrowing silently matches nothing.
- CHANGE ebay-item-status-reconcile.job.ts:78-95 and outbound-sync.service.ts:1795-1830 — both existing observers additionally stamp channelFact / channelFactAt / channelFactVia on the matching ChannelListing rows, JOINED ON ChannelListing.externalListingId === itemId plus channelConnectionId, folding UK→GB (C8). NOT by SKU: SharedListingMembership has no aliasKey and a nullable productId, and a SKU join manufactures the defect the audit REFUTED ('stamps one SKU-level status onto every listing row of that SKU'). Coverage is honest: the job caps at NEXUS_EBAY_ITEM_RECONCILE_MAX (default 100) groups per account (:45,:70) and skips GetItem errors fail-closed, so unreached coordinates stay UNKNOWN and the 'capped from N' figure reaches the surface (C7).
- NEW eBay branch on POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/verify — Trading: getItemListingStatus (ebay-trading-api.service.ts:316). Inventory: GET /sell/inventory/v1/offer?sku=&marketplace_id= reading the offer's `status` and `listing.listingId`, NOT merely whether an offer object came back — an UNPUBLISHED offer exists and is not selling (C16). Four-valued; absent → ABSENT, unpublished → NOT_SELLING, HTTP error → REFUSED, no answer → could-not-ask.
- NEW GATE scripts/check-ebay-market-codes.mjs — derives every distinct eBay market code written or read by any writer FROM SOURCE (never a hardcoded list) and asserts each resolves to a site id in BOTH fold directions, with a positive control in the same run.
- PRESERVE (C11) — the per-market FILE EXCLUSION stamp on Product.categoryAttributes (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:650-660, incident #12). Presence keeps the ChannelListing row alive, so an ENDED/DISCONTINUED/RELEASED intent must become the source of flat-file suppression; the JSON stamp is not removed in the same change that stops deleting rows.
- COMPLIANCE GUARD (C12a) — restore-offer and relist carry complianceEvaluated:false on any EU eBay coordinate and say so in the confirmation. The studio's readiness path never imports evaluateCompliance (readiness.service.ts:52-64); presence must not launder that into a reopen.
- REQUIRED BEFORE ANY relist PROMISE — exercise the Inventory-lane re-publish (and RelistFixedPriceItem if ever built) in sandbox against a captured request and read the BODY, not the status. RelistFixedPriceItem has ZERO occurrences repo-wide (control: ReviseFixedPriceItem, 20+).
- MEASURE BEFORE CLAIMING (new precondition) — which eBay path a ZERO_PIN QUANTITY_UPDATE actually takes. The 25004 guard and its 'NOT applied to the deactivate/offer path' comment live in ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:2414-2423, not in outbound-sync's eBay path; the spec's inference that ZERO_PIN lands on the inventory_item path is unverified.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.channelFact / channelFactAt / channelFactVia / channelFactDetail (from the agreed state model). eBay-specific: NO BACKFILL. The eBay end path already wrote {listingStatus:'ENDED', offerActive:false, externalListingId:null} (ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1958-1960, :1981-1983); a naive derivation reads those three as 'never published'. Rows written before these columns existed derive UNKNOWN.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntent + presenceIntentAt/By/Reason and endedAt/endedBy/endedReason (from the agreed state model). eBay-specific: ENDED must NOT null externalListingId — the ItemID is the only local record of an identity eBay keeps in seller history. PAIRED CONSTRAINT (C10): this lands with sellingRisk and the delete-confirmation copy, because isLiveOnChannel returns true for listingStatus 'ENDED' whenever an id is present (listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:17), so keeping the id turns every ended eBay coordinate into a 'live listing' warning until sellingRisk is what the confirmation reads.
- ADDITIVE — new model ListingIdentity (from the agreed state model). This, not EbayListingIndex, is the authoritative eBay tombstone. eBay-specific fields: externalListingId (the retired ItemID), externalParentId, marketplace stored in the SITE code space with the fold recorded, channelConnectionId (an ItemID is globally unique but only ONE account can end it), and offerSku.
- NO SCHEMA CHANGE, AND CORRECTED SCOPE (C4) — EbayListingIndex. endedAt (schema.prisma:14108) and relistedFromItemId (:14109) exist and are dark (2 hits repo-wide: the schema line and migrations/20260703_e2_ebay_ads_data_layer/migration.sql:155). But the model's own header declares it an ADS-side index, 'Never written by the flat-file paths' (:14075-14078); it has no account column; and its endedAt is driven by a discovery sweep with a truncation guard and a vanish circuit-breaker (marketing/ebay-listing-index.service.ts:241-266) that deliberately refuse end-flips on a partial read. Presence may UPDATE these two fields on rows that already exist; it may NEVER CREATE a row there (that injects source:'DISCOVERED'/matchStatus:'UNMATCHED' rows into the ads product-first resolver as a side effect of a lifecycle verb). Note the second effect: setting endedAt flips that listing's EbayAd rows to STALE (ebay-ads.routes.ts:347, ebay-ads-automation.service.ts:309) — see openQuestions.
- NOT PROPOSED — a per-coordinate eBay lane column. The lane is DERIVED (an ACTIVE SharedListingMembership for the ItemID) and derivable at read time. Storing it creates a fourth place identity can drift; the two that exist already needed a verified re-link service to reconcile.
- NOT PROPOSED, and not needed — widening SharedListingMembership. It already carries marketplace, sku, itemId, parentSku, productId (nullable), status (ACTIVE|ENDED), followPool, stockBuffer, flatFileSnapshot and channelConnectionId, unique on (workspaceId, marketplace, itemId, sku) (schema.prisma:17261-17300). Presence reads it; it does not extend it. Note it has NO aliasKey and a NULLABLE productId, which is exactly why the fact-stamp joins on itemId (C8).
- NO DATA MIGRATION for the GB/UK split in this wave. ChannelListing.region/.marketplace hold 'GB' (canonical fold at flat-file/listing-content-write.service.ts:51-54; region is non-null at schema.prisma ~:1537, so the `?? l.marketplace` fallback at products-catalog.routes.ts:1886 can never rescue it), while SharedListingMembership.marketplace and EbayListingIndex.marketplace hold 'UK'. A bidirectional normaliser makes both resolve. Reconciling stored values is a separate Owner-approved data change.
- SEQUENCING (binding) — apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma. apps/api/.env pointed at a local Docker pg on 09-11 and only Product.version (54 vs 51) separated the two. Discriminate, never infer.
- PRECONDITION, NOT A SCHEMA CHANGE — measure the NULL distribution of ChannelListing.channelConnectionId and aliasKey per eBay coordinate before any five-level narrowing lands. Both unique indexes are declared NULLS NOT DISTINCT in raw SQL because 'every row today' has a NULL alias (schema.prisma:1770-1786); a narrowing that adds those columns to a where-clause against NULL-bearing rows matches nothing and reads as a fix.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED (unchanged from the input, and I did not need it to bend). CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, recorded verbatim at sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx:14-30) says a scope's own verbs are items of the one ⋯ overflow and never a button. Evidence lifecycle strains it: the prop is `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` (:104); MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-50) carries no tone, severity or group; both adapters drop GridAction.danger (0 hits in menuAdapters.tsx against a control of 5 for 'label'); no .nds-menu danger rule exists in any DS stylesheet (control: 20 .nds-menu rules in components.css). Under CH.1 as written, 'End the listing on eBay IT' renders byte-identical to 'Reload'. This proposal does NOT need CH.1 to bend — presence has its own surface. The smallest amendment, IF the Owner wants lifecycle reachable from the sheet: keep the no-new-buttons rule intact and let the overflow gain a typed tone plus a separator group (`separator` already exists on MenuItemDef and a grouping precedent already ships in one adapter). The Owner's ruling to make. Does CH.1 stand as written, with lifecycle living only on the presence surface?
- THE OUT-OF-STOCK PREFERENCE, and what its answer means retroactively. Nexus has never read the eBay account-level out-of-stock preference (0 hits for any eBay-preference read; the test at ebay-trading-xml.vitest.test.ts:30-32 records that OutOfStockControl is deliberately not sent and that the account setting governs keep-alive). If it is OFF, the shipped `deactivate` column has been ENDING listings under a label that says 'the safe hide (ItemID kept)'. Does the Owner want that measured in Seller Hub before Wave 2 designs a hold on top of it, and does the answer change how the shipped column is described TODAY?
- THE ADS COUPLING (new, from C4). Writing endedAt into EbayListingIndex flips ads to STALE for that listing (ebay-ads.routes.ts:347, ebay-ads-automation.service.ts:309). Does the Owner want a presence end to stale the ads automatically — arguably correct and arguably a second irreversible act inside one confirmation — or should presence write only ListingIdentity and leave the ads index to its own discovery sweep?
- GB / UK DATA RECONCILIATION. The two stores disagree on one country: ChannelListing.region and .marketplace hold 'GB' (canonical fold at flat-file/listing-content-write.service.ts:51-54), SharedListingMembership.marketplace holds 'UK' (schema.prisma:17265), EbayListingIndex.marketplace also 'UK'. A bidirectional normaliser makes both resolve without touching data. Reconciling the stored values is a separate Owner-approved data change. Does the Owner want the normaliser only, or the data reconciled?
- RELIST. There is no relist in this codebase; the only 'put it back' is a full re-publish minting a new ItemID, and RelistFixedPriceItem has zero occurrences repo-wide. Does the Owner want relist built at all for eBay, or should the model say plainly that an eBay end is permanent for that ItemID and offer only 'publish again as a new listing' with the lineage recorded via ebay-itemid-relink.service.ts?
- COMPLIANCE ON REOPEN (new, from C12a). sc:compliance is CONFIRMED critical: the studio never runs evaluateCompliance, and eBay EU publish strips the GPSR container while reporting LIVE. Presence cannot fix that. Should `restore-offer` and `relist` be REFUSED on an EU eBay coordinate until compliance is evaluated, or permitted with the confirmation naming that compliance was never checked? Refusing is safer and will block legitimate work today.
- THE LEGACY EBAY SURFACES. The reversible hold, the relink modal and the Action column all live on apps/web/src/app/products/ebay-flat-file/ — a legacy page under the flat-file untouchable rule. Presence's Wave 1 changes the server half of that page's end and deactivate branches. Does the Owner ratify server-side changes to the flat-file ROUTES (ebay-flat-file.routes.ts) as outside the untouchable rule, which reads to me as covering the EDITORS?
- THE CAPPED FACT (new, from C7). NEXUS_EBAY_ITEM_RECONCILE_MAX defaults to 100 groups per account per day. Does the Owner want that raised so presence has fuller coverage — which costs eBay Trading calls against a per-listing revise cap — or should the surface simply say how many coordinates were reached and leave the rest UNKNOWN?

##### Risks
- C3 is the sharpest one: if Guard 2 is extracted without the exclusion set, the presence adapter fails SHUT on every coordinate and reports 'shared' — a green-looking refusal that would take a week to notice because nothing breaks. The guard suite must include a positive control that a NON-shared coordinate is delistable from a plan (not only from the delete service).
- C10's pairing is the second: fixing the route to keep externalListingId, without shipping sellingRisk and the confirmation copy in the same wave, turns every ended eBay coordinate into a 'live listing' warning on the master sheet's delete confirmation — a new false alarm on a population that is already large (a measured family of 20 non-ACTIVE eBay rows with real ItemIDs, listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:15-31).
- The out-of-stock preference (§3) is the load-bearing unknown. If it is OFF on this account, every 'safe hide' Nexus has already shipped through the deactivate column has been ENDING listings, not hiding them — retroactively, not prospectively. Reading it is a precondition to calling withdraw-offer reversible AT ALL, and the answer may reclassify shipped behaviour.
- Three of the four fact sources are partial: the reconcile job is capped at 100 groups per account (C7), the ads discovery sweep has a truncation guard and a circuit-breaker that deliberately skip end-flips, and RT.0 only fires on a write. Presence will therefore show a large UNKNOWN population indefinitely. If the Owner reads that as a regression rather than as honesty, the model fails on reception rather than on mechanics.
- Writing endedAt into EbayListingIndex (even update-only, per C4) couples a lifecycle verb to the eBay Ads read model: ebay-ads.routes.ts and ebay-ads-automation.service.ts both branch on `endedAt: null`, and ebayAd rows are flipped STALE off it. A presence end would silently stale that listing's ads. That may be correct — but it is a second effect the confirmation must name, and it is why the index write must not be load-bearing.
- The GB normaliser touches siteIdForMarket, which every Trading write goes through (C6). A fold applied in the wrong direction sends an IT call to site 3. The gate must assert BOTH directions with a positive control, and the change must be measured against SharedListingMembership's 'UK' code space and ChannelListing's 'GB' one in the same run.
- The five-level narrowing (P8 plus C15) touches deleteMany calls in the eBay delete service. A narrowing that adds channelConnectionId while production rows still carry NULL there would silently match nothing — the unique indexes are declared NULLS NOT DISTINCT in raw SQL precisely because 'every row today' has a NULL alias (schema.prisma:1774-1784). Narrowing must be measured against the actual NULL distribution first, per coordinate, or it becomes a silent no-op that reads as a fix.
- ZERO_PIN's deliberate syncPaused:false (C13) means the intent lock has a legitimate exception from day one. An exception mechanism added to a lock is the usual way locks stop working; it must be a named verb in the plan, never a boolean the caller can set.

##### The specification
# Presence on eBay — the channel adapter (corrected)
## CORRECTIONS
**C1 — `Badge` cannot carry an intent.** `BadgeProps` is `{ program: AdProgram; className?; children }` and renders `nds-badge ${program}` (`apps/web/src/design-system/primitives/Badge.tsx:6-13`). It is the SP/SB/SD ads-program badge, not a general badge. The DS claim "Badge — the intent mark" is false. Corrected: the intent mark is `Tag` (`primitives/Tag.tsx:16-25`, `tone?: TagTone`), the fact mark is `Pill` (`primitives/Pill.tsx:4-24`, `tone: Tone` plus a leading status `dot`), and `PresenceMark` composes the two with the as-of.
**C2 — deriving the connection from `{itemId, marketplace}` BREAKS the Inventory lane.** `connection-resolver.service.ts:212-224` resolves the `itemId` form through `prisma.sharedListingMembership.findFirst` and throws `NoConnectionError` when there is no row. Inventory-lane coordinates have no membership row by definition (§1), so the spec's change #3 would convert working delists into "no connection". Worse, that branch still falls back to `resolveDeclared("EBAY", …)` when the membership carries no `channelConnectionId` — the drift the change was meant to remove. **Corrected:** derive from `{ listingId: channelListing.id }` (which reads `ChannelListing.channelConnectionId`, part of both unique keys, `schema.prisma:1785-1786`); use `{ itemId }` only as the Trading-lane cross-check; and **refuse** when neither yields an attributed account, never accept the DECLARED fallback.
**C3 — Guard 2 cannot be extracted "verbatim".** Its own comment says why: *"The target's own rows are already deleted above, so ANY surviving reference proves the listing still belongs to someone else"* (`ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:695-703`). Called from `POST /presence/plan`, nothing has been deleted, so the coordinate's own `ChannelListing` row is a surviving reference and the guard skips **every** ItemID — a fail-shut that reports as "shared". **Corrected:** extract it as `survivingReferences(itemIds, { excludingListingIds })`, and the cascade at `products-catalog.routes.ts:1852-1899` must pass its own enumerated target ids as the exclusion set. The delete service keeps today's behaviour by passing an empty exclusion (its rows are already gone).
**C4 — `EbayListingIndex` is an ads read-model, not a lifecycle store.** Its header: *"E2 (eBay Ads) — ads-side index … Never written by the flat-file paths"* (`schema.prisma:14075-14078`). It is keyed `(workspaceId, marketplace, itemId)` with **no account column**, and its `endedAt` is driven by a discovery sweep protected by a truncation guard and a vanish circuit-breaker (`marketing/ebay-listing-index.service.ts:241-266`). **Corrected:** presence may `update` `endedAt` / `relistedFromItemId` on rows that already exist, and may **never create** rows there — creating one injects a `source:'DISCOVERED'`, `matchStatus:'UNMATCHED'` row into the ads product-first resolver as a side effect of a lifecycle verb. `ListingIdentity` remains the authoritative tombstone; the index write is a convenience mirror, and no wave may depend on it. (The spec's "no proposal may claim a new table is needed" is still right for the *question* "what ItemID did we retire" — for listings the ads sweep found.)
**C5 — wrong path.** The content writer is `apps/api/src/services/flat-file/listing-content-write.service.ts` (verified: `resolveListingRegion` at :51-54, the three-level `findFirst` at :112-120), not `apps/api/src/services/`. Both §1 and §8 cited the wrong directory.
**C6 — the GB defect is wider than `delistEbay`.** `siteIdForMarket` (`ebay-trading-api.service.ts:18-22`, table at :10-16) is called *inside* `reviseInventoryStatus` (:301), `getItemListingStatus` (:321), `getItemQuantities` (:395), `reviseInventoryStatusBatch` (:408) and `tryRemoveVariationFromListing` (`ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:189`) — each resolving from its own `ctx.market`. So `verify` and the Trading-lane hold throw on `'GB'` exactly as the delist does. The normaliser inside `siteIdForMarket` covers all of them; the gate must therefore derive codes from **every** writer and reader, and the fold must be bidirectional (`ebaySiteMarket` GB→UK for calls, `ebayListingRegion` UK→GB for rows).
**C7 — the reconcile job is a capped SAMPLE, not a daily census.** `const max = Number(process.env.NEXUS_EBAY_ITEM_RECONCILE_MAX ?? 100)` (:45) and `groups.slice(0, max)` **per account** (:70); GetItem errors are counted and skipped fail-closed. The spec's "already measured daily" overstates it. **Corrected:** a coordinate the job did not reach keeps `UNKNOWN`; the job already reports `capped from N` and that number must reach the surface.
**C8 — the fact-stamp must join on ItemID, never on SKU.** `SharedListingMembership` has no `aliasKey` and a nullable `productId` (`schema.prisma:17261-17300`), so "resolved through the five-level coordinate" is not derivable from a membership row. Join `ChannelListing.externalListingId === membership.itemId` **and** `channelConnectionId`, folding UK→GB. Stamping by SKU would manufacture the defect the audit **REFUTED** (`sc:alias-multilisting`: "stamps one SKU-level status onto every listing row of that SKU").
**C9 — there is no PATCH on an eBay offer.** eBay Inventory `updateOffer` is a full PUT. "Offer PATCH-shaped minimal PUT" is not a thing that exists. **Corrected:** GET-merge-PUT — read the live offer, overwrite `availableQuantity`, strip read-only fields, PUT the merged body — and whether the GET body round-trips through PUT is platform knowledge this repo cannot answer. It joins §0's sandbox list.
**C10 — not nulling `externalListingId` flips a shipped, test-pinned classifier.** `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:17` pins `isLiveOnChannel(row({ listingStatus: 'ENDED' })) === true`. Today ended eBay rows escape that only because the route nulls the id (`ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1960,1983`). Fix the route and every ended eBay coordinate becomes "live" to the master sheet's delete confirmation. **Corrected:** the route change and `sellingRisk` (identity held AND intent not terminal, OR fact SELLING) plus the confirmation copy land in the SAME wave. Wave 1 must not ship a new false alarm on every ended row.
**C11 — preserve the flat-file resurrection guard.** `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:650-660` stamps a per-market FILE EXCLUSION on `Product.categoryAttributes` (incident #12, 2026-07-18) because a product with no eBay listing is resurrected on every reload. Presence keeps the row alive, so **intent becomes the source of that suppression**: `ENDED`/`DISCONTINUED`/`RELEASED` excludes the coordinate from the file. The JSON stamp stays until the intent read is wired, and is never removed in the same change that stops deleting rows.
**C12 — two CONFIRMED findings the spec ignored.** (a) `sc:compliance` (critical): the studio never runs `evaluateCompliance` (`readiness.service.ts:52-64` imports six pure checkers and not it), and eBay EU publish strips the GPSR container while still reporting LIVE. Presence cannot fix compliance, but it must not *launder* it: `restore-offer` and `relist` may not re-open an offer whose compliance blockers were never evaluated — the plan carries `complianceEvaluated: false` and the confirmation says so. (b) `sc:delist-relist` (high): the `exclude-child` copy ("the record is kept, so <ItemID> is not lost") reads as a takedown while the variation stays buyable; the reversal field now renders it, so the copy is corrected by construction — but it is a Wave 1 item, not a Wave 3 one.
**C13 — `ZERO_PIN` deliberately un-pauses.** `sync-control.routes.ts:934-948` writes `syncPaused: false` because it must push the zero. An intent lock that refuses enqueue for `HELD` would break the shipped safe-stop. **Corrected:** the lock is keyed on `presenceIntent` and exempts the verb that set it, by name, in the same plan — never by clearing the intent. `ZERO_PIN`'s own row resolution is three-level (`{productId, channel, marketplace}`, :896-898) and joins the narrowing list.
**C14 — citation fixes.** `ebay-columns.ts` lives at `apps/web/src/app/products/ebay-flat-file/ebay-columns.ts` (a LEGACY page): description :156, enum :159 — not :152-164/:157. Deactivate/end row filters are :1849/:1850, not :1848. `buildEndFixedPriceItemXml` :425, `endFixedPriceItem` :442, `reviseInventoryStatusBatch` :403, `getItemListingStatus` :316, `REVISE_INVENTORY_STATUS_MAX_ENTRIES` :51.
**C15 — three more coordinate-blind eBay writes.** Added to P8: the deactivate branch's post-write `channelListing.updateMany({ productId: { in }, channel:'EBAY', region }, { quantity: 0 })` (~:1905-1910); the end branch's parent write over `{ product: { OR: [{id},{parentId}] }, channel:'EBAY', region }` (:1956-1960) — which reaches every child of the product; and the Trading-lane membership pre-check `{ sku, marketplace: mp, status:'ACTIVE' }` (:1940-1946) with no `channelConnectionId`, so account A's membership blocks account B's end.
**C16 — `verify` must read the offer's status.** `GET /sell/inventory/v1/offer?sku=&marketplace_id=` returning an offer object is not "selling": an UNPUBLISHED offer exists. Read `status` and `listing.listingId`; absent → `ABSENT`, unpublished → `NOT_SELLING`, HTTP error → `REFUSED`.
**C17 — no ruling is contradicted, and one claim about a ruling is loosened.** CH.1 (`sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx:14-30`, `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` at :104) is untouched: presence has its own surface. D9 holds on the same reasoning the spec gives. `BulkActionBar` is a generic DS pattern (`{count, children, onClear?, noun?}`, `patterns/BulkActionBar.tsx:4-12`); the audit's "master-only" is about where it is *mounted* (`master/MasterSheet.tsx`, `master/FamilySelectionBar.tsx`), not a property of the component — so mounting it on a presence grid is reuse, not a copy.
---
## The corrected spec, in brief
**§0 Sandbox preconditions (now five).** Unchanged: (a) `RelistFixedPriceItem` has zero occurrences repo-wide (control: `ReviseFixedPriceItem`, 20+); (b) whether a withdrawn item group is re-publishable; (c) the account out-of-stock preference. Added: (d) whether an eBay offer GET body round-trips through PUT (C9); (e) whether the outbound `QUANTITY_UPDATE` path for eBay routes through `inventory_item` (error 25004 territory, guarded only in the flat-file route at :2414-2423) or through the offer — the spec asserted the item path; that is an inference across files and must be measured before `ZERO_PIN` on eBay is called a hold.
**§1 Two lanes, discriminated explicitly.** Unchanged and correct: Trading iff an ACTIVE `SharedListingMembership` exists for the ItemID; the end branch tests this (:1940-1947), the deactivate branch does not (:1849), so a Trading row asked to deactivate dies in `pushOffersOnly` (`ebay-variation-push.service.ts:2255-2257`). Presence carries `lane` on every coordinate and refuses cross-lane.
**§3 The hold.** EBAY-5's refutation stands. `deactivate` ships and works. It is **not exact**: `ebay-trading-xml.vitest.test.ts:30-32` pins that `OutOfStockControl` is deliberately not sent and that the account-level preference governs keep-alive, and Nexus has never read that preference. So `withdraw-offer` on eBay declares `fidelity: 'lossy'` until `verify` can read it. The three defects in `pushOffersOnly` are confirmed at the cited lines — price refusal :2238-2241, policy replace :2168-2185, whole-body rewrite :2260-2278 — so the new `ebay-presence-hold.service.ts` is justified, now specified as GET-merge-PUT (C9).
**§4 End.** `delistEbay`'s `_action` (`channel-delist.service.ts:244-245`) is the whole of EBAY-2. The honest path's writes are confirmed at :1958-1960 and :1981-1983; `isPublished` has **zero** occurrences in that file, and `isPublished` is the only lifecycle gate in the worker (`bullmq-sync.worker.ts:177-190`). Presence writes intent `ENDED`, `endedAt/By/Reason`, `isPublished:false`, keeps `externalListingId` — **with C10's paired change**.
**§6 Guards.** Five confirmed, re-specified per C3.
**§7 GB.** Confirmed and widened per C6. Site-id resolution leaves the auth try/catch (:274-282) and returns `EBAY_UNKNOWN_MARKET`; `applyDelistResultToQueue:332-343` marks non-retryable failures FAILED+isDead on the first attempt, which is why the misclassification is terminal.
**§9 Fact.** Both observers stamp, joined per C8, with C7's honesty about coverage.
**§10 The adapter.** Eight changes stand, with #3 replaced by C2 and #5 by C3. The matcher split is confirmed at :223-234, :227, :231, :296. The gate hole is confirmed: `getEbayApiBaseForMode` exists (`ebay-publish-gate.service.ts`, returning `''` for gated/dry-run precisely so a fetch fails loudly) and has **zero** callers in `ebay-flat-file.routes.ts`, which uses the module constant `EBAY_API_BASE` (:77) after a gated-only check (:1513-1516, :3300-3302); `channel-delist.service.ts`, `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts` and `ebay-trading-api.service.ts` consult it nowhere. `callTradingApi` gates on its own `NEXUS_EBAY_REAL_API` and picks its endpoint from `EBAY_SANDBOX` (:224-228, :234-246).
**§11 Waves.** Wave 1 gains: `sellingRisk` + the delete-confirmation copy (C10), the intent-sourced file exclusion (C11), the `exclude-child` copy (C12b), and the compliance flag on any reopen path (C12a). The tree is not green — the recorded studio run is 2 files / 6 tests failing out of 104 / 1574, in `masterWrite.vitest.test.ts` and one other; that is a precondition to measuring anything, not a follow-up.

#### The Amazon adapter for Presence — corrected: four resolvers, an inverted gate, and three resurrection paths

A per-verb Amazon specification for Presence, corrected against the working tree. Eleven claims in the reviewed draft were wrong or incomplete. The two headline fixes: the Amazon publish gate is INVERTED for exactly the two verbs that matter — `deleteListingsItem` escapes the master flag while `closeMarketOffers` writes `offerClosedAt` on a dry-run that sent nothing — and there are THREE paths that resurrect a withdrawn Amazon offer, not one; `ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate` closes only the third. Also: `Badge` is the ads-program badge and cannot carry a tone; `SourceIndicator.kind` is the cell-provenance vocabulary and must not be reused; the studio's Amazon channel verbs are already refused twice as preview-only, and this proposal deletes that guard, which it must say out loud.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- CORRECTED — primitives/Badge.tsx is NOT a status badge. BadgeProps is { program: AdProgram; children } (:6-10), the advertising SP/SB/SD mark, with no tone. The draft's 'Pill and Badge, the two marks' does not compile. BOTH presence marks are Pill (tone: Tone); the intent/fact distinction is carried by the new presence renderer's tone+label table and by position, never by two different components.
- CORRECTED — components/SourceIndicator.tsx must NOT mark a derived intent. kind: ValueSourceKind is 'master'|'override'|'rule'|'default'|'missing'|'linked'|'channel'|'formula'|'ai'|'warning' (:7) — the cell-VALUE provenance axis. Adding a presence member is the cross-vocabulary mapping renderers/readiness.ts:19-26 refuses by design, and that axis is already RED (layers.vitest.test.ts on unmapped channelSnapshot). The derived-intent mark lives in the new presence renderer; its reason lives in MenuItemDef.description / InfoTip.
- MenuItemDef carries no tone or severity — only separator (Menu.tsx:8-50) — and both adapters drop GridAction.danger (measured: /usr/bin/grep -c danger menuAdapters.tsx = 0, control 5 for label). Zero .nds-menu danger rules in design-system/styles/components.css against 20 .nds-menu rules total. ADD tone?: 'default' | 'danger' plus one .nds-menu danger rule. Filed at .claude/DS-GAPS.md:330-348 (field list stale — title and description have landed).
- NEW GAP the draft missed — actionMenuItems maps a disabled verb's reason to MenuItemDef.title, a hover-only tooltip (menuAdapters.tsx:85), while MenuItemDef.description exists precisely because a tooltip is 'invisible to touch, invisible to a keyboard user… an instruction may never live only on a disabled control' (Menu.tsx:27-48, hub rulings #133/#231). Every presence refusal would land hover-only. FIX: actionMenuItems maps the reason to description, keeping title as the supplement.
- GridAction has no reach and ActionImpact has no reversal — the spine's core type addition — plus asOf?: string on ActionImpact, because an Amazon impact is computed from a channel READ and a confirmation quoting a two-hour-old fact must say so. ADD all three in design-system/grid/actions/registry.ts and a validateAction(action) beside validateImpact, which receives only the impact (:264) and cannot see an action-level field. NOTE: this is an enhancement, not a fix — the audit REFUTED DX-5.
- ActionConfirm has no slot for a PER-TARGET RESULT after the run. Every Amazon bulk primitive returns MarketOfferResult (updated/skippedFba/unchanged/failed/results[]/error/remaining, amazon-market-offer.service.ts:46-55) and a plural verb would collapse to one toast. ADD a ResultSummary to design-system/grid/actions/ listing outcomes by named coordinate, never a count.
- No DS renderer for an INTENT/FACT pair with an as-of. ADD design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts (pure, tone+label table, sibling of readiness.ts, reusing its ReadinessTone union) plus one PresenceMark built on Pill — and it must render UNKNOWN as UNKNOWN, never neutral-green.
- No DS pattern for a STAGED PLAN (compose → review per-coordinate → apply → drain). An EU-wide withdraw is nine SP-API calls. ADD design-system/patterns/PlanReviewBar.tsx in wave 2, or accept a studio hand-roll then. Not needed for wave 1.
- components/DataGrid.tsx must NOT be used. MEASURED: node scripts/check-grid-kit-ratchet.mjs --check exits 1 at 34 against baseline 32 (the draft said 33). The studio importer is channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3, which imports DataGrid from the BARREL ('@/design-system/components') — so a grep for 'components/DataGrid' misses it. Any presence surface on DataGrid ships a pre-push blocker.

##### API
- NEW apps/api/src/services/amazon/amazon-presence.adapter.ts — THE only Amazon writer. One exported function per verb, each taking the five-level coordinate plus offerScope, each returning MarketOfferResult (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:46-55) with NO aggregate success. Owns the four resolvers; calls closeMarketOffers / reopenMarketOffers / the DELETE feed / deleteListingsItem / getListingsItem. Nothing else may call deleteListingsItem except listings/recovery.service.ts.
- A1 (RAISED TO FIRST) — CHANGED apps/api/src/clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1143: deleteListingsItem must call getAmazonPublishMode() instead of reading process.env.AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE. It is the ONE SP-API write that escapes NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH: with the master flag off and AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE=live, the irreversible call still goes out while every other write short-circuits. Also: DeleteListingsItemOptions (:92-96), GetListingsItemOptions (:74-82) and searchListingsItems' opts (:1334-1339) gain accountId?: string so the per-account proxy binding at :1457 is type-reachable; SPAPIResponse.status (:47) must admit string | string[], or getListingsItem normalises the array as amazon.service.ts:1219-1227 already does.
- NEW — NO PRESENCE WRITE ON A DRY-RUN. patchPurchasableOffer and patchListingPrice return {success:true, dryRun:true} under the DEFAULT gated mode (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:792-796, :669-673), and closeMarketOffers persists offerClosedAt unconditionally at :178-194. Every presence write — intent as well as fact — must be conditional on dryRun !== true, the way pricing-outbound.service.ts:181-205 already is (PD.3). Otherwise the studio renders WITHDRAWN over a live, selling offer.
- NEW resolveSellerSku(coordinate, offerScope) — Offer.sku for the named fulfilment method, else Product.sku; refuses AMAZON_NO_SELLER_SKU. PRECONDITION: count Offer rows on Amazon coordinates on BOTH databases first — if near zero, offerScope is inert and must not appear in any confirmation. Producer-side for the cascade: by drain time the Product row is gone, so products-catalog.routes.ts:1852-1900 must join product:{select:{sku:true}} and carry sellerSku on the queue payload; ChannelDelistJob gains sellerSku: string | null; amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts must read it into memory BEFORE its transaction (it deletes at :59-65 before dispatching at :71).
- NEW resolveMarketplaceId(marketplace) over Marketplace.marketplaceId. Deletes AMAZON_MARKETPLACE_IDS (channel-delist.service.ts:75-83), MARKETPLACE_ID_MAP (services/amazon/flat-file.service.ts:33-39) and ALL EIGHT null→IT defaults: channel-delist.service.ts:86, :277 (siteIdForMarket(job.targetRegion ?? 'IT') — the eBay sibling the draft missed), amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:355, :839, :1435, amazon-cockpit-publish.routes.ts:294, flat-file-pull.service.ts:165, flat-file.service.ts:2706. Callers amazon-market-offer.service.ts:117 and :246 today FAIL on NL/BE/PL/SE/IE — markets AMAZON_EU_SHARED_MARKETS treats as live.
- CHANGED apps/api/src/services/amazon-market-offer.service.ts — loadRow (:65-75) takes channelConnectionId and aliasKey and refuses on ambiguity rather than findFirst-ing; the seller id must be resolved PER TARGET inside the loop, not hoisted once at :90 with no account; close must also write offerActive:false (see the skip_offer path below); reopen must revalidate the snapshot price against Product.minPrice/minMargin before replaying it at :264-266, AND run its forced followMasterQuantity:true + immediate QUANTITY_UPDATE enqueue (:275-300) through detectEuIntentConflict — the reopen re-enters the nine-market shared pool even though the close did not.
- CHANGED apps/api/src/services/pricing-outbound.service.ts — pushAmazonPrice selects only {id, platformAttributes} (:140-149) and its listing findFirst is unscoped by connection and alias; patchListingPrice then issues op:'replace' on /attributes/purchasable_offer (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:687-699), re-creating the instance the close removed with op:'delete', and stamps syncStatus:'IN_SYNC'. Extract one shared assertPushAllowed(listingId) from outbound-sync.service.ts:903-910 and apply it here. AND WIRE closedMarketSet — measured: it has ZERO callers across apps/api/src, apps/web/src and packages, while its own comment at :316-318 claims 'Used by every Amazon write stack so nothing can silently resurrect a closed offer'.
- NEW, and the draft missed it entirely — the SECOND resurrection path. closeMarketOffers never sets offerActive:false, so flat-file.service.ts:3417-3419 writes skip_offer:false on the next Amazon submit ('Ensure a previously-paused listing that's now active clears skip_offer'), re-opening the buy box. The adapter writes offerActive:false alongside offerClosedAt, and the submit path consults presenceIntent before clearing skip_offer. This is a sanctioned exception to the spine's one-flag-per-statement gate and must be declared as such.
- NEW, and the draft got its scope wrong — the THIRD resurrection path. getExistingRows ignores listing scope entirely (flat-file.service.ts:2094-2124) and listing-scope.ts:14-26 never reads listingStatus, so a removed SKU is still an ordinary editable row and saving it re-creates the listing. Nothing here reads a ChannelListing, so ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate cannot reach it. Mirror eBay: write Product.categoryAttributes.amazonFileExcluded[MARKET] in the removal transaction, honour it in GET /amazon/flat-file/rows the way ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:262 does, auto-clear on an explicit re-save.
- CHANGED apps/api/src/services/channel-delist.service.ts — delistAmazon uses job.sellerSku and refuses when absent (today: 'externalListingId for Amazon is typically the SKU' at :138-140, contradicting its own docblock at :36 which says ASIN); DELETE the UNPUBLISH→DELETE fallthrough at :151-160; add the SKIPPED_FBA guard closeMarketOffers has at :104-110; delete the duck-typed Shopify updateProduct→deleteProduct fallback at :186-203; stop classifying /item cannot be accessed/ and /invalid item/ as success (:227,:231,:290-293); resolve the eBay connection that OWNS the ItemID rather than tryResolveConnection({channel:'EBAY',primary:true}) at :263.
- CHANGED apps/api/src/services/amazon/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts — capture snapshot and seller SKU first; narrow BOTH the pre-read (:52-56) and the deleteMany (:59-65) to the five-level coordinate — note it also fans out to every CHILD product via parentId (:47-51), which the draft did not name; invert the order so the channel call precedes the local delete; delete locally only on a non-dryRun success, otherwise keep the row as {listingStatus:'ENDED', isPublished:false}; return per-coordinate delistError instead of the bare catch at :82. Same five-level narrowing for the delete-sync updateMany at flat-file.service.ts:3198-3204, a third P8-class site.
- NEW apps/api/src/services/amazon/amazon-asin-ownership.pure.ts + a thin fetching service — CORRECTED SHAPE. Mirror ebay-itemid-relink.pure.ts exactly: a PURE function taking already-fetched liveSkus, returning {verdict: 'verified'|'unverifiable'|'rejected', reason, matchedSkus, foreignSkus}. An EMPTY searchListingsItems result is 'unverifiable', NOT 'rejected' — eBay's own no-SKU arm returns unverifiable (:64-73, 'cannot prove must never collapse into fine'), and an empty Amazon result can be a throttle, an unresolved seller, a wrong marketplaceId, or the auth bug whose comment at :1252-1256 says a bad header made every getListingsItem 404 'which we misread as listing not found'. Must page: searchListingsItems defaults pageSize to 10 (:1354). Both rejected and unverifiable refuse every channel-reaching verb.
- CHANGED apps/api/src/jobs/amazon-sqs-poll.job.ts — persist LISTINGS_ITEM_STATUS_CHANGE into channelFact/channelFactAt/channelFactVia:'inbound-notification'. CORRECTED: the write must sit OUTSIDE the `if (note.isSuppressed && note.asin)` branch at :201-220 — a recovery to BUYABLE publishes nothing today and the message is deleted, so persisting inside it would pin every coordinate at SUPPRESSED forever. And the notification carries asin/sku/marketplaceId only: resolve the connection from its seller id, and REFUSE the write when the five-level coordinate cannot be determined rather than guessing it.
- CHANGED apps/api/src/services/advertising/ads-retail-readiness.service.ts — select listingStatus (the select at :50 does not fetch it) and stop falling back to a sibling market's listing (:62) and buy box (:65). The adapter calls suppressCampaignBids / restoreCampaignBids (ads-bid-suppression.service.ts:58, :171) on withdraw/end/release and restore/relist, joining AdProductAd.asin × Campaign.marketplace, under the Owner's no-pause rule.
- NEW GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/amazon-posture?market=&accountId=&aliasKey= — fulfilment method per Offer row, FBA units per fulfilment centre and condition (reuse the fbaInventory leg of products-catalog.routes.ts:1722-1738), active suppressions (reuse GET /api/products/:id/suppressions, listings-syndication.routes.ts:2391), the publish gate mode, and the EU shared-quantity intent set. Read-only. Note requireCoordinate (product-studio.routes.ts:180-187) today requires only market and channel — presence widens it to account and alias.
- AUTHORISATION — an explicit in-file preHandler naming the required permission on every route above AND on POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove (amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:1801-1819). MEASURED: that file contains ZERO occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate across 29 routes (positive control: 29 route registrations), while looping caller-supplied targets into a hard delete that fans out to child products. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (apps/api/src/lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:30-32).

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntent / presenceIntentAt / presenceIntentBy / presenceIntentReason (nullable, NEVER backfilled). Amazon slice: a row with offerClosedAt set derives a PROVISIONAL intent of WITHDRAWN, carried on the wire WITH its provenance so no surface presents it as an operator decision. CORRECTED: the provenance mark may NOT be components/SourceIndicator — its ValueSourceKind union (:7) is the cell-value axis and extending it is the cross-vocabulary mapping readiness.ts:19-26 refuses.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.channelFact / channelFactAt / channelFactVia / channelFactDetail Json?. Amazon slice: channelFactDetail stores the RAW summaries[].status ARRAY (SP-API returns e.g. ['BUYABLE','DISCOVERABLE'] while SPAPIResponse.status is typed `string` at amazon-sp-api.client.ts:47), the offers[] presence flag, and the issue codes. NO BACKFILL: pre-column rows derive UNKNOWN. This is what lets Presence say 'listed, not buyable' WITHOUT overturning the measured decision at flat-file-pull.service.ts:271-276 (DISCOVERABLE → isPublished:true, 431 blocked rows).
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.endedAt / endedBy / endedReason. Distinct from listingStatus happening to say ENDED, which the Amazon delete-sync writes at flat-file.service.ts:3197-3204 and which the pull then OVERWRITES with Amazon's raw status string at flat-file-pull.service.ts:276 (A3).
- ADDITIVE — new model ListingIdentity, per the spine, with workspaceId String @default(dbgenerated(...)) and productId as a plain string (no Prisma relation) so it survives the purge. Amazon slice: fulfillmentMethod and offerSku are what make a release honest — the tombstone records WHICH seller SKU was surrendered on which ASIN, because Offer.sku differs per fulfilment method (schema.prisma:2294-2296) and Product.sku is only the fallback. CORRECTED SCOPE: doNotRecreate is read by the ChannelListing create/upsert paths and by listing reconciliation — it CANNOT stop the flat-file row loader, which never touches a ChannelListing (flat-file.service.ts:2094-2124, listing-scope.ts:14-26). Claiming it closes the flat-file resurrection would be a false green.
- ADDITIVE (data, not schema) — Product.categoryAttributes.amazonFileExcluded[MARKET] = true, written in the same transaction as any end/release and honoured in GET /amazon/flat-file/rows. This is the eBay ebayFileExcluded precedent (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:649-671, honoured at ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:262); /usr/bin/grep fileExcluded apps/api/src returns eBay hits only. It is the ONLY thing that closes the third resurrection path. Uses an existing Json column — no migration.
- NOT PROPOSED — any Amazon-specific table. AmazonSuppression (schema.prisma:2218-2239), ListingIssue (:2250), FbaInventoryDetail (:9522-9550), ListingReconciliation and ChannelListingSnapshot already hold everything the adapter reads.
- NOT PROPOSED — a column for the Amazon marketplace id. Marketplace.marketplaceId exists (schema.prisma:1947). The two literal maps (services/amazon/flat-file.service.ts:33-39, channel-delist.service.ts:75-83) and all eight `?? …IT` defaults are DELETIONS, not migrations. CORRECTED COUNT: the draft named one default; the sites are channel-delist.service.ts:86 and :277 (eBay siteId), amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:355, :839, :1435, amazon-cockpit-publish.routes.ts:294, flat-file-pull.service.ts:165, flat-file.service.ts:2706.
- NOT DECLARED — ChannelListing.variationExcluded. family-projection.service.ts:389 cites migration 20260911a_vp2_variation_excluded; `ls packages/database/prisma/migrations` shows only 20260911020000_information_formula_destination, 20260911_category_taxonomies and five 20260912_lx* folders. The raw-SQL helpers stay.
- SEQUENCING (binding) — apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma. MEMORY records apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11, with only Product.version (54 vs 51) separating the two. Discriminate, never infer.

##### Open for you
- THE FBA DEFAULT, and whether it is even measurable. amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63 refuses close/reopen on ANY FBA signal, fail-closed, on the Owner's stated rule. offerScope:'FBM' would make 'withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA' expressible — but only if Offer rows exist on Amazon coordinates, which is unmeasured. Does the Owner want offerScope permitted on a mixed coordinate, or does fail-closed stand for every offer on an FBA product? (If it stands, offerScope leaves the design and resolveSellerSku collapses to Product.sku.)
- WAVE 2 IS THE MOMENT THE STUDIO CAN WRITE TO PRODUCTION AMAZON. channelActions.ts:249-251 and :258-261 refuse every channel-reaching verb today as preview-only. Should removing that refusal be a separate Owner approval, taken after wave 1 has run in production with local verbs only — or is approving this proposal approving that too?
- THE GATE FLIP. A1 makes deleteListingsItem obey getAmazonPublishMode(), which means it obeys NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH. Everything Amazon-writing is currently gated by default. Does the Owner want presence's channel verbs to ship gated (honest, and inert until the flag flips) or is the flag being flipped for this programme? The answer changes every confirmation's copy.
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED, and smaller than previously reported. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, verbatim at SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20) says a scope's verbs are items of the one ⋯ overflow and never a button — and it ALREADY names 'a listing's preflight' as one of them. This proposal does not need CH.1 to bend: presence has its own surface. The smallest amendment, if the Owner wants lifecycle reachable from the sheet, is smaller than the earlier draft claimed: SheetToolbar.tsx:253-256 already renders per-item description AND already inserts a separator group, so only `tone?: 'default'|'danger'` on MenuItemDef plus one .nds-menu danger rule is missing. Does CH.1 stand as written, with lifecycle living only on the presence surface?
- D9 — is a presence surface inside the existing per-channel nav group a NEW OBJECT (my reading: the sheet is content, presence is state) or a SECOND WAY IN? If the Owner reads it as a second route, the plural half of the model is unbuildable and only the per-coordinate verbs survive.
- THE FLAT-FILE STAMP AND THE UNTOUCHABLE RULE. Closing the third resurrection path needs Product.categoryAttributes.amazonFileExcluded[MARKET] written by the removal service and honoured in GET /amazon/flat-file/rows — mirroring exactly what eBay already does (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:649-671, ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:262). The editors themselves are untouched. Is a read-filter in the flat-file route inside or outside the untouchable boundary?
- MEASURE THE Offer TABLE FIRST — and on WHICH database. Everything about offerScope, resolveSellerSku's fulfilment branch, and 'which seller SKU did we surrender' depends on Offer rows existing for Amazon coordinates. MEMORY records apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11 with only Product.version separating the two. Who runs that count, against which connection string, and does the Owner want the answer before or after approving the model?
- RBAC ENFORCE. Every guard this proposal adds is a log line while rbacHook defaults to shadow (lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:30-32), and POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove has no route-level guard at all today. Does the Owner want release gated on flipping NEXUS_RBAC_MODE=enforce (blast radius across every route), or shipped behind an in-file preHandler that works regardless?
- IDENTITY CHANGE AND ACCOUNT MOVE — declared out of scope, and I want that ratified. channelConnectionId is in both ChannelListing unique keys (schema.prisma:1785-1786) and no verb here changes it, so agency handover, entity change, consolidating two Amazon accounts and relaunching under a new SKU keeping the ASIN have no writer. critic:scenarios named this as a whole missing axis; folding it in would double the model.

##### Risks
- The gate is INVERTED and wave 2 flips a live switch. deleteListingsItem reads process.env.AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE directly (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1143) while every other write calls getAmazonPublishMode(); with the master flag NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH off and AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE=live, the one irreversible SP-API call still goes out. A1 must land in wave 1, BEFORE any presence surface offers a verb.
- closeMarketOffers writes offerClosedAt on a dry-run (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:178-194 runs unconditionally; patchPurchasableOffer returns {success:true, dryRun:true} under the DEFAULT gated mode at :792-796). Presence would render WITHDRAWN over a live, selling offer — an honesty regression worse than the 'Live' it replaces. Fix before the fact axis ships.
- This proposal DELETES the only guard between an operator and a live Amazon write. channelActions.ts:249-251 refuses every channel verb whose rows carry an externalListingId ('wave-1 verbs do not send to a channel'), and runAction.ts:74-76 short-circuits before the dialog; :258-261 refuses again inside run. Wave 2 removes both. That should be its own Owner approval, not a consequence of approving the model.
- Three resurrection paths, and ListingIdentity closes only one. Reprice (op:'replace' recreates the deleted instance), the next flat-file submit (skip_offer:false is written back because closeMarketOffers never sets offerActive:false), and the flat-file row loader (getExistingRows ignores listing scope entirely, so nothing reads a ChannelListing and nothing can read an identity). Shipping the identity registry alone and calling the resurrection closed would be a false green.
- closedMarketSet has ZERO callers while its own comment claims 'Used by every Amazon write stack so nothing can silently resurrect a closed offer' (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:316-318). A reviewer who reads the comment and stops will believe the belt exists. Every claim of the form 'the guard already exists' in this slice needs its callers counted, not its comment read.
- offerScope may be inert. Offer rows are written by inbound-sync.service.ts:94 and matrix.routes.ts:282, but coverage on Amazon coordinates is UNMEASURED. If it is near-zero, resolveSellerSku always falls back to Product.sku and 'withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA' cannot be expressed — a confirmation offering it would be a promise nothing can keep. Count first, on both databases.
- restore-offer is an EU-pool event even though withdraw-offer is not. reopenMarketOffers forces followMasterQuantity:true and enqueues an immediate QUANTITY_UPDATE (:275-300), which re-enters the nine-market shared pool the guard protects. The draft's 'withdraw is not a fan-out' is right and stops one verb too early.
- The SQS fact write, as drafted, pins coordinates at SUPPRESSED forever: amazon-sqs-poll.job.ts:201-220 acts only inside `if (note.isSuppressed && note.asin)` and deletes the message otherwise, so a recovery to BUYABLE is never recorded. And the notification carries asin/sku/marketplaceId with no connection or alias, so a fact write from it would be guessing its own coordinate — the ambient-targeting hazard in a new place.
- Amazon's marketplace-id defaults to IT in eight places, not one (channel-delist.service.ts:86 and :277 for eBay's siteId; amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:355,839,1435; amazon-cockpit-publish.routes.ts:294; flat-file-pull.service.ts:165; flat-file.service.ts:2706). Deleting one map and leaving the pattern means a presence verb aimed at NL still lands on IT somewhere downstream.
- POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove hard-deletes ChannelListing rows from a caller-supplied target array AND fans out to every child product (amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:47-65), and amazon-flat-file.routes.ts contains ZERO occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate across 29 routes (measured, positive control 29). Securing the operator path with a typed SKU while the endpoint stays open secures the wrong half — and rbacHook defaults to shadow (lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:30-32).
- The amazonFileExcluded stamp touches the flat-file read path, which is under the untouchable rule. It is a stamp in the removal service plus a filter in GET /rows, not an editor change, but the Owner should rule rather than have a lane decide the boundary.
- reversal is an enhancement, not a fix: DX-5 was REFUTED. Presenting it as closing a confirmed finding would be the relayed-claim conversion. It is also in tension with shipped doctrine at ActionConfirm.tsx:29-30 ('the dialog never decides severity, it renders what the preflight found') — compatible only because the preflight produces the field, and that reading should be stated, not assumed.

##### The specification
## CORRECTIONS
Eleven changes. Each was re-derived with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed` on the working tree.
1. **`Badge` cannot be the fact mark.** `BadgeProps` is `{ program: AdProgram; children }` (`primitives/Badge.tsx:6-10`) — it is the advertising SP/SB/SD badge and has no `tone`. The draft's "Pill and Badge, the two marks" is unbuildable. Both marks are `Pill` (`tone: Tone`, `primitives/Pill.tsx:4-6`), separated by the new presence renderer, not by two components.
2. **`SourceIndicator` must not mark a derived intent.** `kind: ValueSourceKind = 'master'|'override'|'rule'|'default'|'missing'|'linked'|'channel'|'formula'|'ai'|'warning'` (`components/SourceIndicator.tsx:7`) is the *cell-value* provenance axis. Reusing it for "this intent was inferred, not stated" is exactly the cross-vocabulary mapping `renderers/readiness.ts:19-26` refuses — and that axis is already red (`layers.vitest.test.ts` on unmapped `channelSnapshot`). Derived-intent provenance belongs in the new presence renderer plus `MenuItemDef.description`.
3. **The ratchet is 34, not 33, and the importer is a barrel import.** Measured: `node scripts/check-grid-kit-ratchet.mjs --check` → `DS DataGrid (<table>) 34 baseline 32 ❌ ROSE`. `ReadinessPanel.tsx:3` imports `{ Card, DataGrid, type Column } from '@/design-system/components'` — via the barrel, so a grep for `components/DataGrid` misses it. Substance stands: do not build presence on `DataGrid`.
4. **DX-5 is REFUTED — `reversal` may not be claimed as closing it.** The audit refuted "ActionImpact cannot express reversibility" as a *proposal, not a shipped defect*, noting `ActionConfirm.tsx:29-30` doctrine: the dialog never decides severity, it renders what the preflight found. `reversal` is still right, and it is consistent with that doctrine (the preflight *produces* the field), but it is an enhancement, not a fix.
5. **The studio's Amazon channel verbs are already refused twice, and this proposal deletes that guard.** `channelActions.ts:249-251` sets `unavailable: "Refused for now: N of these are live listings, and wave-1 verbs do not send to a channel"` whenever a row carries an `externalListingId`; `runAction.ts:74-76` returns `refused` before `ask`; `:258-261` refuses again inside `run`. That preview-only gate is the only thing between an operator and a live Amazon write today. The Amazon adapter removes it. The draft never said so.
6. **There are THREE resurrection paths, and `doNotRecreate` closes one.** The draft claimed the identity registry "refuses the flat-file import and the reconciliation upsert, which are the two paths". Wrong: `getExistingRows` ignores listing scope entirely (`flat-file.service.ts:2094-2124`) and `listing-scope.ts:14-26` never reads `listingStatus` — nothing consults a ChannelListing, so nothing can consult an identity. See §5.
7. **`closeMarketOffers` does not set `offerActive:false`, so the next flat-file submit re-opens the buy box.** `flat-file.service.ts:3417-3419` explicitly writes `skip_offer:false` when `offerActive !== false`, "Ensure a previously-paused listing that's now active clears skip_offer". That is a second resurrection path the draft missed entirely.
8. **`closeMarketOffers` writes `offerClosedAt` on a dry-run.** The persist at `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:178-194` runs unconditionally; `patchPurchasableOffer` returns `{success:true, dryRun:true}` under `gated`/`dry-run` (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:792-796`), and `gated` is the *default*. Nexus records CLOSED while Amazon keeps selling. `pricing-outbound.service.ts:181-205` gets this right (PD.3); the close does not.
9. **`closedMarketSet` has ZERO callers.** Its own comment says "Used by every Amazon write stack so nothing can silently resurrect a closed offer" (`amazon-market-offer.service.ts:316-318`). `/usr/bin/grep -rn closedMarketSet apps/api/src apps/web/src packages` returns only the definition. P5's scope is wider than "pricing-outbound forgot" — the belt exists and nothing wears it.
10. **`checkAsinOwnership` must mirror eBay's shape *including its unverifiable arm*, and be pure.** `checkItemIdOwnership` returns `unverifiable` when the listing reports no SKUs (`ebay-itemid-relink.pure.ts:64-73`) — "cannot prove must never collapse into fine". The draft mapped an empty ASIN search to `rejected`. An empty `searchListingsItems` can be a throttle, an unresolved seller, a wrong `marketplaceId`, or the SP-API auth bug whose own comment says a bad header "made every getListingsItem 404, which we misread as listing not found" (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1252-1256`). Empty → `unverifiable`. Also: eBay's is a *pure* function fed already-fetched SKUs; the Amazon twin splits the same way (`amazon-asin-ownership.pure.ts` + a fetching service), and must page (`pageSize ?? 10`, `:1354`).
11. **The SQS fact write sits inside an `isSuppressed` branch, and the notification cannot name a coordinate.** `amazon-sqs-poll.job.ts:201-220` acts only when `note.isSuppressed && note.asin`; a recovery to BUYABLE publishes nothing and the message is deleted. Persisting inside that branch would pin every coordinate at SUPPRESSED forever. And the notification carries `asin/sku/marketplaceId` — no connection, no alias.
Two smaller ones, folded in: `resolveAmazonMarketplaceId`'s null→IT default has a sibling one line away in the same file — `siteIdForMarket(job.targetRegion ?? 'IT')` (`channel-delist.service.ts:277`); and `MARKETPLACE_ID_MAP[mp] ?? MARKETPLACE_ID_MAP.IT` appears at six further call sites (`amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:355,839,1435`, `amazon-cockpit-publish.routes.ts:294`, `flat-file-pull.service.ts:165`, `flat-file.service.ts:2706`). A2 is a deletion of a *pattern*, not of one map.
---
## 0. Method
Four corrections to my own inputs survive verification. **(a)** `patchListingsItem` is not a method: `/usr/bin/grep -rn patchListingsItem apps packages` returns comments, a `dist/.d.ts` and two vitest mocks. What exists is `submitListingPayload` (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:501`) and `patchPurchasableOffer` (`:772`). **(b)** No `availability_status: DISCONTINUED` attribute is establishable from this repo; the one hit is the aspirational comment at `channel-delist.service.ts:151-153`. **(c)** `deleteListingsItem` reads `process.env.AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE` at `:1143`; every other write calls `getAmazonPublishMode()` (`:519`, `:669`, `:792`, `:884`). **(d)** Three marketplace-id sources disagree: `MARKETPLACE_ID_MAP` (5 markets, `services/amazon/flat-file.service.ts:33-39`), `AMAZON_MARKETPLACE_IDS` (7, `channel-delist.service.ts:75-83`, null→IT at `:86`), and `Marketplace.marketplaceId` (`schema.prisma:1947`). `AMAZON_EU_SHARED_MARKETS` names nine (`amazon-eu-quantity-guard.ts:24-26`), so NL/BE/PL/SE/IE have no id in the map SCT.6 uses and `closeMarketOffers` returns `FAILED: unknown marketplace NL` (`:117`).
## 1. Four resolvers, before any verb
**`resolveSellerSku(coordinate, offerScope)`** — `Offer.sku` for the named fulfilment method (`schema.prisma:2287-2321`, unique on `[workspaceId, channelListingId, fulfillmentMethod]`, comment at `:2294-2296` "Different SKU per fulfillment method"), else `Product.sku`. **Never `externalListingId`**: `schema.prisma:1784` annotates it "Amazon: Parent ASIN … or child ASIN", `flat-file-pull.service.ts:267` writes the ASIN into it, and `channel-delist.service.ts:138-140` then says "externalListingId for Amazon is typically the SKU" — the same file contradicts its own docblock at `:36` sixty lines earlier. Refuse with `AMAZON_NO_SELLER_SKU`.
**Precondition, new: measure the `Offer` population first.** `offerScope:'FBM'` is inert if no Amazon coordinate has `Offer` rows. Writers exist (`inbound-sync.service.ts:94` upsert, `matrix.routes.ts:282` create) but coverage is unmeasured. Count Amazon-coordinate `Offer` rows on **both** databases before promising `offerScope` in any confirmation.
**`resolveMarketplaceId(marketplace)`** — one function over `Marketplace.marketplaceId`. Null or unmapped is a refusal, never IT. Deletes both literal maps and all eight `?? …IT` defaults.
**`resolveSellerAccount(channelConnectionId)`** — `getAmazonSellerId(accountId)` takes an account (`lib/amazon-sp-client.ts:24-27`) and `amazonAccount` already throws `amazon_seller_mismatch` when a passed `sellerId` disagrees (`:19`). But the per-account proxy re-binds only when `NEXUS_WORKSPACES_ENABLED === '1'` (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1457`), which `apps/api/.env.example:205` sets to `0` and which I could not read for prod. So the adapter resolves the seller itself and refuses on mismatch rather than trusting the proxy. **`closeMarketOffers` hoists `const seller = await sellerId()` outside its loop (`:90`) with no account** — a multi-account plan sends every close under one seller. Move it inside, per target.
**`resolveProductType(coordinate)`** — live `summaries[0].productType`, else `platformAttributes.productType`, else `Product.productType`; blank refuses, as `:154-158` already does.
## 2. The gate is inverted for exactly the two verbs that matter
`getAmazonPublishMode()` returns `'gated'` whenever `NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH` is unset (`amazon-publish-gate.service.ts:34-53`). Today:
- The **irreversible** verb escapes it. `deleteListingsItem` reads the env directly, so with `AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE=live` and the master flag OFF, a real SP-API DELETE goes out while every other write is short-circuited.
- The **reversible** verb lies about it. `closeMarketOffers` persists `offerClosedAt` even when the patch was a dry-run, so with the gate at its default the studio will render WITHDRAWN over a live, selling offer.
**A1 (raised to the top of the Amazon slice):** route `deleteListingsItem` through `getAmazonPublishMode()`, and make every presence write conditional on `dryRun !== true` — intent included, not just fact. A dry-run yields `fact: UNKNOWN` **and no intent change**; the operator is told nothing was sent.
## 3. The verb table
| verb | mechanism | reversal |
|---|---|---|
| `hold` / `resume` | local `syncPaused`; honoured at `outbound-sync.service.ts:903-906` | `resume`, exact |
| `withdraw-offer` | `closeMarketOffers` — delete this market's `purchasable_offer` instance | `restore-offer`, exact *after revalidation* |
| `restore-offer` | `reopenMarketOffers` — replay snapshot, `op:'replace'` | — |
| `end` | JSON_LISTINGS_FEED `{sku, operationType:'DELETE'}` (`flat-file.service.ts:2805-2809`, submitted at `amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:675`) | `relist`, lossy |
| `relist` | `listings/recovery.service.ts` ladder; ~30-day same-SKU cooldown (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1119-1122`) | `end`, lossy |
| `discontinue` | intent + `ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate` | exact |
| `release` | `deleteListingsItem` | **none** |
| `verify` | `getListingsItem({includedData:['summaries','offers','issues']})` | n/a |
`end` is the design choice: Amazon's DELETE *is* a release, so the reversible-with-loss "off IT, back next season" an operator means is `withdraw-offer`, and **Amazon has no third state between them** — with one caveat the draft missed. `skip_offer` (`flat-file.service.ts:3413-3420`) is a *second* offer-suppression mechanism, driven by `offerActive`, reaching Amazon only at the next flat-file submit. It is not a third state; it is a second lever on the same one, and the two disagree today (§5).
## 4. Per-verb corrections
**`withdraw-offer` / `restore-offer`.** Five changes, not four. (1) `loadRow` is a `findFirst` on `{productId, channel, marketplace}` (`:66-68`) — five-level key, refuse on ambiguity. (2) `offerScope`, gated on the population measurement above; `isFbaRow` (`:59-63`) stays the default for `'all'`. (3) `reopenMarketOffers` replays the snapshot verbatim at `:264-266` with no margin check, then forces `followMasterQuantity:true` and enqueues an immediate `QUANTITY_UPDATE` (`:275-300`) — a clearance price in March reopens at clearance in October. Preflight shows snapshot price beside computed price and `minPrice`/`minMargin`; below floor refuses without override. (4) **New: that forced re-Follow is an EU event even though the close was not.** The close deletes an offer instance and touches no quantity, correctly; the reopen puts the row back into the shared pool and pushes. Route the reopen's enqueue through `detectEuIntentConflict` and surface the verdict in the *reopen* preflight, or the guard refuses it at dispatch and the operator learns from a failed queue row. (5) Write `channelFact` from the response, and skip the local write on `dryRun`.
**`end`.** `endAmazonOffer(coordinate)` composing the one-message DELETE feed. Mirror `{listingStatus:'ENDED', isPublished:false}` (`flat-file.service.ts:3197-3204`), add `endedAt/By/Reason` and `presenceIntent:'ENDED'` — and narrow that `updateMany`, which is scoped `{productId, channel, marketplace}` today, a third P8-class site. The feed is async: outcome is `PENDING` until the poll cron resolves it; never "ended" on submission.
**`release`.** Unchanged from the draft and correct: ladder walked, fresh `verify` returning ABSENT/NOT_SELLING, `hard-delete-preflight` clear (`products-catalog.routes.ts:1666-1760`, whose FBA leg at `:1728-1738` returns `{marketplaceId, fulfillmentCenterId, quantity, condition}`), `confirmPhrase` = the real seller SKU per `registry.ts:117`, tombstone in the same transaction, and the `SKIPPED_FBA` guard the gentler close already has. Note the shipped counter-example: `BulkActionBar.tsx:1129` arms on the literal string `'DELETE'`.
**`verify`.** Three traps stand (404 → UNKNOWN on first sight; `summaries[].status` is an array while `SPAPIResponse.status` is typed `string` at `:47`; `DISCOVERABLE → isPublished:true` at `flat-file-pull.service.ts:271-276` is a measured decision, 431 blocked rows, **do not overturn**). Correction 11 applies to the SQS leg: move the persist *outside* the `isSuppressed` branch so a recovery is recorded, and refuse to write when the notification cannot be resolved to one five-level coordinate — a fact write that guesses its coordinate is the ambient-targeting hazard in a new place.
## 5. Three resurrection paths
A withdrawn Amazon offer comes back three independent ways.
1. **Reprice.** `pushAmazonPrice` selects only `{id, platformAttributes}` (`pricing-outbound.service.ts:140-149`, and its `findFirst` is unscoped by connection and alias) and `patchListingPrice` issues `op:'replace'` on `/attributes/purchasable_offer` (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:687-699`) — the instance the close removed with `op:'delete'`. Replace-on-absent recreates it, then stamps `syncStatus:'IN_SYNC'`. **Fix: one shared `assertPushAllowed(listingId)` extracted from `outbound-sync.service.ts:903-910`, and make `closedMarketSet` — which today has zero callers — the belt it claims to be.**
2. **Flat-file submit.** `closeMarketOffers` never sets `offerActive:false`, so `flat-file.service.ts:3417-3419` sends `skip_offer:false` on the next submit. **Fix: the adapter writes `offerActive:false` alongside `offerClosedAt`, and the submit path consults `presenceIntent` before clearing `skip_offer`.** Note this crosses the offer/intent boundary the spine's §5 gate script polices — declare it as the one sanctioned pairing.
3. **Flat-file row loader.** `getExistingRows` ignores listing scope (`flat-file.service.ts:2094-2124`) and `listing-scope.ts:14-26` never reads `listingStatus`, so a removed SKU is still an ordinary editable row and saving it re-creates the listing. **`ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate` cannot close this — nothing here reads a ChannelListing.** Mirror the eBay precedent instead: `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:649-671` stamps `Product.categoryAttributes.ebayFileExcluded` per market, honoured at `ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:262`; `/usr/bin/grep fileExcluded apps/api/src` returns eBay hits only. Write `amazonFileExcluded[MARKET]` in the same transaction as any `end`/`release`, honour it in `GET /amazon/flat-file/rows`, auto-clear on an explicit re-save. Note the flat-file editors are untouchable — this is a read-filter and a stamp in the *removal* service, not an editor change; flag it for the Owner.
## 6. FBA, EU, suppression, ownership, ads
Unchanged and verified: the FBA warning text exists at `amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:806-811`, the data at `FbaInventoryDetail` (`schema.prisma:9522-9550`), and every non-exact channel verb must itemise FC and count as `ActionImpact.findings` at `error`. `withdraw-offer` is not an EU fan-out (`amazon-eu-quantity-guard.ts:19-21`); `ZERO_PIN` is, and stays behind `projectActionAndDetect` with `EU_GUARD_REMEDY`. Suppression consumes `GET /api/products/:id/suppressions` (`listings-syndication.routes.ts:2391`) and adds no table. Ownership per correction 10. Ads: `ads-retail-readiness.service.ts:50` never selects `listingStatus` and `:62,:65` fall back to a sibling market's listing and buy box — both confirmed by reading; the adapter calls `suppressCampaignBids`/`restoreCampaignBids` (`ads-bid-suppression.service.ts:58,171`) under the no-pause rule.
## 7. The DS, corrected
`Pill` (tone), `KeyValue`, `EmptyState`, `Banner`, `InfoTip` (`tip: string`), `Modal`, `NexusGrid`, `BulkActionBar`, `Menu`, `registry.ts`, `ActionConfirm.tsx`, `runAction.ts`, `menuAdapters.tsx` all exist with the props assumed. `Badge` and `SourceIndicator` do not (corrections 1–2). Measured gaps: `MenuItemDef` has no tone (`Menu.tsx:8-50`), both adapters drop `GridAction.danger` (`/usr/bin/grep -c danger menuAdapters.tsx` = **0** against a control of 5 for `label`), and `.nds-menu` has **0** danger rules against 20 total in `styles/components.css`. One further gap the draft missed: `actionMenuItems` puts a disabled verb's reason in `title` — a tooltip — while `MenuItemDef.description` exists precisely because "a tooltip is hover-only… the whole point of these rows is that a disabled item explains itself (hub rulings #133/#231)". Every presence refusal ("closing a suppressed offer destroys the evidence") would land hover-only. `actionMenuItems` must map the reason to `description`.
## 8. Rulings
**CH.1 is not strained by this proposal, and it already anticipates lifecycle.** Its verbatim text at `SheetToolbar.tsx:15-17` names "the family verbs, **a listing's preflight**, add alias" as overflow items. And the smallest amendment is smaller than the draft said: `SheetToolbar.tsx:253-256` *already* renders `description` on items and *already* inserts a `separator` before Reload. Only `tone` is missing. Still the Owner's call. **D9**: presence is a distinct noun (state, not content) living as the first item of each existing channel group; the draft's reading holds, and it is in openQuestions. **No page-local hand-rolls**: every gap above is proposed in `design-system/`.
## 9. Honest cost
Wave 1 ships no Amazon channel verb, so the `channelActions.ts:249-251` preview-only refusal stays. Wave 2 deletes it — that is the moment the studio can reach production Amazon, and it should be its own approval, not a consequence of one.

The surface
