#### The Consequence Contract — a destructive-action pattern derived from typed reach and reversibility (corrected)

Severity should be computed from typed facts, not authored in prose, because every lie the audit found is a hand-written sentence no gate can validate. `GridAction` gains `reach`; `ActionImpact` gains `reversal`, `subject`, `acknowledge`, `refusal`. A pure `frictionFor(impact)` derives the minimum friction rung; `validateImpact` refuses anything softer, so a lane may escalate but never soften, and the reversal sentence is rendered from the type. `MenuItemDef` gains `tone`, both adapters map `danger`, destructive items partition below a rule. Two real AA failures inside the DS's own delete dialog are fixed. Two live refusal-masking bugs in the studio's own channel verbs are named and fixed. I corrected twenty claims in the reviewed draft, including an invented token, a wrong CSS mechanism, a misfiled DS component, and one change that would have deleted the family verbs from the sheet toolbar.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- `MenuItemDef` cannot express a destructive item. Verified: Menu.tsx:8-50 has id/label/icon/disabled/onSelect/href/target/title/description/separator and no tone, severity or group key; `/usr/bin/grep` for a `.nds-menu` danger rule over design-system/styles returns rc=1 against a positive control of 20 `.nds-menu` matches in components.css. ADD `tone?: Tone` (primitives/tone.ts) rendered as `data-tone` — the idiom at components.css:1991, :2029-2030, :2105-2106 — plus `.nds-menu button[data-tone='danger'] { color: var(--nds-danger-text) }` (7.36 light / 7.14 dark) and `:hover { background: var(--nds-danger-soft) }` (6.27 / 7.23). No new token. NOTE the cascade: both rules are (0,2,1), the same as `.nds-menu button:disabled` at components.css:748-751, which is LATER in the file, so a disabled danger item correctly keeps --nds-text-2. This gap is filed at .claude/DS-GAPS.md:329-348; its text is stale (it recites MenuItemDef as six fields; title/description/separator have since landed) — APPEND a correction, per scripts/check-ds-gaps-append-only.mjs.
- `ActionImpact` cannot express REACH or REVERSIBILITY. registry.ts:78-121 carries exactly level/cancelled/title/consequences/sideEffects/findings/payload/confirmPhrase/unavailable. ADD `reach`, `reversal{verb,actionId,fidelity,note}`, `subject`, `acknowledge`, `refusal`, plus `frictionFor()` and `reversalSentence()` in registry.ts so the sentence is RENDERED from the type and no lane authors it.
- `GridAction` has one inert severity bit. `danger?: boolean` (registry.ts:183-184) promises in its own doc that destructive verbs are 'grouped apart and rendered with danger tone by every adapter'; `/usr/bin/grep -n danger menuAdapters.tsx` returns rc=1 against a positive control of 5 for `label`. Its ONLY reader in the product is drawer/RecordActions.tsx:66 — and that surface puts the availability reason in `title` on a DISABLED Button at :71, which fires no pointer events in Chrome (the exact trap .githooks/pre-push:185-195 ratchets). ADD `reach?: Reach` to GridAction and a `validateAction(action)` beside `validateImpact`, which takes only the impact (registry.ts:264) and cannot see action-level fields.
- `ActionConfirm` has no acknowledge-checkbox rung. drawer/DrawerConfirm.tsx:191 has both gates (`blocked = (!!req.acknowledge && !acked) || (!!req.requireTyped && …)`); ActionConfirm.tsx:80-81 has only the typed gate. This is the single capability keeping a page-local confirmation implementation alive inside the studio. ADD `acknowledge?: string` to ActionImpact and a `primitives/Checkbox` to ActionConfirm.
- There is no DS confirmation for a verb raised OUTSIDE a grid. components/index.ts has 53 exports and zero matching confirm/dialog/alert (rc=1); useActionConfirm is exported only from grid/index.ts:54, yet ChannelSheet.tsx:585 and :606 already call it with hand-built literals for non-GridAction decisions. MOVE ActionConfirm.tsx to components/, export from components/index.ts, re-export from grid/index.ts, move its CSS from grid/theme/grid.css:1026-1039 to styles/components.css as `.nds-confirm-*`. 🔴 It is NOT free of grid CSS: it renders `nds-cell-strong` at :102, :112, :127, :141, defined only at grid/theme/grid.css:334 — replace with a `.nds-confirm-h` in components.css or the headings lose their weight off-grid.
- No DS reach-acknowledgement pattern. The working one is page-local at ChannelSheet.tsx:1859-1888 (Banner + choice buttons, held write, revert on decline) with the ask-once reset at :670-674 (which keys on channel/marketplace/accountId/locale and NOT aliasKey). PROMOTE to patterns/ReachAcknowledgement, or the next surface that widens a write's reach hand-rolls it again.
- AA failure inside the DS's own destructive dialog. `.nds-grid-confirm-error { color: var(--nds-danger) }` (grid/theme/grid.css:1039) computes to 3.91:1 light and 3.89:1 dark; `.nds-grid-confirm-warn` (:1038) to 3.76 / 4.05. The text tiers minted for exactly this are already in use one line above at :1023. CHANGE to --nds-danger-text (7.36 / 7.14) and --nds-warning-text (8.87 / 8.30). Recomputed independently from tokens.css:99-121 and :475-508.
- `.nds-btn:focus-visible` leaves the danger button with no visible focus at all, and the reviewed draft had the mechanism wrong. `.nds-btn:focus-visible` (primitives.css:33-37) and `.nds-btn.danger` (:77-81) both compute to specificity (0,2,0), and `.danger` is later in the file — so its own `border-color: var(--nds-danger-strong)` WINS and the blue border never paints. There is no 1.57:1 indicator; there is none. Same shape for `.primary` (:58-62, border already --nds-primary, so 1.00:1), `.danger-outline` (:1116-1119) and `.on`. All that remains is --nds-focus-ring, documented at primitives.css:187-189 as 12% alpha = 1.17:1. ADD `outline: 2px solid var(--nds-primary); outline-offset: 1px` — the idiom used 11 times in this one file (grep -c). ⚠ `var(--nds-focus)` DOES NOT EXIST: only --nds-focus-rgb (tokens.css:69), --nds-focus-ring (:210) and --nds-grid-focus-ring (:289).
- AG's context menu has no visible slot for a disabled verb's reason. Verified in node_modules/ag-grid-community/dist/types/src/interfaces/menuItem.d.ts:4-30: MenuItemLeafDef offers `cssClasses?: string[]` (:21) and `tooltip?: string` (:23) and no second line. The DS Menu can do it (`description`, Menu.tsx:26-45) and AG cannot. UNRESOLVED without a browser: accept tooltip-only in AG's right-click menu while the ⋯ menu carries the visible reason, or spike AG's `menuItem` custom-component slot.
- The DS `.d.ts` files are a gate and every type addition here trips it. scripts/check-ds-dts-fresh.mjs exists; registry.d.ts, Menu.d.ts, menuAdapters.d.ts and ActionConfirm.d.ts all change. Note Menu.d.ts is currently FRESH (it carries title and description), so the '⚠ the declaration is stale' warning at menuAdapters.tsx:63-74 is itself now stale and should be corrected in the same pass.

##### API
- Every preflight endpoint that feeds an ActionImpact must return `reach` and `reversal{verb,actionId,fidelity}` PER TARGET rather than leaving the client to assert them — otherwise frictionFor computes a rung from a fact the client guessed, which is a validated-looking guess and arguably worse than the prose it replaces.
- GET /api/products/hard-delete-preflight (products-catalog.routes.ts:1666-1760) gains a per-channel `delistCapability: 'reversible' | 'irreversible' | 'unimplemented'`, so the three radio bodies in apps/web/src/app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx (:1281-1332) stop being hardcoded i18n strings that contradict channel-delist.service.ts:150-166. Its only web caller today is that dialog.
- The publish-gate mode already computed CLIENT-SIDE at _studio/channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:313-332 ('live'/'gated'/'dry-run'/undescribed, and modeForChannel returns null rather than guessing 'live' for Etsy and WooCommerce) should be returned by the server alongside reach, so an impact under a gate downgrades to reach:'local' and carries gateNote()'s existing honest sentence verbatim (:336-373) instead of promising a channel it will not touch. No new route — a field on the responses that already describe a channel operation.
- No new route, no new writer, and no endpoint is called by this proposal. Every change above is an added response field on an existing read.

##### Schema
- NONE — verified. This proposal is a design-system and client-contract change only; it declares no Prisma field, adds no model, and requires no migration. Nothing in it depends on a column existing. The presence/lifecycle state model that motivates it carries its own additive schema, which is that proposal's to justify.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, recorded verbatim at SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20 and :99-104) says a scope's own verbs are ITEMS of the one ⋯ overflow and never a button. THIS PROPOSAL DOES NOT NEED CH.1 TO BEND: `actionMenuItems`/`actionContextMenu` are consumed only by ROW menus (FamilyVariants.tsx:329,333,336; useVariantRowMenu.tsx:18; ChannelSheet.tsx:995,1042; MasterSheet.tsx:484,494), while the toolbar's `overflow` is hand-built by useFamilyVerbs and already inserts its own separator before Reload (SheetToolbar.tsx:255-256). The strain is real but latent: `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` (:104) carries no tone and no group, so under CH.1 as written 'End the listing on Amazon IT' would render byte-identical to 'Reload' if lifecycle ever reached the toolbar. The SMALLEST AMENDMENT I can see keeps CH.1's actual rule intact (no new buttons) and asks only that the overflow gain the same typed tone plus a separator group the row menus get. Does CH.1 stand as written, with lifecycle verbs living only on row menus and a future presence surface?
- SheetToolbar.tsx:254 force-disables every scope verb when `blocked` — `blocked ? { ...item, disabled: true } : item` — with NO reason, while :239 renders `absent` entries WITH one. That is the silent-disabled trap in the surface CH.1 designates for a scope's verbs. I have filed it as a one-line W1 fix (attach 'The sheet is still loading' / 'This sheet could not be read'), but it changes what CH.1's overflow says, so the Owner should confirm rather than have it slipped in.
- D9 — no second way to reach something the grid already is (StudioHeader.tsx:86-96). Promoting `ActionConfirm` to components/ and `ReachAcknowledgement` to patterns/ adds no page and no route; it consolidates. I read that as squarely inside D9. If the Owner reads a DS-level confirmation surface that non-grid pages can call as widening the studio's surface area, W2 should not start.
- The `title` → `description` move. `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:63-69` asserts the disabled reason lands in `title` and cites a measured 900px/1059px menu as the reason. I propose BOTH (title kept, description added) so that test stays green, but the geometry is unmeasured and the doctrine (hub rulings #133/#231, an instruction may never live only on a disabled control) points at description alone. Does the Owner want both, or description alone with the tooltip dropped?
- The AG right-click asymmetry. AG's MenuItemLeafDef has no second line (menuItem.d.ts:4-30), so the visible reason exists in the ⋯ menu and the drawer but is tooltip-only on right-click. Accept the asymmetry and say so, or fund a spike of AG's `menuItem` custom-component slot? I could not settle which renders correctly without a browser.
- The 83-importer Tailwind confirm stack. `components/ui/ConfirmDialog` + `ConfirmProvider` and 57 remaining `window.confirm` calls are a separate migration. This proposal only asks that the DS surface be able to RECEIVE them. Does the Owner want that migration filed as its own programme now, or left until the DS surface has proven itself on the five studio dialogs?
- Type-to-confirm plurality. docs/pes-claims.md:26808-26812 rules one coordinate at a time. R4 therefore refuses a plural typed confirm and offers the per-coordinate verb instead. If a future presence plan wants 'stop selling on nine markets' behind one typed phrase, that ruling has to move first — I have not assumed it will.
- Does the Owner want the `reach`/`reversal` API contract (see apiChanges) treated as a precondition of W1 rather than a follow-up? Without it `frictionFor` computes a rung from a fact the client asserted, which is a validated-looking guess — arguably worse than the honest prose it replaces.

##### Risks
- The partition changes two asserted arrays in menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts (:33, :93). Their asserted intent is which verbs are offered and that AG's clipboard sits below a rule; the position of `delete` is incidental to the fixture. This is a deliberate change to expected values, not a bug found — a reviewer may reasonably rule the other way.
- Adding `description` to every disabled row menu item is an unmeasured geometry change. The adapter's own header records ~900px and 1059px menus, but that was measured with the reason APPENDED TO THE LABEL, a different mechanism; `description` owns a 34ch wrap at components.css:770-778. Menu height is not bounded by anything and I could not run the Playwright gates.
- `frictionFor` is only as honest as the `reach` and `fidelity` a lane declares. It converts a prose lie into a typed lie unless the preflight ENDPOINT returns both. Until the API half lands (see apiChanges), the table validates a client-side assertion and gives the appearance of a check.
- Every ratio here is computed from tokens.css, not measured on a rendered page, and scripts/check-contrast.mjs cannot corroborate any of them (it hardcodes a legacy vocabulary the studio does not use). A composited surface, an opacity or a color-mix ground would move the numbers.
- Promoting ActionConfirm out of grid/ touches a barrel with 53 exports and a re-export path; a stale .d.ts or a missed `nds-cell-strong` ships a confirmation that looks subtly wrong on the surfaces that matter most.
- The React-18 autoFocus ordering (C13) is derived from source, not witnessed in a browser. If ReactDOM's commit ordering differs from my reading, focus is already on Cancel and the change is a no-op rather than a fix — harmless, but the spec should not claim a measurement it does not have.
- Absorbing DrawerConfirm's capture-phase Esc/Tab ownership into a DS component moves keyboard ownership from a page-local card to shared code. DrawerConfirm.tsx:139-188 documents two specific reasons it takes the keyboard in capture; a generalised version that gets this wrong breaks the docked drawer rather than the dialog.

##### The specification
# The Consequence Contract (corrected)
## CORRECTIONS — what I changed in the reviewed draft, and why
Everything below was re-derived with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed` on the working tree. No browser, no endpoint, no repo write.
**C1 — `var(--nds-focus)` does not exist.** The draft's focus-ring fix names a token that is not defined anywhere: `/usr/bin/grep -rn -- "--nds-focus\b" design-system/styles design-system/tokens` returns only `--nds-focus-rgb` (tokens.css:69), `--nds-focus-ring` (:210) and `--nds-grid-focus-ring` (:289). The shipped idiom is `outline: 2px solid var(--nds-primary); outline-offset: 1px`, used **11** times in `primitives.css` (`grep -c` = 11 at :190, :532, :806, :856, :952, :1212, :1297, :1340, :1363, :1465, :1482 — the draft's ":186-189" etc. are the comment lines above each). Fixed to the real token and the real offset.
**C2 — the mechanism of the danger-button focus failure is wrong, and the truth is worse.** `.nds-btn:focus-visible` (primitives.css:33-37) and `.nds-btn.danger` (:77-81) both have specificity (0,2,0); `.danger` is 44 lines later, so **its** `border-color: var(--nds-danger-strong)` wins and the focus rule's blue border never paints. There is no 1.57:1 indicator; there is no indicator at all beyond `--nds-focus-ring`, which primitives.css:187-189 itself documents as 12% alpha compositing to 1.17:1. Same for `.nds-btn.primary` (:58-62, the border is already `--nds-primary`, so 1.00:1 as the draft said) and `.nds-btn.on`/`.danger-outline`. A reviewer who checks 1.57 would reject the section; the finding survives, stronger, on the corrected cause.
**C3 — `spellCheck={false}` and `autoComplete="off"` are already there** (ActionConfirm.tsx:148-149). Only `autoCapitalize="off"` / `autoCorrect="off"` are missing. `Input` spreads `...rest` (Input.tsx:40) so both pass through.
**C4 — `BulkActionBar` is misfiled as a DS component.** `design-system/patterns/BulkActionBar.tsx` is **36 lines** and contains no dialog. Every cited line is `apps/web/src/app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx`, a page-local Tailwind file: `armed = typed.trim().toUpperCase() === 'DELETE'` at **:1129**, `autoFocus` at **:1340**, and `placeholder={t('products.hardDelete.confirmPlaceholder')}` at **:1345** printing the same i18n key the label prints at :1337 — the answer inside the field. Removed from the DS list and renamed throughout.
**C5 — four of five studio-dialog paths are wrong.** Real: `_studio/images/amazon/AmazonMediaWorkspace.tsx:376-377` (`variant="danger"`) and `:278-279` (the R0 undo Banner, `tone="info"`, not warning); `_studio/images/ebay/EbayMediaWorkspace.tsx:307-308` (both buttons variant-less — destroy is pixel-identical to keep, confirmed); `_studio/images/shopify/ShopifyContentWorkspace.tsx:240` (`danger-outline`); `_studio/shopify/ShopifyLinkedWorkspace.tsx:358` (`primary` "Continue" for a generic confirmation) and `:359` (not :361).
**C6 — the draft would have deleted the family verbs from the sheet toolbar.** It proposes deleting "`FamilyBar.tsx:82-95`'s hand-rolled mapping in favour of the adapter". `useFamilyVerbs` maps `actionsFor(actions, contextOf('product-family'), [])` (FamilyBar.tsx:85-96); `actionMenuItems` maps ROW+SELECTION for one row and, by its own test at `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:38-43`, *"a family verb can never reach a row menu"*. They are disjoint sets. It also already does what the draft asks for — `...(availability.kind === 'disabled' ? { description: availability.reason } : {})` at :89 — plus busy-state labels and the add-variation collector the adapter has no concept of. **Struck entirely.** What remains is the smaller, correct ask: make the ROW adapter match what `FamilyBar` already does.
**C7 — the `title` → `description` move breaks a third test the draft did not name, and that test asserts intent.** `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:63-69` asserts the reason lands in `title`; `:71-73` asserts a runnable entry has none. Under the Phase-0 rule, an asserted behaviour is a decision. Corrected position: **add `description`, keep `title`** — the hover affordance stays, the visible line is new, `:63-69` stays green and only the disabled arm of `:71-73` needs no change at all. Also the adapter's width objection (`menuAdapters.tsx:63-74`, measured ~900px and 1059px) is about **appending to the label**, which is a different mechanism from `description`; `description` did not exist when that comment was written and owns a `max-width: 34ch` wrap (components.css:770-778). The height cost of a description on every disabled item is unmeasured and stated as such.
**C8 — the Toast argument is half wrong.** Toast.tsx:14-16 documents `opts.duration` as existing *precisely* so "a toast carrying an interactive verb (an Undo)" gets longer on screen. The timer is not fixed. The valid half stands and is all I now claim: `role="status"`, no action slot, no dismiss (:46-51).
**C9 — `ActionConfirm` is not free of grid CSS.** It renders `className="nds-cell-strong"` at :102, :112, :127 and :141, defined **only** at `grid/theme/grid.css:334`. Promoting it without replacing that class ships a confirmation whose section headings lose their weight on any surface that does not load the grid theme.
**C10 — `.d.ts` freshness is a gate.** `scripts/check-ds-dts-fresh.mjs` exists and the tree tracks `registry.d.ts`, `Menu.d.ts`, `ActionConfirm.d.ts`, `menuAdapters.d.ts`. Every type addition here trips it. Also: `Menu.d.ts` is now **fresh** (it carries `title` and `description`), so the adapter's own "⚠ the declaration is stale" note at :63-74 is itself stale and should be corrected in the same pass.
**C11 — the refusal-masking claim is right, under-cited and over-generalised.** It bites only when a lane pairs `unavailable` with a non-`none` level, which is the case `validateImpact` was written to catch. `familyActions.ts:447-451` and `:455` pair it with `level:'none'` and reach the honest rule-4 sentence. **Two live studio sites do mask**: `channelActions.ts:224-250` (`offerVerb`, `level:'confirm'` + `unavailable: "Refused for now: N of these are live listings…"`) and `:330-346` (broadcast, `level:'type-to-confirm'` + `unavailable: "Refused for now: broadcasting would write to live listings…"`). Both render *"…was refused: preflight failed but still asked for a confirmation — a verb must not run on a guess"* instead of their own sentence.
**C12 — there is no shipped coordinate-shaped phrase.** `channelActions.ts:343` is `confirmPhrase: deps.channel` — the channel alone. `subject.kind:'coordinate'` is new work, not an existing shape.
**C13 — the focus question is settleable from source.** React is `^18.2.0` (apps/web/package.json:35), where `autoFocus` is a commit-phase `.focus()` and renders **no** `autofocus` attribute. Modal's own pass is guarded by `if (dialog && !dialog.contains(document.activeElement))` (Modal.tsx:93), so once React has focused the Input the Modal does nothing. Focus lands on the Input today. Derived from source, not measured on screen — but the fix needs no new API: remove `autoFocus`, put `data-autofocus` on Cancel, which Modal.tsx:93 already honours.
**C14 — counts.** `window.confirm(` = **57** in `apps/web/src` (not 59), plus **28** `window.alert(`/`window.prompt(`. `ui/ConfirmDialog|ui/ConfirmProvider` importers = **83** files; `useConfirm` appears in **85** (not 81).
**C15 — the partition does not reach CH.1's overflow.** `actionMenuItems`/`actionContextMenu` are consumed only by row menus (FamilyVariants.tsx:329,333,336; useVariantRowMenu.tsx:18; ChannelSheet.tsx:995,1042; MasterSheet.tsx:484,494). SheetToolbar's `overflow` is hand-built and already inserts its own `separator` before Reload (SheetToolbar.tsx:255-256).
**C16 — a defect inside CH.1's own overflow.** SheetToolbar.tsx:254 force-disables every scope verb when `blocked`: `blocked ? { ...item, disabled: true } : item`, **with no reason**, while :239 renders `absent` entries *with* one. That is the silent-disabled trap in the one surface CH.1 designates for lifecycle verbs. Added as a one-line W1 fix, not a ruling change.
**C17 — the sole reader of `danger` has the silent-disabled bug.** `drawer/RecordActions.tsx:66` is the only consumer; `:71` puts the availability reason in `title` on a **`disabled`** Button, which fires no pointer events in Chrome — exactly the doctrine `.githooks/pre-push:185-195` ratchets.
**C18 — contrast, recomputed independently.** All the draft's numbers hold: `--nds-danger` #e5484d = **3.91** light / **3.89** dark; `--nds-danger-text` = **7.36** / **7.14**; on `--nds-danger-soft` = **6.27** / **7.23**; `--nds-warning` #b87503 = **3.76** / **4.05**; `--nds-warning-text` = **8.87** / **8.30**.
**C19 — `check-button-vocabulary.mjs` exists and cannot see the eBay pair.** It counts *excess* idioms (Σ idioms − 1), so a dialog whose two buttons are both variant-less scores 0 and passes clean. Good evidence for a new gate rather than an extension of that one.
**C20 — schema NONE is correct.** Verified: nothing here declares a Prisma field.
---
## 1. The one idea
**Severity is derived from typed facts, never authored.** `ActionConfirm.tsx:30-31` already refuses to decide severity. The missing half is that nothing decides it *from anything checkable* either — a lane picks `level` by hand, and every lie the audit found is a hand-written sentence: "Unpublish (recommended) … so you can republish later" over a hard SP-API delete (`app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx:1097` region, `products.hardDelete.channelAction.unpublish.body`, `channel-delist.service.ts:150-166`).
I am not re-litigating DX-5's REFUTED verdict. The verifier is right that the harm was asserted about verbs that do not exist and that `consequences` is the designated home for the sentence today (`familyActions.ts:466`). The argument here is different: *a fact stated only in prose cannot be validated.*
```ts
// registry.ts — additions only, every field optional
type Reach = 'local' | 'local-destructive' | 'channel'
type Fidelity = 'exact' | 'lossy' | 'none'
interface GridAction<T> { …; reach?: Reach }   // grouping + availability, pre-preflight
interface ActionImpact {
  …
  reach?: Reach
  reversal?: { verb: string; actionId?: string; fidelity: Fidelity; note?: string }
  subject?: { kind: 'sku' | 'external-id' | 'coordinate'; value: string }
  acknowledge?: string
  refusal?: { reason: string; whatWouldFix?: string; verb?: { label: string; actionId: string } }
}
```
`reach` sits on both: on `GridAction` because grouping and availability are decided before any preflight runs, on `ActionImpact` because a gate downgrades it — `channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:323-332` already refuses to answer `live` for a channel the server did not describe, and :336-373 has the honest sentence for every other mode.
Two pure functions in `registry.ts`, both tested: `frictionFor(i): ConfirmLevel` (the **minimum**) and `reversalSentence(i): string` (rendered by the DS, never authored).
| reach \ fidelity | exact | lossy | none |
|---|---|---|---|
| `local` | `none` | `confirm` | `confirm` + acknowledge |
| `local-destructive` | `confirm` | `confirm` + acknowledge | `type-to-confirm` |
| `channel` | `confirm` | `confirm` + acknowledge | `type-to-confirm` |
`validateImpact` (today two problems at registry.ts:264-274) gains three:
4. `level` weaker than `frictionFor(impact)` → refuse. A lane may escalate, never soften.
5. `reach !== 'local'` or `fidelity !== 'exact'` with no `reversal` → refuse.
6. `confirmPhrase` matching `/^(delete|remove|confirm|yes|ok|proceed)$/i` → refuse. This makes registry.ts:117 ("A SKU, never \"DELETE\"") enforceable instead of hortatory, and it is the rule that convicts `app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx:1129`.
`reach` on `GridAction` cannot reach `validateImpact`, which takes only the impact (:264), so a sibling `validateAction(action)` refuses a `reach:'channel'` verb declaring no `preflight`.
## 2. The refusal channel — the one fix with two live victims
`runAction.ts:70-76` applies rule 3 (validation) before rule 4 (`unavailable`). Where a lane pairs `unavailable` with a non-`none` level, the generic validator message masks the lane's own sentence. Both studio channel verbs do exactly this (C11). Fix: check `impact.refusal` **first** and return `{kind:'refused', problem: refusal.reason, refusal}`; keep `unavailable` for its narrow meaning ("the preflight could not complete"), and have `validateImpact`'s rule 3 fire only when neither is set. `useActionPress` renders `refusal.reason` in `Banner tone="danger"` with `refusal.verb` as the action — which turns `familyActions.ts:454` ("Resolve that listing before deleting its local record") into a sentence with a button.
## 3. The ladder — five rungs, each with a shipped precedent
**R0 · Run, then offer the way back.** `level:'none'`; on success a `Banner` with `action={<Button>{reversal.verb}</Button>}` (Banner.tsx:26, :50). Never a Toast — no action slot, no dismiss, `role="status"` (Toast.tsx:46-51). Precedent: `images/amazon/AmazonMediaWorkspace.tsx:278-279`.
**R1 · Acknowledge in place, once per coordinate.** Not a modal. For a widening of *reach* rather than a destruction, the write is held and a `Banner tone="warning"` carries the choices as buttons; declining reverts the cell. This ships at `ChannelSheet.tsx:1859-1888` with the ask-once reset at :670-674 (which keys on channel/marketplace/accountId/locale and, note, **not** aliasKey). Promote as `patterns/ReachAcknowledgement`.
**R2 · Confirm.** The existing dialog.
**R3 · Confirm + acknowledge.** `impact.acknowledge` adds a required `Checkbox` (`primitives/Checkbox.tsx`, exported at primitives/index.ts:17). The mechanism exists at `drawer/DrawerConfirm.tsx:191` and is the one capability keeping a second confirmation implementation alive in the studio — which is itself a page-local hand-roll of a DS control.
**R4 · Type the identifier.** Reserved for `fidelity:'none'`, one coordinate at a time. `docs/pes-claims.md:26808-26812` already rules that "one SKU authorising six deletions is a weaker gate than the one approved". A plural R4 is refused with `refusal.whatWouldFix` naming the per-coordinate verb.
## 4. Type-to-confirm, done properly
`ActionConfirm.tsx:134-152` is already 80% right: the phrase is in a full-contrast label, the button says only "Confirm" (hub ruling #133, the measured 2.60:1), and there is no placeholder. Four remaining failures:
1. **The phrase is derived, not chosen.** Computed from `subject`: `sku` locally, `external-id` (the real ASIN/ItemID) for anything reaching a channel, `coordinate` for a scope-wide verb. A phrase that is not the subject's own value is a lane inventing a password. (No coordinate-shaped phrase ships today — C12.)
2. **Matching stays exact and case-sensitive** (:81). Add `autoCapitalize="off"` and `autoCorrect="off"` only (C3). `app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx:1129`'s `.toUpperCase()` fold and :1345's placeholder go.
3. **Focus starts on Cancel at R4.** Remove `autoFocus` from the Input (:147) and put `data-autofocus` on the Cancel `Button` (:91) — Modal.tsx:93 already honours it (C13).
4. **The button must be visibly focusable.** Add `.nds-btn:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--nds-primary); outline-offset: 1px }` — the 11-site idiom in the same file — because the `border-color` half is dead on `.primary`, `.danger`, `.danger-outline` and `.on` (C2).
Plus the two AA fixes in the DS's own destructive dialog: `.nds-grid-confirm-error` → `--nds-danger-text` (3.91/3.89 → 7.36/7.14) and `.nds-grid-confirm-warn` → `--nds-warning-text` (3.76/4.05 → 8.87/8.30). The correct tokens are already in use one line above at grid.css:1023.
## 5. The danger treatment that survives both adapters
**DS Menu.** `tone?: Tone` on `MenuItemDef` (`primitives/tone.ts`), rendered as `data-tone` — the idiom at components.css:1991, :2029-2030, :2105-2106 (those use `--nds-danger-strong`; the menu uses `--nds-danger-text` for the higher ratio). Two rules:
```css
.nds-menu button[data-tone='danger'] { color: var(--nds-danger-text); }          /* 7.36 / 7.14 */
.nds-menu button[data-tone='danger']:hover { background: var(--nds-danger-soft); } /* 6.27 / 7.23 */
```
⚠ Both have specificity (0,2,1), the same as `.nds-menu button:disabled` (components.css:748-751), which is **later** in the file — so a disabled danger item correctly keeps `--nds-text-2` and does not shout in red. State that; do not let it be discovered.
**AG context menu.** `MenuItemLeafDef` carries `cssClasses?: string[]` (verified at `ag-grid-community/dist/types/src/interfaces/menuItem.d.ts:21`), so `actionContextMenu` emits `cssClasses:['nds-menu-danger']` with the same colour rule in the grid theme. Whether AG actually applies it is unmeasured.
**Both adapters partition:** non-danger in declaration order, a `separator`, then danger in declaration order. Deterministic total order, not a reshuffle. In AG this yields `[…verbs, separator, …danger, separator, clipboard]` — one more rule than today (menuAdapters.tsx:170). This changes `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:33` and `:93`, whose fixture marks `delete` as danger at :16 and whose asserted intent is *which* verbs are offered and *that* the clipboard sits below a rule; `registry.vitest.test.ts:88` is about `actionsFor` and stays green.
**Disabled reasons: add `description`, keep `title`** (C7). `FamilyBar` already does this and stays as it is (C6).
## 6. One confirmation surface
Move `ActionConfirm.tsx` to `design-system/components/`, export `useActionConfirm`/`ActionImpact`/`runAction` from `components/index.ts` (53 exports today, zero matching confirm/dialog/alert), re-export unchanged from `grid/index.ts:48-58`. Move its CSS from `grid/theme/grid.css:1026-1039` to `styles/components.css` as `.nds-confirm-*`, applying the two contrast fixes in the move, **and replace `nds-cell-strong` with a `.nds-confirm-h` defined there** (C9). This is not speculative generalisation: `ChannelSheet.tsx:585` and `:606` already call `.ask()` with hand-built literals for non-`GridAction` decisions.
It then absorbs `DrawerConfirm`'s two extra capabilities — `acknowledge` (:191) and the capture-phase Esc/Tab ownership (:152-188) — as an `inline` render mode, so the drawer keeps its in-panel card without a second implementation. What it can then replace: the five studio dialogs at the corrected paths (C5), the two studio `window.confirm` calls (`variants/family/ManageAxesDialog.tsx:81`, raised from inside a DS Modal that has already taken the focus trap; `contracts.tsx:607`, which renders in the browser's language), and eventually the Tailwind `ConfirmDialog`/`ConfirmProvider` stack (83 importers) and the remaining `window.confirm` sites (57). That last migration is out of scope here, but the DS surface must be able to receive it or the product keeps two confirm stacks forever.
## 7. Gates and the lab
- **`scripts/check-destructive-pattern.mjs`** — AST, `--check`, ratchet, wired into `.githooks/pre-push` beside `check-silent-disabled` (:190-195). Five assertions: no `window.confirm|alert|prompt` outside a named allow-list; no JSX `placeholder` on an input whose sibling label contains the confirm phrase; every `<Modal>` whose footer holds a `variant="danger"|"danger-outline"` button is `ActionConfirm` or baselined; every `level:'type-to-confirm'` literal carries a `subject`; **and every footer with ≥2 buttons has ≥2 distinct variants** — the eBay pair passes `check-button-vocabulary.mjs` clean because that gate counts *excess* idioms (C19).
- **`registry.vitest.test.ts`** — the `frictionFor` truth table (12 cases) and the softening refusal.
- **`menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts`** — `danger:true` → `tone:'danger'` in the DS adapter and `cssClasses` in AG's; the partition order; a reason present in *both* `title` and `description`.
- **`scripts/check-ds-dts-fresh.mjs`** must be re-run: four `.d.ts` files change (C10), and the stale-declaration comment at menuAdapters.tsx:63-74 is corrected in the same pass.
- **Grid lab** — extend `#action-confirm` (`/design/grid-lab?tab=gds`) with the five rungs plus a malformed impact per new rule. `docs/pes-claims.md:26828-26843` records that *looking at* that scenario found three defects every test had passed.
## 8. Waves
**W1 — types and honesty; nothing new becomes reachable.** The type additions and their `.d.ts`; `frictionFor`; the three `validateImpact` rules; `reversalSentence`; the refusal channel and the two live studio verbs it unmasks (C11); the two contrast fixes; the focus outline; `MenuItemDef.tone` + both adapters + the partition; `description` alongside `title`; SheetToolbar.tsx:254's reasonless force-disable (C16); RecordActions.tsx:71's unreachable tooltip (C17). Every existing verb declares `reach:'local'` and its real `reversal`; no verb changes level, because none currently under-asks on this table.
**W2 —** `ActionConfirm` promoted with `.nds-confirm-h`; `acknowledge`; the inline mode; the five studio dialogs and two `window.confirm` calls retired; `ReachAcknowledgement` promoted.
**W3 — prerequisite for any lifecycle verb.** R4 wired to the real external identifier and to the operational preflight at `products-catalog.routes.ts:1666-1760`; `app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx`'s hand-rolled dialog replaced.
## 9. What I could not establish
Everything geometric. `check-layout-v2`, `check-control-census`, `check-editor-open` and `check-grid-chrome` drive Playwright against `localhost:3000`, which the brief forbids, so no claim here rests on a rendered pixel: the menu width and height with `description` on every disabled item, the partition's effect on menu height, and whether AG's `cssClasses` actually paints. `scripts/check-contrast.mjs` prints its pass over hardcoded RGB triples for the *legacy* vocabulary (:33-46 — its `danger-strong` is `[190,18,60]`, not `--nds-danger-strong` `#a3211a`), so it is vacuous for this work; every ratio above is computed independently from `tokens.css`. The React-18 focus ordering (C13) is derived from source and React's documented `autoFocus` semantics, not measured on screen — the spec makes the outcome deterministic either way.

#### Say it plain — one presence vocabulary, two axes, one as-of

The studio states listing state in three vocabularies that contradict each other, and every contradiction is an authored sentence. This is the honesty layer over the agreed Presence model: one canonical operator vocabulary with a per-channel native mapping and a scoped ban list; two never-merged marks — a declared intent and a channel-observed fact, the fact always carrying an as-of; a six-valued fact axis so "never checked", "no listing here" and "could not ask" are three different sentences and none is green; explicit renders for a revoked grant, an expired grant, a failed discovery read, a suspended market and a binned product, none of which may make a channel vanish; and one pure meta module plus a scoped parity gate so the descriptor, the drawer, the footer and a confirmation cannot disagree. Corrected: the marks do NOT go on the alias band. Wave 1 is copy, wire fields and one shared derive — no channel-reaching verb.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- NEW — apps/web/src/design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts. Pure, node-testable, beside readiness.ts: PresenceIntent / ChannelFact / PresenceVerdict unions, intentMeta(), factMeta(), presenceVerdict(), presenceLine(), and DERIVED member lists. It imports ReadinessTone from readiness.ts and nothing else — 'imports nothing' is not achievable and not the invariant that matters (no React, no JSX, so apps/web's node-only vitest can reach it). No converter to the readiness vocabularies, mirroring readiness.ts:22-25. Five vitests, the load-bearing one being: no input may yield a success tone while fact === 'UNKNOWN'.
- NEW — PresenceMark, exported from the IdentityBand.tsx family. Two Pills plus a <time>, rendered from presenceLine(). role='img' + aria-label like CompletenessPill (IdentityBand.tsx:146-147) and explicitly NOT aria-hidden like ProvenanceMark (provenanceMark.tsx:93). CORRECTED SCOPE: its wave-1 consumers are the DRAWER and the toolbar descriptor, not the alias band — AliasBandCell.tsx:141-143 records the Owner's 2026-09-05 removal of listing status from that slot, and bandWidth.ts's findKeyBearingBand means a pill added there cannot widen the column, only compress the alias label.
- FIX — CompletenessPill returns null on a null percent (IdentityBand.tsx:137), which drops the whole 'nothing to measure' sentence built at AliasBandCell.tsx:71-78 on any single-alias product. It must render a neutral em-dash pill carrying tip as title/aria-label. No signature change; no caller regresses because a blank was never the intended output. It is also a WIDTH change of the exact class bandWidth.ts records as #742 (65.2 → 70 against a stale constant of 66, five SKUs truncated silently), so the band derivation must be re-run with it.
- MOVE — ago()/when() are studio-local at _studio/drawer/format.ts:11,31. Presence's as-of is printed by the descriptor, the drawer, a roll-up and a confirmation; four copies of a timestamp formatter is the drift that file's own header (:3-7) says it exists to prevent. They belong in design-system/grid/renderers/format.ts beside EMPTY_DASH (:85).
- COPY — readiness.ts:53 and :56 are the two lying strings, and they are DS-owned. `live` → label 'Listed', tone 'info' (.nds-readypill-info exists at design-system/grid/theme/grid.css:419 — note the path, NOT design-system/styles/ — and it reaches the band because readyPillTone at readiness.ts:103 delegates to readinessMeta). `unlisted` → label 'No listing here', hint 'We hold no listing record for this coordinate. Nothing has been checked against the channel.' Measured reach: 26 files reference readinessMeta (13 app files, the rest DS internals, .d.ts and tests) — the gate derives this, the design does not assert a number.
- GAP, already filed and still open — MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-49) carries no tone, no severity and no group key; separator is the only grouping primitive; both adapters drop GridAction.danger (menuAdapters.tsx: 0 hits against a positive control of 5 for 'label'; actionMenuItems at :75-91 maps only {id,label,title,disabled,onSelect}); there is no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet. GridAction.danger's own doc at registry.ts:183-184 promises the opposite. Filed at .claude/DS-GAPS.md:329-348, whose recitation of MenuItemDef is now STALE (title and description have since landed). This design does not need it in wave 1 — 'Check this listing' is a read — but any channel-reaching verb in the sheet overflow does.
- NOT EXTENDED, deliberately — ScopeBarItem has no way to express 'usable but degraded'. CORRECTED REASON: not 'eight consumers outside the studio' (measured: the DS ScopeBar has exactly two component importers, StudioBar.tsx:7 and design/language-axis/LanguageAxisClient.tsx:34; every marketing/ hit is PlacementScopeBar.tsx, a PARKED, NOT-MOUNTED page-local fork that does not import it). The reason is the bar's own rule: 'the bar must never grow a second control for a fact a chip already states… Two controls for one fact is what sank the reverted ads scope bar' (ScopeBar.tsx, `right?` doc). Connection health is stated in the account Listbox option and one GridSheetNote instead.
- NOT ADDED, deliberately — GridSheetNote gains no 'connection' kind; kind='provenance' (warning ink, 'the read is not what it appears') already describes a degraded grant and an unreadable discovery response. And it gains no action slot: MessageNote has none, and the shipped pattern puts the Button BESIDE the note in the .nds-grid-sheet-noteslot span (SheetFooterNote.tsx:50-64), not inside children.

##### API
- EXTEND GET /api/products/:id/studio/sheet — StudioRow.listing gains offerClosedAt/offerClosedBy/offerCloseReason, syncPaused, channelFact/channelFactAt/channelFactVia, presenceIntent (+ its provenance flag); StudioRow.presence carries the derived block; AliasGroup gains the same plus a roll-up. Purely additive. Both client type mirrors (sheet/channel/types.ts and drawer/types.ts) import the unions from presence.ts instead of restating them — RowReadinessState is restated in four places today, which is a set claim that goes stale.
- EXTEND the sheet read's liveness verdict via ONE shared helper in packages/shared (reachable from both apps — apps/web already imports @nexus/shared/products-grid), called by BOTH row builders: studio-sheet.service.ts:1483-1487 and sheet-rows.service.ts:270 (NOT :271). The `live` branch is gated on !TERMINAL.has(listingStatus). Asserted by a parity test — a master-only fix here is a silent channel gap.
- EXTEND GET /api/connections?all=true CONSUMPTION — no server change needed: connections.routes.ts:79-111 already returns authStatus (:95), grantedScopes, scopeDrift (:98-101), token expiries, lastError/lastErrorAt (:108-109) and consecutiveFailures (:110). studio-data.ts:112-113 must stop discarding them: MarketplaceLite['accounts'] gains health: 'ok'|'degraded'|'needs_reauth'|'revoked' and healthNote. AND studio-data.ts:116 must stop stamping connected:false from an empty set when the read FAILED — tri-state, so scopes.ts:116 keeps the channel instead of deleting it from the bar.
- EXTEND GET /api/marketplaces/grouped CONSUMPTION — project Marketplace.isParticipating and participationStatus (schema.prisma:1973-1975) into MarketplaceLite. Other surfaces already honour them; the studio throws them away.
- EXTEND GET /api/products/:id CONSUMPTION — StudioProduct gains deletedAt, already in the response body and dropped at studio-data.ts:40-58, so a binned product renders as a bin. Paired with useMasterSheet.ts:188-190, which must read the 404 BODY's code (`unknown_product`, defined at studio-sheet.service.ts:427) rather than treating every 404 as 'the studio route is not deployed'. CORRECTION: there is no `error === 'unknown_product'` check in the web tree today — the check is on HTTP status alone.
- NEW, wave 1, READ ONLY — POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/verify { coordinates[], reason }. Reads the channel (getListingsItem / getItemListingStatus, both already in the codebase) and writes ONLY the channelFact* columns. Never a write to a listing. Its verdict is four-valued and 'could-not-ask' is load-bearing — REFUSED is split from ABSENT because channel-delist.service.ts:227,231 folds 'item cannot be accessed' / 'invalid item' into ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS and returns {success:true} at :290-293. It extends the operator-triggered read path ChannelTruthPanel + POST /live-channel-images/refresh already established. MUST carry a per-call coordinate cap and an hourly ceiling: the roll-up invites 'check them all' and Amazon reports run a serial queue.
- EXTEND GET /api/products/:id/sync-queue — a `sources: { rejections: number|null, suppressions: number|null, validation: number|null }` block beside the existing counts, null meaning 'not counted', so ErrorsSyncConsole.tsx:375 (NOT :373) can stop making an unconditional measurement-shaped claim ('nothing recorded on this coordinate yet') about three tables it never queries — ListingIssue, AmazonSuppression, ChannelListing.validationStatus.
- AUTHORISATION — the verify route carries an explicit in-file preHandler naming its permission. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (apps/api/src/lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:8-12,30-32: 'resolve + LOG what would be denied, but allow the request'), so an in-file guard is the only one that binds regardless of the mode flip. Note the file lives at lib/auth/, not hooks/.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.channelFact String? / channelFactAt DateTime? / channelFactVia String? / channelFactDetail Json?. The observation and its as-of, deliberately distinct from lastSyncedAt, whose own header (studio-sheet.service.ts:1415-1420) warns it records when a sync last RAN. Null = UNKNOWN. NO BACKFILL, and this is the arm that must not be optimised away: ebay-flat-file.routes.ts already writes {listingStatus:'ENDED', offerActive:false, externalListingId:null}, and a naive derivation reads that as 'never published'.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntent String? plus presenceIntentAt / presenceIntentBy / presenceIntentReason. CORRECTION TO THE AGREED SPINE: the enum member is LISTED, not LIVE — NONE|DRAFT|LISTED|HELD|WITHDRAWN|ENDED|DISCONTINUED|RELEASED. The spine's column comment says LIVE, the renderer union says LISTED, and one of them has to move; it must be the stored token, or the raw value leaks the exact word §2 retires (and ListingsPane.tsx:103 prints raw tokens today). Nullable and never backfilled: null means 'never stated', and the read derives a provisional intent from the legacy columns and carries it on the wire WITH its provenance, so no surface can present a derivation as an operator decision.
- NO CHANGE NEEDED for the biggest single fix — offerClosedAt, offerClosedBy, offerCloseReason (schema.prisma:1593-1595, NOT 1591-1594) and syncPaused (:1651, NOT :1649) already exist and are already written (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:177-195). They are simply not selected: LISTING_SELECT at studio-sheet.service.ts:464-483 omits all four. That is a projection change, not a migration.
- NO CHANGE, but STATE IT — offerActive IS already selected (studio-sheet.service.ts:472) and IS on the wire, coerced at :1414 as `offerActive: listingRow.offerActive !== false`. That is a SERVER-SIDE wire-null default — null becomes true — of exactly the class check-wire-null-defaults.mjs catches on the client, and it is the third off-switch (Amazon-only, applied at the next flat-file submit via flat-file.service.ts's skip_offer). It must render as its own word, not as a synonym for Held or Offer withdrawn.
- NO CHANGE — listingStatus stays the free String it is at schema.prisma:1617 (NOT :1615). Its comment names five values (DRAFT, ACTIVE, INACTIVE, ENDED, ERROR) while the code writes at least ten. Constraining it is separate, breaking work; this design stops RENDERING it raw (ListingsPane.tsx:103) and maps every value through factMeta/intentMeta with an explicit unrecognised-token fallback, the way readinessMeta:82-85 already handles a contract change.
- NO CHANGE — Marketplace.isParticipating (:1973) and participationStatus (:1975) already exist and are already honoured by other surfaces. The studio simply drops them at the loader. Projection change, not a migration.
- SEQUENCING (binding, not optional) — apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma. apps/api/.env pointed at a local Docker Postgres on 09-11 while both databases held 338 products; only Product.version (54 vs 51) separated them. Discriminate, never infer. Related: family-projection.service.ts:389 cites a migration `20260911a_vp2_variation_excluded` that DOES NOT EXIST in packages/database/prisma/migrations (checked: only 20260911020000_information_formula_destination, 20260911_category_taxonomies and five 20260912_lx* folders) — a comment is a claim, not a measurement, and declaring a column the database may not have is an outage for every concurrent session.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, recited verbatim at _studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx:14-18) says a scope's own verbs are items of the one ⋯ overflow and never a button. Evidence that lifecycle strains it: the prop is `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` (:104) and MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-49) carries no tone, no severity and no group; both adapters drop GridAction.danger (0 hits in menuAdapters.tsx against a control of 5 for 'label'); there is no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet, while GridAction.danger's own doc at registry.ts:183-184 promises destructive verbs are 'grouped apart and rendered with danger tone by every adapter'. Under CH.1 as written, 'End the listing on Amazon IT' would render byte-identical to 'Reload'. Also note CH.1's `trailing` rule ('Transient STATUS only… Never a control', :105-106) is ALREADY violated in shipped code: ChannelSheet.tsx:1838 passes SchemaStatus, which renders a <Button> (SchemaStatus.tsx:32). THIS PROPOSAL DOES NOT NEED CH.1 TO BEND — 'Check this listing' is a read, and it is an overflow item with a shipped precedent (ChannelProjection.tsx:399-406). The smallest amendment I can see, if the Owner ever wants a destructive verb in the sheet overflow, keeps CH.1's actual rule intact (no new buttons) and asks only that the overflow gain a typed tone plus a separator group. `separator` already exists and a grouping precedent already ships in one adapter. Does CH.1 stand as written?
- THE ALIAS BAND — the one placement I reversed, and the Owner should confirm the reversal. The submitted design put two presence pills in AliasBandCell's trailing slot. I moved them out, because that slot is the subject of a recorded decision: 'The listing status left the band for the pill's tooltip — the scope tab already says Amazon · Blocked 71% and the publish control carries the verdict' (AliasBandCell.tsx:141-143), taken on 2026-09-05 from two screenshots to remove five measured differences between master's parent row and the channel's. Master has no listing, so a presence mark there is a channel-only slot and reopens exactly that divergence. It is also geometrically unsafe: the band is designed for 240px in the pinned section (AliasBandCell.tsx:12-18) and bandWidth.ts's findKeyBearingBand deliberately derives the column from the VARIANT row, so a third pill on the level-0 band cannot widen anything — it compresses the alias label, the #742 failure that file records. Presence now lives in the toolbar descriptor, the drawer pane and the band's hover title. Does the Owner want it visible on the band anyway, accepting the master/channel asymmetry and a measured re-derivation of the trail?
- THE ROLL-UP'S HOME. I put the coordinate roll-up ('Amazon · IT — 3 selling, 1 ended, 1 not checked') in the sheet toolbar's `descriptor` (ChannelSheet.tsx:1793), which rides inside `count` and adds no control, so CH.1 is untouched. The alternative is the scope chip, which I did NOT extend: ScopeBar's own rule is 'the bar must never grow a second control for a fact a chip already states'. Correcting my input: ScopeBar has only two component consumers (StudioBar.tsx:7 and design/language-axis/LanguageAxisClient.tsx:34) — the marketing/ 'consumers' are a PARKED, unmounted fork — so the blast radius of extending it is much smaller than the submitted design claimed. The decision therefore rests on the chip's one-verdict rule, not on cost. Does the Owner want presence on the chip after all?
- 'CHECK THIS LISTING' — HOW MUCH, HOW OFTEN. The verb reads the channel and writes only channelFact*. It is not a new live-read seam: ChannelTruthPanel + POST /live-channel-images/refresh already establish the operator-triggered read. But a roll-up saying '1 not checked' invites 'check them all', and Amazon reports run a serial queue. Does the Owner want a per-call coordinate cap and an hourly ceiling in wave 1, or is one coordinate per click enough to start?
- THE BAN LIST'S EDGES. 'Deactivate' is banned as prose about a listing but ships as a DATA VALUE an operator types in the eBay flat file (ebay-columns.ts:159), which is untouchable. 'Archive' is banned of a listing but is a real shipped alias verb (archiveAlias) and a DS Pill tone ('Archived→neutral', Pill.tsx:5). 'Unpublish' appears under listings.bulk.* on a surface outside the studio. I scoped the gate to listing/lifecycle key prefixes plus _studio/**, excluding flat-file enums, and made it a ratchet rather than a zero. Does the Owner want the ban extended beyond the studio to listings.bulk.* and products.hardDelete.* in the same wave, or is the studio the boundary?
- THE 'RECOMMENDED' DEFAULT ON A DESTRUCTIVE RADIO. BulkActionBar.tsx:1097 defaults channelAction to 'unpublish', labelled 'Unpublish (recommended)' (en.json:5222), whose body makes three false promises (:5223). That control is on /products, not in the studio. I am fixing the STRING in wave 1. Does the Owner also want the DEFAULT changed to 'none' (local-only) in the same edit — a behaviour change on a shipped destructive control, outside this lane's surface — or does the string fix ship alone?
- ETSY. modeForChannel (channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:323-332) returns null for Etsy and for WooCommerce, and Nexus holds a read-only Etsy client (etsy/read-client.ts:5). Wave 1 states plainly that Etsy presence is READ-ONLY and 'Check this listing' is disabled with that reason, so Etsy is not silently caught by the WooCommerce containment rule. Confirming that Etsy is in scope as a READ surface and out of scope as a verb surface.
- THE RED SUITE. layers.vitest.test.ts:55 is red on `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot` — presence is already leaking into the provenance axis before presence exists — and masterWrite.vitest.test.ts has five failures. I put the channelSnapshot mapping in wave 1. Does the Owner want the masterWrite five fixed as a wave-1 precondition too (they are another lane's), or does this copy layer ship over them with the reds recorded?

##### Risks
- 🔴 THE BAND DECISION. The submitted design put presence marks in AliasBandCell's trailing slot. That reverses a recorded 2026-09-05 decision, written into the file it changed (AliasBandCell.tsx:141-143: 'The listing status left the band for the pill's tooltip'), taken to remove five measured differences between master's parent row and the channel's. I moved presence off the band. If the Owner wants it back there, it is a ruling change and a geometry change, not an implementation detail.
- 🔴 THE TREE IS NOT GREEN, AND PHASE 0 COULD NOT MEASURE IT. tsc-web.txt, tsc2.txt and tsc-listing.txt are all 0 bytes — 'could not measure', not 'measured clean'. I ran it: apps/web tsc --noEmit exits 2 (products/next/TranslateDialog.tsx:65, replaceAll/lib target, pre-existing, outside the studio); apps/api exits with one error (bulk-action.service.ts:1591). Studio vitest: layers.vitest.test.ts:55 red on `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot`, plus 5 failures in masterWrite.vitest.test.ts. Shipping copy over a red suite means the next failure cannot be attributed.
- 🔴 THE GATE AS ORIGINALLY WRITTEN WOULD HAVE FAILED FALSELY. 'Zero banned words in en.json/it.json' convicts nine keys about other objects (stock.locations.deactivate*, pricing.rules.*, drafts.archive*), and 'deactivate' is a data value in ebay-columns.ts:159 inside an untouchable flat-file editor. A gate's RED is a claim too: rescoped by key prefix and ratcheted against a recorded baseline, with a positive control in the same run.
- The footer can show ONE note (SheetFooterNote early-returns at :67-83). Without the priority ladder specified here, a connection-health note would silence the write-refusal note, which is strictly more urgent. The ladder is a design decision an implementer could quietly get wrong.
- Retiring 'Live' touches sixteen-plus surfaces and two locale files at once. If the copy lands and the wire fields do not, operators get a new vocabulary describing the same three lies. The two must land in one write (producer + consumer together).
- Most coordinates will read 'not checked' on day one where today they read a green 'Live' (877 of 977 listings have a null lastSyncedAt, docs/pes-claims.md:14081). This is a visible downgrade in apparent confidence and will read as a regression to anyone who was not told.
- channelFact* must NEVER be backfilled. ebay-flat-file.routes.ts already writes {listingStatus:'ENDED', offerActive:false, externalListingId:null}, and a naive derivation reads those as 'never published' — turning a deliberate end into a Draft. Null means UNKNOWN, and only UNKNOWN.
- POST /presence/verify spends a real SP-API/Trading call per coordinate. Amazon reports run a serial queue and the studio's roll-up invites 'check them all'. Unbounded, this is a rate-limit incident wearing an honesty feature; wave 1 must cap the coordinate count per call and per hour.
- isLiveOnChannel is pinned by listingRisk.vitest.test.ts against a measured family of 20 non-ACTIVE eBay rows holding real ItemIDs. Nothing here changes it. An implementer working in the adjacent lines could 'tidy' it into the new sellingRisk predicate and silently make a live listing deletable.
- This design closes the SENTENCES. It does not close the WRITES. Nine upstream preconditions in the agreed spine (P1–P9) remain open, and a page that states presence honestly over a push path that still resurrects a closed offer (pricing-outbound.service.ts:140-149) is honest about the wrong instant.

##### The specification
# Say it plain — one presence vocabulary, two axes, one as-of
## CORRECTIONS to the proposal I was given
**Structural — one placement would reverse a recorded decision.**
1. **The presence marks must NOT go on the alias band.** The submitted design put `PresenceMark` in `AliasBandCell`'s `trailing` slot beside `CompletenessPill`. That slot is the subject of a recorded Owner decision of 2026-09-05, written into the file it changed: *"The listing status left the band for the pill's tooltip — the scope tab already says 'Amazon · Blocked 71%' and the publish control carries the verdict"* (`AliasBandCell.tsx:141-143`), taken because the two scopes' parent rows had drifted — *"master drew P · thumbnail · GALE-JACKET · 20% · ⋯ and this scope drew P · ★ · 'Primary' · '20 variants' · ACTIVE · 85% — five differences on one row, none a decision the Owner had made"* (`:132-139`). Master has no listing, so a presence mark on the band is a channel-only slot: it re-opens precisely the divergence #710 exists to remove. It is also geometrically unsafe — the band is *designed* for 240px in the pinned section and does not grow (`AliasBandCell.tsx:12-18`), and the column derivation reads the **variant** row, not this one (`bandWidth.ts` `findKeyBearingBand`, which selects `.ag-row:not(.ag-row-level-0)` because *"a channel scope draws an alias BAND row … whose trail carries a status pill plus readiness (130px) where a variant's carries readiness and the ⋯ (104px)"*). A third pill there cannot widen the column; it compresses the alias label. That is exactly the #742 regression the same file records (`CompletenessPill` 65.2 → 70 against a constant of 66, five SKUs truncated silently in the edit that caused it). **Corrected: presence renders in the toolbar descriptor, the drawer pane and the band's hover title — the three places that already carry it — and whether it returns to the band as a visible mark is an Owner question, not mine.**
**Facts I corrected, each re-measured.**
2. **ScopeBar is not "shared with eight surfaces outside the studio."** Measured (`/usr/bin/grep -rn "import .*ScopeBar"`): the DS component is imported by exactly two files — `StudioBar.tsx:7` and `design/language-axis/LanguageAxisClient.tsx:34` — plus a type-only import at `_studio/types.ts:15` and `TokenCatalog.tsx`. Every `marketing/` hit is `PlacementScopeBar.tsx`, a page-local fork whose own header says **"⛔ PARKED 2026-08-17 — NOT MOUNTED"** (`:4-6`) and which does not import the DS bar at all. The decision not to extend ScopeBar stands, on the bar's own reason instead: *"the bar must never grow a second control for a fact a chip already states… Two controls for one fact is what sank the reverted ads scope bar"* (`ScopeBar.tsx`, `right?` doc).
3. **`disabledReason` is not "present and unused."** It is consumed at `ScopeBar.tsx:97` and demonstrated at `TokenCatalog.tsx:1056`. It is unused *by the studio*.
4. **`.nds-readypill-info` is at `design-system/grid/theme/grid.css:419`**, not `design-system/styles/`, and it styles the bar *fill*. Retoning `live` to `info` does reach the band — but only because `readyPillTone` delegates to `readinessMeta(state,'row').tone` (`readiness.ts:103`). The submitted design never stated that mechanism, and without it the edit looks like a no-op.
5. **"All 16 importers" is a stale set claim.** Measured: 26 files reference `readinessMeta` (13 app files, the rest DS internals, `.d.ts` and tests). The gate derives this count; the design does not assert it.
6. **`SheetToolbar.tsx` is not a DS file** — it is `_studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx`. CH.1 is recited at `:14-18`; `overflow` is `:104`; the `trailing` doc is `:105-106`, not `:110`.
7. **The `trailing`-carries-a-control evidence is real, with corrected lines:** `ChannelSheet.tsx:1838` passes `SchemaStatus` (`:1840`), which renders a `<Button>` at `SchemaStatus.tsx:32`.

Not `:2013`/`:2019`.

First paint and the next tick can name two different coordinates. For a revoked grant the message that fires is the *first* branch — *"This channel is unavailable in the selected market"* (`:676`) — which blames the market for a grant that was revoked.

15. **The Retry belongs beside the note, not inside it.** `MessageNote` has no action slot; the shipped precedent is `SavedLayoutNote` (`SheetFooterNote.tsx:50-64`), which places `<Button inline variant="link">` in the `nds-grid-sheet-noteslot` span next to the note.
16. **`LIVE` vs `LISTED`.** The agreed spine's `presenceIntent` enumerates `…|LIVE|…`; the renderer union said `LISTED`. One spelling, or the stored token leaks the very word §2 retires. Standardised on `LISTED`; the spine's column comment must change with it.
17. **`NOT_SELLING` had no vocabulary row** — it is in the fact union with nothing for a surface to print. Added.
18. **`presence.ts` cannot "import nothing"** — it imports `ReadinessTone` from `readiness.ts` (which itself imports nothing). The invariant that matters is no React, no JSX, node-testable.
19. **The vocabulary gate as written is unbuildable.** "Zero banned words in `en.json`/`it.json`" fails on nine keys with nothing to do with listings — `stock.locations.deactivate*`, `pricing.rules.*`, `drafts.archive*`. And `deactivate` is a *data value* in `ebay-columns.ts:159`, inside a flat-file editor the Owner has ruled untouchable. The gate is rescoped and ratcheted.
20. **The tree is not green, and the phase-0 typecheck was "could not measure", not "clean."** `tsc-web.txt`, `tsc2.txt` and `tsc-listing.txt` are all 0 bytes. I ran it: `apps/web` `tsc --noEmit` exits 2 with one error (`products/next/TranslateDialog.tsx:65`, `replaceAll`/lib target, pre-existing, outside the studio); `apps/api` has one (`bulk-action.service.ts:1591`). Studio vitest: `layers.vitest.test.ts:55` red on `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot`, plus five failures in `masterWrite.vitest.test.ts`. Mapping `channelSnapshot` joins wave 1 — presence is already leaking into the provenance axis before presence exists.
21. **`offerActive` was never accounted for.** It *is* selected (`studio-sheet.service.ts:472`) and the wire coerces `offerActive: listingRow.offerActive !== false` (`:1414`) — a server-side null-becomes-true, the same class the client gate catches. It is consumed on Amazon only, at the next flat-file submit. It is the third off-switch and is named as one.
22. **A test pins `isLiveOnChannel`.** `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts` asserts a DRAFT row holding a real ItemID is LIVE. Nothing here changes it, and the design says so, because a reviewer will assume otherwise from the adjacency.
## 0. What I measured
All 24 findings across `sc:state-honesty`, `ux:terminology`, `sc:onboarding-edges`, `ux:states-feedback` are CONFIRMED except TERM-6, REFUTED. Nothing here depends on an UNVERIFIED finding. I do not build on TERM-6: `ebay-columns.ts:156` ships a per-row `deactivate` documented as *"set the live offer to quantity 0 (keeps the ItemID — the safe hide)"*. Any vocabulary that greys out eBay's hold lies about a shipped capability.
**This surface's own instrument is red and I ran it.** `node scripts/check-wire-null-defaults.mjs` → **28 against a baseline of 17, and all 28 offenders are inside `_studio/`**. Its most consequential rows are my subject: `AliasBandCell.tsx:132` prints `alias?.listingStatus ?? 'NOT LISTED'` — an *absent* status rendered as the *assertion* "NOT LISTED"; `PresentationTab.tsx:136` prints `row?.listing?.externalListingId || 'Not published'`; `ComparePane.tsx:246` invents `'No listing on this coordinate.'`. The gate's closing line is the brief: *"a plausible default looks like data."*
## 1. The rule everything else follows
Readiness answers *could I publish this row*. Presence answers *what is true on the channel*. `readiness.ts:12-26` already forbids collapsing its own two vocabularies and exports no converter. **Presence is a third vocabulary and gets the same treatment: one pure meta module, no local mapping, no converter, and no surface may author a presence sentence.** Every lie in the roster is an authored sentence — `readiness.ts:56`, `listingRisk.ts:62-63`, `en.json:5223`, `ErrorsSyncConsole.tsx:375`. Delete the authoring capability and the class closes.
## 2. The canonical vocabulary
**Retire "Live" as an operator word.** Three definitions in three adjacent surfaces (`readiness.ts:53` = `externalListingId && isPublished`; `listingRisk.ts:46-48` = the id alone; `ListingsPane.tsx:103` = the raw DB token). Both are correct answers to *different questions*. Split the questions.
| Word | Axis | Means | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| No listing here | intent | we hold no listing record at this coordinate | absence of a row |
| Draft | intent | a record exists, never sent | `presenceIntent=DRAFT` |
| Listed | intent | our record holds a channel reference | `externalListingId` present |
| Held | intent | we send nothing; the channel keeps what it has | `syncPaused` |
| Offer withdrawn | intent | offer off; listing, identity and reviews stand | `offerClosedAt` |
| Ended | intent | ended on the channel; identity retired | `presenceIntent=ENDED` |
| Discontinued | intent | ended, and never recreate | `presenceIntent=DISCONTINUED` |
| Released | intent | identity deliberately surrendered | `presenceIntent=RELEASED` |
| Selling | fact | the channel confirmed it is selling | `channelFact=SELLING` |
| Not selling | fact | the channel confirmed it is not buyable | `channelFact=NOT_SELLING` |
| Suppressed | fact | the channel is hiding it — their decision | `channelFact=SUPPRESSED` |
| Not checked | fact | nobody has asked the channel | `channelFactAt == null` |
| Could not ask | fact | we asked and could not get an answer | `channelFact=REFUSED` |
| Gone from the channel | fact | it answered and has no such listing | `channelFact=ABSENT` |
**Three off-switches, three words, never two.** `syncPaused` (we stop sending) = *Held*; `offerClosedAt` (the offer is closed on the channel) = *Offer withdrawn*; `offerActive` (Amazon-only, applied at the next flat-file submit via `skip_offer`) = *Excluded from the next Amazon file*. They are not synonyms and no surface may print one for another.
**Verbs, canonical name plus the native term as secondary text.** One name per verb, on every surface, in every state — TERM-5's defect is one item renaming itself between enabled (`ChannelSheet.tsx:1727`) and disabled (`:1722`), with a third form in the Modal title (`:1847`) and a fourth on Variants (`ChannelProjection.tsx:403`). Both label and title derive from one exported constant per channel-kind, so the disabled branch cannot drift.
| Verb | Amazon | eBay | Shopify | Etsy |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Hold / Resume | `syncPaused` | `syncPaused` | `syncPaused` | `syncPaused` |
| Withdraw the offer / Restore | `purchasable_offer` `op:'delete'` per market (`amazon-market-offer.service.ts:166`) | quantity 0, ItemID kept (`ebay-columns.ts:156`) | `status:'draft'` (`channel-delist.service.ts:192`) | **disabled — read-only Etsy client** (`etsy/read-client.ts:5`) |
| End the listing | availability DISCONTINUED (**not wired** — `channel-delist.service.ts:151-155` falls back to `deleteListingsItem`) | EndFixedPriceItem | unpublish | disabled |
| Check this listing | `getListingsItem` | `getItemListingStatus` | product read | disabled, reason stated |
**Banned as operator-facing prose *about a listing*:** delist, unlist, unpublish, deactivate, archive, retire, take down, resolve, preflight, soft-delete, bare "remove". Scoped deliberately: `deactivate` is a data value in an untouchable flat-file editor, `archive` is a shipped alias verb, and `stock.locations.deactivate*` / `pricing.rules.*` / `drafts.archive*` are nine `en.json` keys about other objects.
**"Unpublish (recommended)" goes in wave 1** (`en.json:5222-5223`, `it.json:5222-5223`). All three of its promises are false and `BulkActionBar.tsx:1097` makes it the default radio. Until Amazon's PATCH path is wired its Amazon row reads: *"End the listing — Amazon has no pause here. The SKU offer is deleted; the ASIN survives."* and it is not labelled "recommended".
## 3. `design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts`
Pure `.ts` beside `readiness.ts`; no React, no JSX; imports only `ReadinessTone`.
```ts
export type PresenceIntent = 'NONE'|'DRAFT'|'LISTED'|'HELD'|'WITHDRAWN'|'ENDED'|'DISCONTINUED'|'RELEASED'
export type ChannelFact   = 'UNKNOWN'|'SELLING'|'NOT_SELLING'|'SUPPRESSED'|'ABSENT'|'REFUSED'
export type PresenceVerdict = 'agrees'|'pending'|'diverged'|'unknown'|'unreachable'
export interface PresenceMeta { tone: ReadinessTone; label: string; axis: 'intent'|'fact'; hint: string }
export function intentMeta(i: PresenceIntent): PresenceMeta
export function factMeta(f: ChannelFact): PresenceMeta
export function presenceVerdict(p: {
  intent: PresenceIntent; intentAt: string | null
  fact: ChannelFact; observedAt: string | null
  inFlight: boolean; now: number; freshnessMs: number
}): PresenceVerdict
export function presenceLine(p: Presence): { intent: PresenceMeta; fact: PresenceMeta; asOf: string | null; sentence: string }
export const PRESENCE_INTENTS, CHANNEL_FACTS   // derived from the tables, never restated
```
Five vitests:
1. `factMeta('UNKNOWN').tone === 'neutral'`, label `not checked` — the verbatim word already shipping at `ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:96`. **No input may return a `success` tone while `fact === 'UNKNOWN'`.** That single assertion is the doctrine.
2. `presenceVerdict` returns `'unknown'`, never `'agrees'`, when `observedAt` is null or older than the last intent change with nothing in flight. Silence never ages into agreement.
3. `presenceLine` returns a sentence; callers render it. A gate asserts no surface concatenates presence words.
4. No converter to `RowReadinessState`, mirroring `readiness.ts:22-25`.
5. An unrecognised token renders verbatim and neutral, the way `readinessMeta:82-85` already handles a contract change — required, because `listingStatus` is a free String (`schema.prisma:1617`) whose comment names five values while the code writes at least ten.
## 4. Where the marks go — corrected
**Two marks, never merged, in this order: intent, fact, as-of.**
> `Listed` · `Selling — Amazon confirmed 3 hours ago`
> `Offer withdrawn` · `not checked`
Each mark is a DS `Pill` (`primitives/Pill.tsx`) with `dot` on the fact pill — the prop exists exactly *"for a pill reporting the health of something CONTINUOUS … where the dot is what the eye reads before the word"* (`Pill.tsx:18-26`). The as-of is a `<time dateTime>` through the same `ago()`/`when()` pair the drawer uses (`drawer/format.ts:11,31`), which **moves to `design-system/grid/renderers/format.ts`** beside `EMPTY_DASH` (`:85`) — four copies of a timestamp formatter is the drift that file's own header exists to prevent.
- **Scope chip** — unchanged. Readiness only.
- **Sheet toolbar `descriptor`** (`ChannelSheet.tsx:1793`) — the coordinate roll-up: `Amazon · IT — 3 selling, 1 ended, 1 not checked`. A set's confidence is its weakest member's and its as-of its oldest; one `UNKNOWN` makes the set `UNKNOWN`; a set is never "mostly selling". This also fixes OE-6: when unlisted, the alias count is suppressed, so "Not listed · 1 listing" becomes "No listing here". CH.1-legal — `descriptor` rides inside `count`, not `trailing`, and adds no control.
- **Alias band** — **no new mark.** Presence joins `bandTitle` (`AliasBandCell.tsx:65-83`), the hover sentence where the listing status was deliberately sent on 2026-09-05. Whether it returns as a visible mark is an Owner question.
- **Drawer** (`ListingsPane.tsx`) — the full two-mark line. The `dl` gains `Intent` and `Confirmed by <channel>`; `Status` at `:103` stops printing the raw Prisma token and renders `intentMeta`/`factMeta`. The pane's guarantee — *"Every fact comes from the row object the sheet already holds. No second fetch, so this pane and the sheet's readiness chip cannot disagree"* (`:17-18`) — is preserved and extended. `ListingsPane.tsx:80`'s `state === 'live' || state === 'ready'` green tick drops the `live` arm.
- **Confirmations** — `ActionImpact.findings` (`registry.ts:102`) labels come from `presenceLine`, never templated by the lane.
- **Footer** — `GridSheetNote kind="provenance"` carries the connection sentences (§6).
**Two timestamps, never conflated.** `lastSyncedAt` is when *our sync ran* (`studio-sheet.service.ts:1415-1420` says so in its own comment) and is labelled "Last synced". `channelFactAt` is when *the channel last told us* and is labelled "Confirmed by <channel>".
## 5. "Never checked" vs "no listing here" vs "could not ask"
- **`unlisted`** (`readiness.ts:56`) ships label "Not listed" / hint "This row has no listing on this channel" — a claim *about the marketplace* from the absence of *our row*, contradicting `readiness.ts:13` eleven lines above. A shipped path makes it a lie: `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:645-647` hard-deletes the row while GUARD 2 (`:695`) skips the eBay call for a shared ItemID. **New copy:** label `No listing here`, hint *"We hold no listing record for this coordinate. Nothing has been checked against the channel."*
- **`UNKNOWN`** is the default, never backfilled, neutral: *"Nobody has asked <channel> about this listing. That is not the same as saying it matches."*
- **`REFUSED`** is split from `ABSENT` because `channel-delist.service.ts:227,231` folds `/item cannot be accessed/` and `/invalid item/` into `ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS` and returns `{success:true}` at `:290-293` — an ownership refusal recorded as a completed removal.
The `live` state is detoxified rather than removed: label `Listed`, tone `info` (reached via `readyPillTone` → `readinessMeta`, `readiness.ts:103`; `.nds-readypill-info` exists at `grid/theme/grid.css:419`), hint *"Our record holds a channel reference. Whether it is selling is on the presence line."* And the verdict stops asserting it for terminal rows: **one shared helper** gates the `live` branch on `!TERMINAL.has(listingStatus)` in *both* row builders (`studio-sheet.service.ts:1483-1487` and `sheet-rows.service.ts:270`) — two builders, one rule, because a master-only fix is a silent channel gap. `isLiveOnChannel` (`listingRisk.ts:46-48`) is **unchanged**: `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts` pins it as intended against 20 measured non-ACTIVE eBay rows holding real ItemIDs.
`CompletenessPill`'s `if (pct == null) return null` (`IdentityBand.tsx:137`) renders a neutral em-dash pill carrying `tip` instead: today three files' worth of deliberate "nothing to measure" honesty (`AliasBandCell.tsx:71-78`) renders as a blank indistinguishable from a loading failure.
## 6. Revoked, expired, unreadable, suspended, binned
None may make a channel disappear. Today a revoked grant deletes the channel from the bar and every tab (`studio-data.ts:111-115` → `scopes.ts:116`), and the bookmark blames the market (`contracts.tsx:676`).
| Case | Detection | Render |
|---|---|---|
| Grant revoked | `isActive=false` | Channel **kept** with `connected:false`; chip `disabled` + `disabledReason` (`ScopeBar.tsx:44-52`, consumed at `:97`): *"The <channel> account is disconnected. Reconnect it in Settings → Channels to work on this listing."* Presence from the last record, fact `UNKNOWN`. |
| Grant expired | `authStatus` — on the wire at `connections.routes.ts:95`, discarded at `studio-data.ts:112-113` | Chip normal (content editing is fine). Account option suffixed `· needs reconnecting`. One footer note. Check is disabled with that reason. Never a green fact. |
| Discovery read failed | `marketplacesFailed` (`studio-data.ts:100,115`), destructured into `_marketplacesFailed` and discarded (`StudioFrame.tsx:35`) | Do **not** stamp `connected:false` from an empty set (`studio-data.ts:116`). Keep each channel; chip `disabled`, reason *"Channel availability could not be read. This does not mean the product has no listings."* Footer note plus a Retry. Delete the false comment at `StudioFrame.tsx:28-32`. |
| Market suspended | `isParticipating`/`participationStatus` (`schema.prisma:1973-1975`), honoured elsewhere | Market option labelled `(not participating)`; verdict `unreachable`; readiness percentage suppressed rather than shown against an unsellable destination. |
