| Product binned | `deletedAt`, in the `/products/:id` body and dropped at `studio-data.ts:40-58` | Opens as `StudioPlaceholder` — "This product is in the bin" — not a green "Active" pill over an empty grid. `useMasterSheet.ts:188-190` must read the 404 body's `code` (`unknown_product`, `studio-sheet.service.ts:427`) instead of reading every 404 as "the studio route is not deployed". |
**Footer priority ladder**, because `SheetFooterNote` early-returns and renders one note (`:67-83`): `offline` → `refused` → **connection health** → `layoutRecovery` → hint. A write that did not reach the server outranks a stale grant. The Retry sits beside the note in the `nds-grid-sheet-noteslot` span, exactly as `SavedLayoutNote` (`:50-64`) already does — `MessageNote` has no action slot and must not grow one for this.
**Etsy** is stated, not silently caught by the WooCommerce containment rule: `modeForChannel` (`channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:323-332`) returns null for Etsy, so wave 1 says plainly that Etsy presence is READ-ONLY and "Check this listing" is disabled with that reason.
## 7. How four surfaces are made unable to disagree
1. **One producer** — `derivePresence(listingRow, now)` in `packages/shared` (reachable from both apps: `apps/web` already imports `@nexus/shared/products-grid`), called by the sheet read, the readiness route and the presence read.
2. **One carrier** — `StudioRow.presence` and `AliasGroup.presence`, the same object the drawer already reads.
3. **One renderer** — `presence.ts`. Both type mirrors (`sheet/channel/types.ts`, `drawer/types.ts`) import the union instead of restating it; `RowReadinessState` is restated in four places today, which is a set claim that goes stale.
4. **One gate** — `scripts/check-presence-vocabulary.mjs`, deriving its member list from `presence.ts` (never hardcoded), asserting: every file rendering listing state imports from `presence.ts`; **zero banned words under the listing/lifecycle key prefixes** (`products.hardDelete.*`, `listings.*`, studio-owned keys) and in `_studio/**` JSX text, excluding flat-file column enums; both row builders call the shared derive; and `check-wire-null-defaults` at or below its baseline. It runs a positive control in the same execution, and it starts as a **ratchet against a recorded baseline**, not an absolute zero, because a red gate that convicts nine unrelated keys is a false conviction.
## 8. Wave 1 — what I am asking approval to build
Copy and wire only: the vocabulary table and the scoped ban list; `presence.ts`; the `CompletenessPill` null fix; the `format.ts` move; the additive wire fields (`channelFact*`, `offerClosedAt`, `offerCloseReason`, `syncPaused` — all four already exist and are simply not in `LISTING_SELECT`, `studio-sheet.service.ts:464-483`); the terminal-status gate in both builders; connection health through the loader; the five renders of §6; the footer ladder; mapping `channelSnapshot` in `drawer/types.ts` so `layers.vitest.test.ts:55` goes green; `Check this listing` as an item of the one `⋯` overflow (CH.1-legal, precedent at `ChannelProjection.tsx:399-406`); and the copy fixes at `readiness.ts:53,56`, `listingRisk.ts:62-63`, `familyActions.ts:455`, `ErrorsSyncConsole.tsx:375`, `ImagesTab.tsx:162-171` (which today names Shopify on every non-Amazon/eBay scope), `en.json`/`it.json:5222-5223`. **No channel-reaching verb ships**, so nothing here can write to a marketplace.
## 9. The honest cost
Most coordinates will read `not checked` on day one where today they read a confident green "Live". `docs/pes-claims.md:14081` records `lastSyncedAt` null on 877 of 977 listings. The studio will look emptier and less certain, because it is. That is the change, not a regression, and the Owner has to want it before it ships.
## 10. What I could not establish
I did not run a browser: `check-control-census.mjs`, `check-layout-v2.mjs` and `check-grid-chrome.mjs` all drive `localhost:3000`, and local dev is wired to the production API. Since the marks no longer land in the 240px band, no geometry claim is load-bearing — but if the Owner rules the band should carry a visible mark, the band's trail must be measured with `measureBandSlots` on **both** row kinds first, and `findKeyBearingBand`'s deliberate choice of the variant row re-examined, before anything is added. I also did not establish what Etsy returns for a listing read, so "Check this listing" on Etsy is specified as disabled with a stated reason rather than as a working verb.

#### Presence — a listing surface, a pinned coordinate, and a second state axis (corrected)

Keeps the reviewed proposal's spine: a Presence surface whose rows are five-level coordinates, two never-merged axes (declared intent, observed fact with a mandatory as-of), typed `reach`/`reversal` on the action registry, and a server-computed plan/apply pair bound to a preflightToken. Corrected on twenty points found by opening the files. The headline typecheck claim is false (exit 2, one diagnostic). `StudioCoordinate` is mis-cited to the wrong file. The URL-normalisation fix is silently swallowed by the studio's own scope-change guard. The NAV-4 fix as written is a regression. The eBay and Shopify holds already ship and the proposal would have us rebuild them on an untouchable file. The `reversal` field is an enhancement the audit REFUTED as a defect, and must be sold as such.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- NEW MODULE — design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts, beside readiness.ts: the tone/label table for INTENT, FACT and the derived VERDICT, same {tone,label,vocabulary,hint} shape, pure, NO converter to or from either readiness vocabulary (readiness.ts:8-26 argues why). This replaces the roster's SH-1 fix, which would have added `impaired`/`ended` to the readiness ROW table and conflated the two axes.
- registry.ts — GridAction gains `reach?: 'local' | 'local-destructive' | 'channel'`. On the ACTION because it gates availability and grouping before any preflight runs. Ship with a regenerated registry.d.ts in the same write.
- registry.ts — ActionImpact gains `reversal?: { verb: string; fidelity: 'exact' | 'lossy' | 'none' }`. HONESTY LABEL, corrected: the audit REFUTED this as a defect (DX-5) and explicitly graded it 'a legitimate contract-enhancement proposal … not a confirmed defect in shipped code'. ActionConfirm.tsx:90-96 already renders a different footer for a permanent verb, and `consequences` is the DS's designated home (familyActions.ts:466). The case for the field is that a GUARD can then enforce what is currently prose — nothing stronger.
- registry.ts — NEW `validateAction(action)` beside `validateImpact`. validateImpact receives only the impact (registry.ts:264-273) and structurally cannot see an action-level field — a limitation DX-5's own fixCritique identified. It must refuse a reach:'channel' verb whose impact carries no reversal, and a fidelity:'none' at any level below type-to-confirm.
- ActionConfirm.tsx — the reversibility sentence is RENDERED from ActionImpact.reversal, never accepted as prose from a lane.
- menuAdapters.tsx + Menu.tsx — CORRECTED FIX. Adopt the audit's CONFIRMED fix, not the reviewed proposal's: add `tone?: Tone` to MenuItemDef, a `.nds-menu-item[data-tone="danger"]` rule in components.css, AG `cssClasses: ['nds-menu-danger']` in actionContextMenu, and map action.danger → tone in BOTH adapters. Do NOT overload `description`: Menu.tsx:26-42 documents it as the home for the reason an item is unavailable, deliberately not a tooltip, and putting severity there is two facts in one slot.
- .claude/DS-GAPS.md — the entry '`Menu` cannot express a destructive item, or explain a disabled one' recites MenuItemDef as six fields and is STALE (Menu.tsx now carries title, description, separator). CONSTRAINT the reviewed proposal missed: the file is APPEND-ONLY under scripts/check-ds-gaps-append-only.mjs (I ran it — green, 4184 lines, 80 headings). Editing a line in place is permitted; removal is not. Correct the line, do not replace the entry.
- EVERY DS type change above must regenerate its committed .d.ts in the SAME write. menuAdapters.tsx:70-74 records what a stale one costs ('makes the next lane build a worse one') and that check-ds-dts-fresh 'has been red from uncommitted work all session'.
- NO CHANGE to SheetToolbar — and note it is PAGE-LOCAL, at apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx, not in design-system/. CH.1 verified verbatim at :14-19 and :100; overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[] at :104; trailing at :106 (the proposal said :105).
- NO CHANGE to ScopeBar.tsx. A scope chip is per-channel and presence is per-coordinate, so a chip could only ever show a distribution, and a second meter on a radiogroup chip is the 'two controls for one fact' failure ScopeBar.tsx:81-88 records.

##### API
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence — every real coordinate plus every Opening (connected channel × served market with no row): the five-level key, intent with provenance when derived, fact + as-of, derived verdict, offer state (offerClosedAt/syncPaused), lastSyncedAt, gate mode, and per-verb availability WITH reach and reversal, answered by the server so a preflight is never a client guess. It should WRAP GET /api/products/:id/all-listings (marketplaces.routes.ts:264-292), which already returns every ChannelListing with only flatFileSnapshot and overrideData omitted. THREE CORRECTIONS the reviewed proposal missed: (a) that route returns a Record<channel, listings[]>, not a flat array — the wrapper must flatten; (b) its where clause is {productId} alone, with workspace scoping supplied by scopedPrisma/workspace-context.ts, not by the route — verify that, do not assume it; (c) it carries no in-file guard, and the wrapper must not inherit that.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/one?channel=&market=&accountId=&aliasKey= — one coordinate; 400 on any missing dimension, reusing requireCoordinate's shape verbatim (product-studio.routes.ts:180-187, ruling #573) widened to require account and alias.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/verify { coordinates[], reason } — READS the channel, writes only the channelFact* columns. Not a fourth live-read seam: it extends the operator-triggered read path that already ships (ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:6-13) with a listing-existence field, reusing getListingsItem / getItemListingStatus. Four-valued, and 'could not ask' is load-bearing: REFUSED is not ABSENT.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/plan { verb, targets[], options } — the server-side preflight. Returns an ActionImpact-shaped plan with per-coordinate consequences, reach, reversal{verb,fidelity}, gate mode, server-computed fan-out named as coordinates and never as a count, refusals each NAMING the verb that resolves them, the operational-preflight result for any local-destructive verb, plus a planId and a short-TTL preflightToken binding the enumerated five-level target set and each target's ChannelListing.version. No writes.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/apply { planId, preflightToken, confirmPhrase? } — applies exactly that plan; refuses a missing, expired or set-mismatched token; 409s per target on version drift; per-target results in the shipped MarketOfferResult shape (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:46-55) with NO aggregate success field. THIS MUST BE THE ONLY WRITER, enforced by a gate, or plan/apply becomes a fourth ChannelListing removal path.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/history — ProductEvent for the coordinate, so lifecycle writes to the store Activity already reads.
- PATCH /api/products/:id/aliases/:aliasId { status: 'ACTIVE' | 'ARCHIVED' } — one new body field plus a restoreAlias writer beside archiveAlias (listing-alias.service.ts:~193-197). I re-verified there is no restore writer anywhere: /usr/bin/grep 'restoreAlias|unarchiveAlias' over apps/api/src and apps/web/src returns rc=1, against a positive control of archiveAlias in 6 files. Wave 3, and inert until PES.5-ii drops the legacy indexes (createAlias still raises AliasCreationBlockedError at listing-alias.service.ts:98).
- PRECONDITION P1 (wave 1) — narrow PATCH /api/products/:id/offer-availability to the full five-level coordinate. CORRECTED: it hardcodes aliasKey:'' at marketplaces.routes.ts:**318** (the reviewed proposal said 317) AND AGAIN at :418 in the bulk path. Narrowing one and not the other leaves the defect.
- PRECONDITION P2 (wave 2 blocker, MISSED by the reviewed proposal) — narrow amazon-market-offer.service.ts:65-68, whose loadRow does findFirst({ where: { productId, channel:'AMAZON', marketplace } }) with no channelConnectionId and no aliasKey. This is the reference path withdraw-offer is modelled on; on a multi-account or aliased coordinate it closes an arbitrary row. Same narrowing for both the pre-read and the delete in ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts (:636-639 and :645-647) and amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts (:52-55 and :60-62), and for sync-control.routes.ts:895-898. Narrowing a delete without its pre-read is strictly worse than today.
- PRECONDITION P3 (wave 1) — an explicit in-file preHandler naming the required permission on every route above AND on the two the presence read leans on. Measured: preHandler|requirePermission occurs 0 times in marketplaces.routes.ts and 0 times in product-studio.routes.ts, and the global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (apps/api/src/lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:30-32, 'resolve + LOG what would be denied, but allow the request').
- PRECONDITION P4 — enqueue the delist rows with productId:null and channelListingId:null (both columns already nullable), coordinate in the row's own columns plus payload, and report channelCascadeDispatched read back AFTER commit rather than the pre-commit create count. No migration needed.
- PRECONDITION P5 — pushAmazonPrice must select and honour offerClosedAt/presenceIntent. It selects only {id, platformAttributes} (pricing-outbound.service.ts:140-149) and patchListingPrice issues op:'replace' on /attributes/purchasable_offer (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:687-699) — the instance the close removed with op:'delete' — so a reprice silently resurrects a closed offer and stamps syncStatus:'IN_SYNC'. The refusal exists one path away at outbound-sync.service.ts:904-910. Extract it as one shared assertPushAllowed().
- PRECONDITION P6 — no adapter may escalate: remove the Amazon UNPUBLISH→DELETE fallback (channel-delist.service.ts:155-158) and the duck-typed Shopify updateProduct→deleteProduct fallback (:192-203). Refuse instead. And stop classifying 'item cannot be accessed' / 'invalid item' as success (:227,231,290-293) — that is an ownership refusal recorded as a completed removal.
- PRECONDITION P7 — applyDelistResultToQueue must set holdUntil on a retryable park, and dispatchSync must REFUSE lifecycle syncTypes outright rather than routing them into update paths (outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623 has no lifecycle branch). This is also the whole of the WooCommerce containment measure.
- PRECONDITION P8 — move isLiveOnChannel UNCHANGED into packages/shared as identityHeld, and add the new sellingRisk beside it; repoint products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682 and :1857-1860. isLiveOnChannel is NOT redefined: listingRisk.vitest.test.ts pins 'a DRAFT row holding a real ItemID is LIVE' as intended, and listingRisk.ts:47-49 is exactly `trimmed(row.externalListingId) !== ''`.
- PRECONDITION P9 (wave 1) — map ValueSource 'channelSnapshot'. attribute-resolver.ts:25 defines it, drawer/types.ts has no entry, and layers.vitest.test.ts:55 is RED on 'unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot'. Presence is already leaking into the provenance axis before presence exists.
- REQUIRED BEFORE ANY 'recoverable with loss' PROMISE — exercise POST /products/:id/listings/:listingId/snapshots/:snapshotId/restore (product-studio.routes.ts:938-948, verified) against a captured request and read the BODY, not the status. It ships with zero web callers and SNAPSHOT_FIELDS deliberately excludes externalListingId. Until it round-trips, no reversal field may claim fidelity better than 'lossy'.
- ETSY IS READ-ONLY, FOR A REASON DISTINCT FROM WOOCOMMERCE'S. modeForChannel returns null for BOTH Etsy and WooCommerce (channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:323-332), but Etsy's real constraint is that Nexus holds only a read client — etsy/read-client.ts:5, 'Account-scoped, read-only access to Etsy Open API v3'. Wave 1 must state Etsy presence is read-only in those words, and must NOT let it be silently swept into the WooCommerce containment rule.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntent String?: the DECLARED intent (NONE|DRAFT|LIVE|HELD|WITHDRAWN|ENDED|DISCONTINUED|RELEASED). Nullable and NEVER backfilled: null means 'never stated', and the read derives a provisional intent from the legacy columns carried on the wire WITH its provenance so no surface presents it as an operator decision. The naive derivation is a verified trap: ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1961 and :1984 write {listingStatus:'ENDED', offerActive:false, externalListingId:null} on listings that certainly were published, so a derivation keyed on a null external id reads ENDED as 'never listed'.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntentAt DateTime? / presenceIntentBy String? / presenceIntentReason String?. The reason is what makes DISCONTINUED distinguishable from ENDED to the next operator.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceEffectiveFrom DateTime? / presenceUntil DateTime?. Wave 1 STORES and DISPLAYS them and nothing executes them; the surface must say so in words. See the scheduling open question.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.channelFact String? / channelFactAt DateTime? / channelFactVia String? / channelFactDetail Json?. Deliberately distinct from lastSyncedAt, whose own header (studio-sheet.service.ts:1380-1384) warns it records when a sync last RAN. Null = UNKNOWN. NO BACKFILL — rows written before these columns existed derive UNKNOWN, never DRAFT and never SELLING.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.endedAt DateTime? / endedBy String? / endedReason String?: 'ended by us, deliberately, then', distinct from listingStatus happening to read ENDED because a reconcile job wrote it.
- CONSTRAINT THE REVIEWED PROPOSAL MISSED — ChannelListing carries TWO six-field unique keys, both including workspaceId: @@unique([workspaceId, productId, channelMarket, channelConnectionId, aliasKey]) at schema.prisma:1785 and @@unique([workspaceId, productId, channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, aliasKey]) at :1786, with part of the alias uniqueness enforced in raw SQL (:1778-1784, 'Prisma cannot express that'). Every presence upsert must NAME which key it addresses; the legacy channelMarket key is still live and still enforced.
- NOT PROPOSED — a new ListingPresenceEvent table. ProductEvent already exists, is FK-free by design (schema.prisma:15475-15478), already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing', is already emitted and already read by audit-log.routes.ts. Presence emits there with aggregateId = ChannelListing.id and the full five-level coordinate plus external id in `data`. A third store would recreate the confirmed defect that lifecycle history is unreachable because Activity reads the event store while lifecycle writes the audit log.
- NOT PROPOSED IN THIS SCOPE, NAMED AS THE WAVE-3 DEPENDENCY — a ListingIdentity tombstone/registry model, which is what makes RELEASED mean anything and what a create path must consult before recreating a surrendered identity. If it lands it must carry the workspaceId dbgenerated default that 412 of 432 models carry, and a plain-string productId with NO Prisma relation so it survives the product purge (the AuditLog property at products-catalog.routes.ts:1902-1908).
- NOT DECLARED — ChannelListing.variationExcluded. family-projection.service.ts:389 cites migration `20260911a_vp2_variation_excluded`; I re-checked packages/database/prisma/migrations and it does not exist (only 20260911020000_information_formula_destination, 20260911_category_taxonomies and five 20260912_lx* folders). CONTEXT the reviewed proposal missed: pes-claims.md's ratified MIGRATION STAGING RULE keeps an UNAPPLIED migration folder OUT of that directory, so absence is expected and is not evidence the column is missing from the database. Either way the conclusion holds — declaring a column the database may not have is an outage for every concurrent session. The raw-SQL helpers stay.
- SEQUENCING (binding) — apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma. MEMORY records apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11 with only Product.version (54 vs 51) separating the two. Discriminate, never infer.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, verbatim at _studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx:14-19 and :100) says a scope's verbs are items of the one ⋯ overflow and never a button. Evidence it strains under lifecycle: the prop is `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` (:104) and MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-50) carries no tone, no severity, no group; both adapters drop GridAction.danger (0 hits vs a control of 5); there is no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet. Under CH.1 as written, 'End the listing on Amazon IT' renders byte-identical to 'Reload'. THIS PROPOSAL DOES NOT NEED CH.1 TO BEND — presence has its own surface with its own button-based verb bars and SheetToolbar gains nothing. The smallest amendment I can see, if the Owner wants lifecycle reachable from the sheet at all, keeps CH.1's actual rule (no new buttons) and asks only that the overflow gain a typed tone plus a separator group. Does CH.1 stand as written, with lifecycle living only on the presence surface?
- D9 — is a presence surface inside the existing per-channel nav group a NEW OBJECT (my reading: the sheet is content, presence is state; zero overlap in rows and in written fields) or a SECOND WAY IN? D9's reasoning is encoded at StudioHeader.tsx:86-97 and its §11 form (pes-claims.md:17821) forbids restoring a link-out to a legacy surface. Presence RETIRES two link-outs — the legacy hub and the coordinate-dropping list-wizard handoff at PublishMenu.tsx:17-19, whose own comment at :8-9 admits 'the legacy creation wizard does not accept a bound account/listing and cannot take this handoff'. If the Owner reads presence as a second route to the sheet, only the per-coordinate verbs survive.
- A NEW TAB ID. `listings` must be added to StudioTabId (types.ts:46) and STUDIO_TABS (:66). The only precedent is hub ruling #128, which added 'errors' on PES.3's request; and the Owner removed 'relationships' on 2026-09-12 saying the page was never needed. Does the Owner grant the tab id, and does Presence appear under THIS PRODUCT, under each channel group, or both?
- THE eBay CLOSE/REOPEN DESIGN POSITION. sync-control.routes.ts:806-816 states in code: 'SCT.6 — per-market offer CLOSE/REOPEN (Amazon only; eBay has real per-listing quantities and never needs this).' Presence's eBay hold delegates to the shipped qty-0 deactivate rather than mirroring offerCloseSnapshot, which honours that position. If the Owner now wants an eBay snapshot/restore parity with Amazon, that reverses a written design position and needs a hub ruling, not a lane's edit.
- DELEGATION VS EXTRACTION ACROSS THE UNTOUCHABLE LINE. The eBay reversible hold's only writer is inside ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1841-1897 (untouchable). Does the Owner want the studio verb to CALL that route, to have its service half extracted (a write to an untouchable file), or to be omitted on eBay until the flat-file programme lifts the freeze?
- SCHEDULING. Intent carries effectiveFrom/until; wave 1 stores and displays them and nothing executes them. The studio's one scheduling surface is behind a hardcoded false and its cron is off (hub ruling #4: prod boot log says 'scheduled-image-publish: disabled'; ScheduledImagePublish has ZERO rows ever). Does the Owner want an executor built for presence, or should 'back next season' remain a date the operator reads and acts on by hand?
- THE FBA DEFAULT. amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63 refuses close/reopen on ANY FBA signal, fail-closed, on the stated rule that Amazon owns FBA logistics end-to-end. `offerScope: 'all'|'FBM'|'FBA'` (below the coordinate, keyed on Offer's own unique) makes 'withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA' expressible. Does the Owner want offerScope:'FBM' permitted on a mixed coordinate, or does fail-closed stand for every offer on an FBA product?
- RBAC ENFORCE. Every guard this proposal adds is a log line while rbacHook defaults to shadow. Presence's irreversible verb is the strongest argument yet for flipping NEXUS_RBAC_MODE=enforce, and that is an ops decision with blast radius across every route. Gate `release` on the flip, or ship it behind an in-file guard that works regardless?
- THE LEGACY LISTING HUB. MasterDataTab.tsx:915-1252 ships a working per-coordinate hub today over GET /api/products/:id/all-listings, with a Live/Draft/Paused/Ended/Error vocabulary already separate from readiness and a shipped plural verb ('Pause non-IT', :1170-1192). The studio swap is a capability-LOSS event unless presence lands first. Hold the swap until wave 2, or keep the legacy hub mounted alongside?
- IDENTITY CHANGE AND ACCOUNT MOVE — declared out of scope by me, and I want that ratified. channelConnectionId is in BOTH unique keys (schema.prisma:1785,1786) and no verb here changes it, so agency handover, entity change and consolidating two eBay accounts have no writer; same for relaunching under a new SKU keeping the ASIN. critic:scenarios named identity change as an entire missing axis; folding it in would double the model.
- WOOCOMMERCE CONTAINMENT — confirm the scope. Out of scope as a channel (Owner, 2026-09-12). The one containment measure: dispatchSync must REFUSE lifecycle syncTypes rather than route them into an update path (outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623), and Woo presence renders read-only with gateNote's existing honest sentence. Confirm this is containment and not channel work — and note that Etsy also returns null from modeForChannel and must NOT be swept up in it: Etsy is read-only because etsy/read-client.ts:5 is a read client, a different reason with a different remedy.

##### Risks
- THE PROPOSAL'S OWN MEASUREMENTS WERE WRONG ONCE. The headline 'tsc exit 0, zero diagnostics' is false — I reproduced exit 2 with one diagnostic in products/next/TranslateDialog.tsx:65. If the Owner approves on the strength of the measurements, the first thing built lands on a tree the proposal said was clean. Re-run every gate at implementation start and record the result, do not inherit this document's.
- A NEW TAB IS THE OWNER'S TO GRANT AND HE JUST TOOK ONE AWAY. `relationships` was removed on 2026-09-12 with 'the page was never needed' (types.ts:40-45), and the only precedent for adding one is hub ruling #128 for 'errors' (pes-claims.md:26876,26888). If the Owner reads Presence as a second way into the sheet, the plural half of this model is unbuildable and only the per-coordinate verbs survive.
- DELEGATING THE HOLD TO THE FLAT-FILE WRITER CROSSES AN UNTOUCHABLE. The eBay `deactivate` writer lives behind ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1841-1897, and the flat-file editors are on the untouchable list. Delegation means calling that route or extracting its service without editing the editor — if extraction turns out to require touching the file, the hold cannot ship on eBay in wave 1 and the wave must shrink rather than the rule bend.
- THE URL-NORMALISATION BYPASS IS A SAFETY CONTROL BEING WEAKENED. Bypassing canChangeEditor() to write the coordinate is correct in reasoning and dangerous in practice: if the bypass is ever reused for a real navigation, an unsaved editor loses its guard. It must be a single named writer with the reason at the call site and a test that asserts no other patch shape reaches it.
- MOST COORDINATES WILL READ UNKNOWN ON DAY ONE where today they read a confident green Live. The studio will look emptier and less finished. This is the intended change and it will be received as a regression unless the Owner has agreed to it in advance.
- THE PLAN/APPLY PAIR CAN BECOME A FOURTH ChannelListing REMOVAL PATH beside the two flat-file deleteMany services and the delist cascade. It must be the only writer, enforced by a gate, or the model has multiplied the problem it was built to end.
- EVERY PERMISSION GUARD THIS ADDS IS A LOG LINE while rbacHook defaults to shadow. Securing the operator path while the endpoint stays open secures the wrong half — and marketplaces.routes.ts and product-studio.routes.ts both contain zero in-file preHandlers today.
- THE REFERENCE PATH IS THREE-LEVEL. amazon-market-offer.service.ts:65-68 loads by (productId, channel, marketplace) with no connection and no alias. Building a five-level UI on top of a three-level writer produces a confirmation that names a coordinate the write does not respect — the exact dishonesty this whole model exists to end.
- A DECLARED INTENT NOTHING ENFORCES IS THE FAILURE MODE THE MODEL EXISTS TO PREVENT. presenceEffectiveFrom/presenceUntil are stored and displayed in wave 1 with no executor. Unless the surface says in words that the date is a note and not an alarm, an operator will schedule a takedown that never happens.

##### The specification
# Presence — corrected proposal
## CORRECTIONS (what I changed, and how I know)
Every item below I re-derived by opening the file. `/usr/bin/grep` throughout.
**1. The typecheck claim is FALSE, and it is the proposal's headline.** I ran the identical command, `npx tsc --noEmit -p apps/web/tsconfig.json --incremental false`: **exit 2**, one diagnostic — `apps/web/src/app/products/next/TranslateDialog.tsx(65,219): TS2550 Property 'replaceAll' does not exist on type 'string'`. Not "exit 0, zero diagnostics". The grid-kit ratchet claim I *did* reproduce: `DS DataGrid (<table>) 34 baseline 32 ❌ ROSE`, and `channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3` imports `DataGrid` from `@/design-system/components`. A proposal whose first measured claim is wrong must not be approved on its measurements; wave 1 may not claim a green tree.
**2. `StudioCoordinate` is at `types.ts:69-73`, not `contracts.tsx:954-957`.** contracts.tsx:950-953 only *constructs* it. Both the briefing and the proposal mis-cite the type. An implementer told to widen "contracts.tsx:954-957" edits the wrong file.
**3. `listingId` is already in the URL and already in scope state.** `contracts.tsx:667` reads `search.get('listing')`; `:669` passes it to `useWorkspaceDestination`; `setListing` exists. So "the coordinate cannot name the target" is true of the **type** and half-false of the **frame**: the alias is the only level genuinely absent. The widening is smaller than sold, and selling it as new capability is the parity dishonesty D9 exists to punish.
**4. The URL-normalisation fix is inert exactly when it matters.** `push()` at contracts.tsx:846-851 refuses outright when any registered scope-change guard fails: `changingScope` includes `market` and `account`, and `if (changingScope && !canChangeEditor()) return`. With an unsaved editor open — the state in which a wrong coordinate is most dangerous — the normalisation silently does nothing and the URL keeps lying. Corrected below: normalisation is not a scope *change*, it is a statement of the scope already on screen, so it writes at the `enqueue` layer, below the guard.
**5. The NAV-4 fix as written is a regression.** `active: scope === MASTER_SCOPE && tab === id` would leave *nothing* current on `?scope=EBAY&tab=images`: `visibleTabs('EBAY')` returns variants/images/errors/analytics/activity, and `views` renders them all (StudioSubheader.tsx:29-32). The audit itself offers that predicate only as "the one-line alternative" and prefers omitting the duplicate. Corrected predicate below.
**6. The eBay and Shopify holds already ship — the proposal is silent about both.** eBay: `ebay-columns.ts:152-164` defines a per-row `Action` enum `['', 'deactivate', 'end', 'skip']`, `deactivate` documented verbatim as "set the live offer to quantity 0 (keeps the ItemID — the safe hide)… to this market only"; the server half runs at `ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1841-1897`. A channel-agnostic reversible hold also ships as `ZERO_PIN` at `sync-control.routes.ts:894-963`, reversed by `FOLLOW`. Shopify: `channel-delist.service.ts:192-196` writes `{product:{id,status:'draft'}}`. The flat-file editors are on the **untouchable** list, so the studio verb must *delegate to the shipped writer*, never fork it — the audit's own refutation of that finding says a second writer "would be a second, divergent writer for the same operation".
**7. A standing in-code design position blocks any eBay `offerCloseSnapshot` mirror.** `sync-control.routes.ts:806-816`: "SCT.6 — per-market offer CLOSE/REOPEN (Amazon only; eBay has real per-listing quantities and never needs this)." Reversing that is a hub/Owner ruling, not a lane's. Added to openQuestions.
**8. `reversal` is an ENHANCEMENT; the audit REFUTED it as a defect (DX-5).** Its verdict: `ActionConfirm.tsx:90-96` already differentiates a permanent verb (`variant={needsTyping ? 'danger' : 'primary'}` plus a required typed phrase) from a reversible one; `consequences` is the DS's designated home and `familyActions.ts:466` uses it that way; "the dialog never decides severity, it renders what the preflight found" is stated doctrine. So: *a structured `reversal` lets a guard enforce what is currently prose* — that is the whole and honest case. Filing it as a hole the audit found would burn Owner trust. DX-5's own fixCritique already named the `validateImpact`-cannot-see-the-action problem; the `validateAction` split answers it and should credit it.
**9. The menu-severity fix should be the audit's, not the proposal's.** The CONFIRMED finding proposes `tone?: Tone` on `MenuItemDef` plus `.nds-menu-item[data-tone="danger"]` and AG `cssClasses`. The proposal instead maps `danger` onto `description` — but `Menu.tsx:26-42` documents `description` as the home for *the reason an item is unavailable*, deliberately not a tooltip; overloading it puts two facts in one slot. Also: `.claude/DS-GAPS.md` is **append-only under `scripts/check-ds-gaps-append-only.mjs`** (I ran it: green, 4184 lines, 80 headings). Editing a line in place is allowed, removal is not — so "correct the stale entry" is permitted, "replace it" is not. Say which.
**10. `ProductListingPresets` is not doorless, and it is a WRITE surface.** It is mounted by `presets/BoundPresetDestination.tsx:15`, itself mounted by `list-wizard/steps/Step1Channels.tsx:114`. It POSTs `/api/wizard-templates/:id/apply`. Hanging it off an Openings row arms a write from a brand-new surface; wave 1 must not.
**11. The second hardcoded `aliasKey:''` was missed.** `marketplaces.routes.ts:**318**` (not 317) *and* `:418`, the bulk path. Narrowing one leaves the defect.
**12. The reference path for `withdraw-offer` is itself three-level.** `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:65-68`: `findFirst({ where: { productId, channel:'AMAZON', marketplace } })` — no `channelConnectionId`, no `aliasKey`. On a multi-account or aliased coordinate it closes an arbitrary row. Wave-2 blocker; added as a precondition.
**13. `SheetToolbar.tsx` is page-local, not DS** — `_studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx`. Listing it under "DS gaps" sends an implementer to `design-system/` where it does not exist. (CH.1 verified verbatim at :14-19 and :100; `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` at :104; `trailing` at :106, not :105.)
**14. `ScopeBar` has THREE renderers, not two** — `StudioBar.tsx:34`, `LanguageAxisClient.tsx:34`, and `design-system/catalog/TokenCatalog.tsx:1047`. Conclusion (do not change it) unchanged; the count is not.
**15. The Phase 0 ground-truth files do not exist.** The scratchpad holds only `audit-{critics,findings,maps,probes}.json`; there is no `ground-*.json`. Every claim sourced to "ground:measure" is unsourced. I re-derived the two `danger` consumers myself: `FamilySelectionBar.tsx:53` and `RecordActions.tsx:65`; menus drop it (`menuAdapters.tsx` 0 hits, control `label` = 5).
**16. `all-listings` returns a channel-grouped map, not a flat list** (`marketplaces.routes.ts:264-292`), and its `where` is `{productId}` alone — workspace scoping comes from `scopedPrisma`/`workspace-context.ts`, not from the route. Wrapping it is right; the wrapper must flatten, must not inherit the missing in-file guard, and must verify the scoping rather than assume it.
**17. Adding a tab id is a HUB ruling with a precedent, not a lane's move** — `'errors'` was added by ruling #128 (pes-claims.md:26876, 26888) — and the Owner *removed* `relationships` on 2026-09-12, "the page was never needed" (types.ts:40-45).
**18. The `live` relabel's blast radius is studio-only, which strengthens it.** `readinessMeta(…, 'row')` has exactly two call sites I found — `ListingsPane.tsx:56` and `ChannelSheet.tsx:1796`; every other consumer (`products/next`, `listing-readiness`, `ScopeBar`) asks the `'scope'` vocabulary, which has no `live`. Say so; it converts a scary shared-component edit into a contained one.
**19. Every DS type addition must regenerate its `.d.ts` in the same write.** `.d.ts` files are committed beside each source; `menuAdapters.tsx:70-74` records what a stale one costs ("makes the next lane build a worse one") and that `check-ds-dts-fresh` "has been red from uncommitted work all session".
**20. `ChannelListing` carries TWO unique keys**, both six-field including `workspaceId`: `schema.prisma:1785` (legacy `channelMarket`) and `:1786`. Alias uniqueness is partly enforced in raw SQL (`:1778-1784`). A presence upsert must name which key it uses.
## What survives unchanged
The verdict, and the reasoning under it. Lifecycle cannot attach because `ChannelListing` is unique on six fields (`schema.prisma:1786`) while the studio's coordinate type carries three (`types.ts:69-73`), and the two it omits — connection and alias — are the ones that decide which live listing an irreversible verb reaches. Every studio read is coordinate-scoped (`product-studio.routes.ts:387,421`; `requireCoordinate` 400s at `:180-187`), so a confirmation that must say "this ends IT and leaves DE selling" has no read that returns the siblings. The noun exists one page over (`MasterDataTab.tsx:915,999`), so this is reskin, not invention.
The two never-merged axes stand: **INTENT** `NONE|DRAFT|LIVE|HELD|WITHDRAWN|ENDED|DISCONTINUED|RELEASED` with at/by/reason; **FACT** `UNKNOWN|SELLING|NOT_SELLING|SUPPRESSED|ABSENT|REFUSED` with a mandatory `observedAt`/`observedVia`; **VERDICT** derived on read, never stored. `UNKNOWN` is the default, never backfilled, never green — the doctrine ships one tab away at `ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:6-13`. `REFUSED` splits from `ABSENT` because `channel-delist.service.ts:227,231` folds "item cannot be accessed" into already-ended patterns and returns success. The roll-up is a distribution, its confidence its weakest member's, its as-of its oldest.
The vocabulary is a third module, `design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts`, beside `readiness.ts`, same `{tone,label,vocabulary,hint}` shape, **no converter** — `readiness.ts:8-26` argues at length why a converter is the failure, and SH-1's fix (adding `impaired`/`ended` to the readiness table) is declined for exactly that reason.
Plural intents remain a **server-computed plan** bound to a short-TTL `preflightToken` enumerating every five-level target and its `ChannelListing.version`, with 409-per-target on drift. Selection lives on the Presence grid's own rows with `GridSelectionActions` + `BulkActionBar`; the sheet toolbar is untouched, so **CH.1 needs no amendment for this design to ship**.
## The corrected fixes
**Coordinate.** Widen `StudioCoordinate` at `types.ts:69-73` (not contracts.tsx) with `aliasKey` and `listingId`, noting that `listingId` already flows through the frame at `contracts.tsx:667-669` and only needs promoting onto the type.
**URL normalisation.** Add a dedicated `normalise(patch)` writer that calls `enqueue(patch, false)` directly, bypassing `canChangeEditor()` — with a one-line reason at the call site: a guard exists to stop an operator *moving* while unsaved, and stating the coordinate they are already on is not movement. Never `router.replace`; never `window.history.state` (the `__NA` trap is already guarded at `contracts.tsx:812-826`).
**NAV-4.** Keep `active: tab === id` on THIS PRODUCT and suppress it only for the pair a channel group already owns: `active: tab === id && !(scope !== MASTER_SCOPE && channelGroups.some(g => g.id === scope && g.items.some(i => i.tab === tab)))`. Or take the audit's preferred form — omit the channel group's `Listing information` item when `scope === channel.id` — which removes the duplicate by construction. Either is one line; the proposal's is the one that regresses.
**Verbs.** `reach: 'local' | 'local-destructive' | 'channel'` on `GridAction` (it gates availability before any preflight); `reversal: {verb, fidelity}` on `ActionImpact` (only a preflight knows). `validateAction(action)` beside `validateImpact`, refusing a `reach:'channel'` verb whose impact carries no `reversal`. `ActionConfirm` renders the sentence from the field. Reach is **downgraded by the gate, never promised**: `modeForChannel` returns `null` for Etsy and WooCommerce (`syncQueue.ts:323-332`), and Etsy has only a read client (`etsy/read-client.ts:5`), so Etsy presence is READ-ONLY in every wave and says so in words — it must not be silently swallowed by the WooCommerce containment rule.
Severity rendering: verbs live on **button** surfaces (`FamilySelectionBar` renamed generic, `RecordActions`), both of which already honour `danger`. The `⋯` menu is discovery only until `MenuItemDef` gains `tone`.
**Holds delegate.** `hold`/`resume` on eBay routes to the shipped `deactivate`/blank pair or to `ZERO_PIN`/`FOLLOW`; on Shopify to the shipped draft write; on Amazon to `syncPaused` behind the EU shared-quantity guard (`amazon-eu-quantity-guard.ts`), because a quantity-based hold on Amazon *is* a nine-market fan-out while a per-market offer close is not. No new writer.
## Waves
**Wave 1:** the twelve preconditions; the widened coordinate; guard-bypassing URL normalisation; the corrected NAV-4 predicate; the Presence read rendered honestly with two marks and an as-of; `presence.ts`; the `live` relabel (two call sites); the DS type additions with regenerated `.d.ts`; and only local-reach verbs — `hold`/`resume` (delegating), `open-listing`, `copy-identity`, plus `verify` (a read). No channel-reaching write. No create path: `ProductListingPresets` stays behind its existing door.
**Wave 2:** `withdraw-offer`/`restore-offer`, `end`/`relist`, plan/apply, plural selection — each blocked on the three-level narrowing of `amazon-market-offer.service.ts` and the two flat-file removal services.
**Wave 3:** `release`, alias restore (no `restoreAlias` writer exists; `/usr/bin/grep restoreAlias|unarchiveAlias` over `apps/api/src` + `apps/web/src` → rc=1, control `archiveAlias` in 6 files; and `createAlias` still raises `AliasCreationBlockedError` at `listing-alias.service.ts:98`).
## The honest cost, restated
Most coordinates read UNKNOWN on day one where today they read a confident green "Live". That is the change, not a regression. And the tree is not green: one tsc diagnostic, one red ratchet, and `layers.vitest.test.ts:55` fails on `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot` (`attribute-resolver.ts:25` defines it; `drawer/types.ts` has no entry — presence is already leaking into the provenance axis before presence exists). Wave 1 fixes that mapping and says out loud which gates it did not turn green.

#### One bar, six zones, one collapse ladder — the sheet toolbar redesign (corrected)

A fixed six-zone structure for the sheet toolbar, rendered by the DS and shared by all four callers; a deterministic collapse ladder driven by the bar's own inline size through one DS hook that absorbs the studio's existing, unused tier hook; a chip count contract where the producer declares the unit and the renderer never re-derives it; and a typed status slot that makes "never a control" a type error. Fourteen of the original proposal's claims were wrong or stale and are corrected here — most consequentially, the DS already ships the menu-selection mechanism it asked for, ScopeBar has 3 consumers not 13, the specified status tooltip is hover-only, and the U13 gate fix as written would have reduced coverage rather than raised it.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- NEW — apps/web/src/design-system/grid/toolbars/useToolbarOverflow.ts. The DS has no priority-collapse-to-menu hook. Takes a container ref plus a ranked item list and returns {tier, hiddenFromIndex}, measuring natural widths once per content change with a 24px hysteresis band. CORRECTED SCOPE: the studio already ships a page-local tier hook — apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/useContainerTier.ts (A >=1300 / B 1000-1299 / C <1000, layout-v2 spec 3.4) with a ResizeObserver and a change-only setState, and ZERO consumers (/usr/bin/grep -rn 'useContainerTier|tierFor(' returns only its own three lines). The DS hook must SUBSUME it: useContainerTier re-exports A/B/C from the DS hook so 3.4's ratified thresholds are unchanged, and the DS hook supplies the hysteresis useContainerTier lacks (its change-only setState still oscillates on a boundary drag). ScopeBar.tsx:147-160 solves the adjacent problem by scrolling its chip track; build the hook so ScopeBar can adopt it later, but do NOT change ScopeBar in this pass.
- NEW — apps/web/src/design-system/grid/toolbars/GridToolbarChips.tsx. The bounded chip group with a trailing +N FilterChip opening a DS Menu of the chips that did not fit. Rule encoded in the component, not the caller: the ACTIVE chip and a danger-toned chip with a non-zero count never collapse. The collapsed active chip is marked with Menu's existing selectedId, not a hand-rolled tick (see the retracted gap below).
- GridToolbar (patterns/GridToolbar.tsx:28-36) has three slots — count, children, right — and hard-codes <span className="grow"/> between children and right. SheetToolbar passes `right` and no `children` (SheetToolbar.tsx:116,128), so the spacer lands immediately after the count, which is why grid.css:1192 switches it off and :1204 substitutes margin-inline-start:auto — an auto margin that resolves to 0 exactly when it is needed. ADD an opt-in section API (find / views / filters / status / actions) beside the existing props; 67 files mount GridToolbar (measured) and must stay byte-identical.
- GridViewsMenu returns its prompt INSTEAD of its trigger (GridViewsMenu.tsx:198-238), injecting an Input style={{width:180}} plus two buttons into the bar's flow, and without the .nds-grid-views class so the sheet's nowrap rules do not reach it. ADD a portalled popover prompt using the DS's own usePopoverPosition, keeping the trigger mounted. No API change, so /products/next inherits the fix.
- RETRACTED — the original claimed MenuItemDef has no selected/checked field and worked around it with `icon` carrying a tick. FALSE: MenuProps.selectedId exists (Menu.tsx:55, read at :95, emitted as data-selected + aria-current="page" at :168-169 and :196) and has a live style rule (components.css:2668-2669). Chip selection is single-valued (contracts.tsx:229-232), so selectedId is exactly sufficient. No DS gap, no workaround.
- GAP THAT SURVIVES — MenuItemDef (components/Menu.tsx:8-49) has no `tone`, and there is no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet. Independently confirmed: /usr/bin/grep -c danger menuAdapters.tsx = 0 against a positive control of 5 for `label`; actionMenuItems maps only {id,label,title,disabled,onSelect} (menuAdapters.tsx:75-91). Already recorded at .claude/DS-GAPS.md:330-348. This proposal does not need it — no destructive verb enters this bar — but it is the DS half of the CH.1 tension in openQuestions.
- UNRECORDED FORK — two different ToolbarButton components, both exported from DS barrels: components/ToolbarButton.tsx (minimal; sets title={label} on a possibly-disabled button, the U13 anti-pattern) at components/index.ts:64, and primitives/ToolbarButton.tsx (Tooltip-wrapped, with active/size/variant/tone/badge) at primitives/index.ts:31. .claude/DS-GAPS.md records gaps only against the primitives one. Pick the primitives implementation, re-export it from components, delete the copy — before this design uses ToolbarButton for the S4 search toggle.
- --nds-gutter-content (css-vars.ts:447, tokens.css:226, tokens-global.css:227) has zero var() readers, confirmed with a positive control of 11,985 var(--nds- uses. This design mints --nds-toolbar-gutter defaulting to the existing 6px rather than consuming the unread token, because 6-vs-16 is an open Owner question (UXLD-3/UXLD-5) and quietly resolving it inside a width fix would bury a design decision in a bug fix. This matches the audit's own fixCritique on UXLD-5.
- No sheet chip supplies compactLabel (only ChannelProjection.tsx:98,106), and the container query that reveals it fires at 900px of the bar's inline size (grid.css:1420-1424) while the bar only stops being one row below a 1279px VIEWPORT (:1207) — so the compact form is only ever reachable after the failure it exists to prevent. Make compactLabel required on ViewChip (eleven producer sites) and move the reveal onto the S2 step.
- GATE GAP — check-control-census.mjs:400 iterates `button, input, [role="note"]`. A Pill is a <span> and `Views unavailable` (SheetToolbar.tsx:177-182) is a <span>, so a clipped status is invisible to the only instrument that could see it. Widen the selector to include [role="status"], [role="alert"] and .nds-pill as part of this work, or the typed status slot ships ungated.
- GATE GAP — check-silent-disabled.mjs carries a single global BASELINE=27 (script:49) and detects only `disabled && title && !aria-disabled` (script:70). MEASURED: pointing it at _studio yields 19 sites, which passes on the spot (19<27), ratchets nothing, and drops the ads root. Needs (i) a roots table with a per-root baseline, and (ii) a second rule for a permission- or precondition-gated control disabled with no explanation at all — Customise (SheetToolbar.tsx:222) and Import (:242) are silent and currently invisible to the ratchet.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, recorded verbatim at SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20 and pes-claims.md:35368) says a scope's own verbs are items of the one ⋯ overflow and never a button. THIS DESIGN DOES NOT NEED CH.1 TO BEND: no destructive verb enters this bar, and Classification/Requirements move INTO the overflow, which is what CH.1 already ordered. But the DS half of the contract is inert — MenuItemDef carries no tone, menuAdapters drops GridAction.danger (0 hits against a control of 5), and there is no .nds-menu danger rule anywhere — so once lifecycle verbs land, 'End the listing on Amazon IT' will render byte-identical to 'Reload'. The smallest amendment I can see keeps CH.1's actual rule intact (no new buttons) and asks only that the overflow gain a typed tone plus a separator group; `separator` already exists (Menu.tsx:44-48) and SheetToolbar already emits one at :265. This is the Owner's ruling to make.
- CH.1 AND WIDTH-DRIVEN COLLAPSE — a second, narrower question the original did not ask. CH.1's measured claim was 'the bar is IDENTICAL on every scope … same button list, both 1660/1660'. This ladder moves Export and Import into the ⋯ below a 1120px bar and Customise below 860px. That keeps the bar identical ACROSS SCOPES at any given width, which I read as inside CH.1. But it means that below 1120px the bar no longer shows the controls CH.1 named. Is width-driven collapse inside CH.1, or is it an amendment?
- THE GUTTER, still open (UXLD-3/UXLD-5). This design mints --nds-toolbar-gutter defaulting to 6px and refuses to resolve 6-vs-16 inside a width fix. 16px costs ~20px of bar width, roughly one compact chip at S2. Does the toolbar land on the 16px edge the header, scope bar and footstrip use, or stay at 6px because the 40px band needs the width?
- THE UNIT VOCABULARY. The replacement is 'cells | rows | columns | variants | combinations | axes | drafts'. `combinations` is retained only because FamilyVariants.tsx:280 ships it; it and `variants` may be the same unit worn twice. And Required renders '7/137' rather than a chip-shaped count — a slash reads as a ratio and removes the collision by construction, but it is a visible vocabulary change on a control the Owner named. Approve the set, or cut it.
- THE TEST THAT MUST CHANGE. viewChips.vitest.test.ts:91-94 pins viewChipColumnCountLabel -> '3 columns' under the title 'explains 63 mapping errors as three columns across 21 rows'. That is a design decision, not a bug: 'how much of the sheet will this narrow' is coherent for one chip family. I am asking to change it because the same words now sit two pixels from a preset that narrows columns for real, and because the file has zero `noun` coverage so the branch that mis-renders ai-drafts was never exercised. Ratify the change or the units fix cannot land.
- TRANSFER — I did NOT merge Export and Import into a single 'Transfer ▾'. Both already open the same drawer (MasterSheet.tsx:1767,1774 differ only in setTransferIntent), so one control would recover ~90px at every step and cost nothing an operator does today. But it removes a control CH.1 named, so it is the Owner's call, not mine.
- THE FOUR-CALLER SURFACE. ProjectionToolbar (variants/channel/ProjectionToolbar.tsx:45) is the fourth caller and appears in ZERO of the 180 findings (positive control: SheetToolbar = 88). It passes no `trailing` and declares views absent. Everything here reaches it unreviewed by the audit. Should VP.4's lane review the ladder against that surface before it is approved, or is a four-surface change acceptable on a three-surface audit?
- SCOPEBAR — corrected, and a scope question follows. The original justified leaving ScopeBar alone by '13 consumers outside the studio'; measured, `<ScopeBar` has THREE call sites total (LanguageAxisClient, StudioBar, TokenCatalog), one of them the DS catalog. ScopeBar.tsx:147-160 has the same silent-hiding defect this design fixes in the toolbar, and it is now cheap to fix. Does the Owner want it in this pass, or held as its own item?

##### Risks
- EVERY WIDTH NUMBER IS UNMEASURED. The step boundaries (1760/1420/1120/860/600) and the per-step fit estimates are derived from CSS on a calibration taken from the 09-11 browser run (~7.2px/char at 12.5px/600, from the measured 278px actions group). The 09-11 numbers themselves were taken against a bar that was flex-wrap:wrap; padding:6px 16px — CSS the tree no longer has (grid.css:1179-1189 is now padding:0 6px; flex-wrap:nowrap; overflow-x:auto). If the real widths differ by more than ~40px, the boundaries move. Re-run check-control-census.mjs against a signed-in session and re-derive before implementing; do not treat the table as measured.
- ST-02 IS REFUTED AND CORRECTED TO LOW BY THE AUDIT'S OWN VERDICT. This design is justified by the two CONFIRMED findings plus a latent clip, not by a measured overflow at 1280-1728. If the Owner accepts the audit's correction at face value, the collapse ladder is prophylaxis and the honest wave-1 scope is only the units fix, the typed status slot, the portalled views prompt and the padding specificity fix.
- REPLACING trailing: ReactNode WITH A TYPED ARRAY IS A BREAKING PROP CHANGE ON FOUR CALLERS, and two of them (ClassificationDialog, SchemaStatus) are trigger+Modal units owning their own open state (ClassificationDialog.tsx:53-54, SchemaStatus.tsx:32-33). Each must be split into a controlled dialog plus an overflow item. A partial migration leaves a verb in a slot the type no longer admits, or a dialog mounted nowhere.
- MAKING compactLabel REQUIRED AND CHANGING ViewChip.count TO AN OBJECT breaks eleven producer sites at once across four lanes (MasterSheet x2, ChannelSheet x4, FamilyVariants x3, ChannelProjection x3, useAiDraftLayer x1). Nothing is committed in this programme, so a half-landed change is not recoverable by revert. It must land as one write with the renderer, or not at all.
- THE SILENT-DISABLED RATCHET CANNOT ENFORCE HALF THE FIX. Measured: the rule is `disabled && title && !aria-disabled`, so Customise and Import — disabled with no explanation whatsoever — are invisible to it. Without the second rule proposed in the DS gaps, the type obligation is the only enforcement and will erode.
- overflow: hidden ON THE BAR clips focus rings on edge controls (outline-offset 1-2px) even though every popover portals. A focus-visible outline on the first or last control may be shaved. Needs a browser check at S0 and S5 before acceptance.
- THE 24px HYSTERESIS BAND IS A GUESS. It is not measured against any real drag, and too small a band oscillates while too large a band leaves the bar in the wrong tier by up to 24px. useContainerTier shipped with no hysteresis at all and no consumer ever exercised it, so there is no prior art in this tree to calibrate against.
- S5 (<600px) LETS THE BAR TAKE TWO ROWS, which reintroduces the exact geometry the 09-11 measurement recorded as a defect (2-3 rows, 76px content box). It is defensible below 600px and indefensible above it, but nothing in the ladder prevents a future step from widening that exemption.

##### The specification
# One bar, six zones, one collapse ladder — the sheet toolbar redesign (corrected)
## CORRECTIONS — what I changed, and why
I re-opened every file the proposal cites and ran one gate. Fourteen claims were wrong, stale, or would have shipped a defect. Each correction below is measured, not argued.
**C1. `MenuItemDef` does NOT need a `selected` field — the DS already ships it, and the workaround is deleted.** `MenuProps.selectedId` exists (`Menu.tsx:55`, consumed at `:95`, emitted as `data-selected` + `aria-current="page"` at `:168-169` and `:196`) and has a live style rule (`components.css:2668-2669`). Chip selection is single-valued by design (`contracts.tsx:229-232`), so `selectedId` is exactly sufficient. The original's "worked around with `icon` carrying a tick" and its DS-gap filing are removed.
**C2. ScopeBar has THREE consumers, not thirteen.** `/usr/bin/grep -rln "<ScopeBar" apps/web/src` returns exactly three files: `design/language-axis/LanguageAxisClient.tsx`, `_studio/StudioBar.tsx`, and `design-system/catalog/TokenCatalog.tsx` (the DS catalog page). So one outside the studio, plus a catalog. The decision not to touch ScobeBar in this pass stands, but on honest grounds: it is a different bar with a different job and its own audit findings, not a blast radius of thirteen.
**C3. The audit's `ChannelSheet.tsx` line numbers are stale by ~285 lines and the original inherited them mixed with fresh ones.** The working-tree file is 2115 lines; `git diff --stat` on it reads `60 insertions(+), 345 deletions(-)`, so the audit read HEAD. Corrected working-tree lines: the CH.1 "no longer overflows its 1660px" comment is **:1806** (not :2085); `<SheetToolbar` mounts at **:1787**; `trailing` spans **:1838-1857** — `SchemaStatus` :1840, the preflight `Modal` :1843-1852, `exportNote` :1854; the `pendingMasterWrite` `Banner` is a sibling at **:1859-1881**. `MasterSheet` mounts the toolbar at **:1742**. grid.css lines are each one lower than cited: `> * { flex-shrink: 0 }` **:1190**, `.grow { display:none }` **:1192**, `.nds-grid-search` **:1193**, the viewport wrap **:1207-1210**, the container declaration **:1420** and its query **:1421-1424**.
**C4. There is no `.nds-pill.sm` rule.** `sm` is the *default* `.nds-pill` (`primitives.css:209-217`, `padding: 3px 9px`, `font-size: xs`), and `.nds-pill.md` (`:256-261`) is the 28px toolbar tier. The 28-vs-22px mismatch is real — `MasterSheet.tsx:1788` passes `md`, `FamilyBar.tsx:102` passes `sm` and both land in the same slot via `familyVerbs.status` (`FamilyBar.tsx:98-106`, rendered at `MasterSheet.tsx:1791` and `FamilyVariants.tsx:600`) — but the citation was not.
**C5. `Pill` wrapped in `InfoTip` is HOVER-ONLY, and the original specified it as the design.** `InfoTip.tsx:55-58` sets `tabIndex={children ? undefined : 0}` and `aria-label={children ? undefined : tip}`, with the docblock stating a wrapped control "is already focusable and already labelled". A `Pill` without `onClick` is a `<span>` — neither. So the reason for "Setup incomplete" is reachable by mouse only, today and under the original spec. Fixed in §5.
**C6. `max 2, asserted` is too small and a render-time assertion is a crash.** MasterSheet's slot can hold three at once: the schema pill, `no children`, and `problem`. And `problem` is `<span role="alert">` (`FamilyBar.tsx:105`) — a live region. Replacing `trailing` with a plain array silently drops a failure announcement. Fixed in §5.
**C7. The unit set dropped a live producer.** `ViewChip.noun` is `'columns' | 'variants' | 'combinations' | 'axes'` (`types.ts:295`), and `combinations` ships at `FamilyVariants.tsx:280`. Restored.
**C8. `viewChipColumnCountLabel` does not discard `noun` unconditionally.** `viewChips.ts:20` reads `if (chip.noun && chip.noun !== 'columns') return String(chip.count)`. The defect is narrower and sharper than stated: `ai-drafts` supplies **no** `noun` (`useAiDraftLayer.ts:67-83` — `count` is `pending`, `cells` are the draft cells), so its pending-draft count is silently replaced by a column count. Master's `missing-required` is the same shape: `count: cells` (`MasterSheet.tsx:760-766`) rendered as columns.
**C9. `viewChipCountLabel` already exists** (`viewChips.ts:71-73`) and returns the bare number. Part of §4 is a re-point, not a new function.
**C10. The U13 remedy as written would have LOWERED coverage. Measured.** I ran `SILENT_DISABLED_BASELINE=0 node scripts/check-silent-disabled.mjs "apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio"` → **19 sites**, including `SheetToolbar.tsx:202` (the chip: `disabled` + `title`) and `:225` (Export). Two consequences the original missed. (a) `BASELINE` is a single global `27` (`script:49`), so simply passing `_studio` as `process.argv[2]` passes on the spot (19 < 27), ratchets nothing, **and stops scanning the ads root entirely**. (b) The rule is `disabled && title && !aria-disabled` (`script:70`): Customise (`:222`) and Import (`:242`) carry no title at all, so they are silent *and* invisible to the ratchet. Fixed in §5.
**C11. The 09-11 measurement does not settle the padding tie, and I say so.** The measured `padding: 6px 16px` is the media query's `padding-block: 6px` longhand over patterns.css's `padding: 14px 16px` shorthand — evidence patterns.css won *at that date*, against a grid.css base rule that has since been rewritten to `padding: 0 6px`. Separately, `grid.css:975-982`'s own comment asserts `.nds-gridcard .nds-toolbar` computes `0 16px`, contradicting `patterns.css:497` from inside the same file. The specificity fix stands; the claim that today's winner is known does not.
**C12. `useContainerTier` already exists, is Owner-ratified, and has zero consumers.** `_studio/useContainerTier.ts` defines A ≥1300 / B 1000–1299 / C <1000 from layout-v2 §3.4 with a `ResizeObserver` and a change-only setState (`:19-45`); `/usr/bin/grep -rn "useContainerTier|tierFor("` returns only its own three lines. The original minted a second ladder in silence. Reconciled in §3.
**C13. The census cannot see a clipped status.** `check-control-census.mjs:400` iterates `button, input, [role="note"]`. A `Pill` is a `<span>`; `Views unavailable` (`SheetToolbar.tsx:177-182`) is a `<span>`. Neither is measured. Widening the selector is now part of the work.
**C14. Two smaller ones.** The `.grow` diagnosis: SheetToolbar passes `right` and no `children` (`:116`, `:128`), so `GridToolbar`'s hard-coded spacer (`GridToolbar.tsx:32`) lands immediately after the count — that is why it is switched off, not a generic flaw. And "one duplicate `aria-live` region goes away" is overstated: the count slot carries `role="status"` only on the blocked branch (`:118`); the footer strip is the live region (`GridSheet.tsx:130`).
## 0. What is proven, and what is not
I could not measure on screen: `check-control-census.mjs` and `check-layout-v2.mjs` drive a browser at `localhost:3000`, which is forbidden here, and the census refuses without a signed-in session (`studio-browser-auth.mjs:37-45`). What I did run: the silent-disabled ratchet (C10), and `/usr/bin/grep` set claims with positive controls.
**The bar has FOUR callers**, not two: `MasterSheet.tsx:1742`, `ChannelSheet.tsx:1787`, `FamilyVariants.tsx:550`, `variants/channel/ProjectionToolbar.tsx:45`. `ProjectionToolbar` appears in zero of the 180 findings (positive control: `SheetToolbar` = 88) and passes **no** `trailing`. Every change below is a four-surface change.
**ST-02's status, honestly.** The audit refuted it and corrected it to *low*: "clips silently, compact mitigation dead, no measured overflow at 1280–1728." The repo holds later browser measurements (`pes-claims.md:35921-35925`: children ~1670 against 1660, actions on a second row at y=188; the consequence swept at `:1353-1356` and `:1856-1858` — 2 rows at 1728, 3 rows and a 76px content box at 1440 and 1280, on the master sheet as well as variants). But those were taken against a bar that was `flex-wrap: wrap; padding: 6px 16px`, CSS the tree no longer has. They prove the **content is over budget**; they do not measure today's clip. **This design is justified by the two CONFIRMED findings (units, `trailing`) plus the latent one — not by an unmeasured overflow.** The census must run against a signed-in session before acceptance.
## 1. The defects being fixed
- **Nothing yields.** `grid.css:1190` sets `flex-shrink: 0` on every child; only `.nds-grid-search` has a floor (`:1193`). The actions group is pushed right by `margin-inline-start: auto` (`:1197-1206`), which resolves to zero the instant content overflows — pinned-right to off-screen in one step.
- **The mitigation is unreachable.** `compactLabel` (`FilterChip.tsx:48`, `primitives.css:1611-1612`) is revealed at 900px of the bar's inline size (`grid.css:1421-1424`), but the bar only stops being one row below a **1279px viewport** (`:1207`). And no sheet chip supplies it — the only producers are `ChannelProjection.tsx:98,106`.
- **The wrap rule is a viewport query.** The mapping dock removes 420px with no viewport change (measured, `pes-claims.md:36362`). A container query can see that; the container is already declared (`grid.css:1420`).
- **Three units, one word** (C8): Required prints `${x.columns.length} columns` and narrows COLUMNS (`SheetToolbar.tsx:164`); the chips print a column count and filter ROWS (`:193`); `ai-drafts` and `missing-required` count drafts and cells respectively and print columns.
- **`trailing` carries controls in two of four callers** — `ClassificationDialog`'s `Button variant="ghost"` (`ClassificationDialog.tsx:53`) and `SchemaStatus`'s `Button variant="secondary"` (`SchemaStatus.tsx:32`) — plus a `<Modal>`, two bare spans, and two Pills at different heights (C4).
- **Saving a view widens the bar mid-task.** `GridViewsMenu.tsx:198-238` returns the prompt *instead of* the trigger — an `Input style={{width:180}}` plus two buttons — and without the `.nds-grid-views` class, so the sheet's nowrap rules never reach it.
- **Two padding rules at identical specificity** (C11).
- **Two refusals nobody can read** (C10), and `Views unavailable` is a bare `<span>` with a `title`: mouse-only, no `tabIndex`, though `grid.css:1102` gives it a `:focus-visible` outline it can never show.
## 2. The fixed structure
`GridToolbar` gains an **opt-in** section API beside `count`/`children`/`right`, so all **67** current consumers stay byte-identical (measured; the precedent this codebase accepted for `GridViewsMenu manage="minimal"`).
```
[ IDENTITY ] [ FIND ] [ VIEWS ] [ FILTERS … ] <grow> [ STATUS ] [ ACTIONS ]
```
- **IDENTITY** — rows + descriptor. `flex: 0 1 auto; min-width: 0`, ellipsised, full text in `title`. Today `white-space: nowrap` and unshrinkable (`:1191`, `:1190`); it becomes the first thing that yields.
- **FIND** — unchanged (`flex: 1 1 180px; min-width: 80px; max-width: 340px`).
- **VIEWS** — the trigger and the Required chip.
- **FILTERS** — the state chips in a `flex: 0 1 auto; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden` box with a trailing `+N` chip. The only group that collapses by measurement.
- **`<grow>`** — a real `flex: 1 1 0` spacer between FILTERS and STATUS (C14).
- **STATUS** — typed, at most three (§5). Never collapses.
- **ACTIONS** — Customise · Export · Import · ⋯, `flex-shrink: 0`. The ⋯ can never be displaced.
`overflow-x: auto` becomes **`overflow: hidden`**: `scrollWidth` still reports overflow, so `check-control-census.mjs:399` still fires, but there is no scroll region absorbing the failure. Every popover in the bar already portals (`Menu.tsx` via `createPortal`, `InfoTip.tsx:66`), which the parent `.nds-gridcard`'s own `overflow: hidden` already forced.
## 3. The collapse ladder
Driven by the bar's inline size (container at `grid.css:1420`), never the viewport. Bar ≈ viewport − 66; a further −420 with the dock open.
| step | bar w | what changes |
|---|---|---|
| S0 | ≥ 1760 | everything expanded |
| S1 | 1420–1759 | identity → 16ch + ellipsis; views trigger drops its count; find basis 180 |
| S2 | 1120–1419 | chips → `compactLabel`; identity → count only; Required → no ratio; find basis 140 |
| S3 | 860–1119 | find basis 100; JS collapse begins |
| S4 | 600–859 | find → icon toggle expanding over FILTERS |
| S5 | < 600 | two rows (`height: auto; min-height: 40px` per row) |
Census sweep: 1728→S1, 1440→S2, 1280→S2, 1024→S3, 768→S4, 640/375/320→S5; 1440 + dock→S3.
**Reconciliation with `useContainerTier` (C12).** That hook is the ratified ladder for the merged chips+tabs row (layout-v2 §3.4), not for this bar; different rows may take different thresholds. But it must not stay a second implementation: the new DS hook subsumes it, `useContainerTier` re-exports A/B/C from it verbatim so §3.4's thresholds are unchanged, and the hook gains the hysteresis `useContainerTier` lacks — its change-only `setState` still oscillates when a drag sits on a boundary.
One JS step, a single `ResizeObserver` in the DS, measuring natural widths **once per content change** with a **24px hysteresis band**, hiding from the tail in a declared order: (1) state chips from the right into the `+N` menu — never the active chip, never a `danger`-toned chip with a non-zero count; (2) Export, then Import, into ⋯ with labels preserved; (3) Customise; (4) the Required chip into the Views menu as a `selectedId` entry (C1).
Chip order today is registration order (`contracts.tsx:1005-1021`) — a mount-order property with no test pinning it. `ViewChip` gains `rank: number` and the registry sorts by it.
## 4. The chip count contract
`ViewChip.count: number | null` and `noun` are replaced by:
```ts
count: { n: number; unit: 'cells' | 'rows' | 'columns' | 'variants' | 'combinations' | 'axes' | 'drafts' } | null
```
`combinations` is retained (C7). **The renderer prints the producer's `n` and never re-derives it** — `viewChipCountLabel` (C9) becomes the single renderer path. `null` still means "not counted" and still never renders as `0` (`viewChips.ts:61-70`, untouched).
If every visible chip declares the same unit, each prints a bare number and the unit appears once, in each chip's tooltip and accessible name (`viewChipSummary` already builds the breakdown, `:26-32`). If units differ, every chip prints its word. On the sheet all four are `cells` → four bare numbers. On variants they genuinely differ (`variants`, `variants`, `combinations`) so the words stay.
Required renders `Required 7/137` — a slash is unmistakably a ratio, ~28px narrower than `7 columns`, and removes the collision by construction. At S2 it drops to `Required`.
**A test pins the behaviour I am changing.** `viewChips.vitest.test.ts:91-94` asserts `viewChipColumnCountLabel` → `'3 columns'` under *"explains 63 mapping errors as three columns across 21 rows"*. That intent is coherent for one chip family and it is why the column unit exists. It should change because the same words now sit two pixels from a preset that narrows columns for real, and because the file has **zero** `noun` coverage (104 lines; positive control: `viewChipColumnCountLabel` at `:93`), so the branch that mis-renders `ai-drafts` was never exercised. The replacement test is the mutation that would have caught it: `{ n: 42, unit: 'cells' }` whose cells span 7 columns must render "42" and must never render "7".
`compactLabel` becomes required on `ViewChip`, which is a compile-error-driven change across the eleven chip literals (`MasterSheet` ×2, `ChannelSheet` ×4, `FamilyVariants` ×3, `ChannelProjection` ×3, `useAiDraftLayer` ×1 — the reveal moves onto the S2 step).
## 5. The typed status slot
`trailing?: ReactNode` is deleted and replaced by:
```ts
status?: readonly SheetStatus[]   // soft cap 3; the 4th and beyond coalesce, never throw
interface SheetStatus {
  tone: 'neutral' | 'info' | 'warning' | 'danger'
  label: string
  /** Rendered on a focusable trigger — never on a bare span. */
  detail?: string
}
```
Corrections C5 and C6 are load-bearing here. **`tone: 'danger'` renders inside `role="alert"`**, preserving the announcement `FamilyBar.tsx:105` makes today. **A `SheetStatus` with a `detail` renders as `Pill` with `onClick`** — `Pill` already becomes a real `<button>` when given one (`Pill.tsx:46-58`), which makes it focusable, so `InfoTip` labels it and the reason is keyboard-reachable. A pill with no `detail` stays a span. Size is fixed at `md` by the renderer, so the 28-vs-22 mismatch cannot recur. The cap is a dev-mode warning plus a lint rule; at runtime the surplus coalesces into one neutral `+N` pill. Callers cannot pass a node, so no verb can re-enter the slot.
`ClassificationDialog` and `SchemaStatus` are trigger+Modal units that own their own `open` state (`:53-54` and `:32-33`). Moving the verbs to `overflow` is therefore a split, not a prop move: each becomes a controlled dialog mounted in the footer beside `familyVerbs.dialogs` (`MasterSheet.tsx:1809`) plus an overflow item — exactly the shape CH.1 already used for the family verbs. The preflight `<Modal>` and the `exportNote` span leave the slot the same way; `exportNote` goes to `GridSheetNote` in the footer, where a sentence belongs.
`Views unavailable` stops being a bare span: the Views trigger renders **`aria-disabled`** with the reason on focus and on activation — the remedy shape `check-silent-disabled.mjs:29-38` prescribes — so the slot stays occupied and the reason is keyboard-reachable.
Disabled reasons become a type obligation: `importDisabled?: false | { reason: string }`, same for `customiseDisabled` and `exportDisabled`. This also fixes the confirmed lie: Export currently says "Nothing to export until the sheet has loaded" when the cause is `!has('products.export')` (`MasterSheet.tsx:1769`). **The transient blocked state keeps the native `disabled` attribute** — U13's docblock exempts "transient, resolves itself" — which keeps `sheetGridStates.vitest.test.ts:39` green, since it asserts `<button ... disabled="">` for All attributes, Customise, Export and Import while loading.
**The gate work (C10), stated as two changes, not one.** (i) Give `check-silent-disabled.mjs` a roots table with a baseline per root, so `_studio` is added at its own measured 19 without dropping the ads root or inheriting its slack. (ii) Add a second rule for `disabled` with no explanation on a control the caller marked as permission- or precondition-gated — the type obligation above is what makes that detectable at all. Without (ii), Customise and Import stay invisible to the ratchet forever.
## 6. The views prompt, and the cascade tie
`GridViewsMenu` keeps its trigger mounted and renders the save/rename/duplicate/delete prompt in a portalled popover anchored to it, via the DS's `usePopoverPosition`. No API change, so `/products/next` inherits it — shared means exactly the same.
The padding tie is settled by rule: the sheet's declaration becomes `.nds-gridcard.nds-grid-sheet .nds-toolbar` at (0,3,0), with `padding-block: 0` and `padding-inline: var(--nds-toolbar-gutter, 6px)`. **The default stays 6px** — the 6-vs-16 gutter question is open (UXLD-3; `--nds-gutter-content` has zero `var()` readers, confirmed against a control of 11,985 `var(--nds-` uses) and 16px costs 20px of bar width. Minting the token makes the answer one value, not six edits. This is exactly the audit's own `fixCritique` on UXLD-5.
## 7. What it costs an operator
Selection count moves to the footer, where it already renders (`GridSheet.tsx:130-141`) beside the verbs that use it. Classification and Requirements cost one extra click, but the staleness *fact* stays visible as a Pill — and gains a keyboard-reachable reason it does not have today. Export/Import cost one extra click below 1120px, Customise below 860px; both already open drawers. Chips beyond the fit cost one click; the active chip and a live danger chip never collapse. The views count and the identity descriptor go to `title` and the ScopeBar — the descriptor is the one real information loss, recoverable on hover. Four sheet chips trade "N columns" for a bare number with the unit one hover away, recovering ~150px and removing the collision.
## 8. What I did not do
I did not touch ScopeBar (C2 — it is a different bar, not a blast radius), the 213px chrome stack (CS-1, the Owner's to re-baseline), or `useHeaderCollapse` (ratified at `docs/2026-09-01-layout-v2-spec.md:508-530`). I did not merge Export and Import into `Transfer ▾`: that removes a control CH.1 named, so it is a question. And I measured nothing on screen. The census must run signed-in, with the widened selector from C13, before any of this is accepted.

#### Presence on screen — corrected: where the lifecycle verbs live, what they say, and how a closed coordinate renders

The placement design is sound and survives review: lifecycle hangs on the alias band's already-wired ⋯, on a new coordinate-level Listing state surface, and on a plural COLLECT → PLAN → CONFIRM → APPLY flow — with the sheet toolbar and CH.1 untouched. Twelve claims about current behaviour were wrong and are corrected here, each re-measured by me. The load-bearing ones: the ratchet fix does NOT clear the ratchet (34 → 33 against baseline 32); `.nds-menu-item` does not exist as a class, so the proposed danger rule would be a silent no-op; `SheetToolbar` is a lane file, not a DS component; two different `BulkActionBar`s were cited as one; the typed-DELETE gate is case-insensitive; the D9-safe fallback would mount a coordinate-keyed panel on a surface that renders with no coordinate; and one claimed DS gap is a one-line reorder in `runAction`, not a new type.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- `design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts` DOES NOT EXIST. The DS owns exactly one state vocabulary — `readiness.ts` (row: ready|missing|errors|live|unlisted; scope: ready|warn|blocked|absent) — and it measures pre-publish completeness. Add a sibling pure module exporting PresenceIntent, PresenceFact, PresenceVerdict and `presenceMeta(intent, fact, observedAt) -> {tone,label,hint,verdict}`, plus a `PresencePill`. It must NOT early-return null the way `CompletenessPill` does at IdentityBand.tsx:137 — UNKNOWN renders a neutral 'Not checked' pill with its as-of, because a blank is indistinguishable from a load failure.
