The drawer's one-line re-export (`_studio/drawer/readinessMeta.ts`) is the shipped precedent for how a lane consumes it.
- CORRECTED SELECTOR — `MenuItemDef` cannot express severity or grouping (Menu.tsx:8-50 is {id,label,icon,disabled,onSelect,href,target,title,description,separator}). Add `tone?: Tone`. The CSS rule must be `.nds-menu button[data-tone="danger"], .nds-menu a[role="menuitem"][data-tone="danger"] { color: var(--nds-danger-strong) }` — NOT `.nds-menu-item[data-tone="danger"]`, which matches nothing: Menu.tsx:82-84 emits only `.nds-menu-item-text` / `-name` / `-desc` and the row itself is a bare button or `a[role=menuitem]` styled by descendant selectors (components.css:712-713). Positive control: 20+ `.nds-menu*` rules exist in that stylesheet, so the absence is measured, not a grep miss.
- Both menu adapters DROP `GridAction.danger` — measured `/usr/bin/grep -c danger menuAdapters.tsx` = 0 against a control of 5 for `label`, contradicting registry.ts:183's written promise that destructive verbs are 'grouped apart and rendered with danger tone by every adapter'. Map `action.danger -> tone:'danger'` in `actionMenuItems`, `cssClasses:['nds-menu-danger']` in `actionContextMenu`, and emit a `separator` before the first danger item in both. The grouping precedent already ships in the AG adapter (menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:93).
- `actionMenuItems` puts a disabled verb's reason in `title` (menuAdapters.tsx:86) — hover-only, and Menu's own keyboard navigation skips disabled items (Menu.tsx:122 and :140 query `[role="menuitem"]:not([disabled])`). The DS contradicts itself in its own source: Menu.tsx:24 says `title` is 'Supplementary only — never the sole home of a reason; see description'. The channel lane's hand-written items already do it correctly (ChannelSheet.tsx:1722,1729,1737). Change both adapters to `description: reason`, keeping `title` supplementary.
- `MenuAdapterOptions` cannot say that a row IS a container — `offered()` (menuAdapters.tsx:55-58) filters ROW+SELECTION and deliberately excludes context verbs, so a `contextOf('alias-group')` verb can never reach the alias band even though registry.ts:29-33 minted that axis for exactly this surface. Add `contextAxisFor?: (row: T) => ContextAxis | null`; when it answers, offer that axis's context verbs INSTEAD of row/selection verbs. Ordinary rows stay isolated. The band's ⋯ is already wired end to end (IdentityBand.tsx:88-97 renders from menuItems; ChannelSheet.tsx:1440 passes it; :992 gates it to variants; AliasBandCell.tsx:59-61 states the intent), so this is the only change needed.
- `GridAction` has no REACH and `ActionImpact` has no REVERSIBILITY — one inert bit, `danger?: boolean` (registry.ts:183-184). Add `reach: 'local'|'local-destructive'|'channel'` to `GridAction` (it gates availability and grouping before any preflight) and `reversal: { verb: string; fidelity: 'exact'|'lossy'|'none' }` to `ActionImpact` (it must reach the renderer). `validateImpact` takes only the impact (registry.ts:264) so it cannot see an action-level field: add a sibling `validateAction(action)` refusing a `reach:'channel'` verb whose preflight returned no `reversal`. The finding that proposed this is REFUTED as a defect; it is proposed as a contract enhancement on the different argument that every lie in the roster is an authored sentence.
- SMALLER THAN CLAIMED — the typed-refusal gap is mostly a one-line reorder, not a new type. `runAction.ts` refuses on `validateImpact` (rule 3, :70-73) BEFORE it refuses on `impact.unavailable` (rule 4, :74-76), so `offerVerb`'s honest sentence (channelActions.ts:249-251) is shadowed by the developer string 'preflight failed but still asked for a confirmation' (registry.ts:270-272 -> useActionPress.tsx:57). Swapping rules 3 and 4 surfaces the real reason today with no new field. Only `refusal?: { reason; whatWouldFix?; verb?: { label; actionId } }` — specifically the OFFERED VERB, which turns sheet/master/familyActions.ts:454's dead end into one click — is genuinely new. The reorder is a behaviour change: read runAction.vitest.test.ts and registry.vitest.test.ts first.
- `ActionConfirm` / `useActionConfirm` are exported only from `grid/index.ts:54`; `design-system/components/index.ts` has zero matches for confirm/dialog/alert. The presence panel and the drawer's Listings pane are not grid hosts. Move `ActionConfirm.tsx` to `design-system/components/` (it imports only Modal, Button, Input) and re-export from `grid/index.ts`. Retires `useDrawerConfirm` and both raw `window.confirm` calls (contracts.tsx:609 — corrected from :607 — and ManageAxesDialog.tsx:81, which renders in the OS language against the English-console rule).
- `ActionConfirm` renders three blocks and cannot show TARGETS or REVERSAL. Add a leading 'Targets' block itemising each coordinate by name with its external id plus the gate note verbatim, and a trailing 'Putting it back' block rendered FROM `impact.reversal` — a danger-toned strip immediately above the footer when fidelity is 'none'. A lane may not author the reversal sentence. `Modal size="lg"` is available and valid for the TargetPicker (Size = xs|sm|md|lg|xl in primitives/size.ts; `.nds-modal.lg` at components.css:415).
- BLOCKING — the DS primary Button has no perceptible keyboard focus. `.nds-btn:focus-visible` (primitives.css:33-37) sets `border-color: var(--nds-primary)` + `box-shadow: var(--nds-focus-ring)`; on `.nds-btn.primary` the border is ALREADY that colour (:59-62) so the change is a no-op, and `--nds-focus-ring` is `0 0 0 2px rgb(var(--nds-focus-rgb) / 0.12)` with `--nds-focus-rgb: 31 111 222` (tokens.css:69, :210). Every REVERSIBLE lifecycle confirmation's Confirm button is `variant="primary"` (ActionConfirm.tsx:94 keys danger tone on `needsTyping`, so only the typed confirms get a colour change). Fix before any lifecycle confirm ships.
- `scripts/check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs` CRASHES — I ran it: `Error: has no block matching /\.dark \{([\s\S]*?)\n\}/` at :81, because tokens.css:450 declares a compound `.dark, .dark body:has(...), .dark body .nds-theme-responsive {`. No gate can currently vouch for a new presence tone's contrast in dark; hand-compute from token source until it is repaired. Its RED convicts nothing today.
- CORRECTED — `check-grid-kit-ratchet --check` is RED at 34 vs baseline 32, and the studio's import swap does NOT clear it. I re-ran the gate's own predicate over its own file set: 34 importers, exactly ONE in the studio (`_studio/channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3`, importing DataGrid from `@/design-system/components`). Swapping it to `@/design-system/grid/datagrid` (same Column / DataGridProps contract on the AG engine, grid/datagrid/index.ts:1-9, uncounted) yields 33 — still ROSE. The other riser is outside this lane (the 34 are dominated by marketing/ads-console/** and fleet/**). Wave 1 may claim the studio contributes zero; it may NOT claim a green ratchet. Note also that the studio's own precedent for small tables is NexusGrid, not DataGrid (AnalyticsAdsTab.tsx:55-60 explains why), so any new small table in the presence panel should use the grid/datagrid shim or NexusGrid, never components/DataGrid.

##### API
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence — every real coordinate for the product, each carrying the five-level key, intent (with provenance when derived), fact + observedAt + observedVia, derived verdict, gate mode, and per-verb availability WITH its typed reach and reversal. The UI never computes availability; a preflight is never a client guess. Feeds the presence grid, the alias band pill and the drawer's Listings pane.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/one?channel=&market=&accountId=&aliasKey= — one coordinate, 400 on any missing dimension, reusing requireCoordinate's shape (product-studio.routes.ts:180-187, ruling #573) widened to require account and alias. Feeds the alias band without a second full read.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/plan { verb, targets[], options } — the server-side preflight behind the TargetPicker. Returns an ActionImpact-shaped plan: per-coordinate consequences, reach, reversal{verb,fidelity}, gate mode, server-computed fan-out named as coordinates (never a count), refusals each NAMING the verb that resolves them, the operational-preflight result for any local-destructive verb, plus planId, per-target expectedVersion, and a short-TTL preflightToken binding the enumerated five-level set. Writes nothing. Its response becomes ActionImpact.payload so `run` applies the approved snapshot — ruling #118, correctly cited: named at sheet/master/familyActions.vitest.test.ts:246 and :329 and exercised at :59, :73, :253, :337, :374 (the reviewed draft's citation of :301-311 was a different rule, the failed-read refusal).
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/apply { planId, preflightToken, confirmPhrase? } — applies exactly that plan. Refuses a missing, expired or set-mismatched token; 409s per target on version drift. Per-target results in the shipped MarketOfferResult shape (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:46-55) with NO aggregate success field, so the UI cannot render a green summary it was not given. confirmPhrase must be matched EXACTLY and CASE-SENSITIVELY: apps/web/src/app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx:1129 currently uppercases before comparing, so 'delete' arms the app's most destructive verb today. THIS IS THE ONLY WRITER — plan/apply must not become the fourth ChannelListing removal path.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/verify { coordinates[], reason } — READS the channel and writes only the fact columns. Four-valued, with 'could-not-ask' load-bearing. Extends the existing operator-triggered read path (ChannelTruthPanel + live-channel-images refresh) rather than opening a fourth live-read seam, reusing getListingsItem / getItemListingStatus. Enqueued automatically after every lifecycle write so the receipt's third stage has something real to report; also raised by hand from the band's 'Verify on the channel' item.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/history — ProductEvent for the coordinate plus retired identities from ListingIdentity, feeding the presence panel's per-row history disclosure. No new event store.
- PATCH /api/products/:id/aliases/:aliasId { status: 'ACTIVE' | 'ARCHIVED' } — the one new body field the band's 'Archive locally…' / 'Restore' pair needs. Today only archiveAlias exists (listing-alias.service.ts:194-198) and nothing writes ACTIVE back, so the band item would be a one-way door. Refused while the alias holds identity with a non-terminal intent; inert until PES.5-ii drops the legacy indexes (listing-alias.service.ts:98 still raises AliasCreationBlockedError).
- PROJECTION, NOT A MIGRATION — the presence read must carry offerClosedAt / offerClosedBy / offerCloseReason in LISTING_SELECT (studio-sheet.service.ts:464-478; the columns already exist), plus lastSyncedAt and the fact's observedAt on the channel sheet's SheetListing mirror and on AliasGroup (sheet/channel/types.ts:458-480 currently omits both), or the alias band physically cannot render an as-of.
- AUTHORISATION on every route above, as an explicit in-file preHandler naming the permission, and as a wave-1 precondition on the existing ones — the global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (rbac-hook.ts:9-13,31: resolve + LOG what would be denied, but allow), and amazon-flat-file.routes.ts contains ZERO occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate over a route that hard-deletes ChannelListing rows from a caller-supplied target array. Securing the operator path with a typed ASIN while leaving the endpoint open secures the wrong half.
- PRECONDITIONS P1-P9, all nine Wave-1 blockers rather than follow-ups. P1 enqueue the delist rows with productId:null and channelListingId:null (both already nullable) and report channelCascadeDispatched read back AFTER commit, not the pre-commit create count. P2 the worker must read targetChannel from the queue row it just loaded, not from job.data (bullmq-sync.worker.ts:88,146 vs outbound-enqueue.ts:52-58). P3 Amazon delist must resolve the seller SKU (Offer.sku, or product.sku) and never pass externalListingId, which flat-file-pull.service.ts:267 populates with the ASIN. P4 eBay delist must resolve the connection that OWNS the ItemID, not tryResolveConnection({channel:'EBAY', primary:true}), and must stop classifying 'item cannot be accessed' / 'invalid item' as success (channel-delist.service.ts:227,231,290-293). P5 pushAmazonPrice must select and honour offerClosedAt/presenceIntent — today it selects only {id, platformAttributes} (pricing-outbound.service.ts:140-149) and patchListingPrice issues op:'replace' on /attributes/purchasable_offer (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:687-699), the very instance close removed with op:'delete', so a reprice silently resurrects a closed offer and stamps IN_SYNC; extract the refusal at outbound-sync.service.ts:904-910 as one shared assertPushAllowed(). P6 applyDelistResultToQueue must set holdUntil on a retryable park, and dispatchSync must REFUSE lifecycle syncTypes outright rather than routing them into the ordinary update path. P7 no adapter may escalate: remove the Amazon UNPUBLISH->DELETE fallback (channel-delist.service.ts:155-158) and the duck-typed Shopify updateProduct->deleteProduct fallback (:192-203); refuse instead. P8 narrow both removal services to the full five-level coordinate INCLUDING the pre-read (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:636-639 and :645-647; amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:52-55 and :60-62) — narrowing the delete alone is strictly worse than today; same for amazon-market-offer.service.ts:66-68, sync-control.routes.ts:895-898 and marketplaces.routes.ts:317's hardcoded aliasKey:''. P9 move isLiveOnChannel (UNCHANGED, as identityHeld — it is pinned as intended by sheet/master/listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:15-32 against a measured family of 20 non-ACTIVE eBay rows with real ItemIDs) and the new sellingRisk into packages/shared, and repoint products-catalog.routes.ts:1679-1682 and :1857-1860 at them.
- REQUIRED BEFORE ANY relist PROMISE — exercise restoreToDraft against a captured request and read the BODY, not the status. The route ships at product-studio.routes.ts:938-946 with ZERO web callers, and SNAPSHOT_FIELDS deliberately excludes externalListingId (listing-snapshot.service.ts:70-83). 'Recoverable with loss' is a promise this path keeps.
- DS-SIDE, NOT AN API CHANGE, BUT A WAVE-1 BLOCKER AND CHEAPER THAN THE DRAFT CLAIMED — reorder rules 3 and 4 in runAction.ts (:70-76) so impact.unavailable is surfaced before validateImpact's redundant guard. This alone makes every existing lane's honest refusal reachable, including offerVerb's (channelActions.ts:249-251), with no new type. Verify against runAction.vitest.test.ts and registry.vitest.test.ts before landing; it is a behaviour change, not a tidy-up.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceIntent / presenceIntentAt / presenceIntentBy / presenceIntentReason. Nullable and NEVER backfilled: null means 'never stated', and the read derives a provisional intent from the legacy columns and carries it on the wire WITH its provenance, so no surface can present a derivation as an operator decision. The presence pill's hint says which it is.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.channelFact / channelFactAt / channelFactVia / channelFactDetail. Null = UNKNOWN. NO BACKFILL: rows written before these columns existed derive UNKNOWN, never DRAFT — the eBay end path already writes {listingStatus:'ENDED', offerActive:false, externalListingId:null} (ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1961,1984) and a naive derivation reads that as 'never published'. Deliberately distinct from lastSyncedAt, whose own header (studio-sheet.service.ts:1416-1420) warns it records when a sync last RAN.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.presenceEffectiveFrom / presenceUntil. Wave 1 STORES and DISPLAYS these and nothing executes them; the surface must say plainly that the date is a note, not an alarm, until the Owner rules on a scheduler.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.endedAt / endedBy / endedReason: 'ended by us, deliberately, then', distinct from listingStatus happening to say ENDED. The presence pill's as-of reads from this, not from lastSyncedAt.
- ADDITIVE — new model ListingIdentity (the tombstone AND the registry, because it is READ, which is what makes RELEASED mean something). CORRECTED FIELD LIST — the reviewed draft dropped workspaceId from the fields while keeping it in the unique key: workspaceId String @default(dbgenerated("NULLIF(current_setting('nexus.workspace_id', true), '')")) [412 of 432 models carry this]; id; productId String as a PLAIN STRING with NO Prisma relation so it survives the purge (the AuditLog property at products-catalog.routes.ts:1902-1908); sku; channel; marketplace; channelConnectionId String?; aliasKey String @default(""); externalListingId; externalParentId; fulfillmentMethod String?; offerSku String?; firstSeenAt; lastSeenAt; retiredAt DateTime?; retiredReason; retiredBy; doNotRecreate Boolean @default(false); lastSnapshotId String?. @@unique([workspaceId, channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, aliasKey, externalListingId]). READ BY every create/publish path (46 non-test channelListing.create/upsert sites in apps/api/src): a RELEASED or doNotRecreate identity refuses recreation at that coordinate without an explicit operator override.
- MISLABELLED IN THE REVIEWED DRAFT AND MOVED OUT OF SCHEMA — offerClosedAt / offerClosedBy / offerCloseReason ALREADY EXIST as ChannelListing columns. Adding them to LISTING_SELECT (studio-sheet.service.ts:464-478) and to the wire types is a PROJECTION change with NO migration. Filing it under Schema as 'ADDITIVE' invites an implementer to author a migration that would fail. `/usr/bin/grep` finds ZERO occurrences of offerClosedAt in all of apps/web/src, which is why a deliberately closed market renders as selling.
- NOT PROPOSED — a new ListingPresenceEvent table. ProductEvent already exists, is already FK-free by design (schema.prisma:15475-15478), already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing', is already emitted and already read by audit-log.routes.ts. A third store would re-create the confirmed defect that lifecycle history is unreachable because Activity reads the event store while lifecycle writes the audit log. EbayListingIndex already answers 'what ItemID did we retire' for eBay.
- NOT DECLARED — ChannelListing.variationExcluded. family-projection.service.ts:389 cites migration `20260911a_vp2_variation_excluded`; the migrations directory holds only 20260911020000_information_formula_destination, 20260911_category_taxonomies and five 20260912_lx* folders — the cited folder DOES NOT EXIST. The raw-SQL helpers stay; declaring a column the database may not have is an outage for every concurrent session.
- SEQUENCING (binding, not optional) — apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma. MEMORY records that apps/api/.env pointed at a local Docker pg on 09-11 and that only Product.version (54 vs 51) separated the two. Discriminate, never infer.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — DOES IT STAND AS WRITTEN? This proposal does NOT need it to bend: lifecycle lives on the alias band ⋯, the row menu, the presence panel and the drawer, never in the sheet toolbar's overflow. Three measured facts if the Owner ever wants lifecycle reachable from the sheet: MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-50) carries no tone, no severity and no group; there is no `.nds-menu` danger rule in any DS stylesheet (positive control: 20+ `.nds-menu*` rules exist), so 'End the listing on Amazon IT' would render byte-identical to 'Reload'; and SheetToolbar.tsx:254 blanket-disables every overflow item while a write is pending, so the verb would go dead during autosave with no reason shown. The smallest amendment keeps CH.1's actual rule (no new buttons) and asks only that the overflow gain a typed tone plus a separator group — `separator` already exists and one adapter already groups (menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:93). Note the amendment touches TWO files, not one: MenuItemDef in the DS and the lane's own SheetToolbar.tsx. This is the Owner's ruling to make.
- D9 — is a per-channel 'Listing state' tab a NEW OBJECT (my reading: the sheet is content, presence is state) or a SECOND WAY IN? The precedent cuts against me and I want that on the record: StudioSubheader.tsx:34-37 already refuses a per-channel Variants item on exactly these grounds, and StudioHeader.tsx:87-97 encodes D9 as 'the Datasheet is the sheet … A second way to reach a thing the grid already is makes the page bigger, not more capable.' If the Owner reads presence as a second route to the sheet, the whole plural half of the model is unbuildable and only the per-coordinate verbs survive.
- THE RATCHET. Wave 1 removes the studio's only contribution and the gate STAYS RED at 33 against baseline 32 because of another lane's importer. Does the Owner want Wave 1 held until that lane clears, the baseline re-frozen with a stated reason, or Wave 1 landing with an attributed red? 'Never bypass pre-push' makes this a decision, not a detail.
- SCHEDULING. Intent carries effectiveFrom/until; Wave 1 stores and displays them and nothing executes them. The studio's one scheduling surface is behind a hardcoded false and its cron is off (hub ruling #4: prod boot log says 'scheduled-image-publish: disabled'; ScheduledImagePublish has ZERO rows ever). Five sc:scheduling-bulk findings are CONFIRMED, one critical. Does the Owner want a scheduler built for presence, or should 'back next season' remain a date the operator reads and acts on by hand? I have NOT designed an executor.
- THE FBA DEFAULT. amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63 refuses close/reopen on ANY FBA signal, fail-closed, on the Owner's stated rule that Amazon owns FBA logistics end to end. `offerScope` makes 'withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA' expressible. Does the Owner want offerScope:'FBM' permitted on a mixed coordinate, or does fail-closed stand for every offer on an FBA product?
- THE LEGACY LISTING HUB. MasterDataTab.tsx ships a working Listing Hub today: one row per coordinate over GET /api/products/:id/all-listings, a Live/Draft/Paused/Ended/Error vocabulary already separate from readiness, and a shipped plural verb ('Pause non-IT'). The studio swap is therefore a capability-LOSS event unless presence lands first. Hold the swap until Wave 2, or keep the legacy hub mounted alongside?
- RBAC ENFORCE. Every permission guard this proposal adds is a log line while rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (rbac-hook.ts:9-13,31). Presence's irreversible verb is the strongest argument yet for flipping NEXUS_RBAC_MODE=enforce, and flipping it is an ops decision with blast radius across every route. Gate `release` on that flip, or ship behind an in-file guard that works regardless?
- IDENTITY CHANGE AND ACCOUNT MOVE — declared out of scope by me, and I want that ratified. channelConnectionId is in both unique keys and no verb in this model changes it, so agency handover, entity change and consolidating two eBay accounts have no writer; same for relaunching under a new SKU keeping the ASIN and its reviews. critic:scenarios named identity change as an entire missing axis; I have not designed it because it is an identity operation on a live coordinate, not a state transition, and folding it in would double the model.
- CROSS-PRODUCT STOP-SALE / RECALL. Presence gives 'stop selling everywhere, keep everything, record why' as a plural plan, but the API is product-scoped and a recall arrives on a LOT. LotRecall exists in the schema and its only downstream effect today is that FEFO consume skips the lot. File it as a separate programme on /products under D9, or expose a product-scoped stop-sale now and accept that the recall path stays manual?
- WOOCOMMERCE CONTAINMENT — confirm the scope. Out of scope as a channel (Owner, 2026-09-12). The one containment measure proposed: dispatchSync must REFUSE lifecycle syncTypes rather than route them into an update path (outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623), and Woo presence renders read-only with gateNote's existing honest sentence. Confirming this is containment and not channel work.
- ETSY. PublishReadiness (channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:317-322) describes amazon, ebay and shopify only, and modeForChannel (:323-332) returns null for Etsy AND WooCommerce. Either it gains an etsy branch, or Wave 1 states plainly that Etsy presence is READ-ONLY (Nexus holds a read-only Etsy client at etsy/read-client.ts:5). It must not be silently caught by the WooCommerce containment rule.

##### Risks
- THE RATCHET STAYS RED AFTER WAVE 1. Measured with the gate's own predicate: 34 importers vs baseline 32; the studio owns exactly one (ReadinessPanel.tsx:3). Swapping it gives 33. The pre-push gate will still fail on another lane's work, and 'never bypass pre-push' binds. Either that lane clears its importer first, or Wave 1 ships with a stated, attributed red — it must not be described as a clean gate.
- AN HONEST MODEL MAKES THE STUDIO LOOK EMPTIER. Most coordinates read UNKNOWN on day one where today they read a confident green 'Live'. That is the change, not a regression, and the Owner has to want it before it ships.
- THE `runAction` RULE REORDER IS A BEHAVIOUR CHANGE, NOT A TIDY-UP. Every verb that currently sets `unavailable` with a non-'none' level starts surfacing its own sentence instead of the developer string. runAction.vitest.test.ts and registry.vitest.test.ts must be read and re-run before it lands; a test may pin the current message.
- EVERY GEOMETRY CLAIM IS UNMEASURED. The four gates that measure the screen drive a dev server wired to the PRODUCTION API. Two pills at the 240px band FLOOR (bandWidth.ts:35; the band is clamped to [240,420], not fixed at 240), the band ⋯ width at ten items with two-line descriptions, the presence panel above ReadinessPanel, and any bar-height change are build-time measurements, not assumptions to ship on. check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs crashes, so no gate can vouch for a new presence tone in dark.
- A NEW TAB IS FOUR FILES (StudioTabId in _studio/types.ts:46, STUDIO_TAB_LABELS + ICONS in navigation.tsx:12, a StudioTabHost branch, a studioChannelViewHref entry) — AND THE SUBHEADER ALREADY CARRIES A D9 REFUSAL IN THAT EXACT POSITION. StudioSubheader.tsx:34-37 refuses a per-channel Variants item on D9 grounds. If the Owner reads presence the same way, the plural half of the model is unbuildable and only per-coordinate verbs survive.
- THE D9-SAFE FALLBACK IS UNSAFE AS DRAFTED. ErrorsSyncTab.tsx:48 returns `<ReadinessPanel />` with NO coordinate. A coordinate-keyed presence grid mounted there renders coordinate-less on master and on any unresolved destination — reintroducing the exact ambient-coordinate hazard the design exists to kill. It must render an EmptyState naming the missing dimension.
- THE SHEET OVERFLOW FORCE-DISABLES ITS ITEMS DURING A PENDING WRITE (SheetToolbar.tsx:254 maps `blocked ? {...item, disabled:true}`). Any future decision to put a lifecycle verb there inherits a silent dead control during autosave, with no reason rendered.
- THE TYPED-DELETE GATE IS WEAKER THAN REPORTED: `typed.trim().toUpperCase() === 'DELETE'` (apps/web/src/app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx:1129) accepts lowercase. Any migration to `confirmPhrase` must be exact-match and case-sensitive, or it ships the same weakness under a new name.
- `presenceEffectiveFrom` / `presenceUntil` are STORED AND DISPLAYED with nothing executing them. A declared intent nothing enforces is precisely the failure the intent-as-a-lock section exists to prevent. The surface must say plainly that the date is a note, not an alarm, until the Owner rules on a scheduler.
- THE PRESENCE PILL SHARES `IdentityBand.trailing` WITH `CompletenessPill`, WHOSE EARLY RETURN AT :137 THIS PROPOSAL CRITICISES. Specify explicitly that presence always renders and completeness may be absent, so the band trail is never empty on a band row — otherwise the honest pill inherits the dishonest blank it was meant to replace.

##### The specification
# CORRECTIONS
I re-derived every cited line with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed`, and ran the two permitted instruments plus the red test. Twelve corrections; six change what an implementer would build.
**1. The ratchet fix does NOT clear the ratchet — load-bearing.** I re-ran the gate's own predicate over its own file set (tracked + untracked `.tsx`/`.ts` under `apps/web/src/`, tests and `design-system/` excluded, comments stripped). Result: **34 importers, of which exactly ONE is in the studio** — `_studio/channel-ops/ReadinessPanel.tsx:3`. Swapping it yields 33 against baseline 32: the gate stays **RED**. The remaining riser is outside this lane (the 34 are dominated by `marketing/ads-console/**` and `fleet/**`). Wave 1 may claim *the studio contributes zero importers*; it may not claim a green ratchet. Say so, or an implementer hits a red pre-push and blames this design. Verified as a by-product: `sheet/FormulaBulkDialog.tsx:6` and `sheet/FormulaHistoryDialog.tsx:6` already import from `grid/datagrid` and are correctly uncounted.
**2. `.nds-menu-item` is not a class.** `Menu.tsx:82-84` emits only `.nds-menu-item-text` / `-name` / `-desc`; the row itself is a bare `<button>` or `<a role="menuitem">`, styled by descendant selectors (`components.css:712-713`). The proposed `.nds-menu-item[data-tone="danger"]` would match nothing and ship as a silent no-op — the same class of defect as `GridAction.danger` being inert. Correct rule: `.nds-menu button[data-tone="danger"], .nds-menu a[role="menuitem"][data-tone="danger"] { color: var(--nds-danger-strong) }`. Positive control: 20+ `.nds-menu*` rules exist in that stylesheet, so the absence is real and not a grep miss.
**3. `SheetToolbar` is a LANE file, not a DS component** — `_studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx`. CH.1's verbatim text is at `:15-20` ✓ and `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` at `:104` ✓, but the CH.1 amendment therefore touches **two** places (`MenuItemDef` in the DS **and** this lane file), not one. Also measured there: `:254` force-disables every overflow item when the sheet is `blocked` — a lifecycle verb in that overflow would go silently dead during a pending write. That is a second, independent argument for the CH.1 question.
**4. Two different `BulkActionBar`s were cited as one file.** The DS pattern is `design-system/patterns/BulkActionBar.tsx` (`if (count <= 0) return null` at `:19` ✓). The typed-DELETE dialog is `apps/web/src/app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx` (`:1104` fetches `hard-delete-preflight` — I confirmed it is that endpoint's only web caller; `:1129` arms). And `:1129` is `typed.trim().toUpperCase() === 'DELETE'` — **case-insensitive**, so `delete` arms it. Worse than stated.
**5. One claimed DS gap is a one-line reorder, not a new type.** `runAction.ts` refuses on `validateImpact` (rule 3, `:70-73`) **before** it refuses on `impact.unavailable` (rule 4, `:74-76`). Swapping those two blocks delivers `offerVerb`'s honest sentence (`channelActions.ts:249-251`) to the operator **today**, with no new field — the developer string is reached only because the redundant guard runs first. Only `refusal.verb` (the offered next verb, which turns `sheet/master/familyActions.ts:454`'s dead end into a click) is genuinely new. Ship the reorder in Wave 1; read `runAction.vitest.test.ts` first, because it is a behaviour change.
**6. The D9-safe fallback reintroduces the ambient-coordinate hazard.** `ErrorsSyncTab.tsx:48` is `if (!coordinate) return <ReadinessPanel />`. Mounting a coordinate-keyed presence panel above it means presence renders **coordinate-less** on master and whenever the destination has not resolved — exactly the targeting model this design exists to kill. The fallback must render a DS `EmptyState` naming the missing dimension, never a presence grid.
**7. Line-number corrections** (each opened): `contracts.tsx` market fallback `defaultMarket(baseOptions)` is at **:652** (not :649); the `readLastMarket()` correcting effect is **:884-891** (not :888-893) — the one-tick-divergence argument is sound; `window.confirm` is **:609** (not :607). `GridSheet` renders `{toolbar}{children}{footer}` at **:69-71**. `ScopeBar.onAdd` at **:79** and **:269-272**; `ScopeBarReadiness` at **:26-38**. `ListingsPane` Status at **:101-104**, Offer at **:121-135**.
**8. Ruling #118 is not "asserted five times at `familyActions.vitest.test.ts:301-311,331-337`."** In `sheet/master/familyActions.vitest.test.ts`, #118 is named at **:246** and **:329**; five call sites run with the approved impact (`:59, :73, :253, :337, :374`). `:301-311` is a *different* rule — a failed listings read must refuse rather than soften. Cite it accurately or a reviewer reads the whole plan/apply argument as padded.
**9. Paths:** `familyActions.ts` and `listingRisk.ts` are under `_studio/sheet/master/`.
**10. The band is 240–420px, not 240px.** `bandWidth.ts:35` `BAND_WIDTH_FLOOR = 240`, clamped to `[240, 420]` (`:41`). The unmeasurable question is whether two pills fit at the **floor** — narrower and more precise than stated.
**11. A projection change was filed under Schema.** `offerClosedAt` / `offerClosedBy` / `offerCloseReason` already exist as columns; adding them to `LISTING_SELECT` is a wire change. Labelling it `ADDITIVE` under a schema heading invites a migration that would fail. Moved to API. Also restored `workspaceId` to the `ListingIdentity` field list, which the draft dropped while keeping it in the `@@unique`.
**12. "One nav item" is four files.** A new tab needs `StudioTabId` (`_studio/types.ts:46`), `STUDIO_TAB_LABELS` + `STUDIO_TAB_ICONS` (`navigation.tsx:12`), a `StudioTabHost` branch and a `studioChannelViewHref` entry.
**What I re-verified and found SOUND**, so a reviewer does not re-litigate it. Typecheck **green**: `npx tsc --noEmit -p apps/web/tsconfig.json --incremental false` → exit 0, zero diagnostics. `drawer/layers.vitest.test.ts` **RED**: 1 failed / 17 passed, `unmapped ValueSource members: channelSnapshot` — `attribute-resolver.ts:25` defines it, `drawer/types.ts:352-376` has no entry. `check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs` **crashes** at `:81` (`tokens.css:450` is a compound `.dark` selector); its red convicts nothing. `menuAdapters.tsx` has **0** `danger` against a control of **5** for `label`; `MenuItemDef` (`Menu.tsx:8-50`) carries no tone and no group; `registry.ts:183` promises a property the code never had. `menuAdapters.tsx:86` puts a disabled reason in `title` while `Menu.tsx:24` says in the DS's own words that `title` is *"Supplementary only — never the sole home of a reason"*, and `Menu.tsx:122,140` skip disabled items in keyboard nav. `.nds-btn:focus-visible` (`primitives.css:33-37`) sets a border already set on `.primary` (`:59-62`) plus a 0.12-alpha ring (`tokens.css:69,210`) — the primary Confirm has no perceptible keyboard focus. `IdentityBand:88-97` renders the ⋯ from `menuItems`; `ChannelSheet.tsx:1440` passes it; `:992` gates it to variants; `AliasBandCell.tsx:59-61` states the intent verbatim. `CompletenessPill` early-returns null at `:137`. `Modal size="lg"` is valid (`Size = xs|sm|md|lg|xl`; `.nds-modal.lg` at `components.css:415`). `useActionConfirm` is exported only from `grid/index.ts:54`; `components/index.ts` has zero confirm/dialog/alert. `pickMarkets: async () => null` at `ChannelSheet.tsx:850`. The pending-write `Banner` is a **sibling** of the self-closed `SheetToolbar` inside `GridSheet`'s `toolbar` fragment — ST-01 correctly REFUTED, and that slot is the shipped precedent for the receipt. `Toast.tsx:30-38` self-removes on a fixed timeout with no dismiss and no hover-pause. `provenanceMark.tsx:72,84,93` are `aria-hidden` with `title`. `isLiveOnChannel` (`sheet/master/listingRisk.ts:46-48`) is pinned as intended by `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:15-32` and is **not redefined**. `modeForChannel` (`channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:323-332`) returns null for Etsy and Woo; `gateNote` (`:347-373`) is the honest sentence. D9's reasoning is at `StudioHeader.tsx:87-97` verbatim.
---
# 1. Placement — unchanged, and it holds
Four levels, three existing surfaces, one new one. **Variant × listing** → the row ⋯ and right-click. **Listing (alias)** → the alias band's ⋯, wired and structurally empty. **Coordinate** → a new *Listing state* surface. **Plural** → the same surface's roll-up and its `BulkActionBar`.
The sheet toolbar is untouched. No lifecycle verb enters `overflow`, `trailing` or a button. `PublishMenu` and `StudioHeader` are untouched, so D9's ⋯-removal is not reversed.
The adapter needs one option — `contextAxisFor?: (row: T) => ContextAxis | null` — consulted first in `offered()`: a row that answers `'alias-group'` gets that axis's context verbs instead of row/selection verbs; every other row is unchanged, so the isolation `menuAdapters.tsx:55-58` got right survives. `registry.ts:29-33` minted `'alias-group'` for exactly this surface.
Band items, separator before the destructive group: `Verify on the channel` · `Hold pushes` / `Resume pushes` · — · `Withdraw the offer…` / `Restore the offer…` · `End the listing…` / `Relist…` · `Discontinue…` · — · `Rename…` · `Archive locally…` / `Restore` · `Release the identity…`
# 2. Two marks, never one
`ScopeBar` is not changed — it has consumers outside the studio. New pure DS module `design-system/grid/renderers/presence.ts` beside `readiness.ts`, exporting `PresenceIntent`, `PresenceFact`, `PresenceVerdict` and `presenceMeta(intent, fact, observedAt) → {tone, label, hint, verdict}`, plus a `PresencePill`. It must **not** early-return null: `UNKNOWN` renders a neutral pill reading *Not checked* with its as-of, because a blank is indistinguishable from a load failure — the exact shape of `CompletenessPill:137`.
The drawer already consumes the DS through a one-line re-export (`_studio/drawer/readinessMeta.ts`), so a `presence.ts` adapter has a shipped precedent.
Intent and fact are two pills, never one. `HELD` + `SELLING` is the honest reading of a hold. `WITHDRAWN`/`ENDED` + `SELLING` renders `danger`. Every pill's accessible name carries the as-of, and **never `aria-hidden`**.
Copy corrections in `readiness.ts`, tones unchanged so `projection.vitest.test.ts:28-43`'s delegation assertion stays green (I confirmed no test pins the string `Live`): `live` label → **`Published`**, hint → *"We hold a channel reference for this row and pushing is on. This is our record, not a check against the channel."*; `unlisted` hint → *"We hold no listing record for this coordinate. That is not a check against the channel"* — closing the contradiction between `:13` and `:56`.
**New, from correction 17 below:** on a band row the trail is never empty. Presence always renders; completeness may be absent. Order: presence pill, then `CompletenessPill`. Single-pill fallback (fact as a second line) if the floor measurement says two do not fit.
# 3. Choosing the scope of a takedown
A `reach: 'channel'` verb never inherits the scope bar. `TargetPicker` — DS `Modal size="lg"`, one `grid/datagrid` `DataGrid` fed by the server's presence read, a checkbox column, and a plain-English plan line the operator can read back. Current coordinate pre-ticked; nothing else ever is; no select-all default. Cancel returns `null` before any confirmation.
The chosen set POSTs `/presence/plan`; the response becomes `ActionImpact.payload` so `run` applies the approved snapshot. On drift, `apply` 409s per target and the UI **restates the new set** rather than retrying.
# 4. The confirmation
Promote `ActionConfirm.tsx` to `design-system/components/` and re-export from `grid/index.ts` — retiring `useDrawerConfirm` and both raw `window.confirm` calls (`contracts.tsx:609`, `ManageAxesDialog.tsx:81`, the latter rendering in the OS language against the English-console rule).
Five blocks: **Targets** (every coordinate by name with its external id, never a count; `gateNote()` verbatim when the gate is not `live`, reach badge downgraded to *local*) · consequences · findings · side effects · **Putting it back**, rendered from `impact.reversal`, never authored.
`reach` on `GridAction`, `reversal` on `ActionImpact`, `validateAction(action)` beside `validateImpact` (which receives only the impact, `registry.ts:264`). **Corrected:** the typed-refusal work is now *reorder rules 3 and 4 in `runAction`* (Wave 1, one line) plus `refusal.verb` for the affordance.
Typed phrase = the real external id (`registry.ts:117`; `ActionConfirm` renders no placeholder, `:143-150`), matched exactly and case-sensitively. The primary-button focus fix blocks any lifecycle confirm.
# 5. Post-action feedback
Never a Toast. In flight → `busy` plus a `pending` fact with a spinner, never a tick. The receipt → a persistent `Banner` in the sibling-of-toolbar slot, tone from the *worst* per-target result, `action` slot carrying the reversal verb, per-target results in the shipped `MarketOfferResult` shape with **no aggregate success**. The durable record → the fact flips only when the auto-enqueued `verify` reports; a transport failure renders `UNKNOWN`.
# 6. Wave 1 — what an implementer builds
After the nine upstream preconditions: the DS type additions (`reach`, `reversal`, `refusal.verb`, the `runAction` rule reorder, `MenuItemDef.tone` with the **corrected selector**, `contextAxisFor`, `description`-not-`title`, primary-button focus, `ActionConfirm` promotion); the presence read + `verify` + history; two-mark rendering; the readiness copy corrections; the alias band ⋯ carrying **only** local-reach verbs; the presence panel read-only with every channel-reaching verb disabled *with a reason*. `ReadinessPanel`'s import swapped — which removes the studio's only ratchet contribution but **leaves the gate red at 33/32**; `channelSnapshot` mapped in `drawer/types.ts` to clear the red test. No channel-reaching verb ships.
**Added Wave-1 precondition:** the CONFIRMED `ux:sheet-toolbar` defect — `trailing` is documented "status only, never a control" and both callers put a modal-opening verb there. Presence's receipt and CH.1 both depend on `trailing` meaning what it says.
# 7. What I could not establish
Every geometry claim. The four gates that measure the screen drive a dev server wired to the production API. Named build-time measurements: two pills at the band **floor** (240px, not the 240–420 range) at 1280/1440/1728 via `check-control-census.mjs`; the band ⋯ width at ten items with two-line descriptions; whether the presence panel pushes `ReadinessPanel` below the fold; any bar-height change. `check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs` crashes, so presence tones must be hand-computed from token source.

Scenarios

#### Identity moves — RELAUNCH-SKU, MOVE-ACCOUNT and ATTRIBUTE, corrected against source

Three identity operations the studio cannot express, on the Presence surface. The reviewed draft is largely sound and two of its central rules are wrong at source. MOVE-ACCOUNT's "both grants are the same seller" test is unsatisfiable on active rows — a partial unique index forbids two active connections sharing an externalAccountId — so the source grant is normally INACTIVE and the candidate query must read the full population. eBay is blocked on MAP.4, not on `verify`, and its dispatch resolves the primary connection regardless of the row, so a move there is cosmetic until that is fixed. A third kind, ATTRIBUTE (null → account), is added. RELAUNCH-SKU's `recreate` is downgraded from lossy to unrecoverable-until-rehearsed, and SkuAlias is stripped of a promise it cannot keep. Eleven preconditions.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx:8-50) carries no tone, severity or group key, and there are ZERO danger rules in design-system/styles/components.css against a positive control of 20 `.nds-menu` rules — both re-measured. ADD an optional `tone?: 'neutral' | 'danger'` and `group?: string`, plus a `.nds-menu` danger rule. ALREADY FILED at .claude/DS-GAPS.md:328-348; that entry is partly stale (it recites MenuItemDef as six fields; `title` and `description` have since landed), so the fix is an APPEND — check-ds-gaps-append-only must stay green.
- Both DS menu adapters discard GridAction.danger — /usr/bin/grep -c danger menuAdapters.tsx = 0 against a control of 5 for `label`; actionMenuItems (:75-90) emits {id,label,title,disabled,onSelect} only. ADD `reach` on GridAction and `reversal{verb,fidelity}` on ActionImpact per the agreed state model, and render from `reach`, not from the inert bit.
- validateImpact(impact) (registry.ts:264-273) receives only the impact and cannot see an action-level field. ADD a sibling validateAction(action) refusing a reach:'channel' verb whose preflight returns no reversal, plus a rule that an IdentityMove impact with any coordinate strategy unset is a bug, not a lenient confirm.
- No DS component for a per-target STRATEGY table: one row per coordinate, a required choice from a per-channel option set, refusals inline, no default. PROPOSE design-system/patterns/TargetStrategyTable.tsx on NexusGrid + primitives/Select, with the invariant that an unset row blocks the confirm. Do NOT hand-roll in _studio/.
- ActionConfirm has no slot for a BEFORE→AFTER identifier pair. ADD `identifiers?: Array<{label, before, after}>` to ActionImpact, rendered above `consequences` (ActionConfirm.tsx:100-105), so the pair is typed rather than smuggled into a consequences string.
- CORRECTED MEASUREMENT: 52 of 243 committed DS .d.ts are stale (check-ds-dts-fresh --check, exit 1) — not 51. components/AccountSwitcher.d.ts and components/AccountsPanel.d.ts are both on the list, i.e. the two components the account picker is told to reuse. Regenerate before this work starts.
- The DS focus ring is documented as invisible at its own call site: components.css:2518 carries the comment 'the focus-ring token is 12% alpha = 1.17:1, no visible ring'. The Confirm button of every type-to-confirm dialog rides that token. This is a precondition for shipping any irreversible verb behind that dialog, not a follow-up. (I verified the comment and the absence of a .nds-btn focus-visible rule in components.css; I did not re-measure the contrast myself.)

##### API
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/identity/plan { kind: 'RELAUNCH_SKU' | 'MOVE_ACCOUNT' | 'ATTRIBUTE', newSku?, fromAccountId?, toAccountId?, strategies?: Record<coordinateKey, 'carry'|'recreate'|'leave'|'refuse'> } — the server-side preflight. Returns an ActionImpact-shaped plan: every five-level coordinate enumerated BY NAME with its current fact and as-of, permitted strategies, consequence sentence, reach and reversal{verb,fidelity}; the before→after identifier pairs; refusals each NAMING the verb that resolves them; a planId and a short-TTL preflightToken binding the enumerated set and every target's expectedVersion. NO WRITES. For RELAUNCH_SKU the target set is ALWAYS every coordinate of the product and a caller-supplied subset is refused, because Product.sku is global.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/identity/apply { planId, preflightToken, confirmPhrase } — the only writer. Refuses a missing, expired or set-mismatched token; 409s per target on version drift. confirmPhrase is the CURRENT SKU for RELAUNCH_SKU and the target's externalAccountId for MOVE_ACCOUNT (registry.ts:117). ATTRIBUTE has no typed phrase — it is reversible. Per-target results in the shipped MarketOfferResult shape with NO aggregate success; a transport failure is UNKNOWN, never success, and the Product.sku write does not proceed on it.
- GET /api/accounts/:id/reattribution-candidates?channel= — CORRECTED (C1): it must query the FULL ChannelConnection population, not listActiveConnections (connection-resolver.service.ts:160-161 filters isActive:true), because the active-account unique index (migration 20260819a:221-223) forbids two ACTIVE grants sharing an externalAccountId on the same channel and marketplace — so the source is normally inactive and an active-only query returns an empty list in exactly the case the feature exists for. Returns each candidate's isActive, authStatus, ConnectionScope marketplace/shop rows, and sameSeller: true | false | 'unknown'. 'unknown' (either externalAccountId null) is a REFUSAL, not a warning.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence — extended (Presence's own route, not a new one): must list coordinates whose ChannelConnection is INACTIVE, marked `unreachable` with the account's own accountLabel. Today they are doubly invisible — studio-data.ts:111 keeps only `c.isActive && c.isManagedBy !== 'pending'`, and byId throws NoConnectionError for an inactive row (connection-resolver.service.ts:167-176) so even a hand-built ?account= URL is refused.
- resolveWorkspaceDestination gains a read-only mode (services/pim/workspace-destination.ts) — CORRECTED (C12): it must relax TWO latches, not one. The connection latch at :56 (resolveChannelConnectionId → byId) AND the alias latch at :57-62, which refuses any ProductListingAlias whose status is not 'ACTIVE'. Resolve both for DISPLAY; refuse every write with the real authStatus or alias status as the sentence.
- AUTHORISATION — an explicit in-file preHandler naming the required permission on every route above and on the existing recovery routes. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (rbac-hook.ts:9-13,31). A typed SKU in the dialog secures the operator path only.
- PRECONDITION I1 (WIDENED) — bulk-edit.service.ts:1176-1232's guard must key on identityHeld (externalListingId present) instead of listingStatus:'ACTIVE' AND isPublished:true (:1189-1191), and must name COORDINATES, not channels. Keep its existing parent→child coverage (:1191-1193, :1203-1209). Point its 'use the channel-safe rename (coming soon)' message at the plan endpoint. NEW (C11): writerAcceptsField must also refuse 'sku' outright (master-field-gate.ts:104-109), so the rename does not depend on field-registry.service.ts:76's `editable:false` display flag as its second latch.
- PRECONDITION I2 — recovery.service.ts:334-345's channelListing.updateMany filters on (productId, channel, marketplace) only, rewriting externalListingId and listingStatus across every account and alias in the market. Narrow to the full five-level coordinate INCLUDING the pre-read at :97-104, which selects no coordinate columns at all.
- PRECONDITION I3 — recovery.service.ts:303 calls getAmazonSellerId() with no accountId. Pass the coordinate's channelConnectionId; amazonAccount() already refuses a mismatch with amazon_seller_mismatch (amazon-sp-client.ts:19). The comment at :298-301 ('SP-API merchant id comes from env, not a per-row column') is stale — getAmazonSellerId(accountId?) resolves through the account (amazon-sp-client.ts:24-27) — and must be corrected in the same edit.
- PRECONDITION I4 — recovery.service.ts marks the event SUCCEEDED at :390-403, after the delete and rename and BEFORE the wizard recreate (which is a URL hand-off at :401-403, not an API call). The honest terminal is AWAITING_RECREATE.
- PRECONDITION I5 — the eBay label guard's REVISION must become intent-aware. ensureListingLabels reads the live Item.SKU and issues ReviseFixedPriceItem on any difference (ebay-label-guard.service.ts:165-184), scheduled '15 */6 * * *' and default-ON when NEXUS_EBAY_REAL_API=true (ebay-label-guard.job.ts:37-51). It must skip any coordinate with a terminal presence intent or identityDriftAt set, and must not fire during an in-flight relaunch. UNMEASURED: whether the cron is armed on prod — I did not read prod env.
- PRECONDITION I5b (NEW — C7) — the label guard's IDENTITY BACKFILL is a coordinate-blind write to the column identityHeld is defined on. ebay-label-guard.service.ts:148-156 runs channelListing.updateMany({ where: { productId, channel:'EBAY', OR:[{marketplace},{region}], externalListingId: null }, data: { externalListingId: t.itemId } }) — no channelConnectionId, no aliasKey, and marketplace OR region widens it again. Every six hours this can re-attach an ItemID to a deliberately RELEASED or ENDED coordinate, on the wrong account and the wrong alias, silently restoring sellingRisk and flipping the corrected rename guard. Narrow it to the five-level coordinate and refuse any row with a terminal intent or identityDriftAt set.
- PRECONDITION I6 (RESCOPED — C4) — do NOT promise that the old SKU keeps resolving. Enumerate the resolvers that will be taught SkuAlias in this work and gate the list: the flat-file item_sku match key (services/amazon/flat-file-merge.ts:77,:100 — CORRECTED PATH, the draft said services/pim/) and ListingReconciliation's externalSku match. relinkNullPoolMemberships (ebay-label-guard.service.ts:143-160) matches on Product.sku exactly and is covered by I5b's coordinate narrowing rather than by an alias lookup. Every other SKU-keyed table is named in the plan as splitting at the rename.
- PRECONDITION I7 — MOVE_ACCOUNT and ATTRIBUTE must move ProductListingAlias in the same transaction as their ChannelListing rows. The alias unique is [workspaceId, productId, channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, position] (schema.prisma:1860); leaving it behind diverges the alias's account from its listings'. A position collision is enumerated in the plan, never surfaced as a P2002.
- PRECONDITION I8 — MOVE_ACCOUNT must state and enforce which referrers move. blastRadius() counts all six at accounts.routes.ts:205-215 (CORRECTED line range). MOVE: ChannelListing, VariantChannelListing, SharedListingMembership, ProductListingAlias. LEAVE: Order (re-attributing an order falsifies which grant took it — POST /accounts/:id/disconnect goes out of its way to preserve attribution, accounts.routes.ts:437-440), SyncChannelPolicy, EbayCampaign.
- PRECONDITION I9 — all three moves emit a ProductEvent with the request's authUser. The literal 'default-user' appears at ten non-test sites in apps/api/src (e.g. products-images.routes.ts:276, orders-reviews.routes.ts:133) and recovery reads as automation-triggered. NOTE the dependency: under rbacHook's SHADOW default there may be no authUser to stamp, so this precondition is entangled with the RBAC question.
- PRECONDITION I10 (NEW — C8) — MOVE_ACCOUNT and ATTRIBUTE must refuse a coordinate holding PENDING or PROCESSING OutboundSyncQueue rows, or re-point them in the same transaction. The queue carries NO channelConnectionId column — only productId? and channelListingId? (schema.prisma:6824-6828) — so the grant is resolved at dispatch and an in-flight row would push under the new account against a listing that account may not own. On eBay this is worse: outbound-sync.service.ts:1259 resolves { channel:'EBAY', primary:true } regardless of the row, so the move does not change who pushes at all.
- PRECONDITION I11 (NEW — C5/C6) — REQUIRED BEFORE ANY recreate PROMISE. patchListingsItem DOES NOT EXIST on the SP-API client; its write methods are patchListingPrice (:647), patchPurchasableOffer (:772), putListingsItem (:843), deleteListingsItem (:1133). Rehearse deleteListingsItem-then-putListingsItem against a fixture ASIN and read the BODY, not the status. Until that rehearsal, RELAUNCH_SKU recreate carries fidelity:'none': the delete puts the old SKU into a ~30-day cooldown (:1119-1123), so a failed create leaves the coordinate dark with no fallback. Also correct the client doc at :1128, which names 'listing-recovery.service.ts' — a file that does not exist; the caller is services/listings/recovery.service.ts.
- ETSY — unchanged from the Presence model: PublishReadiness (channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:317-322) describes amazon, ebay and shopify only and modeForChannel returns null for Etsy. Both moves refuse Etsy coordinates by name. It must not be silently caught by the WooCommerce containment rule.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE, no migration — SkuAlias.source gains the value 'RELAUNCH'. The field is already `String @default("MANUAL") // MANUAL | IMPORT | AUTO` (schema.prisma:479). CORRECTED SCOPE (C4): writing this row buys exactly one thing — a future stock import still matches the old SKU (stock-import.service.ts:212 is one of only two readers; the other is the upsert at :2044). It does NOT make order ingestion, ListingReconciliation.externalSku, the eBay pool relink, ProductReadCache or any analytics table resolve the old SKU. Two constraints the plan must pre-check and refuse by name rather than surfacing a P2002: `alias` is normalized lowercase-and-trimmed and unique on [workspaceId, alias] (:477,:483), so another product may already own it and a case-only rename self-collides; and productId is onDelete: Cascade (:476), so the alias does not survive a product purge — the durable record is ListingIdentity, which the Presence model deliberately gives no Prisma relation.
- ADDITIVE — ListingIdentity (the model the Presence proposal already requires) gains `supersededByExternalListingId String?`, and its retiredReason value set is extended with 'sku-relaunched' | 'account-reattributed' | 'attributed'. NOT SEPARABLE: if ListingIdentity is not approved, these moves have nowhere to record what they retired and must be rejected with it.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.identityDriftAt DateTime? / identityDriftReason String?, set by the RELAUNCH_SKU `leave` strategy. Nullable, NEVER backfilled; null means 'no drift observed', not 'in sync' (the studio's own doctrine, ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:3-13). DEPENDENCY, stated because it is load-bearing: every push path must refuse a coordinate carrying identityDriftAt, which means the shared assertPushAllowed() that Presence P5 requires must exist first. Without it this column is a flag nothing honours.
- NOT PROPOSED, deliberately — a sellerSku column on ChannelListing. It is the obvious fix for the fact that ChannelListing carries no seller SKU (verified by reading the whole model, schema.prisma:1510-1799) and it is a much larger programme: 46 non-test channelListing.create/upsert sites would have to populate it, every read path would have to prefer it over Product.sku, and until every one does, two sources of truth is worse than one. If the Owner wants it, it is its own approval, sequenced BEFORE RELAUNCH_SKU wave 4.
- NOT DECLARED — no change to any unique key. Both ChannelListing uniques already carry channelConnectionId and aliasKey (schema.prisma:1785-1786) and ProductListingAlias's carries channelConnectionId and position (:1860). All three moves are expressible against the schema exactly as it stands; only a writer is missing. NOTE for the implementer: both ChannelListing indexes are declared NULLS NOT DISTINCT in raw SQL because Prisma cannot express it (:1774-1782) — so a NULL channelConnectionId is constrained, which is what makes ATTRIBUTE's destination check meaningful.
- SEQUENCING (binding) — apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma. MEMORY records apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11, with only Product.version (54 vs 51) separating the two. Discriminate, never infer.

##### Open for you
- MOVE_ACCOUNT's source will normally be an INACTIVE or REVOKED connection — the active-account unique index (migration 20260819a:221-223) forbids two active grants sharing an externalAccountId on the same channel and marketplace. Is the Owner content that a move reads identity off a revoked grant, on the strength of a stored externalAccountId nobody can re-verify at the channel? The alternative is to refuse every move whose source cannot be re-verified, which in practice refuses all of them.
- eBay MOVE_ACCOUNT is blocked on MAP.4 (externalAccountId is NULL for every eBay row) AND on the primary-connection dispatch at outbound-sync.service.ts:1259. Does the Owner want MAP.4 pulled forward as a precondition of this work, or eBay account moves filed as a separate programme? A GetUser call could establish the identity, but I have not measured whether the granted scopes permit it.
- ATTRIBUTE (null channelConnectionId → an account) is the third kind and probably the commonest. Its evidence rule is weaker than a move's: a verify must show the target grant owning the coordinate's external id. Does the Owner accept a channel-read as sufficient evidence to bind an unattributed listing to an account, or should this stay manual?
- RELAUNCH_SKU recreate on Amazon: does the Owner want it attempted at all before a rehearsal on a fixture ASIN establishes that the new SKU can be created against the same ASIN? If Amazon refuses a second offer, the only path is delete-then-create with a dark window and a 30-day cooldown on the fallback — which I would refuse to ship.
- Should 'sku' be removed from writerAcceptsField's accepted set (master-field-gate.ts:104-109)? Today it is accepted there and kept out of the formula and cell writers only by field-registry.service.ts:76's `editable: false` — a display flag as the second latch on an identity write. Removing it makes the rename reachable only through the identity plan, but it is a shared leaf and I will not touch it without a ruling.
- D9: these two moves live on the Presence grid, whose own status under D9 is an open question in the Presence proposal (StudioHeader.tsx:87-97). If the Owner rules the Presence surface is a second way in, both moves have no home and this proposal lapses with it. I am not proposing an alternative surface, because the sheet cannot host a per-coordinate strategy table without straining CH.1.
- IDENTITY MERGE is still out of scope and I want that ratified. Merging two duplicate ChannelListing rows onto one external id, and consolidating two eBay accounts into one, are set operations on identity rather than state transitions on a coordinate. critic:scenarios named identity change as a whole missing axis; I have designed three of its operations and not that one.
- Who is the actor? Nine of the paths this touches stamp an inherited or literal actor; 'default-user' appears as a literal at ten non-test sites (e.g. products-images.routes.ts:276, orders-reviews.routes.ts:133). I9 requires the request's authUser on both moves — but rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (rbac-hook.ts:31), so an unauthenticated caller reaching these routes has no authUser to stamp. Does release-class gating wait on NEXUS_RBAC_MODE=enforce, or ship behind an in-file guard?

##### Risks
- The whole proposal is downstream of two unratified decisions: whether ListingIdentity is approved (without it neither move has anywhere to record what it retired, and both must be rejected with it) and whether the Presence surface survives D9 (StudioHeader.tsx:87-97). Neither is mine to settle.
- RELAUNCH_SKU recreate has no acked precedent in this codebase. recovery.service.ts deletes and hands off to a wizard URL (:401-403); the recreate is manual today. Whether putListingsItem can create a second seller SKU against an ASIN the seller already had an offer on — and whether Amazon refuses it as a duplicate offer — is UNMEASURED. Until it is rehearsed, delete-then-create leaves a window in which the coordinate is dark and the old SKU is in a ~30-day cooldown (amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1119-1123): an unrecoverable state, not a lossy one.
- MOVE_ACCOUNT on eBay is cosmetic even after MAP.4: outbound-sync.service.ts:1259 resolves the eBay grant as tryResolveConnection({channel:'EBAY', primary:true}) unless the payload names one, so a moved row still pushes under the primary connection.
- I6 as the draft wrote it — 'every SKU resolver must consult SkuAlias' — is not a precondition but an unbounded programme. Sixteen tables key on the SKU string with no FK, and SkuAlias has two readers today. The corrected scope buys exactly one thing (stock import still matches) and says so; anyone who reads the old wording will size this wrong.
- identityDriftAt is a new push-refusal clause on assertPushAllowed(), which the Presence model's P5 has not built yet. If P5 slips, `leave` sets a flag nothing honours and the coordinate keeps being pushed under the wrong label — worse than refusing the strategy.
- The label guard's identity backfill (I5b) and the recovery updateMany (I2) are the same class of defect as Presence P8. If the three are fixed by three lanes independently they will diverge; they want one shared coordinate-narrowing helper and one gate.
- Both moves make an existing capability slower and louder. Today a rename is one cell in a legacy tab. Operators who currently rename freely will experience the corrected guard (I1) as a regression before either move ships, because I1 lands in wave 1 and RELAUNCH_SKU does not land until wave 3.

##### The specification
# Identity moves — corrected
## CORRECTIONS to the reviewed draft
**C1 — the MOVE-ACCOUNT permission rule is unsatisfiable as written.** The draft permits a move when `from.externalAccountId === to.externalAccountId`, both non-null, both presumably usable. `ChannelConnection_active_account_key` is `UNIQUE ("channelType", COALESCE("marketplace",'~'), COALESCE("externalAccountId",'~')) WHERE "isActive" = true` (`packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260819a_map2_account_dimension/migration.sql:221-223`). Two **active** connections on the same channel and marketplace with the same `externalAccountId` cannot coexist — the index forbids exactly the configuration the rule requires. The real handover shape is: the old grant is revoked or deactivated, a new grant is created carrying the same marketplace identity. So the **source is normally inactive**, and the candidate query must read the full `ChannelConnection` population, never `listActiveConnections`, which filters `isActive: true` (`apps/api/src/services/connection-resolver.service.ts:160-161`). Corrected rule below.
**C2 — eBay is blocked on MAP.4, and a `verify` does not unblock it.** `externalAccountId` is `String?` and its own schema comment says "NULL until MAP.4 adds eBay's identity scope" (`packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma:6648-6652`). The draft says eBay is "refused until a `verify` establishes it" — but `verify` reads the **listing**, not the account. Nothing in this design populates eBay's account identity. Worse, `outbound-sync.service.ts:1259` resolves the eBay connection as `tryResolveConnection({ channel: "EBAY", primary: true })` unless a payload carries an id, so even a completed move would not change which grant pushes. eBay MOVE-ACCOUNT is therefore **not shippable** in this design; it is named as blocked, with its two blockers.
**C3 — the commonest case is missing.** `resolveWorkspaceDestination` throws `'This listing has no account attribution. Select an attributed listing to continue.'` for a row whose `channelConnectionId` is null (`apps/api/src/services/pim/workspace-destination.ts:51`). Such a row has no `from`, so MOVE-ACCOUNT cannot express it. Added as a third kind, **ATTRIBUTE**, with a stricter evidence rule than a move.
**C4 — `SkuAlias` does not do what the draft says.** It has exactly two readers, both in the stock-import path: `stock-import.service.ts:212` (matching) and `:2044` (upsert), plus CRUD at `stock.routes.ts:3474,:3509`. Nothing else in `apps/api/src` reads it. It is an import-matching table, not a SKU resolution registry, so "the old SKU as a resolvable `SkuAlias` so lookups do not break" is false for order ingestion, `ListingReconciliation.externalSku`, the eBay pool relink, `ProductReadCache` and every analytics table. `alias` is also normalized lowercase-and-trimmed and unique on `[workspaceId, alias]` (`schema.prisma:477,483`), so a case-only rename self-collides, and `productId` is `onDelete: Cascade` (`:476`), so the alias does not survive a purge. Rescoped below.
**C5 — `patchListingsItem` does not exist on the SP-API client.** Its write methods are `patchListingPrice` (`apps/api/src/clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts:647`), `patchPurchasableOffer` (`:772`), `putListingsItem` (`:843`), `deleteListingsItem` (`:1133`). The Presence model's P7 asserts it "exists in the client and is not wired"; it does not exist. `recreate` is `deleteListingsItem` + `putListingsItem`.
**C6 — `recreate` is not "lossy".** `deleteListingsItem` puts the old SKU into a ~30-day cooldown (`amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1119-1123`). If the `putListingsItem` leg then fails, the coordinate is dark **and** cannot be restored under the old SKU for ~30 days. That is unrecoverable, not lossy. And there is no existing acked recreate to copy: `recovery.service.ts` deletes, renames, then hands off to a wizard URL (`:401-403`) — the recreate is manual today.
**C7 — the eBay label guard has a second, worse defect the draft misses.** `ebay-label-guard.service.ts:148-156` runs `channelListing.updateMany({ where: { productId, channel:'EBAY', OR:[{marketplace},{region}], externalListingId: null }, data: { externalListingId: t.itemId } })` — coordinate-blind on account and alias, and it writes **the identity column**. Every six hours this can re-attach an ItemID to a deliberately released coordinate, on the wrong account. New precondition I5b.
**C8 — in-flight queue rows survive the move and re-aim.** `OutboundSyncQueue` has **no** `channelConnectionId` column; it carries `productId?` and `channelListingId?` only (`schema.prisma:6824-6828`). The grant is resolved at dispatch. New precondition I10.
**C9 — three measurements are wrong.** `check-grid-kit-ratchet` reports DS DataGrid **34 vs baseline 32** (draft said 33). `check-ds-dts-fresh --check` reports **52 of 243** stale (draft said 51) — and `components/AccountSwitcher.d.ts` and `components/AccountsPanel.d.ts` are both on the list, i.e. the two components the account picker is told to reuse. `blastRadius` is at `accounts.routes.ts:205-215`, not `:206-213`.
**C10 — `Stepper` is a component, not a primitive.** `apps/web/src/design-system/components/Stepper.tsx`; `primitives/NumberStepper.tsx` is a different thing. `Select` is `primitives/Select.tsx`.
**C11 — the rename guard has a second, weaker latch worth closing.** `'sku'` is in `ALLOWED_MASTER_FIELDS` (`master-field-gate.ts:23`), and `writerAcceptsField` therefore accepts it (`:104-109`). It is kept out of the formula and cell writers only by the column carrying `editable: false` (`field-registry.service.ts:76`; the test at `cell-formula.service.ts:926,958` is `col.editable === false || !writerAcceptsField(...)`). A display flag is the second latch on an identity write. Also, the bulk guard already covers a **parent's** rename via child listings (`bulk-edit.service.ts:1191-1193,1203-1209`), which the draft did not say.
**C12 — the read-only destination mode must cover the alias latch too.** `resolveWorkspaceDestination` refuses an alias that is not `status:'ACTIVE'` (`workspace-destination.ts:57-62`), independently of the connection latch. Both must relax for display.
**C13 — this proposal's surface is unratified.** Both moves live on the Presence grid, whose existence is itself an open D9 question. D9's reasoning is at `StudioHeader.tsx:87-97`: "A second way to reach a thing the grid already is makes the page bigger, not more capable." If the Owner rules Presence is a second way in, these moves have no home. Stated as a dependency, not assumed away.
Everything else in the draft I re-derived and confirmed: the bulk rename guard and its wrong predicate (`bulk-edit.service.ts:1176-1232`, `listingStatus:'ACTIVE' AND isPublished:true` at `:1189-1191`) against `isLiveOnChannel` (`listingRisk.ts:47-49`) pinned by `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:10-32`; the label guard's unattended 6-hourly revision (`ebay-label-guard.service.ts:165-184`, `ebay-label-guard.job.ts:37-51`); `getAmazonSellerId(accountId?)` (`amazon-sp-client.ts:24-27`) versus the stale comment and account-less call at `recovery.service.ts:298-303`; the coordinate-blind `updateMany` at `recovery.service.ts:334-345` and its pre-read at `:97-104`; `SUCCEEDED` before the recreate (`:390-403`); `ChannelListing` carries no seller-SKU column (read the whole model, `schema.prisma:1510-1799`); Shopify's SKU fallback (`content-publisher.ts:182`); the FNSKU warning (`amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:809`); `ProductListingAlias`'s unique (`schema.prisma:1860`); `blastRadius`'s six referrers; disconnect preserving attribution (`accounts.routes.ts:437-440`); zero `danger` in `menuAdapters.tsx` against a control of 5 for `label`; 20 `.nds-menu` rules and **0** danger rules in `styles/components.css`.
## 1. The object
One noun, `IdentityMove`, on the Presence surface, sharing Presence's plan/apply mechanism: five-level target enumeration, a short-TTL `preflightToken`, a real `verify` before and after, a `ListingIdentity` record, a `ProductEvent`. Three kinds:
```
kind:     'RELAUNCH_SKU' | 'MOVE_ACCOUNT' | 'ATTRIBUTE'
targets[]: five-level coordinates, each with expectedVersion
strategy:  per-coordinate, explicit, never defaulted
```
## 2. RELAUNCH-SKU
Product-wide by construction: `ChannelListing` has no seller-SKU column, so `Product.sku` is the single seller SKU for every channel × market × account × alias and the endpoint refuses a caller-supplied subset. Only the *consequences* are per-coordinate.
**Preserves:** the ASIN/ItemID; reviews (ASIN-bound); the `ChannelListing` row with overrides, snapshots, `platformAttributes`, aliases; order history.
**Does not preserve, stated on screen:** FBA units stay bound to the old FNSKU (`Product.fnsku` is updated by no rename path); Shopify variants with no stored `shopifyVariantId` stop matching; and sales/analytics history splits at the rename across sixteen SKU-keyed tables with no FK. The plan says "history before *date* stays under *oldSku*", never "continuity".
**What the old SKU buys you, honestly (C4):** one `SkuAlias` row, written in the same transaction, means a future **stock-import spreadsheet** still matches. It does not make anything else resolve. Because `alias` is normalized and workspace-unique, the plan must pre-check the alias for collision and refuse by name — never surface a P2002 — and must refuse a case-only rename outright.
**Per-coordinate strategy, never defaulted:**
| strategy | channel | what happens | reversal |
|---|---|---|---|
| `carry` | eBay | `ReviseFixedPriceItem` sets the live custom label deliberately, inside the plan, instead of letting the 6-hourly guard do it later | `carry` back, exact |
| `recreate` | Amazon | `deleteListingsItem(oldSku)` then `putListingsItem(newSku)` against the same ASIN | **`fidelity:'none'` until rehearsed** (C6) |
| `leave` | any | the channel keeps the old label; `identityDriftAt` is set and every push path refuses the coordinate | reconcile |
| `refuse` | Etsy; Shopify without stored variant ids | named reason | n/a |
`Product.sku` is written **last**, in one transaction with the `SkuAlias` insert and the `ListingIdentity` rows, only after every `carry`/`recreate` coordinate has an acked channel result. A transport failure is UNKNOWN, and the rename does not proceed.
## 3. MOVE-ACCOUNT — corrected
The listing does not move; the pointer on our row moves. The permission rule, corrected for C1:
| case | test | ruling |
|---|---|---|
| Same seller, new grant | `from.externalAccountId === to.externalAccountId`, both non-null, read from the **full** connection population — the source is expected to be inactive or revoked | **allowed** |
| Different seller | ids differ | **refused**; the honest path is Presence `end` + create, named in the refusal |
| Either id null | | **refused**; on eBay this is every row, and MAP.4 is the blocker (C2) |
Target must be `isActive` and not in `{revoked, needs_reauth, disconnected}` — mirroring `amazonAccount()`'s test at `amazon-sp-client.ts:18`. Target grant must reach the market (read `ConnectionScope`, never assume). The destination coordinate must be free; a collision is enumerated by name.
**Moves:** `ChannelListing`, `VariantChannelListing`, `SharedListingMembership`, and `ProductListingAlias` in the same transaction (its unique carries `channelConnectionId` and `position`). **Stays:** `Order`, `SyncChannelPolicy`, `EbayCampaign`.
`reach: 'local'` is correct for grouping and misleading as a sentence: the move re-aims every *future* push. The consequence line must say so, and I10 handles the in-flight ones.
## 4. ATTRIBUTE — the null case (C3)
`from` is null, so there is no identity to match. The rule is evidence, not equality: a completed `verify` on the target grant must return the coordinate's own `externalListingId` as owned by that account. Absent that, refuse. This is `reach: 'channel'` (it reads) and `reversal: { verb: 'unattribute', fidelity: 'exact' }`.
## 5. Seeing the listing at all
`GET /presence` must list coordinates whose connection is inactive **and** whose alias is archived, marked `unreachable` with the account's own `accountLabel`; `resolveWorkspaceDestination` gains a read-only mode that relaxes both latches for display and refuses every write with the real state as the sentence (C12).
## 6. Waves
**Wave 1** (with Presence wave 1): I1–I11; the read that shows unreachable coordinates; `verify`; the DS type additions. No move ships.
**Wave 2:** ATTRIBUTE, then MOVE-ACCOUNT on Amazon and Shopify only.
**Wave 3:** RELAUNCH-SKU `carry` (eBay).
**Wave 4:** RELAUNCH-SKU `recreate`, gated on a rehearsal against a fixture ASIN.
**Not scheduled:** eBay MOVE-ACCOUNT, blocked on MAP.4 and on the primary-connection dispatch.
## 7. Honest cost
Move A is slower and louder than today's one cell plus a quiet cron. Move B will refuse every eBay attempt and most Amazon ones, and will read as broken. The `recreate` leg has no acked precedent in this codebase. All three are the design, and the Owner should want them before they ship.

#### Restore — the relist half of "put it back", corrected: a revalidation gate whose PASS is earned, a diff in the idiom the studio already ruled for, and edits that land locally before they leave

The five-class model (REPLAY / REVALIDATE / REMINT / REFUSE / RE-ASK) is sound and survives. Six things do not. R1's freshness check cannot PASS: its evidence store is written best-effort by four files, none of them the studio, and carries no account or alias — absence of a price event is not freshness. The diff cannot be a three-column table in a 420px drawer: ComparePane already rejected exactly that shape and the proposal cites it as support. The diff cannot be editable inside ActionConfirm: AskToConfirm returns a boolean and there is no channel for edits. Restore already has an Owner-ruled home (a mode in History, not a second surface). _versionHistory has two writers and, measured, zero rows. And an edited price that reaches Amazon but not ChannelListing is overwritten by the next follow-master push.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- ActionImpact cannot carry a structured review. It has consequences: string[], sideEffects: string[] and a flat findings[{rowId,label,severity}] (registry.ts:85-105); ActionConfirm renders exactly those as <li> lists (:100-133). ADD an optional `review?: { columns; rows: {id, cells, cls: 'replay'|'revalidate'|'remint'|'reask'|'exclude', check?, excludedBecause? } }` to ActionImpact, rendered read-only. Do NOT make it editable: AskToConfirm returns boolean (runAction.ts:33) and Pending.resolve is (ok: boolean) => void (ActionConfirm.tsx:42-45), so there is no channel for edits to return through. Widening that seam changes the DS's nine safety rules and is a separate Owner decision
- EVERY DS type is restated in a hand-written .d.ts and editing only the .tsx is a silent no-op: SourceIndicator.d.ts:1, tone.d.ts:2, registry.d.ts:81/172/188-189. Every addition below lands in BOTH files, plus a gate asserting the pair agree. This is the 'a token scale can be defined TWICE' trap in type form and the proposal did not mention it
