- Tone has no `unknown`. Tone = 'neutral'|'info'|'success'|'warning'|'danger' (primitives/tone.ts:2 and tone.d.ts:2), so 'we could not ask' renders as neutral — the same mark as 'nothing notable'. ADD a narrow design-system/primitives/UnknownMark.tsx with a mandatory becauseText rather than widening the shared union; put the wider change to the Owner separately
- SourceIndicator.ValueSourceKind (SourceIndicator.tsx:7, restated in SourceIndicator.d.ts:1) has no member for a replayed, recomputed, refused or excluded value. ADD 'replayed'|'recomputed'|'refused'|'excluded' to that union and its ICONS map in both files. Safe: the union's only studio consumer is sheet/channel/value-source.ts:1-13, and drawer/layers.vitest.test.ts:55 asserts over a DIFFERENT union (ValueSource from attribute-resolver), so it is unaffected
- No DS component expresses an as-of. ADD design-system/components/AsOf.tsx taking { at: string|null; via: string|null }, rendering 'not checked' — not an em dash, not a blank — when either is null
- GridAction has no `reach` and ActionImpact no `reversal`. THIS IS A CONTRACT ENHANCEMENT, NOT A DEFECT FIX: the audit REFUTED 'ActionImpact cannot express reversibility' on the grounds that ActionConfirm.tsx:90-96 already differentiates a permanent verb and that consequences is the designated home. The CONFIRMED neighbour is that both menu adapters drop GridAction.danger (0 occurrences in menuAdapters.tsx, control 5 for `label`) — fix that first, then add reach/reversal so a guard can enforce what is currently prose

##### API
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/relist/plan { verb: 'restore-offer'|'relist'|'republish', targets[], snapshotRef?, edits? } — runs revalidateRelist per coordinate. Returns per target: the three-view diff rows with their class (REPLAY/REVALIDATE/REMINT/RE-ASK/EXCLUDE), the ten RelistCheck results with four-valued verdicts AND the granularity each answered at, the roll-up (weakest check decides; unknown never rolls up to pass; a market-wide pass on a five-level target renders warn), the snapshot's fidelity and as-of, the gate mode from getAmazonPublishMode(), and a short-TTL preflightToken binding the enumerated five-level set plus each target's version. NO WRITES; it MAY issue read-only channel calls (getListingsItem at clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1212, getItemListingStatus at ebay-trading-api.service.ts:316) and must report which it managed and which it could not. `edits` are accepted here, not on apply, so the operator's changes are re-gated before they are confirmed.
- POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/relist/apply { planId, preflightToken, confirmPhrase? } — applies exactly that plan; edits are already baked into it. Refuses a missing, expired or set-mismatched token; 409s per target on version drift; re-runs the gate server-side before each channel call (the diff the operator saw is the input, never the authority). Per-target results in the shipped MarketOfferResult shape (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:46-55) with NO aggregate success. THIS IS THE ONLY WRITER.
- GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/relist/sources?channel=&market=&accountId=&aliasKey= — enumerates every snapshot this coordinate could relist FROM across all stores: ChannelListingSnapshot (via listSnapshots, services/pim/listing-snapshot.service.ts:141), offerCloseSnapshot, platformAttributes._versionHistory (written by BOTH ebay-cockpit.routes.ts:900-906 and amazon-cockpit.routes.ts:159-175), and ListingRecoveryEvent identifiers. Each entry carries store, reason, capturedAt, fidelity ('live'|'db'|'unknown') and sizeBytes, so 'nothing was ever captured' is visible up front instead of arriving as a FAILED string at the end.
- MODIFIED — reopenMarketOffers() (apps/api/src/services/amazon-market-offer.service.ts): route through revalidateRelist before patchPurchasableOffer (:263-265); strip elapsed discounted_price windows; RESTORE the captured control block (:189-191) instead of force-setting followMasterQuantity: true / quantityOverride: null (:282-283); return NEEDS_REVIEW instead of FAILED when a check blocks; gate the DB update and the QUANTITY_UPDATE enqueue (:276-299) on !res.dryRun; emit a ProductEvent with the real actor.
- MODIFIED — closeMarketOffers(): stop fabricating {currency:'EUR', our_price:[{schedule:[{value_with_tax: cl.price ?? 0}]}]} when the live read fails (:143-152) — store snapshotSource:'none' with no purchasableOffer; add capturedAt/costPriceAtCapture/currencyAtCapture/taxInclusiveAtCapture/liveReadSucceeded (:177-195); narrow loadRow's three-level filter (:66-76) to the full five-level coordinate; gate the DB write on !res.dryRun; emit a ProductEvent with the real actor, not 'sync-control'.
- PRECONDITION P10 — restoreToDraft must not blind-write platformAttributes. It copies every SNAPSHOT_FIELDS key verbatim (services/pim/listing-snapshot.service.ts:225-228) and the bag is written by at least three other paths, so the clobber is not limited to _versionHistory. Define a merge policy for the whole bag; add a vitest that captures, has another writer add a key, restores, and asserts the key survives. LATENT, not live: schema.prisma:1885-1889 measured _versionHistory on 0 of 977 listings on 2026-09-01 — re-run that count, against the right database, before grading it as data loss.
- PRECONDITION P11 — no local lifecycle mutation on a dryRun result, in both close and reopen. getAmazonPublishMode() returns 'gated' unless NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH is set and 'dry-run' for anything not literally 'live'/'production' (amazon-publish-gate.service.ts:44-54); patchPurchasableOffer then returns {success:true, dryRun:true} with no HTTP (clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts:794-795). Both paths already put 'dry-run' in the row detail (:200, :299) while the DB write has already happened — the label makes it harder to notice, not easier. Read the two env vars before grading this; I did not.
- PRECONDITION P12 — reopen must stop nulling offerClosedAt (:278) while it is the snapshot's only timestamp. Order: write capturedAt on the capture side first, then the clear is safe.
- PRECONDITION P13 — never store cl.price ?? 0 as an offer; reopen replays it verbatim through op:'replace'. An absence is honest, a zero is not.
- PRECONDITION P14 — apps/api/src/routes/listing-recovery.routes.ts has no auth guard and says so at :18-19 (verified: zero occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate). Add an in-file preHandler on all three routes; narrow executeRecovery's channelListing.updateMany from three levels to five (recovery.service.ts:334-348) AND write a ListingIdentity tombstone before it sets externalListingId: null (:344-348) for NEW_ASIN_* actions; refuse the global Product.sku rename (:360-377) until its cross-channel fan-out is enumerated and shown. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW, so an in-file guard is the only one that works today.
- PRECONDITION P15 (new, closes finding #71) — relist must NOT route through the master status cascade, which skips every ENDED/ERROR listing: a rollback that skips exactly the rows being restored under-restores silently, and the skipped list exists only in the audit log.
- PRECONDITION P16 (new, closes the sc:permissions-audit finding) — fix the false actor on the two lifecycle paths that already keep a record (recovery reads 'triggered by automation', offer-close reads 'sync-control') BEFORE emitting new ProductEvents beside them, or the new events inherit the lie.
- PRECONDITION P17 (new) — an edited REVALIDATE value must land locally through the ordinary mutator, emitting its PriceChangeEvent, before the channel call. Otherwise ChannelListing.price still holds the pre-relist value and the next follow-master push overwrites the channel. ComparePane.tsx:32-36 already rules this shape: no second write path, because a second write path is a second provenance story.
- PRECONDITION (DS) — every type addition lands in the .tsx AND its hand-written .d.ts (SourceIndicator.d.ts:1, tone.d.ts:2, registry.d.ts:81/172/188-189), with a gate asserting the pair agree. Editing only the .tsx is a silent no-op for consumers that resolve the declaration.
- REQUIRED BEFORE ANY relist PROMISE — exercise restoreToDraft against a captured request and read the BODY, not the status. It ships at product-studio.routes.ts:938-946 with ZERO web callers (verified: /usr/bin/grep -rn snapshots apps/web/src finds no reference to /products/:id/listings/:listingId/snapshots; control: restore-points = 6 hits). 'Recoverable with loss' is a promise this path keeps.
- AMAZON END — there is no patchListingsItem in the client to call (verified: zero hits for 'patchListingsItem(' in apps/api/src/clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts; only a comment at :56). Either wave 2 writes it, or the Amazon verb table says plainly that Amazon has a reversible offer close and a permanent delete and nothing between them.
- ETSY — refuse relist with a capability sentence, not a disabled control: apps/api/src/services/etsy/read-client.ts:23 returns {get, shopId} only, and apps/api/src/services/marketplaces/etsy.service.ts PATCHes price (:398), listing quantity (:423) and variation quantity (:448) and nothing else on a listing. Etsy must not be silently swept up by the WooCommerce containment rule.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE, no migration — five keys written INSIDE the existing ChannelListing.offerCloseSnapshot Json? column (schema.prisma:1596) by amazon-market-offer.service.ts:177-195: capturedAt, costPriceAtCapture, currencyAtCapture, taxInclusiveAtCapture, liveReadSucceeded. NOTE it is still a wire-shape change for that JSON's only reader (reopen, amazon-market-offer.service.ts:247-252), which must tolerate their absence on pre-existing snapshots and derive `unknown` — never a default. Their age is genuinely unknown and the gate must say so.
- ADDITIVE, no migration — the captured `control` block (followMasterQuantity, quantityOverride, syncPaused, written at :189-191) gains a READER. Reopen currently ignores it and force-sets followMasterQuantity: true, quantityOverride: null at :282-283. This is a code change, not a schema change, and it is the correction the original proposal missed.
- NOTHING — ChannelListingSnapshot gains no field. It already carries channel, marketplace, aliasKey, reason, publishEventId, payload, capturedBy, createdAt, restoredAt, restoredBy (schema.prisma:1890-1934). What it lacks is CALLERS: captureSnapshot has exactly one production caller (product-studio.routes.ts:918) and it is not the publish path, so doctrine clause 1 (listing-snapshot.service.ts:9) is unimplemented. Wiring, not schema.
- NOT PROPOSED — a relistedFromItemId equivalent on ChannelListing. EbayListingIndex already carries relistedFromItemId (schema.prisma:14109) and endedAt (:14108) and its rows are never deleted. Re-verified with /usr/bin/grep across apps/api/src, apps/web/src and packages: the ONLY hits are schema.prisma:14109 and packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260703_e2_ebay_ads_data_layer/migration.sql:155. Zero code. The field needs a writer (the eBay relist path), not a sibling column.
- NOT PROPOSED — a relist-plan table. The plan is short-lived and belongs in the preflightToken; lifecycle history belongs in ProductEvent, which is already FK-free by design (schema.prisma:15475-15478) and already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing' — restoreToDraft already emits there (listing-snapshot.service.ts:302-317).
- BREAKING and NOT PROPOSED — making ChannelListingSnapshot.channelListingId nullable. Re-verified: the column is non-null at schema.prisma:1896 with `onDelete: Cascade` declared at :1893, so a hard delete takes the snapshots with it. The release precondition (a confirmed-gone fact before any purge) plus the parent's ListingIdentity tombstone cover the case. Named so no implementer proposes it as a shortcut.
- NOT A SCHEMA CHANGE BUT A PRECONDITION — PriceChangeEvent (schema.prisma:6331-6361) has no channelConnectionId and no aliasKey, and its index is [productId, channel, marketplace, changedAt]. R1 and R4 therefore cannot answer at the five-level coordinate. Adding those two columns WOULD be additive and would make R1 coordinate-accurate; I have NOT proposed it because the deeper problem is coverage (four writers, none of them the studio), and a finer key over a sparse table buys precision on a measurement that is mostly absent. If the Owner wants it, it is additive and must be applied to BOTH databases and verified before being declared — apps/api/.env pointed at a local Docker pg on 09-11 and only Product.version (54 vs 51) separated them.

##### Open for you
- THE RELIST REVIEW'S HOME. RestoreMode.tsx:4-9 records an Owner decision of 2026-09-01: restore is a MODE inside the History pane, not a fifth pane, specifically so there is 'no second home for the word restore'. I have moved the relist review there rather than into a presence-row drawer. But History would then carry three mechanisms that RestoreMode's own copy already works hard to separate. Does the Owner want relist as a third mode in History, or as its own surface with the 2026-09-01 reasoning amended?
- CH.1 — THE TENSION, STATED, NOT RESOLVED. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, verbatim at SheetToolbar.tsx:15-17 and :100-104) says a scope's verbs are ITEMS of the one overflow and never a button. This design does not need CH.1 to bend: the review lives in History and the plural selection uses the DS BulkActionBar, so the sheet toolbar is untouched. The smallest amendment, IF the Owner wants relist reachable from the sheet, keeps CH.1's actual rule (no new buttons) and asks only that MenuItemDef (Menu.tsx) gain a typed tone plus a separator group — `separator` already exists and one adapter already groups (menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:93). This is the Owner's ruling to make, and I am not presenting the amendment as settled.
- THE EDITABLE-DIFF SEAM. An editable review cannot live inside ActionConfirm: AskToConfirm returns a boolean (runAction.ts:33). I have put edits BEFORE the preflight, so the confirm renders a final read-only plan. The alternative is widening AskToConfirm to return a result object — a change to the DS's nine-rule safety seam, which exists because a five-live-listing delete once became a one-click delete. Does the Owner accept edit-then-replan, or want the seam widened?
- AMAZON END. patchListingsItem does not exist in the client (verified: /usr/bin/grep -n 'patchListingsItem(' apps/api/src/clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts returns nothing; channel-delist.service.ts:151-155 says so itself and falls back to DELETE). Should wave 2 include WRITING that client method so Amazon gains a non-destructive end, or does Amazon ship with only the reversible offer close and the permanent delete, with the UI saying exactly that?
- THE _versionHistory COLLISION'S SEVERITY. The mechanism is real, but schema.prisma:1885-1889 records a measurement of 0 of 977 listings on 2026-09-01, and amazon-cockpit.routes.ts:159-175 has since become a second writer. Should P10 be graded as live data loss (which requires re-running the count against the right database — apps/api/.env pointed at a local Docker pg on 09-11) or as a latent precondition? I have written it as latent.
- THE /recover PAGE. D9 already says 'Recover is History' (StudioHeader.tsx:87-97), and listing-recovery.routes.ts has no auth gate by its own admission (:18-19) while executeRecovery permanently deletes, nulls externalListingId across every account and alias in a market (recovery.service.ts:334-348) and renames Product.sku globally (:360-377). Does the Owner want it guarded and folded into History in wave 3, or taken offline now and rebuilt inside presence?
- EDITED VALUES AND PROVENANCE. §3.4 makes an edited REVALIDATE value land locally through the ordinary mutator before the channel call, per ComparePane.tsx:32-36 ('no compare-specific write path, because a second write path is a second provenance story'). That means a relist can change the local record even if the channel call then fails. Is that the right ordering, or should the local write be conditional on the channel call succeeding — accepting that the two can then diverge?
- ETSY. PublishReadiness (channel-ops/syncQueue.ts:317-322) describes amazon, ebay and shopify only. Either it gains an etsy branch, or wave 1 states plainly that Etsy presence and relist are READ-ONLY because Nexus holds a read-only Etsy client. Etsy must not be silently caught by the WooCommerce containment rule. Confirming which.

##### Risks
- R1 and R4 are only as good as PriceChangeEvent, whose writer swallows its own errors by design (price-history.service.ts:77-80) and whose four callers exclude every studio write path. Until the studio emits price events (P17), the gate will return `unknown` on most coordinates and the diff will look useless. That is honest and it will read as broken; the Owner has to want it
- The gate is a server-side computation over inputs that are frequently null: Product.costPrice and minMargin are both nullable (schema.prisma:216-217). R3 will be `unknown` far more often than `pass`, and the weakest-check rule means a mostly-unknown gate blocks nothing and reassures nobody
- Moving the relist review into the History pane as a mode makes History carry three different mechanisms (master record restore, PES.5 listing restore, channel relist). RestoreMode.tsx:12-19 already spends 8 lines distinguishing two. A third may exceed what one pane's copy can honestly separate, and the failure mode is an operator picking the wrong 'restore'
- P10's merge policy for platformAttributes is not obviously derivable: there is no schema for that bag, so 'merge current over restored' can resurrect a key the operator meant to roll back, and 'restore whole' clobbers concurrent writers. Either choice is wrong for some key, and the code has no way to know which
- The Amazon `end` verb has no non-destructive implementation to call (patchListingsItem does not exist in the client). Anything the UI says about ending an Amazon listing without deleting the SKU is a promise about code nobody has written
- No arm of this design has been exercised. restoreToDraft has never run from a UI, reopenMarketOffers' behaviour under a live (non-dry-run) mode is unmeasured, and I read prod env for neither. Every 'recoverable with loss' sentence is a prediction
- The whole plural half depends on the presence surface the parent programme proposes, which the Owner has not ruled on. If presence is refused, plan/apply has no selection surface and only the single-coordinate review survives

##### The specification
# CORRECTIONS
## A. Paths an implementer would not find
1. `listing-snapshot.service.ts` → **`apps/api/src/services/pim/listing-snapshot.service.ts`**.
2. `amazon-sp-api.client.ts` → **`apps/api/src/clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts`** (not `services/`).
3. `ebay-columns.ts` → **`apps/web/src/app/products/ebay-flat-file/ebay-columns.ts`** — it is a **web** column contract, not an API one. `row_action` is at :152-162.
4. `shopify.service.ts` → **`apps/api/src/services/marketplaces/shopify.service.ts`**; `updateProduct` :383, `deleteProduct` :407.
5. `etsy/read-client.ts` → **`apps/api/src/services/etsy/read-client.ts`**; the `{get, shopId}` return is at **:23**, not :5.
6. **Two files are called `BulkActionBar.tsx`.** The hard-delete-preflight caller is `apps/web/src/app/products/_components/BulkActionBar.tsx:1104` (65 KB, page-local). The DS pattern is `apps/web/src/design-system/patterns/BulkActionBar.tsx` (1 KB: `count`, `children`, `onClear`, `noun`). The proposal's DS list is right; the citation is a different file.
7. `recovery.service.ts` line numbers are off: the three-level `updateMany` is **:334-348** (not :325-345); the global `Product.sku` rename is **:360-377** (not :357-370).
8. The brief's `ground-*.json` files **do not exist** in the scratchpad. Phase 0's output is `vitest-studio.txt`, `tests-index.txt`, `api-tsc.txt`, `tsc-web.txt`. I read those instead.
## B. Claims about current behaviour that are wrong
9. **`_versionHistory` has two writers, not one.** `apps/api/src/routes/amazon-cockpit.routes.ts:159-175` writes it with the same strip-then-prepend shape as the eBay cockpit. "Written and read only by the eBay cockpit" is false.
10. **The collision is latent, not live.** The schema's own comment above `ChannelListingSnapshot` (`packages/database/prisma/schema.prisma:1885-1889`) records a measurement: *"it has never actually run — measured 2026-09-01, `_versionHistory` is present on 0 of 977 listings."* P10 stands as a precondition; "silently destroys every eBay cockpit snapshot taken since" is a prediction about a store that held zero rows when last counted. Re-run the count before grading it as data loss.
11. **And §0a understates it in the other direction.** `restoreToDraft` writes `platformAttributes` **whole** (`listing-snapshot.service.ts:226-228` copies each `SNAPSHOT_FIELDS` key verbatim). It clobbers *every* key any writer added since capture — the eBay category bag, the Amazon `productType` the reopen path reads at `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:252`, anything. A single-key strip is not the fix; a merge policy for the whole bag is.
12. **R1 cannot PASS.** `price-history.service.ts:82` is a **writer** (`recordPriceChange`), whose own header at :77-80 says *"Best-effort: swallows and logs any error so the caller's price write is never disrupted."* Callers, measured with `/usr/bin/grep -rln`: **four files** — `jobs/repricing-evaluator.job.ts`, `routes/pricing.routes.ts`, `services/promotion-scheduler.service.ts`, and the service itself. `apps/api/src/services/pim/` and `routes/product-studio.routes.ts` contain **zero** calls, so a studio price edit emits no event at all. Absence of a newer `PriceChangeEvent` is therefore not evidence of freshness. **R1 returns `unknown` when no event exists — never `pass`.**
13. **R1 cannot be evaluated at the coordinate.** `PriceChangeEvent` (`schema.prisma:6331-6361`) carries `productId, sku, channel, marketplace, fulfillmentMethod` and **no `channelConnectionId`, no `aliasKey`**; its index is `[productId, channel, marketplace, changedAt]`. On a multi-account or aliased coordinate R1 answers market-wide at best and must say which granularity it answered at.
14. **`patchListingsItem` does not exist in the client.** `/usr/bin/grep -n "patchListingsItem(" apps/api/src/clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts` returns nothing (only a comment at :56). What exists: `putListingsItem` :843, `deleteListingsItem` :1133, `getListingsItem` :1212, `patchListingPrice` :647, `patchPurchasableOffer` :772. Any design claiming "PATCH availability=DISCONTINUED exists and is not wired" is wrong — it must be **written**. `channel-delist.service.ts:151-155` says so in its own words: *"isn't wired in the client yet."*
15. `patchListingPrice`'s `op:'replace'` patch body is **:676-699**; the audit's own verdict flags the roster's `:687-699` as off by one (`:686-699`).
16. **§5.4 is right but incomplete, and the truth is worse.** Both close and reopen already put the mode in the row result: `detail: res.dryRun ? 'dry-run' : undefined` (:200, :299). So the operator is *told* "dry-run" while the DB write at :177-195 / :276-286 has already happened. The label makes the divergence harder to see, not easier.
17. **A reopen defect the proposal missed, inside its own subject.** Close captures `control: { followMasterQuantity, quantityOverride, syncPaused }` (:189-191). Reopen **discards it** and force-sets `followMasterQuantity: true, quantityOverride: null` (:282-283). A coordinate that carried a manual quantity before the close comes back on follow-master. That is a REPLAY field the code silently reclassifies as REMINT.
18. **The `reversal` DS gap is a REFUTED finding.** The audit's verdict on *"ActionImpact cannot express reversibility"* is REFUTED, reasoned: the harm was asserted about two verbs that do not exist, `ActionConfirm.tsx:90-96` already differentiates a permanent verb's footer, and the DS's stated doctrine is *"the dialog never decides severity, it renders what the preflight found"* (:29-30). `reversal` stays in this design as a **contract enhancement**, not a defect closed. The CONFIRMED neighbour is `GridAction.danger` being dropped by every menu adapter (0 hits in `menuAdapters.tsx`, control 5 for `label`).
## C. Contradicts a standing ruling, or the precedent it cites
19. **The proposal cites ComparePane as support and then builds what ComparePane rejected.** `ComparePane.tsx:20-28`: *"The shape is one field × a handful of coordinates — not a matrix. Rendered as columns in a 380–900px panel, five targets get ~70px each, which is why the table needed a horizontal scroller. Needing one inside a drawer was the smell. So: one section per field, one row per coordinate."* A three-column `SummaryTable` inside a `Drawer` (`DrawerProps.width` defaults to 420px, :32-37) **is** that table. Corrected below.
20. **Restore already has an Owner-ruled home.** `drawer/panes/RestoreMode.tsx:4-9`: *"A MODE inside the History pane, not a fifth pane (Owner, 2026-09-01)… the mode switch is what keeps both readable **without a second home for the word 'restore'**."* A relist-review drawer is a second home. Corrected below.
21. **`ListingsPane` argues against the chosen surface, by name.** `:5-9`: *"Read-only, and that is a decision rather than an omission… a 'Publish' button inside a record drawer is exactly how a preview-only channel gets published by reflex."* A channel-reaching relist behind a drawer row menu is that button.
22. **D9 already ruled on `/recover`.** `StudioHeader.tsx:87-97`: *"the Datasheet is the sheet; **Recover is History**."* §8's "wave 3: folded in or retired" is not an open choice.
## D. DS mechanics the proposal assumes and the code does not provide
23. **An editable review cannot live inside `ActionConfirm`.** `runAction.ts:33` types `AskToConfirm = (impact) => boolean | Promise<boolean>`, and `ActionConfirm`'s `Pending.resolve` is `(ok: boolean) => void` (:42-45). There is no channel for the operator's edits to return through. Widening it would change the DS's single most safety-critical seam — the nine rules at `runAction.ts:38-52`, which exist because a five-live-listing delete once became a one-click delete.
24. **Every DS type is restated in a hand-written `.d.ts`.** `SourceIndicator.d.ts:1`, `tone.d.ts:2`, `registry.d.ts:81,172,188-189`. Adding `reach`, `reversal`, `review` or three `ValueSourceKind` members to the `.tsx` alone is a silent no-op for consumers that resolve the declaration — the "a token scale can be defined TWICE" trap in type form.
25. `Stepper` (`StepperProps:27-36`) is a display-only `<ol>` of `steps` + a `current` index. It can *show* where a resolution flow is; it cannot *be* the flow.
26. `SummaryTable` takes `rows: {id, cells: readonly ReactNode[]}`, so an `Input` in a cell compiles — but its own doc (:9) reads *"Compact, **read-only** comparisons inside cards and drawers. Interactive datasets use NexusGrid."* Making it editable stretches a DS contract by stealth.
## E. Findings left open that are this proposal's own subject
27. **#4 CONFIRMED** — promotions and pricing snapshots are written to ENDED listings and the timeline records a promo that started on an unbuyable listing (`promotion-scheduler.service.ts:58-75,106-137,145-160`). R1 and R5 read that timeline. Precondition, not context.
28. **#71 CONFIRMED** — *"Undoing a bulk take-down silently under-restores: rollback re-runs the master status cascade, which **skips every ENDED/ERROR listing**."* A relist routed through that cascade skips precisely the rows being relisted. New **P15**.
29. **sc:permissions-audit CONFIRMED** — the two lifecycle paths that do keep a record write a **false actor**: recovery reads *"triggered by automation"*, offer-close *"sync-control"*. §5.5's new `ProductEvent` inherits the lie unless the actor is fixed with it. New **P16**.
30. **§0d misses the sharpest half.** `executeRecovery`'s three-level `updateMany` also sets **`externalListingId: null`** for `NEW_ASIN_*` actions (`recovery.service.ts:344-348`), across every account and alias in the market. That is identity surrender with no tombstone, and it is the concrete reason the parent programme's `ListingIdentity` must be written on this path.
## F. Schema
Nothing is mislabelled. The one BREAKING change named is correctly **NOT PROPOSED**: `ChannelListingSnapshot.channelListingId` is non-null at `schema.prisma:1896` with `onDelete: Cascade` at :1893. One addition: writing five new keys into `offerCloseSnapshot` needs no migration but **is** a wire-shape change for its only reader (reopen, :247-252), which must tolerate their absence on pre-existing snapshots and derive `unknown` — never a default.
---
# Restore — the relist half of "put it back" (corrected)
## 1. The one sentence (unchanged, and sound)
Relist is not a replay. Every field falls into exactly one class, and the class — not the snapshot — decides what is sent. **REPLAY** (title, description, bullets, images, aspects, variation theme, category: verbatim). **REVALIDATE** (price, sale price, promo windows, quantity, handling time, business policies: recomputed from today's inputs; the snapshot value is context, never payload). **REMINT** (identity the channel issues — `SNAPSHOT_FIELDS` already excludes `externalListingId` for exactly this reason, `listing-snapshot.service.ts:62-68`). **REFUSE** (`version`, sync bookkeeping, `platformAttributes._versionHistory`). **RE-ASK** (GPSR, EPR, hazmat, category-schema version: a snapshot proves what we *said*, not that it is still true).
A sixth class the corrections force: **EXCLUDE**. `RestoreMode.tsx:25-30` already rules it — *"the server grades a field `uncertain` when it changed after the chosen time and no prior value was ever recorded. Restoring one would write a value NOBODY HAS — an invention wearing the costume of a rollback."* A REPLAY field whose snapshot value is absent is excluded and listed with its reason, not defaulted.
Today's only reversible relist puts everything in REPLAY: `reopenMarketOffers` pushes `offerCloseSnapshot.purchasableOffer` with `op:'replace'` (`amazon-market-offer.service.ts:263-265`) — no pricing engine, no floor, no margin, no currency check, no inspection of `discounted_price` windows — **and discards the control block it captured** (:189-191 vs :282-283).
## 2. Per channel (unchanged except Amazon's `end`)
Amazon's reversible hold is the `purchasable_offer` delete (:165-172); eBay's is offer qty 0 with the ItemID kept (`ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1873-1901`, contract at `ebay-columns.ts:152-162`); Shopify's is `status:'draft'` (`channel-delist.service.ts:192-195`); **Etsy has none** — `etsy/read-client.ts:23` returns `{get, shopId}` and `marketplaces/etsy.service.ts` writes price (:398), listing quantity (:423) and variation quantity (:448) only. Etsy relist is **refused with a capability sentence**, never greyed with a shrug, and never swept up by the WooCommerce containment rule.
Correction to Amazon `end`: there is no `patchListingsItem` to call (§B14). Until one is written, Amazon has **no non-destructive end** — only the offer close (reversible) or `deleteListingsItem` (permanent). The verb table must say that, not promise availability=DISCONTINUED.
Identity survives on Amazon (ASIN and seller SKU) and **not** on eBay (SellSimilar mints a new ItemID) or Shopify (new product id, handle, variant ids). Lost even on success: offer age and buy-box history; watchers, item sales history and Best Offer history; handle, SEO redirects, publication memberships and metafields — none of which are in `SNAPSHOT_FIELDS`, so Shopify's `status:'active'` is a status flip, not a relist, and its fidelity is `lossy`.
## 3. The revalidation gate
`revalidateRelist(coordinate, snapshot, verb)` runs server-side, per coordinate, before any channel write, returning `RelistCheck[]` of `{id, verdict: 'pass'|'warn'|'block'|'unknown', sentence, evidence, granularity}`.
R1 freshness · R2 floor (`Product.minPrice` at `schema.prisma:218`, `RepricingRule.minPrice` at :1220, consumed today via `master-field-gate.ts:29-31`) · R3 margin (`costPrice` :216, `minMargin` :217) · R4 cost drift · R5 promo window · R6 currency/tax shape (`Marketplace.taxInclusive` :1961) · R7 zero price · R8 identity (`getListingsItem`; `getItemListingStatus`, `ebay-trading-api.service.ts:316`) · R9 coordinate (reuse `coordOf`, `listing-snapshot.service.ts:87`) · R10 operational (`GET /products/hard-delete-preflight`, `products-catalog.routes.ts:1666`).
**`ProductVariation.costPrice/minPrice/maxPrice` (`schema.prisma:1331-1333`) is off-limits** — the model's own header at :1307 reads *"Do not add new reads or writes to this model."*
Four rules govern the roll-up, one of them new:
- **The weakest check decides, and `unknown` never rolls up to `pass`.** The doctrine already ships at `ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:5-9` and `ebay-item-status-reconcile.job.ts:14-16`.
- **A check's PASS must be earned by a positive observation, never by an absence.** This is the correction. R1 and R4 read `PriceChangeEvent`, whose writer swallows its own errors and whose four callers exclude the studio (§B12) — so "no newer event" means `unknown`. Each check declares the **granularity it actually answered at**: R1 is coordinate-blind below the market (§B13), and a market-wide `pass` on a five-level target renders as `warn`, not `pass`.
- **Staleness is a comparison, not a clock.** No invented TTL; a captured price older than a real newer event is stale by construction.
- **A `block` is not a `FAILED`.** It returns `NEEDS_REVIEW` naming the check.
### 3.4 An edited REVALIDATE value lands locally FIRST (new)
If the operator edits the price in the diff and `apply` sends it only to Amazon, `ChannelListing.price` still holds last season's value and the next follow-master push overwrites the channel. `ComparePane.tsx:32-36` already rules this shape: *"A copy is a WRITE on the target's scope, through the host's mutator — the same `PATCH /api/products/bulk` every other edit uses. No compare-specific write path, because a second write path is a second provenance story."* So an edited REVALIDATE value is written locally through the ordinary mutator, **emitting a `PriceChangeEvent` for a price** (which closes the R1 hole for the next relist), and only then sent. Apply refuses an edit it could not land locally.
## 4. The diff, and where it actually lives
Three views of every field the adapter will write — **As captured / As it is now / What will be sent** — because two columns cannot tell the operator whether a difference is the snapshot or the drift. That much is right. The *rendering* is corrected:
**Idiom:** one section per field, one row per view, in the card idiom — the shape `ComparePane.tsx:20-28` chose after measuring that a coordinate table inside a 380–900px panel needed a horizontal scroller. Not a three-column `SummaryTable` in a 420px `Drawer`. `SummaryTable` remains the right component when the review is opened in a `Modal` at a stated width, where three columns fit; the drawer path uses sections. Both render the same data; neither hand-rolls a table.
**Home:** a **mode inside the existing History pane**, beside RestoreMode — the Owner's 2026-09-01 shape, whose stated purpose is *"no second home for the word restore."* `RestoreMode.tsx:12-19` already distinguishes two mechanisms (master record restore vs PES.5 listing restore); it gains a third (channel relist) and says which is which. This is the D9-conforming answer and it does not depend on the presence surface being approved.
**Editing:** read-only in the review. Edits are made in a field beneath the row, *before* the preflight; the surface then calls `plan` again and gets a fresh impact. `ActionConfirm` renders the **final** plan read-only. This preserves `runAction` rule 7 (`run` receives the impact its own preflight produced, :47-49), ActionConfirm rule 4 (:29-30), and the boolean `AskToConfirm` seam (§D23) untouched.
**Verbs:** the review never runs the channel call. It produces a plan; apply happens through the staged plan/apply path — the answer to `ListingsPane.tsx:5-9`, which is otherwise a direct objection to a channel-reaching button in a drawer.
DS components as claimed, all verified present: `SummaryTable.tsx`, `Drawer.tsx`, `KeyValue.tsx`, `Banner.tsx`, `primitives/Pill.tsx` + `tone.ts`, `SourceIndicator.tsx`, `EmptyState.tsx`, `patterns/BulkActionBar.tsx`, `grid/actions/registry.ts`, `ActionConfirm.tsx`. `Stepper` is demoted to a progress indicator (§D25).
## 5. The Amazon reopen fix
1. **Capture carries its own as-of** — `capturedAt`, `costPriceAtCapture`, `currencyAtCapture`, `taxInclusiveAtCapture`, `liveReadSucceeded`, inside the existing `Json?` column. Reopen nulls `offerClosedAt` at :278, which is the snapshot's only timestamp today.
2. **Never store a fabricated price** — `:143-152` falls back to `{currency:'EUR', our_price:[{schedule:[{value_with_tax: cl.price ?? 0}]}]}`. A null `cl.price` stores a **zero offer** that reopen replays verbatim, and `'EUR'`/`value_with_tax` are wrong for UK, SE and PL. Store `snapshotSource:'none'` with no `purchasableOffer`.
3. **Route reopen through the gate**, strip elapsed `discounted_price` windows, name them in the diff.
4. **Restore the control block** (new) — reopen honours the captured `followMasterQuantity`/`quantityOverride`/`syncPaused` (:189-191) instead of force-setting follow (:282-283).
5. **Do not act on a fabricated success** — `getAmazonPublishMode()` returns `gated` unless `NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH` is set, and `dry-run` for anything not literally `live`/`production` (`amazon-publish-gate.service.ts:44-54`); `patchPurchasableOffer` then returns `{success:true, dryRun:true}` with no HTTP (`clients/amazon-sp-api.client.ts:794-795`). Gate every local write and the `QUANTITY_UPDATE` enqueue on `!res.dryRun`. **I did not read prod env and do not assert which mode prod runs.**
6. **Record it, with the real actor** — `/usr/bin/grep -c 'auditLog\|productEvent' amazon-market-offer.service.ts` = 0. Emit `ProductEvent` with `aggregateType:'ChannelListing'`, the five-level coordinate, and the **operator**, not `'sync-control'` (§E29).
## 6. When the snapshot is unusable — five arms
`NO_SNAPSHOT` (the common case: `captureSnapshot` has exactly one production caller, `product-studio.routes.ts:918`, and it is **not** the publish path, so doctrine clause 1 at `listing-snapshot.service.ts:9` is unimplemented) — offer *"Relist from current values"*, labelled a **build, not a restore**. `LOW_FIDELITY` (`snapshotSource:'db'`, stored at :147, surfaced nowhere). `STALE_BEYOND_USE`. `BLOCKED`. And `CANNOT_ASK` — the verify read failed: not a pass, not a fail, never a silent green.
## 7. Plural relist
A plan, not a loop, bound to a short-TTL `preflightToken` over the enumerated five-level set, 409 per target on version drift, results in the shipped `MarketOfferResult` shape (:46-55) with **no aggregate success**. Selection uses the DS `BulkActionBar` on the presence grid; CH.1 governs the sheet toolbar and is untouched.
## 8. Sequencing
**Wave 1:** P10–P17; the capture extension; `verify`; the diff **read-only** over `offerCloseSnapshot`, as a mode in History, so an operator can see what a reopen *would* send before any reopen verb exists. **Wave 2:** reopen through the gate; then `relist` per channel; then plan/apply. **Wave 3:** `release`; `/recover` folded into History, which D9 already decided.
Preconditions, on top of the programme's P1–P9:
- **P10** — a merge policy for `platformAttributes` on restore, not a `_versionHistory` strip (§B11). Latent today (§B10); re-run the 0-of-977 count before grading it.
- **P11** — no local lifecycle mutation on a `dryRun` result.
- **P12** — `offerCloseSnapshot.capturedAt` before reopen is allowed to clear `offerClosedAt`.
- **P13** — never store `cl.price ?? 0` as an offer.
- **P14** — an in-file `preHandler` on all three `/recover` routes (`listing-recovery.routes.ts:18-19` says *"No auth gate yet"*, and the file contains zero `preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate`); narrow `executeRecovery`'s `updateMany` to five levels; write a `ListingIdentity` tombstone before it nulls `externalListingId` (§E30); refuse the global `Product.sku` rename until its fan-out is enumerated.
- **P15** (new) — relist must not route through the master status cascade, which skips ENDED/ERROR rows (#71).
- **P16** (new) — fix the false actor on recovery and offer-close before adding events beside them.
- **P17** (new) — an edited REVALIDATE value lands locally through the ordinary mutator, emitting its `PriceChangeEvent`, before the channel call (§3.4).
- **Every DS type addition lands in the `.tsx` **and** its hand-written `.d.ts`** (§D24), with a gate asserting the two agree.
**Exercise obligation before any `relist` promise:** `restoreToDraft` ships at `product-studio.routes.ts:938-946` with **zero web callers** (verified: `/usr/bin/grep -rn snapshots apps/web/src` finds no reference to `/products/:id/listings/:listingId/snapshots`; control `restore-points` = 6 hits). Build the call from a captured request and read the **body**, not the status.
**The tree is not green:** 2 files, 6 tests failing (`vitest-studio.txt`: `layers.vitest.test.ts`, `masterWrite.vitest.test.ts`). Nothing here should be graded against a red baseline.

#### Fulfilment lanes, corrected: the offer level as a bounded pair inside the coordinate — with the durable axis journalled, not cascaded

The lane thesis survives review: FulfillmentMethod is a closed two-member enum and Offer is unique per (channelListing, fulfillmentMethod), so the set is bounded at two and fits a cell rather than a fourth nesting level. Fourteen things in the reviewed draft do not survive. The rename is refused — three test assertions pin the wording and the new name collides with the spine's own hold verb. The durable lane axis cannot live on Offer alone: Offer cascades on ChannelListing delete, so the removal paths the spine is fixing would erase it silently. Fulfilment is modelled five ways live, not two, in two vocabularies. The new sheet column is withdrawn as a drift generator. And amazon-mapper's reachability is established, not open — it defaults a null fulfilment to FBA on a live outbound path.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- NEW: apps/web/src/design-system/grid/renderers/FulfilmentLaneCell.tsx — a bounded multi-slot cell (at most two lanes, each with its own state mark, SKU and optional unit figure). Nothing in renderers/ holds two independently-marked sub-states in one cell; ProjectionCell.tsx is the nearest and holds one. It must inherit ProjectionCell's fill-handle discipline verbatim — params.value a scalar, all other facts read from the row through a callback (ProjectionCell.tsx:14-27) — or AG's fill stamps one coordinate's offer SKU down a whole column.
- NEW: apps/web/src/design-system/primitives/AsOfMark.tsx — a tokenized 'observed at / not measured' mark. Today an as-of is hand-rolled per surface (ago() at apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/drawer/format.ts:31 — NOT sheet/format.ts, which does not exist) and the difference between 'zero units' and 'never measured' has no DS affordance. Presence, the lane cell and ChannelTruthPanel all need the same one.
- CORRECTED GAP — the DS-GAPS entry is half stale. .claude/DS-GAPS.md:330 says MenuItemDef has 'no per-item title'. That half is CLOSED: MenuItemDef today carries title?: string, description?: ReactNode (Menu.tsx:42) and separator?: boolean (:49). The LIVE half is tone/severity and group — both adapters drop GridAction.danger (measured 0 occurrences of 'danger' in grid/actions/menuAdapters.tsx against a positive control of 5 for 'label'), and I re-measured the CSS: no .nds-menu danger rule anywhere in design-system/styles/ (positive control: 20 nds-menu hits in components.css). 'Withdraw the FBA offer' renders byte-identical to 'Reload'. The amendment is the Owner's ruling, not this proposal's.
- TYPE GAP: GridAction has no `reach` and ActionImpact no `reversal` (/usr/bin/grep -n reach on design-system/grid/actions/registry.ts returns rc=1). Both are needed before any lane verb ships, so the reversal sentence is rendered from the type instead of authored. Note that validateImpact(impact) receives only the impact (registry.ts:264-273) and cannot see an action-level field, so `reach` needs a sibling validateAction(action).
- TYPE GAP CORRECTED: offerScope does not exist anywhere in the repo (grep over apps/api/src and apps/web/src returns nothing), but it CANNOT be 'a required field on GridAction' as the reviewed draft proposed — a GridAction carries no request body. It belongs on the plan/apply API contract and, client-side, inside ActionImpact.payload, which registry.ts:105-116 documents as exactly the preflight-to-run channel for this.
- SourceIndicator's `kind` is a CLOSED union (components/SourceIndicator.tsx:7: master|override|rule|default|missing|linked|channel|formula|ai|warning). None of the fulfilment signal sources ('offer'|'listing-column'|'product-column'|'stock'|'fba-detail'|'platform-attributes') maps onto it. Either the signal set maps down to existing kinds explicitly, or the DS union is extended in design-system/ — never a page-local icon.

##### API
- EXTEND — GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence gains lanes[] per coordinate: { lane: 'FBA'|'FBM', label, sku, declared (+provenance, and whether it came from the ProductEvent journal or the Offer cache), observed, observedAt, observedVia, evidence[], units|null with its scope label, verbs[] with reach and reversal }. One or two entries, never zero. Amazon emits two; EBAY/SHOPIFY/ETSY emit one FBM-keyed lane labelled Merchant (C7).
- EXTEND — GET /api/products/:id/studio/presence/one?channel=&market=&accountId=&aliasKey= returns the same lanes[] for one coordinate, 400 on any missing dimension, reusing requireCoordinate's shape verbatim (product-studio.routes.ts:180-187, ruling #573).
- NEW — GET /api/products/:id/fulfilment-posture?channel=AMAZON&marketplace=IT — returns the signal SET, never a scalar: [{ source, lane, value, units?, scope: 'per-market'|'eu-pooled'|'account-unattributed', asOf }]. Implemented by lifting findFbaDeleteRows' derivation (flat-file.service.ts:2626-2645) with its record_action=delete filter DROPPED, not wrapped — wrapping returns empty for every product-scoped caller. Units from FbaInventoryDetail, buckets derived from the returned condition values.
- EXTEND — POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/verify writes the new Offer.offerObserved* columns per lane. Reuses getListingsItem (already called at amazon-market-offer.service.ts:132-137) plus an FbaInventoryDetail read. Four-valued; 'could-not-ask' is load-bearing and is NOT 'no units'.
- EXTEND — POST /api/products/:id/studio/presence/plan and /apply take a REQUIRED offerScope ('FBA'|'FBM'|'ALL'), 400 on offerScope !== 'ALL' for a non-Amazon channel. Fan-out computed per (verb, LANE, channel, market): one market for a purchasable_offer close (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:165-172), nine for any quantity-based hold (amazon-eu-quantity-guard.ts:24-26). offerScope lives on THIS contract, not on GridAction, which carries no request body (C13); client-side it travels in ActionImpact.payload (registry.ts:105-116).
- PRECONDITION P0 (blocking, read-only) — count Offer rows on BOTH databases and report WHICH answered. Offer.workspaceId defaults from current_setting('nexus.workspace_id'), so first establish whether RLS is enabled on the table: a count taken without that setting can return 0 for a non-empty table, which is a vacuous green, not a measurement. If genuinely empty, deriveFulfillmentMethod has been silently falling through to stock and legacy columns at all five call sites (listings-syndication.routes.ts:386, fulfillment.routes.ts:11060, stock.routes.ts:328, product-read-cache.service.ts:211, list-products.service.ts:1021).
- PRECONDITION P1 (RESTATED) — one fulfilmentSignals() in packages/shared/fulfilment-posture.ts, COORDINATE-scoped, returning the signal set. deriveFulfillmentMethod (fulfillment-derivation.service.ts:29-49) is a PURE function over pre-bucketed input and diverges from nothing — repointing it is a no-op; what must be shared is the QUERY that fills it, and its five callers all pass channelListing:{productId:{in:…}}, so one FBA offer anywhere makes every coordinate read FBA. Repoint the seven divergent predicates and reconcile location.type==='AMAZON_FBA' vs location.code==='AMAZON-EU-FBA' explicitly. packages/shared resolves subpaths through dist, so this needs an exports entry plus a build.
- PRECONDITION P2 (CORRECTED) — FBA_STRANDED_NOTICE becomes one exported constant in packages/shared, imported by amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:806-810 and by the studio preflight. The gate asserts the literal occurs exactly once under apps/*/src and packages/*/src — NOT 'in the repo', which would be vacuously red against apps/api/dist and packages/shared/dist. The literal carries a Unicode right single quote; match bytes.
- PRECONDITION P3 — closeMarketOffers/reopenMarketOffers must resolve the seller SKU from Offer.sku for the NAMED lane rather than cl.product.sku (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:88,231), and refuse when the lane has no Offer row and no unambiguous fallback. Its loadRow (:66-76) must also widen to the five-level coordinate — it findFirsts on (productId, channel, marketplace) alone.
- PRECONDITION P4 — POST /api/products/:id/fulfillment (product-channel-data.routes.ts:386-466) runs its writes under Promise.allSettled and returns { ok:true, updated: updates.length } at :466 regardless of what settled, reporting a rejected write as a success. It also hardcodes aliasKey:'' (:406,:416) and the primary connection (:398), so it cannot address a multi-account or multi-alias coordinate at all. Fix before any lane verb calls it.
- PRECONDITION P5 — repair or retire the three broken Offer writers before anything treats the table as authoritative: matrix.routes.ts:181-190 and :282-292, inbound-sync.service.ts:94-113. All sit behind (prisma as any). Retire is my recommendation for matrix.routes — no web caller (/usr/bin/grep 'matrix/offer' over apps/web/src returns rc=1) though it IS registered at index.ts:681, so retiring it is a capability removal and an Owner call.
- PRECONDITION P6 (CORRECTED SCOPE) — do NOT rename the verb id. Keep offer-toggle; change only the operator-facing wording to 'Pause selling on X' / 'Resume selling on X', reserving Hold for syncPaused and Offer for the lane. The write must include the three copy assertions at channelActions.vitest.test.ts:131,136,141 and the three DS doc comments at registry.ts:172, registry.d.ts:177 and menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:238. Separately fix the live bug found in passing: the preflight returns level:'confirm' TOGETHER with `unavailable` (channelActions.ts:246,:262-264), and runAction's rule 3 (runAction.ts:70-73) fires BEFORE rule 4, so a live selection is refused with the internal string 'preflight failed but still asked for a confirmation…' instead of the wave-1 sentence. Set level:'none' when unavailable is set.
- PRECONDITION P7 — expose ChannelListing.offerClosedAt / offerClosedBy / offerCloseReason (schema.prisma:1593-1596) on the studio wire. /usr/bin/grep -rln offerClosedAt over apps/web/src returns rc=1 against a positive control of 5 files for offerActive: the SCT.6 per-market close state is invisible to every web surface today, so a lane cannot say a market offer is already closed.
- PRECONDITION P8 — AUTHORISATION. Every route above needs an explicit in-file preHandler naming its permission. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:31-33: 'resolve + LOG what would be denied, but allow'), and amazon-flat-file.routes.ts — which hard-deletes ChannelListing rows from a caller-supplied target array at :1806-1820 — contains ZERO occurrences of preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate.
- PRECONDITION P9 (NEW — promoted from the reviewed draft's open items; reachability ESTABLISHED, not open) — amazon-mapper.service.ts:107,134 reads `product.fulfillmentChannel || 'FBA'` into an Amazon variation payload, and bulk-edit.service.ts:958-961 lets the studio's editable Fulfilment cell clear that field to null. The chain is live: workers/bullmq-sync.worker.ts:16,297 → variation-sync-processor.service.ts:14,75 → amazon-mapper.service.ts. (outbound-sync.service.ts:704 only MENTIONS phase 9 in a comment — that is what the earlier grep read.) Remove the silent 'FBA' default: an unset fulfilment must omit the field or refuse the payload, never assert FBA.
- PRECONDITION P10 (NEW) — product-channel-data.routes.ts:454 runs prisma.offer.findFirst({ … isActive: true }).catch(() => null): the FBM-cascade hold's FBA-offer signal degrades silently to 'no FBA offer' on any error, inside the guard that decides whether to flip a product's fulfilmentMethod to FBM. A thrown query must HOLD the flip, not clear it — 'could not measure' is not 'measured empty'.

##### Schema
- NOT PROPOSED — no new column on Offer for lane identity, SKU, price, quantity or on/off state. fulfillmentMethod, sku, price, quantity, isActive, offerMetadata and lastSync* (schema.prisma:2287-2325) already carry the whole declared axis. Resist adding leadTime/minPrice/maxPrice/costPrice: three write sites already pretend those exist and every one throws.
- ADDITIVE — Offer.offerObservedState String? / offerObservedAt DateTime? / offerObservedVia String? — the observed axis per lane, deliberately distinct from lastSyncedAt, whose meaning is documented as 'when a sync last RAN' (studio-sheet.service.ts:1416-1420). Null = UNKNOWN. NO BACKFILL: a lane written before these columns existed derives UNKNOWN, never SELLING. RENAMED from the reviewed draft's observedState/observedAt/observedVia so a grep for the spine's ChannelListing observation columns cannot return two vocabularies (C6).
- ADDITIVE — Offer.offerIntent String? / offerIntentAt DateTime? / offerIntentBy String? / offerIntentReason String? — the lane's declared intent (NONE|ACTIVE|HELD|WITHDRAWN). RENAMED from presenceIntent: the spine already puts ChannelListing.presenceIntent on an eight-member vocabulary, and two columns of the same name with different closed sets is reference_token_scale_defined_twice (C6). Nullable, never backfilled; a null derives provisionally from isActive and travels on the wire WITH its provenance.
- 🔴 CORRECTION TO THE REVIEWED DRAFT — these Offer columns are a CACHE, not the record. Offer declares onDelete: Cascade to ChannelListing (schema.prisma:2287-2288), and the two shipped deleteMany paths the spine's P8 narrows (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:645-647, amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:60-62) would erase an operator's declared lane intent, author and reason with no trace. Every lane intent write is therefore journalled to ProductEvent in the SAME transaction — FK-free by design (schema.prisma:15475-15478), already declaring aggregateType 'ChannelListing' — and the lane identity (fulfillmentMethod, offerSku) is recorded in the spine's ListingIdentity, which already carries both fields. A presence read whose cache is missing reconstructs from the journal; a cache disagreeing with the journal renders UNKNOWN, never the cache.
- NOT PROPOSED, and not needed — a new FulfilmentLane table. Offer IS the lane table: @@unique([workspaceId, channelListingId, fulfillmentMethod]) at schema.prisma:2322 is exactly the lane key. A second store recreates the drift this design exists to remove.
- NOT PROPOSED in wave 1 — no change to ChannelListing.fulfillmentMethod (schema.prisma:1531). It stays as written and becomes a derived cache only after the Owner rules on FCF.1-vs-Offer. Changing it first would be the design deciding on the Owner's behalf.
- NOT DECLARED, and not needed — no change to FbaInventoryDetail (schema.prisma:9522-9550). Two limits must be carried on the wire rather than fixed in schema: its unique key (workspaceId, sku, marketplaceId, fulfillmentCenterId, condition) at :9545 has NO channelConnectionId, so on a multi-account workspace every figure is account-unattributed; and its condition set is SELLABLE|UNFULFILLABLE|INBOUND|RESERVED|RESEARCHING (:9532), five members, so unit buckets are derived from the returned data, never from a hardcoded four (C11).
- SEQUENCING (binding) — apply the additive Offer columns to BOTH databases and verify before declaring them in schema.prisma, and run P0 first. Records show apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11 with only Product.version separating the two; discriminate, never infer.

##### Open for you
- THE FBA DEFAULT. amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63 refuses close/reopen on ANY FBA signal, fail-closed, on the Owner's stated rule that Amazon owns FBA logistics end-to-end. offerScope makes 'withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA' expressible. Does the Owner want offerScope:'FBM' permitted on a mixed coordinate, or does the fail-closed rule stand for every offer on an FBA product?
- FCF.1 vs Offer — the cardinality contradiction. ChannelListing.fulfillmentMethod (schema.prisma:1531) is a single nullable value per coordinate: fulfilment as EXCLUSIVE. Offer says a coordinate can hold both at once. Nothing reconciles them. I propose NO change to FCF.1 in wave 1 — it becomes a derived cache only after the Owner rules. Which is authoritative when they disagree?
- THE MASTER SHEET'S FULFILMENT CELL. field-registry.service.ts:108 is editable, two-valued, cannot express BOTH, writes Product.fulfillmentChannel which no FBA guard reads, and feeds a live outbound payload that defaults null to FBA (C3). Making it read-only removes a capability. I propose only re-labelling it 'Fulfilment (product default)' in wave 1. Does the Owner want it made read-only, or left editable with the mapper's default fixed instead?
- CH.1 — stated, not resolved, and this proposal does NOT need it to bend. CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, recorded at SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20) says a scope's verbs are items of the one overflow and never a button. Under it, 'Withdraw the FBA offer' would render byte-identical to 'Reload': MenuItemDef carries no tone and no group, both adapters drop GridAction.danger, and there is no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet (all three re-measured). Lane verbs live on the presence surface, so the sheet toolbar is untouched. The smallest amendment, IF the Owner wants lifecycle reachable from the sheet at all, keeps CH.1's actual rule (no new buttons) and asks only that the overflow gain a typed tone plus a group — `separator` already exists at Menu.tsx:49. This is the Owner's ruling to make.
- THE VOCABULARY. referenceLabels.ts:44-53 already names AMAZON_EU / AMAZON_NA / AMAZON_JP / AMAZON_IN alongside FBA/FBM/AFN/MFN/DEFAULT. A binary lane cannot hold those. Are the regional values dead ingest artefacts that should be normalised to FBA, or a real distinction the lane must carry?
- THE THREE BROKEN OFFER WRITERS. matrix.routes.ts has no web caller (/usr/bin/grep 'matrix/offer' over apps/web/src returns rc=1) but IS registered at index.ts:681. My recommendation is RETIRE for matrix.routes and REPAIR for inbound-sync.service.ts, which is on the SP-API catalog ingest path. Retiring a registered route is a capability removal and so an Owner call.
- ETSY. Nexus holds a read-only Etsy client (etsy/read-client.ts), so the Etsy lane can observe but never declare. Does the Owner want the Etsy lane rendered read-only with its verbs disabled-and-explained (the registry's stated rule), or hidden entirely?

##### Risks
- Offer may be empty in both databases: all three writers are broken against the schema (matrix.routes.ts:181-190 and :282-292 write leadTime/minPrice/maxPrice/costPrice; inbound-sync.service.ts:94-113 upserts on a unique named channelListingId_sku), none of which exist in the DDL (packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260502_phase31b_channellisting_marketplace/migration.sql:95-110) or the model (schema.prisma:2287-2325). All three sit behind (prisma as any), which is why typecheck is green. If Offer is empty, the tier-1 lane signal never fires and every lane reads UNKNOWN on day one.
- A zero row count for Offer may be a measurement failure, not a fact: Offer.workspaceId defaults from current_setting('nexus.workspace_id'). A count taken in a session without that setting can return 0 for a non-empty table. P0 must establish whether RLS is enabled before treating zero as empty.
- The lane's durable axis is cached on a cascade-deleted child. Even with the journal (C5), a ChannelListing deleteMany silently drops the Offer cache, so the presence read must reconstruct lane intent from ProductEvent when the cache is missing — a code path exercised rarely and therefore the one most likely to be wrong.
- The wording change touches three test assertions and three design-system doc comments (registry.ts:172, registry.d.ts:177, menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:238). A partial change leaves the DS documenting a verb that reads differently on screen.
- Making the studio honest makes it look emptier: most lanes will read 'not measured' where today the master sheet shows a confident FBA or FBM. That is the change, not a regression, and the Owner has to want it.
- packages/shared resolves subpath exports through dist, so lifting fulfilmentSignals() and FBA_STRANDED_NOTICE requires an exports entry plus a build. Any gate asserting 'occurs exactly once' must be scoped to apps/*/src and packages/*/src or it will be vacuously red against the built copies.
- The plural half of this design depends entirely on the spine's presence surface existing. If the Owner rules that presence is a second way into the sheet (D9), only the per-coordinate lane slots survive and offerScope has nowhere to be chosen.

##### The specification
# CORRECTIONS — what I changed, and why
I re-derived every claim with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed` against the working tree. The lane thesis holds. Fourteen things in the reviewed draft do not.
**C1 — The rename is REFUSED. The claim that no test pins the wording is false.** The draft says the old wording "appears in the suite only as a fixture literal (`drawer/recordActions.vitest.test.ts:41`), not as a copy assertion." Three copy assertions exist: `sheet/channel/channelActions.vitest.test.ts:131` (`'Activate 2 offers on eBay · IT'`), `:136` (`'Activate 2 of 3 offers on eBay · IT'`), `:141` (`'Pause offer on eBay · IT'`). Worse, the *id* `offer-toggle` is the canonical worked example for the callable-label feature in the design system's own documentation — `design-system/grid/actions/registry.ts:172`, `registry.d.ts:177`, `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:238`. Renaming the id falsifies three DS doc comments.
**C2 — And the proposed new name is the wrong word anyway.** The draft renames `offer-toggle` → `listing-hold`. The spine already defines `hold`/`resume` as the coordinate-wide *local* verb writing `syncPaused`. `offer-toggle` writes `ChannelListing.offerActive` via `PATCH /products/:id/offer-availability` (`channelActions.ts:163,262-274`). Those are two different mechanisms; giving them one word is the exact defect the draft opened by naming. **Corrected:** keep the id. Change the operator-facing word to **"Pause selling on X" / "Resume selling on X"**, reserving *Hold* for `syncPaused` and *Offer* for the lane. The three assertions and the three DS comments move in the same write, named here so nobody discovers them at build time.
**C3 — `amazon-mapper` reachability is ESTABLISHED, and it is a live hazard.** The draft filed it as "reachability unestablished". The chain is `workers/bullmq-sync.worker.ts:16,297` → `variation-sync-processor.service.ts:14,75` → `amazon-mapper.service.ts:107,134`. (Only `outbound-sync.service.ts:704` mentions phase 9, and that is a comment, not an import — the draft's grep was reading the comment.) The mapper reads `product.fulfillmentChannel || 'FBA'` and `child.fulfillmentChannel || product.fulfillmentChannel || 'FBA'`. `bulk-edit.service.ts:958-961` accepts empty as `null`. So **clearing the studio's editable Fulfilment cell makes an outbound Amazon variation payload assert FBA** — on a field no FBA guard reads. Promoted from an open question to precondition P9.
**C4 — Fulfilment is modelled FIVE ways live, in TWO vocabularies, not twice.** `Product.fulfillmentMethod` (`schema.prisma:121`), `Product.fulfillmentChannel` (`:262`, "default for all variations"), `ChannelListing.fulfillmentMethod` (`:1531`, FCF.1), `Offer.fulfillmentMethod` (`:2292`), and `ChannelListing.platformAttributes.fulfillmentChannel` written as **`'AFN'`/`'MFN'`** by `product-channel-data.routes.ts:405-407`. `ProductVariation.fulfillmentMethod` (`:1363`) is a sixth but is dead — hub ruling #1 in `docs/pes-claims.md` records `ProductVariation` at 0 rows. And the studio already normalises three different column ids into one label map including `AMAZON_EU`/`AMAZON_NA`/`AMAZON_JP`/`AMAZON_IN` (`_studio/sheet/referenceLabels.ts:44-53`) — a vocabulary a binary lane cannot hold.
**C5 — The durable lane axis cannot live on `Offer` alone; cascade destroys it.** `Offer` declares `onDelete: Cascade` to ChannelListing (`schema.prisma:2287-2288`). The spine's P8 exists because `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:645-647` and `amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:60-62` `deleteMany` ChannelListing on a three-level filter. Putting `presenceIntent` on `Offer` means the operator's declared "withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA", with its author and its reason, is erased with no trace by the very paths being narrowed. The spine chose `ProductEvent` (FK-free by design, `schema.prisma:15475-15478`) and `ListingIdentity` (plain-string `productId`, no relation) precisely to survive this. **Corrected:** every lane intent write is journalled to `ProductEvent` in the same transaction, and the lane's identity (`fulfillmentMethod`, `offerSku`) is recorded in the spine's `ListingIdentity`, which already carries both fields. The `Offer` columns become a **read cache of the journal**, not the record of it.
**C6 — `Offer.presenceIntent` collides by name with `ChannelListing.presenceIntent` and carries a different closed vocabulary.** The spine's is `NONE|DRAFT|LIVE|HELD|WITHDRAWN|ENDED|DISCONTINUED|RELEASED`; the draft's lane one is `NONE|ACTIVE|HELD|WITHDRAWN`. One grep, two answers — the shape of `reference_token_scale_defined_twice`. **Corrected:** `Offer.offerIntent`, `Offer.offerObservedState`, `offerObservedAt`, `offerObservedVia`.
**C7 — `'MERCHANT'` is a label, not a key.** `enum FulfillmentMethod { FBA FBM }` (`schema.prisma:10-13`) is closed and `Offer.fulfillmentMethod` is non-null. A lane keyed `'MERCHANT'` cannot address an `Offer` row on eBay, Shopify or Etsy. **Corrected:** the key is always the enum — `FBM` on merchant channels, per `ChannelListing.fulfillmentMethod`'s own comment (`:1528`, "always FBM on eBay/merchant channels"). *Merchant* is what the slot is **called** there.
**C8 — The additive read-only Fulfilment column on the Amazon channel sheet is WITHDRAWN.** `field-registry.service.ts:108` already registers an **editable** column labelled `Fulfillment`, and `referenceLabels.ts:51-53` already names three fulfilment column ids. A fourth header reading "Fulfilment", read-only, with different semantics from the master's editable one, is `reference_two_column_builders_drift` by construction. **Corrected:** no new sheet column in wave 1. The lane lives on the presence surface. The master sheet's *existing* cell is re-labelled and re-sourced (§5), which is where the decision actually is.
**C9 — P1's framing is wrong for one of the six.** `deriveFulfillmentMethod` (`fulfillment-derivation.service.ts:29-49`) is a **pure** function over pre-bucketed input; its own header says "Pure function; no I/O. Callers do the queries." It reads no column and diverges from nothing. Repointing it is a no-op. What must be shared is the **query that fills its input** — and its five callers all pass `channelListing: { productId: { in: … } }`, which is the actual defect.
**C10 — A seventh predicate exists, and it swallows its own error.** `product-channel-data.routes.ts:454`: `prisma.offer.findFirst({ … isActive: true }).catch(() => null)`. The FBM-cascade hold's FBA-offer signal degrades silently to "no FBA offer" on any error — "could not measure" rendering as "measured empty" *inside the guard that decides whether to flip a product to FBM*. New precondition.
**C11 — `FbaInventoryDetail` has no account dimension, and its condition set is larger than four.** Its unique is `(workspaceId, sku, marketplaceId, fulfillmentCenterId, condition)` (`schema.prisma:9545`) — no `channelConnectionId`. A lane inside a five-level coordinate therefore cannot get per-account units. And `condition` is documented as `SELLABLE|UNFULFILLABLE|INBOUND|RESERVED|RESEARCHING` (`:9532`) — the draft's four-field object drops `RESEARCHING`, which is a hardcoded member list of a set that lives in data.
**C12 — The MenuItemDef gap claim is half stale.** `MenuItemDef` today carries `title?: string`, `description?: ReactNode` (`Menu.tsx:42`) and `separator?: boolean` (`:49`). The DS-GAPS entry the draft relays (`.claude/DS-GAPS.md:330`, written 2026-08-26) says there is "no per-item `title`" — that half is closed. The **live** gap is `tone`/severity and `group` only. I re-measured the CSS half: no `.nds-menu` danger rule in `design-system/styles/` (positive control: 20 `nds-menu` hits in `components.css`).
**C13 — `offerScope` cannot be a required field on `GridAction`.** A `GridAction` carries no request body. It belongs on the plan/apply contract and, client-side, inside `ActionImpact.payload` — the channel the registry documents for exactly this (`registry.ts:105-116`).
**C14 — Citation drift, corrected throughout.** `ActionImpact.sideEffects` is `registry.ts:93` (not 88-92); `findings` `:102` (not :104); `confirmPhrase` `:118` (not :117); `danger` `:184`. The quantity-summation guard is `sheet/channel/rows.ts:15,295` and `sheet/channel/rows.vitest.test.ts:108`. `ago()` is `_studio/drawer/format.ts:31`, not `_studio/sheet/format.ts`, which does not exist. DS-GAPS heading is `:330`.
---
# 1. The unit is a LANE — preserved, and why it holds
The operator already holds three levels: product/family, ambient coordinate, variant row × attribute column. Offers as child rows would be a fourth. They need not be, because the set is **bounded at two by the schema**: `FulfillmentMethod` is closed (`schema.prisma:10-13`) and `Offer`'s unique key `@@unique([workspaceId, channelListingId, fulfillmentMethod])` (`:2322`) permits at most two rows per coordinate, forever.
So the offer level is a **cardinality correction on the coordinate, rendered as a fixed pair of lane slots inside the coordinate's own row.** Amazon renders two, FBA and FBM. eBay, Shopify and Etsy render one slot, **labelled** *Merchant* and **keyed** `FBM` (C7). No expander, no child row, no new breadcrumb.
`offerScope` becomes a mandatory discriminated field on the plan/apply contract — `'FBA' | 'FBM' | 'ALL'`, 400 on anything but `'ALL'` for a non-Amazon channel. `/usr/bin/grep -rn offerScope` over `apps/api/src` and `apps/web/src` returns nothing, so this is new surface, not a rename.
# 2. `OfferLane`: two axes, never merged — with the durable one journalled
Presence's record gains `lanes: OfferLane[]` — one or two, never zero; an absent lane is `declared: 'NONE'`.
- **`lane: 'FBA' | 'FBM'`** — the enum. **`label`** carries *Merchant* on non-Amazon channels.
- **`sku: string | null`** — `Offer.sku` for that lane. This answers "which seller SKU does a verb target", and nothing answers it today: `closeMarketOffers` uses `cl.product.sku` (`amazon-market-offer.service.ts:88`) with no lane.
- **`declared`** — `NONE | ACTIVE | HELD | WITHDRAWN`, with `at / by / reason`. **The record of it is a `ProductEvent` row; `Offer.offerIntent` is a cache** (C5). A cache that disagrees with the journal renders `unknown`, never the cache.
- **`observed`** — `UNKNOWN | SELLING | NOT_SELLING | ABSENT | REFUSED`, with mandatory `observedAt` and `observedVia`. **`UNKNOWN` is the default, never backfilled, never rendered as agreement** — the doctrine already ships at `ChannelTruthPanel.tsx:3-13`.
- **`evidence: FulfilmentSignal[]`** — itemised, each with source and as-of. A lane never shows a verdict it cannot explain.
- **`units`** — FBA only; buckets **derived from the `condition` values actually returned**, not from a hardcoded four (C11), each carrying `scope: 'per-market' | 'eu-pooled' | 'account-unattributed'`. `null` means *not measured*, a different mark from zero.
Verdict (`agrees | pending | diverged | unknown`) is derived on read, never stored.
# 3. One signal set where there are seven predicates today
| where | signals |
|---|---|
| `fulfillment-derivation.service.ts:29-49` | **pure**; consumes offers → stock buckets → fallback (C9) |
| `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63` | `ChannelListing.fm` OR `Product.fm` — no Offer, no stock |
| `fba-flip-guard.job.ts:44-52` | CL.fm OR P.fm OR StockLevel `code='AMAZON-EU-FBA'` OR active FBA Offer |
| `flat-file.service.ts:2626-2645` | `Product.fm` OR StockLevel `code='AMAZON-EU-FBA'` |
| `products-catalog.routes.ts:1246-1256` | StockLevel code OR active FBA Offer |
| `product-channel-data.routes.ts:452-458` | other FBA listing OR StockLevel code OR active FBA Offer — **`.catch(() => null)`** (C10) |
| `amazon-mapper.service.ts:107,134` | `Product.fulfillmentChannel`, defaulting **null → `'FBA'`** (C3) |
The two stock tests differ: `location.type === 'AMAZON_FBA'` in the derivation callers, `location.code === 'AMAZON-EU-FBA'` in the guards. A location with the right type and a different code is FBA to one and not the other.
**P1 (restated).** One `fulfilmentSignals(coordinate)` in `packages/shared/fulfilment-posture.ts`, returning the **signal set, never a scalar**; `effectiveLanes()` and `fbaEvidence()` derive from it. Implementer note: `packages/shared` resolves subpaths through `dist` (`package.json` `exports`), so this needs an explicit `exports` entry and a build — and that dist copy is why the P2 gate cannot say "once in the repo". Repoint all seven; reconcile the two stock tests explicitly. A gate asserts no file outside that module computes an FBA verdict.
# 4. The stranded-units warning, and how it reaches the operator first
`amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:764` calls `findFbaDeleteRows`; `:806-810` emits the sentence. `findFbaDeleteRows` is row-shaped — it filters `record_action === 'delete'` (`flat-file.service.ts:2618-2621`) — so a product-scoped helper must **drop** that filter, not wrap it, or it returns empty for every studio caller. It returns **SKUs, not units** (`:2645`); units are a separate read.
**P2 (corrected).** `FBA_STRANDED_NOTICE` becomes one exported constant in `packages/shared`. The gate asserts the literal occurs **exactly once under `apps/*/src` and `packages/*/src`** — not "in the repo", which would be vacuously red against `apps/api/dist` and `packages/shared/dist`. The literal contains a Unicode right single quote (`Amazon’s`); the gate must match bytes, not a re-typed ASCII approximation.
**Units come from `FbaInventoryDetail` (`schema.prisma:9522-9550`), not the stock roll-up.** `StockLevel` at `AMAZON-EU-FBA` is one EU aggregate by design (`fba-pan-eu.service.ts:3-9`), so a count from it is labelled `eu-pooled` and never rendered as "60 units in IT". Per C11, on a multi-account workspace every figure is additionally labelled `account-unattributed`. The pan-EU cron is opt-out and adapter-gated (`fba-pan-eu-sync.job.ts:39-47`), so `units` will often be null.
**Three escalating surfaces, no new persistent region:**
1. **On the lane slot** — a `Pill` with the figure, its as-of and its scope label, or the words *not measured*.
2. **In the preflight** — `FBA_STRANDED_NOTICE` into `ActionImpact.sideEffects` (`registry.ts:93`), figures itemised in `findings[]` (`:102`), named never counted.
3. **At the gate** — when `reversal.fidelity` is `'none'` or `'lossy'` AND (`units.sellable > 0` OR `units == null`), `level: 'type-to-confirm'` with `confirmPhrase = lane.sku` (`registry.ts:118`: "A SKU, never 'DELETE'"). **`units == null` blocks exactly as a positive count does.**
# 5. Verbs, fan-out, and the one word change
`withdraw-offer` on the FBM lane is a per-market Seller Central offer-close (`amazon-market-offer.service.ts:165-172`) and fans out to **one** market — `amazon-eu-quantity-guard.ts:18-20` says so in its own header. Any **quantity-based** hold on either lane fans out to **nine** (`:24-26`). So fan-out is computed per **(verb, lane, channel, market)**. A confirmation that cries pooled-fan-out on the one safe per-market verb trains operators to click through the one that is not.
`withdraw-offer` on the FBA lane stays **refused by default** (`isFbaRow`, `:59-63`, the Owner's fail-closed rule). `offerScope: 'FBM'` on a mixed coordinate is the Owner's call, not mine.
**The word change (C1/C2).** Keep the id `offer-toggle`. Label becomes "Pause selling on X" / "Resume selling on X". *Hold* is reserved for `syncPaused`; *Offer* for the lane. The write must include `channelActions.vitest.test.ts:131,136,141` and the three DS doc comments at `registry.ts:172`, `registry.d.ts:177`, `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:238`.
**The master sheet's existing cell (C8).** `field-registry.service.ts:108` is an editable two-value enum writing `Product.fulfillmentChannel` — a field no FBA guard reads and the mapper defaults to FBA. It cannot express `BOTH`, which `deriveFulfillmentMethod` returns. Making it read-only removes a capability, so it is the Owner's decision; the honest interim I do propose is to **re-label it "Fulfilment (product default)"** so it stops reading as the coordinate's answer.
# 6. UI, honouring D9 and CH.1
Presence rows get a `Fulfilment` column rendered by `design-system/grid/renderers/FulfilmentLaneCell.tsx`, a sibling of `ProjectionCell.tsx` and built to its rules verbatim: `params.value` is a **scalar**, every other fact read through a `facts` callback (`ProjectionCell.tsx:14-27`), so AG's fill handle cannot stamp one coordinate's offer SKU down a column. Lane verbs live in the presence row's own `⋯`.
**The sheet toolbar is untouched, so CH.1 stands as written and this proposal needs no amendment to it.** No new sheet column (C8). `ListingsPane` gains one `<dt>Fulfilment</dt>` beside the existing `<dt>Offer</dt>` (`ListingsPane.tsx:122-135`) and stays read-only — a written decision at `:5-9` that nothing here overturns.
# 7. What I could not establish
- **Whether any `Offer` row exists.** I issued no query. Given the three broken writers (P5), the honest prior is empty or stale — every lane renders `UNKNOWN` rather than falling through to FBM. P0 adds one thing the draft missed: `Offer.workspaceId` defaults from `current_setting('nexus.workspace_id')`, so a count taken in a session without that setting may return 0 for a non-empty table. Verify whether row-level security is enabled on `Offer` **before** treating a zero as empty; otherwise the count is a vacuous green.
- **Whether `FbaInventoryDetail` is populated.** Cron registered (`index.ts:283`), opt-out and adapter-gated.
- **Anything about pixels.** Browser gates drive a dev server wired to the production API and were not run.

#### Stop-Sale — recall, suspension and end-of-life for the Product Edit Studio (corrected)

One verb, `stop-sale`, that stops selling on every coordinate of a product while destroying nothing — a plural presence plan carrying a first-class cause.
