It closes four verified gaps: an open LotRecall has zero effect on published quantity or on any listing; the only cross-channel take-down (Product.status → INACTIVE) sends Amazon an empty patch and records SUCCESS; SP-API participation is measured, already on the wire, and dropped at the studio's parse boundary; and there is no end-of-life state. Adds a SaleStop cause record with a control snapshot, a recall quarantine term in the published-quantity function, participation on the market chips, and Product.lifecycleState on the existing default-on scheduler. Fifteen corrections to the original, including two false set claims and one false reversal promise.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- NO new DS component is required for this family — measured, not assumed. Banner, BulkActionBar, ActionConfirm, ScopeBarItem.disabled/disabledReason and ListboxOption.disabled/trailing/title were each opened and carry the props assumed.
- GridAction carries no `reach` and ActionImpact no `reversal{verb,fidelity}`. Stop-sale's reversal differs per coordinate (Amazon reopen lossy; eBay/Shopify exact ONLY with a control snapshot — see C3; FBA unreachable) and would otherwise be authored prose. This is the presence spine's DS addition; stop-sale consumes it and must not duplicate it.
- GridAction.danger is inert, measured: `/usr/bin/grep -c danger apps/web/src/design-system/grid/actions/menuAdapters.tsx` = 0 against a positive control of 5 for `label`. Stop-sale relies on ActionImpact.level, never on `danger`.
- ActionImpact has no slot for a per-target NAMED HANDOFF ('Nexus cannot stop this FBA offer; close it in Seller Central'). Today it would be squeezed into consequences[] or a findings[] warn, both of which read as informational. Proposed DS addition in registry.ts: `handoffs?: Array<{ target: string; instruction: string }>`, rendered by ActionConfirm as a distinct block above the confirm control.
- ActionImpact.findings[].severity is 'info'|'warn'|'error' (registry.ts:102). A recall preflight's open orders and FBA units are neither warnings nor errors — they are WORK. Either a fourth severity ('action-required') or the handoffs slot above; do not overload 'warn', which the confirmation renders as a reason to hesitate rather than a task list.
- NOT a DS gap, corrected: CH.1's text and the `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` prop live in a PAGE-LOCAL file (apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20, :102-104), not in design-system/. Any CH.1 amendment is a studio change plus a DS Menu change, not a DS change alone.

##### API
- P0 (precondition, no new route) — outbound-sync.service.ts:1063-1074: the empty-patch arm returns success:true, status:'SKIPPED' with NO dryRun field, and the status writer at :750-752 keys the green line on dryRun alone, so the row lands SUCCESS. Every other skip in syncToAmazon (syncPaused :904, offerClosedAt :909) returns success:false. Return success:false, error:'empty-patch', retryable:false, or add noop:true and honour it in BOTH writers (outbound-sync :750-752 and bullmq-sync.worker.ts). Until this lands nothing can distinguish 'confirmed stopped' from 'nothing was sent'. Ops decision — see openQuestions.
- P1 — extract one shared assertPushAllowed(listingId). Measured: syncPaused appears at outbound-sync.service.ts:904 (syncToAmazon, 879-1129) and :1164 (syncToEbay, 1130-1861), and ZERO times in syncToShopify (1862-2161) or syncToWoocommerce (2163-2418). The helper gains three refusals: an OPEN SaleStop covering the coordinate; a marketplace not isActive or with participationStatus SUSPENDED/ACCESS_DENIED/NOT_PARTICIPATING; and a terminal presenceIntent.
- P2 CORRECTED (C9) — channel-delist.service.ts:151-166 is NOT a fallback. The action==='unpublish' branch only LOGS, then falls through to deleteListingsItem unconditionally; there is no non-delete path. Every Amazon unpublish permanently deletes today. Wire patchListingsItem (it exists in the client, unwired) or refuse. Separately, remove the duck-typed Shopify updateProduct→deleteProduct fallthrough at :192-203 — updateProduct DOES exist at marketplaces/shopify.service.ts:383, so it is dormant, and a rename would silently convert an unpublish into a permanent delete.
- P3 — channel-delist.service.ts:227 (/item cannot be accessed/) and :231 (/invalid item/) must leave ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS. An ownership refusal must be UNKNOWN, not the {success:true} returned at :290-293.
- P4 NEW (C13) — applyDelistResultToQueue (channel-delist.service.ts:313-343) parks a retryable failure as PENDING with NO holdUntil; the 60s cron then re-routes it through dispatchSync (outbound-sync.service.ts:611-623), which has no lifecycle branch, into the ordinary update path. Set holdUntil on the park AND make dispatchSync refuse lifecycle syncTypes outright.
- P5 NEW (C14) — one shared isFbaCoordinate(). Two different fail-closed FBA predicates guard one verb today: amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63 (two clauses) and sync-control.routes.ts:905-909 (three, including fulfillmentMethod == null && product FBA).
- P6 — the worker must read targetChannel from the queue row it just loaded, not from job.data (bullmq-sync.worker.ts:88).
- P7 CORRECTED (C10) — every sale-stop write names all five coordinate levels INCLUDING the pre-read. amazon-market-offer.service.ts:66-68 and sync-control.routes.ts:895-898 load on the blind (productId, channel, marketplace) triple; marketplaces.routes.ts:318 (not :317) hardcodes aliasKey:''. Narrowing a write without narrowing its pre-read is strictly worse than today.
- NEW — POST /api/products/:id/sale-stop/plan { causeKind, causeRef?, reason, scope: 'all' | targets[] }. Server-computed, no writes. Returns an ActionImpact-shaped plan: every five-level coordinate with its primitive, reach, reversal{verb,fidelity} (per-coordinate, weakest reported for the set, and 'lossy' for any pinned row until the control snapshot lands — see C3), expectedVersion, the Amazon EU shared-set fan-out named coordinate by coordinate (never a count), the FBA handoffs, the operational preflight reusing the shape at products-catalog.routes.ts:1666-1760 SHOWN not blocking, a planId, and a short-TTL preflightToken binding the enumerated set.
- NEW — POST /api/products/:id/sale-stop/apply { planId, preflightToken }. THE ONLY WRITER. Refuses a missing/expired/set-mismatched token; 409s per target on version drift. Per coordinate, in this order: (1) capture controlSnapshot, (2) cancel racing PENDING queue rows (copying amazon-market-offer.service.ts:196-199), (3) send the stop, (4) arm the hold ONLY on a confirmed non-empty SUCCESS. Order is load-bearing: arming syncPaused first makes the guard at :904 skip the very push that stops the sale, which is why ZERO_PIN sets syncPaused:false at sync-control.routes.ts:942. Results in the shipped MarketOfferResult shape (amazon-market-offer.service.ts:46-55) with NO aggregate success.
- NEW — POST /api/products/:id/sale-stop/:saleStopId/lift { reason }. Refuses while any OTHER OPEN SaleStop covers the coordinate. Resume is a separate approved plan, never an automatic consequence of lifting.
- NEW — GET /api/products/:id/sale-stops and GET /api/products/:id/sale-stops/:id/file (the traceability file, frozen at approval and never recomputed: coordinates with external ids, lots and unitsRemaining, serials via serial.service.ts:221's lotRecalled, open orders, master AND component bundles, FBA units, and each coordinate's outcome with observedAt/observedVia).
- CHANGED — GET /api/marketplaces/grouped (marketplaces.routes.ts:245-250) gains ?includeInactive=1. VERIFIED: it is a bare findMany with NO select and spreads {...mp}, so isParticipating / participationStatus / participationCheckedAt ALREADY reach the browser; only `where: { isActive: true }` needs the flag. The studio drops them at its own boundary (_studio/types.ts:78-89, eight fields, none of them participation), so widening MarketplaceLite is a WEB-side change, not an API widening. Also correct deriveScopeOptions, which drops connected===false at _studio/scopes.ts:119,127 (not :108-125).
- CHANGED — available-to-publish.service.ts: add `quarantined: number` to AvailableToPublishInput (:19-34) as a REQUIRED field, subtracted at :151 alongside pendingReserved (the function is at :140, not :146). CORRECTED SET CLAIM (C1): 15 sites, not 14 — sync-drift-detection.job.ts:107, products.routes.ts:603, ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1639 and :1667, control-tower.routes.ts:259, product-channel-data.routes.ts:306, shopify/offer-sync.service.ts:44, ebay-shared-fanout.service.ts:179, follow-master.service.ts:221 and :357, outbound-sync.service.ts:969, :1180 and :2077, fulfillment-pool-drift.service.ts:109, PLUS the in-file call at available-to-publish.service.ts:78 inside planFollowingQtyClamp which the original list omitted. ebay-inventory-readback.service.ts is NOT a call site: it does carry NUL bytes (579) and plain grep does skip it, but its single occurrence is an unused import at :24 — delete it. Quarantine applies to FBM_WAREHOUSE only; lot-precise FBA linkage does not exist (returns-defect-recall.service.ts:11-14).
- CHANGED — scheduled-changes.job.ts: VALID_KINDS at :30 gains 'LIFECYCLE'. The cron is default-on (:21) and registered at apps/api/src/index.ts:279 — no new cron. A LIFECYCLE row calls sale-stop/plan+apply and NEVER masterStatusService, which is both the empty-patch path of P0 AND, per correction C11, a cascade that silently skips every ENDED and ERROR listing (TERMINAL_LISTING_STATUSES, master-status.service.ts:20-26 applied at :164-166). A STOCK_ZERO trigger not yet met reschedules +1h and, past a hard expiresAt, records FAILED with the reason rather than looping.
- AUTHORISATION — every new route carries an explicit in-file preHandler naming its permission. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (apps/api/src/lib/auth/rbac-hook.ts:8-13, mode() at :30-32: resolve + LOG what would be denied, but allow the request), so an in-file guard is the only one that binds today.

##### Schema
- ADDITIVE — new model `SaleStop`. workspaceId String @default(dbgenerated("NULLIF(current_setting('nexus.workspace_id', true), '')")); id; productId String (plain string, NO Prisma relation — the AuditLog property at products-catalog.routes.ts:1902-1908 that lets a record survive a product purge); causeKind String; causeRef String?; reason String (REQUIRED); enumeratedTargets Json; controlSnapshot Json (NEW, per correction C3 — {followMasterQuantity, quantityOverride, quantity, syncPaused, offerActive} per coordinate, captured BEFORE the stop; without it 'resume, exact' is a false promise for any pinned row); status String @default("OPEN"); openedAt/openedBy/liftedAt/liftedBy/notes.
- CORRECTED (C4) — SaleStop idempotency. The proposal's `@@unique(... WHERE status='OPEN')` does not work as written: causeRef is nullable and Postgres treats NULLs as distinct, so causeKind='OPERATOR' with causeRef=null admits unlimited OPEN duplicates. Required raw SQL in the migration: CREATE UNIQUE INDEX "SaleStop_one_open_per_cause" ON "SaleStop"("workspaceId","productId","causeKind", COALESCE("causeRef",'')) WHERE status='OPEN'. Also corrected: LotRecall's equivalent is NOT at schema.prisma:9452-9455 (that is the model's comment block) — it is raw SQL at packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260509_l4_recall/migration.sql:40, because Prisma cannot declare a partial unique at all.
- ADDITIVE — ChannelListing.saleStopId String?. Which SaleStop holds this coordinate down. Nullable, never backfilled; null means no stop. Read by assertPushAllowed alongside syncPaused/offerClosedAt/presenceIntent.
- ADDITIVE — Product.lifecycleState String? (null | 'END_OF_LIFE' | 'DISCONTINUED'). Deliberately NOT a value added to Product.status. CORRECTED SET CLAIM (C2): that free-text column's three values are restated in AT LEAST 24 independent hardcoded lists, not thirteen — a floor derived from one pattern family with a positive control, not a census. Confirmed sites include scheduled-changes.job.ts:61,66; products.routes.ts:1051; flat-file-unified.routes.ts:42; products-catalog.routes.ts:922,2076; products/bulk-upload.service.ts:91; products/bulk-edit.service.ts:365; flat-file/registry/master-fields.ts:257 with enumMode:'strict' at :258 which REJECTS an unknown value; bulk-action.service.ts:1031,2831; pim/field-registry.service.ts:80; and on the web side products/next/BulkEditModal.tsx:24, products/next/useBulkActions.ts:92, _components/GridView.tsx:323, _components/BulkActionBar.tsx:248, _modals/ScheduleChangeModal.tsx:61,210, bulk-operations/UnifiedFilterExtras.tsx:53, bulk-operations/_operations/configs.tsx:154, bulk-operations/components/FilterDropdown.tsx:24, catalog/[id]/edit/schema.ts:153. TWO entries in the original list are the WRONG FIELD: flat-file/registry/channel-fields.ts:326 and products/ebay-flat-file/ebay-columns.ts:530 enumerate ['DRAFT','ACTIVE','INACTIVE','ENDED','ERROR'] — that is ChannelListing.listingStatus. Repo-wide 'DISCONTINUED' occurs once, as a comment at channel-delist.service.ts:152.
- ADDITIVE — Product.endOfLifeAt DateTime? / Product.endOfLifeReason String? / Product.doNotRecreate Boolean @default(false). See openQuestions: this collides with the presence spine's ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate and only one grain may be authoritative.
- BREAKING-ADJACENT, NEWLY IDENTIFIED (C8) — enum SyncChannel has no ETSY member (schema.prisma:57-66) and OutboundSyncQueue.targetChannel is SyncChannel @default(AMAZON) (schema.prisma:6834), while ChannelListing.channel is a plain String whose comment lists ETSY (:1944). An Etsy coordinate can therefore exist locally and can NEVER be enqueued. Any Etsy lifecycle work needs ALTER TYPE "SyncChannel" ADD VALUE 'ETSY' first — additive in Postgres, but not rollback-able and not transaction-safe on older versions. Filed as its own migration and its own open question, NOT bundled with the additive columns.
- NO CHANGE — LotRecall. openRecall/closeRecall/listRecalls/releaseReservationsForRecall consumed unchanged. Correction C12: releaseReservationsForRecall (lot.service.ts:399-410) releases EVERY open reservation for recall.lot.productId, not only the recalled lot's — any surface offering it must say so.
- NOT PROPOSED — a new lifecycle-event table. ProductEvent exists, is FK-free by design (schema.prisma:15475-15478: 'plain string FKs (no Prisma relation) so we never block deletes on the parent'), already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing' (:15479), and is already emitted and read. Stop-sale emits there with the full coordinate and the SaleStop id in `data`.
- NOT PROPOSED — ScheduledProductChange schema change. `kind` is a free String whose own comment at schema.prisma:14496 says "'STATUS' | 'PRICE' (extend without migration)"; 'LIFECYCLE' is a code change to VALID_KINDS at scheduled-changes.job.ts:30.
- SEQUENCING (binding, two rules, one of them newly cited per C5). (1) PES.0 hub ruling #12, docs/pes-claims.md:112-128: an UNAPPLIED migration folder stays OUT of packages/database/prisma/migrations/ — author it in lane space and move it in at apply time; once applied it stays permanently, because removing an applied folder manufactures applied-but-missing-locally drift for the whole programme. (2) Apply every additive migration to BOTH databases and verify before declaring the columns in schema.prisma — MEMORY records apps/api/.env pointing at a local Docker pg on 09-11 where only Product.version (54 vs 51) separated the two. Discriminate, never infer.

##### Open for you
- AUTOMATIC OR PROPOSED. Does opening a LotRecall raise a stop-sale PROPOSAL (my recommendation — openRecall is reached from an inventory route at stock.routes.ts:2494-2513 and an inventory action that silently reaches four marketplaces is the endpoint-safety-is-not-interaction-safety failure), or does it fire automatically with the existing 5-minute holdUntil as the undo window? A recall is the one case where hours matter.
- P0's BLAST RADIUS. Flipping the empty-patch arm to a visible non-success makes every no-op STATUS_UPDATE currently landing green go red. Does the Owner want that measured first (a 30-day count) and shipped as an announced ops change, or shipped immediately on the grounds that a green line that means nothing is worse than a red one?
- THE ZERO_PIN CONTROL SNAPSHOT (C3). Restoring a pinned quantity after a stop-sale requires capturing {followMasterQuantity, quantityOverride, quantity} before the write. Does the Owner want resume-sale to RESTORE the prior pin, or to leave the coordinate Following the pool and make the operator re-pin deliberately? Restoring is friendlier; re-pinning is the honest default when the pool has moved during a recall. I have specified restore-with-a-shown-diff but this is the Owner's call.
- ETSY (C8). SyncChannel has no ETSY member, so an Etsy coordinate can exist in ChannelListing and can never be enqueued. Does the Owner want the enum value added now (a small but non-trivial migration, see risks) so Etsy joins the presence model in wave 2, or does Etsy stay read-only indefinitely and the UI say so plainly?
- THE FBA HANDOFF vs offerScope. The presence spine proposes offerScope:'FBM' to make 'withdraw the merchant offer, keep FBA' expressible. This proposal instead emits a named Seller Central handoff, on the Owner's stated fail-closed rule. These are different answers to one question and only one should ship. Which?
- CROSS-PRODUCT RECALL. A recall arrives on a LOT, and one lot maps to one product — but a supplier notice or a compliance withdrawal arrives on a supplier or a category. The sale-stop API is product-scoped. Does the Owner want a cross-product stop-sale filed as a separate programme on /products (D9 says it does not belong in the studio), or is product-scoped enough for now with the multi-product case handled by repeating the verb?
- DISCONTINUED's TWO FLAGS. Product.doNotRecreate here and ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate in the presence spine. Which grain is authoritative? If both, who owns the single helper every create path calls?
- LIFTING A STOP. I have specified that lifting a SaleStop NEVER auto-resumes selling — resume is a separate approved plan. That is deliberately more friction than an operator will want after a false-alarm recall. Does the Owner want a one-click 'lift and resume' for a stop whose cause was closed within some window, or does the two-step stand?
- RBAC ENFORCE. Every guard this adds is a log line while NEXUS_RBAC_MODE defaults to shadow. Does the Owner want stop-sale gated on flipping it to enforce (blast radius across every route), or shipped behind in-file preHandlers that bind regardless?
- WOOCOMMERCE CONTAINMENT — confirm the scope. Out of scope as a channel (Owner, 2026-09-12). The containment I propose is narrow: syncToWoocommerce gains assertPushAllowed (it has zero syncPaused checks today, so a hold an operator believes is channel-wide does not gate it at all), and dispatchSync refuses lifecycle syncTypes rather than routing them into the Woo update path. Woo presence renders read-only. Confirming this is containment and not channel work.
- CH.1 — noted, not contested. This family needs no amendment: the verb lives on the presence surface's row menu and its own BulkActionBar, and the sheet toolbar is untouched. Recording only that CH.1's text and the `overflow` prop are page-local (SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20, :104), not DS, so any future amendment is a studio change first.

##### Risks
- P0 is an OPS EVENT, not a code change. Every no-op STATUS_UPDATE that lands SUCCESS today (outbound-sync.service.ts:1063-1074 → :750-752) becomes a visible non-success the moment it ships. Nobody has measured how many rows that is. Measure the 30-day count of AMAZON STATUS_UPDATE rows with syncStatus SUCCESS before flipping it, or the first symptom of an honesty fix is a dashboard nobody can read.
- C3's control snapshot is a NEW dependency on a shape nothing reads today. `offerCloseSnapshot.control` has been written since SCT.6 and read by nothing — so every row closed before resume-sale ships has an UNVERIFIED snapshot. Treat pre-existing snapshots as unproven: a first resume against one must be exercised against a captured request and read from the BODY, not the status.
- Making `quarantined` a required field turns 15 sites into compile errors ACROSS four route files, three services, two jobs and the follow-master engine — including three sites inside outbound-sync.service.ts, the hottest push path in the repo. Each site must decide what its quarantine query is; a lane that passes 0 to make it compile has silently opted that path out, which is exactly the silent subset the required field was meant to prevent. The gate must assert no literal `quarantined: 0` outside tests.
- Etsy (C8) needs an enum value added to SyncChannel before any Etsy lifecycle work. `ALTER TYPE ... ADD VALUE` is additive in Postgres but cannot run inside a transaction block in older versions and cannot be rolled back — and Prisma's shadow-database check will want the value present in both databases before the client regenerates. This is the one migration in this proposal that is not trivially safe.
- The FBA handoff depends on a fail-closed predicate that today has TWO inconsistent implementations (C14). Until they are unified, a coordinate can be 'FBA, refuse' on one path and 'FBM, push' on the other — and the push path is the dangerous one, because a merchant QUANTITY_UPDATE that succeeds for an FBA SKU flips the offer to FBM (fba-flip-guard.job.ts:32-41).
- The studio test tree is not green: vitest-studio.txt records 2 files and 6 tests failing of 1574. Landing wave 1 on top of a red suite means the first regression this work causes will be indistinguishable from the ones already there.
- Product.doNotRecreate (this proposal) and ListingIdentity.doNotRecreate (the presence spine) are two flags for one rule at two grains. If both land, a create path must consult both, and the first one a lane forgets is the one that lets a discontinued listing come back. They must be reconciled before either ships — most likely as product-level intent plus coordinate-level tombstone, with ONE helper that reads both.
- Every permission guard here is a log line while rbacHook defaults to shadow (rbac-hook.ts:30-32). An in-file preHandler is the only thing that binds today, and adding one to a new route while the existing hard-delete routes stay open secures the wrong half.
- The recall quarantine changes published quantity with no operator action. On a product with an open recall on one lot of five, marketplace quantity drops the moment the term lands — correct, and also a silent quantity push to four channels from a migration. It must ship behind the same 5-minute holdUntil grace as any other quantity change, and the first run should be dry-run reported to the Owner.

##### The specification
# Stop-Sale — recall, suspension and end-of-life (corrected)
## CORRECTIONS to the proposal under review
**C1 — the `quarantined` set claim is wrong in both directions.** The proposal says plain `grep` misses `ebay-inventory-readback.service.ts` because of a NUL byte, implying a missed consumer. Measured: the file does carry NULs (`/usr/bin/grep -c $'\x00'` = 579; recursive grep reports "Binary file … matches"), but its one occurrence is an **unused import** at `ebay-inventory-readback.service.ts:24` — zero calls. It will not become a compile error. Meanwhile the list of 14 **omits a real site**: `available-to-publish.service.ts:78`, the in-file call inside `planFollowingQtyClamp`. Corrected: **14 external + 1 in-file = 15**, plus one dead import to delete. Positive control: `computeAvailableToPublish(` = 31 hits including tests.
**C2 — "thirteen hardcoded copies of `Product.status`" is an undercount, three paths are wrong, and two entries are the wrong field.** One pattern family yields ≥24 non-fixture sites, adding `products.routes.ts:1051`, `products/bulk-upload.service.ts:91`, `products/bulk-edit.service.ts:365`, `products/next/BulkEditModal.tsx:24`, `products/next/useBulkActions.ts:92`, `_components/GridView.tsx:323`, `_components/BulkActionBar.tsx:248`, `_modals/ScheduleChangeModal.tsx:61,210`. Wrong paths: `ebay-columns.ts` is `apps/web/src/app/products/ebay-flat-file/ebay-columns.ts`; `UnifiedFilterExtras.tsx` / `configs.tsx` / `FilterDropdown.tsx` are under `apps/web/src/app/bulk-operations/`; `schema.ts` is `apps/web/src/app/catalog/[id]/edit/schema.ts`. And `channel-fields.ts:326` and `ebay-columns.ts:530` enumerate `['DRAFT','ACTIVE','INACTIVE','ENDED','ERROR']` — that is `ChannelListing.listingStatus`, **not** `Product.status`. The conclusion (never add a value to `Product.status`) survives and is stronger; the number is restated as a derived floor, not a census. `master-fields.ts:258` `enumMode:'strict'` confirmed.
**C3 — the proposal ships a false reversal promise, which is the exact class of lie this programme exists to remove.** It calls the Amazon-EU and eBay `ZERO_PIN` reversal "`FOLLOW`, exact". Measured: `sync-control.routes.ts:940-943` writes `{quantity:0, quantityOverride:0, followMasterQuantity:false, syncPaused:false}` with **no snapshot of the prior control state**, and `FOLLOW` writes `{followMasterQuantity:true, quantityOverride:null, quantity:poolAvailable}` (`follow-master.service.ts:17,62`). For a row that was **pinned** at a number, ZERO_PIN destroys the pin and FOLLOW cannot restore it — the row silently rejoins the pool at a different quantity. Corrected: fidelity is `exact` only for rows already Following, `lossy` otherwise — **unless** the write first snapshots the control state. I have therefore added `SaleStop.controlSnapshot Json` as a **required** field and made capturing it a wave-2 precondition. `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:184-191` already writes exactly `control:{followMasterQuantity, quantityOverride, syncPaused}` into `offerCloseSnapshot`, and — measured — reads it nowhere (`snap` at :247-250 destructures only `purchasableOffer` and `productType`). Reuse that shape; make `resume-sale` its first reader.
**C4 — the `SaleStop` idempotency index as written does not work.** `causeRef` is nullable and Postgres treats NULLs as distinct in a unique index, so `causeKind:'OPERATOR', causeRef:null` — the commonest case — admits unlimited OPEN duplicates. Corrected to `COALESCE("causeRef",'')` in the index expression. Also: LotRecall's partial unique is **not** in `schema.prisma`; it is raw SQL at `packages/database/prisma/migrations/20260509_l4_recall/migration.sql:40` (`LotRecall_one_open_per_lot`). The cited `schema.prisma:9452-9455` is the model's comment block. Prisma cannot declare a partial unique at all — it must be authored as raw SQL in the migration.
**C5 — the migration section violates a standing hub ruling it does not cite.** PES.0 ruling #12 (`docs/pes-claims.md:112-128`) binds all lanes: an **unapplied** migration folder stays OUT of `packages/database/prisma/migrations/` and moves in at apply time; once applied it stays permanently. Added to sequencing.
**C6 — "Errors & Sync" is not a tab.** The tab id is `errors`, labelled **"Needs attention"** (`_studio/navigation.tsx:15`). Renamed throughout.
**C7 — `BulkActionBar` is not already the studio's channel selection bar.** `FamilySelectionBar.tsx:23` mounts it for the **master** scope only, and a CONFIRMED audit finding says exactly this: "the channel sheet collects a multi-row selection and counts it twice, but mounts no selection bar — the DS BulkActionBar is master-only". The presence surface reuses the DS component but the mount is new work, not inherited.
**C8 — Etsy is untypeable in the queue, not merely unrouted.** `OutboundSyncQueue.targetChannel` is `SyncChannel @default(AMAZON)` (`schema.prisma:6834`) and `enum SyncChannel` (`schema.prisma:57-66`) is AMAZON|EBAY|SHOPIFY|WOOCOMMERCE|GOOGLE|META|TIKTOK — **no ETSY**. `ChannelListing.channel` is a plain `String` whose comment lists ETSY (`:1944`), so an Etsy coordinate exists locally and can never be enqueued. "Etsy read-only" is a schema fact, not a policy the Owner can reverse by wiring a switch case; it needs `ALTER TYPE "SyncChannel" ADD VALUE 'ETSY'` first. New open question.
**C9 — the Amazon "UNPUBLISH → DELETE fallback" is not a fallback.** `channel-delist.service.ts:151-166`: the `action === 'unpublish'` branch only *logs*, then falls through to `deleteListingsItem` **unconditionally**. There is no non-delete path. So this is not a dormant risk a rename could trip — every Amazon unpublish already permanently deletes today. P2's Amazon half moves to the top of wave 1 as a live mislabelling, not a hardening.
**C10 — line citations corrected.** Preflight predicate `products-catalog.routes.ts:1680-1683` (not 1679-1682); `deriveScopeOptions`'s `connected === false` drop at `_studio/scopes.ts:119,127` (not 108-125); hardcoded `aliasKey:''` at `marketplaces.routes.ts:318` (not 317); the subtraction inside `computeAvailableToPublish` at `:151`, function at `:140` (not 146); CH.1's text at `apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio/sheet/SheetToolbar.tsx:15-20` and `overflow?: readonly MenuItemDef[]` at `:104` — a **page-local** file, not a DS one, which matters for who may amend it; empty-patch arm at `:1063-1074`; scheduler default-on comment at `scheduled-changes.job.ts:21`.
**C11 — `masterStatusService` also silently skips the rows a recall most cares about.** `TERMINAL_LISTING_STATUSES` (`master-status.service.ts:20-26`, applied at `:164-166`) skips every ENDED and ERROR listing. The proposal's P0 says the push is empty; it did not say the cascade also under-covers. The ban on routing lifecycle through it is extended from `LIFECYCLE` rows to every path.
**C12 — `releaseReservationsForRecall` is product-wide, not recall-scoped.** `lot.service.ts:399-410` selects **every** open reservation for `recall.lot.productId`. Any surface offering it must say so.
**C13 — new P4.** `applyDelistResultToQueue` (`channel-delist.service.ts:313-343`) parks a retryable failure `PENDING` with no `holdUntil`; the cron re-routes it through `dispatchSync`, which has no lifecycle branch (`:611-623`).
**C14 — two different FBA predicates guard one verb.** `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:59-63` and `sync-control.routes.ts:905-909` disagree in shape. One shared `isFbaCoordinate()` is a precondition.
**C15 — the brief's Phase-0 ground truth does not exist.** `ls .../scratchpad/ground-*.json` returns no match. I read what is there: `vitest-studio.txt` records **2 files failed, 6 tests of 1574 failed** — so the tree is not green, and wave 1 must include those. I did not assert a green gate I could not see.
Everything else in the proposal I re-derived and it holds: the empty-patch arm (`outbound-sync.service.ts:1063-1074`) returns `success:true, status:'SKIPPED'` with no `dryRun`, and the writer at `:750-752` keys green on `dryRun` alone; `syncPaused` appears 0 times in `syncToShopify` (1862-2161) and `syncToWoocommerce` (2163-2418); `isParticipating = status==='PARTICIPATING' || status==='SUSPENDED'` (`amazon-participations.service.ts:94`); `/marketplaces/grouped` is a bare `findMany` with no `select` (`marketplaces.routes.ts:247-250`) and `MarketplaceLite` declares eight fields, none of them participation (`_studio/types.ts:78-89`); `pickLotsForConsume` excludes OPEN-recall lots (`lot.service.ts:266-268`) while `consumeReservation` passes `allowShortfall:true` (`stock-level.service.ts:250-262`); `deleteProduct` and `updateProduct` both exist (`shopify.service.ts:383,407`); `ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS` folds `/item cannot be accessed/` (`:227`) and `/invalid item/` (`:231`); `AMAZON_EU_SHARED_MARKETS` is nine (`amazon-eu-quantity-guard.ts:24-26`) and its own header says per-market suppression is not a quantity operation (`:18-20`); `OutOfStockControl` appears only in the test asserting it is not sent; `ScheduledProductChange.kind` is free String (`schema.prisma:14496`) and `VALID_KINDS` is a code constant (`scheduled-changes.job.ts:30`); hub ruling #4 confirms it is `ScheduledImagePublish`, not this cron, that is off. Every named DS component exists at its path with the props assumed: `Banner` (`tone='danger'` → `role="alert"`, `action` slot), `ScopeBarItem.disabled`/`disabledReason`, `ListboxOption.disabled`/`trailing`/`title`, `Pill`, `BulkActionBar`, `ActionConfirm`, `runAction`, `registry`, `readiness`, `DetailHeader`, `Button`. `GridAction.danger` is inert: 0 hits in `menuAdapters.tsx` against a control of 5 for `label`.
## 1. Preconditions, in order
**P0 — the empty patch.** `outbound-sync.service.ts:1063-1074` must return `success:false, error:'empty-patch', retryable:false`, or `noop:true` honoured by both writers. Until it does, no stop-sale can distinguish "confirmed stopped" from "nothing was sent". Blast radius is an ops decision (Q7): every no-op STATUS_UPDATE currently landing green becomes visibly non-green.
**P1 — one dispatch guard.** Extract `assertPushAllowed(listingId)` and call it from all four adapters, not two. It gains three refusals: an OPEN `SaleStop` covering the coordinate; a marketplace that is not `isActive` or whose `participationStatus` is SUSPENDED / ACCESS_DENIED / NOT_PARTICIPATING; and `presenceIntent` in a terminal set (the presence spine's layering).
**P2 — no adapter may escalate.** Amazon: the unpublish branch deletes unconditionally (C9) — wire `patchListingsItem`, or refuse. Shopify: remove the duck-typed `updateProduct → deleteProduct` fallthrough (`:192-203`).
**P3 — split refusal from absence.** `/item cannot be accessed/` and `/invalid item/` become UNKNOWN, not the `{success:true}` returned at `:290-293`.
**P4 — a parked failure must not be re-routed.** Set `holdUntil` on a retryable park, and make `dispatchSync` refuse lifecycle syncTypes outright.
**P5 — one FBA predicate** (C14). **P6 —** the worker must read `targetChannel` from the queue row, not `job.data` (`bullmq-sync.worker.ts:88`). **P7 —** every write names all five coordinate levels **including the pre-read**; `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:66-68`, `sync-control.routes.ts:895-898` and `marketplaces.routes.ts:318` load on a blind triple today, and narrowing a write without its pre-read is strictly worse than now.
## 2. Stop-sale is a cause plus a plan
`SaleStop` (shaped like `LotRecall`: idempotent, one OPEN per cause, never deleted) carries `productId`, `causeKind ∈ {LOT_RECALL, COMPLIANCE, SUPPLIER, MARKET_SUSPENSION, OPERATOR}`, `causeRef?`, `reason` (required, rendered verbatim), `openedAt/By`, `liftedAt/By`, `status`, `enumeratedTargets Json` and — new, per C3 — `controlSnapshot Json` capturing `{followMasterQuantity, quantityOverride, quantity, syncPaused, offerActive}` per coordinate before the stop.
Two rules make it load-bearing: **resume refuses while any other OPEN SaleStop covers the coordinate** (a recall and an expired CE certificate may stand together), and **the reason is required, verbatim, everywhere** — a recall's paperwork is the deliverable. `reach:'channel'`; `reversal:{verb:'resume-sale', fidelity: weakest across the plan}`, never an average.
## 3. Per channel, and the order that matters
Each coordinate is two phases: **send the stop; on a confirmed non-empty SUCCESS, arm the hold.** Arming first makes the guard at `:904` skip the very push that stops the sale — which is why ZERO_PIN sets `syncPaused:false` (`sync-control.routes.ts:942`). Anything else leaves the coordinate UNKNOWN and the roll-up says so.
Amazon FBM all-EU: zero the shared pool once (correct and fastest here; `projectActionAndDetect` returns no conflict when all targets agree) — name all nine markets, never cry wolf. Amazon FBM one market: `closeMarketOffers`, ASIN and reviews kept; reversal **lossy** (reopen replays the snapshot verbatim). Amazon FBA: **not reachable** — `isFbaRow` fails closed and a merchant QUANTITY_UPDATE that succeeds flips the offer to FBM (`fba-flip-guard.job.ts:32-41`). The honest output is a named handoff carrying the seller SKU and marketplace, not a greyed control. eBay: quantity 0, ItemID kept; whether the ItemID survives is per-account Seller Hub configuration (`OutOfStockControl` is not sent), so the coordinate stays **pending** until `verify` answers. Shopify: `status:'draft'`. Etsy: unreachable by schema (C8).
Every target cancels racing PENDING queue rows in the same transaction, copying `amazon-market-offer.service.ts:196-199`.
## 4. Recall propagation
**Level 1 — quarantine, automatic, quantitative.** Add `quarantined: number` to `AvailableToPublishInput` (`:19-34`), **required** so the 15 sites become compile errors, subtracted at `:151`. Source: `SUM(unitsRemaining)` over lots of this product/variation with an OPEN recall. A partial recall then reduces marketplace quantity with no operator action — the everyday case a full stop-sale over-answers. **FBM warehouse pool only**: lot-precise FBA linkage does not exist (`returns-defect-recall.service.ts:11-14`), and a proportional FBA deduction would be a fabricated number. The FBA pool renders unquarantined with its reason and a removal-order handoff.
**Level 2 — stop-sale, proposed, never automatic.** `openRecall` is reached from a stock route (`stock.routes.ts:2494-2513`); an inventory action that silently reaches four marketplaces is the endpoint-safety-is-not-interaction-safety failure. Proposal with one click; automatic-with-grace is Q1.
**The preflight shows, it does not block.** `GET /products/hard-delete-preflight` (`:1666-1760`) enumerates open orders, master and component bundles, and FBA units. For a delete these are blockers; for a recall they are **the work**. They go into the traceability file. Its own `liveListings` predicate (`:1680-1683`) uses `listingStatus IN (ACTIVE,INACTIVE) AND externalListingId NOT NULL` — an AND that excludes the DRAFT/ENDED rows holding real ItemIDs that `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:15-31` pins as live. Stop-sale uses `identityHeld` (`listingRisk.ts:47-49`, unchanged), not this.
## 5. A suspended or exited market
One rule: **a market a product still has listings on must never disappear.** Today `deriveScopeOptions` drops `connected === false` (`scopes.ts:119,127`), the route drops `isActive:false` before that, and the studio says "This market is unavailable. Choose an available market." (`contracts.tsx:677`) — a sentence about the chip that reads as a sentence about the listing, while the sync worker keeps pushing.
**No DS addition needed.** Project `isParticipating`, `participationStatus`, `participationCheckedAt` onto `MarketplaceLite` (they already reach the browser), keep the market tagged rather than filtered, render the chip `disabled` with `disabledReason` = state plus as-of, and the option with `trailing:'Suspended'`. Do **not** add a state to `ScopeReadinessState` — that vocabulary measures pre-publish completeness and is shared far beyond the studio. Render `participationCheckedAt` explicitly: nothing refreshes it on a schedule (two route callers only), so an unrefreshed participation is UNKNOWN, never green. Participation is Amazon-only; elsewhere it reads "not measured". A suspension is **not** auto-stop-sold — it offers a proposal with `causeKind: MARKET_SUSPENSION`.
## 6. Discontinued and sell-through
Add `Product.lifecycleState` (`null | END_OF_LIFE | DISCONTINUED`), `endOfLifeAt`, `endOfLifeReason`, `doNotRecreate`. Never a value in `Product.status` (C2). END_OF_LIFE sells through, then each coordinate ends. DISCONTINUED sets `doNotRecreate`, which every create/publish path consults — the disease behind "a removed Amazon listing comes straight back in the flat file". Executor: extend `VALID_KINDS` with `'LIFECYCLE'` on the existing default-on cron; a `STOCK_ZERO` trigger not yet met reschedules +1h and, past `expiresAt`, records FAILED. A LIFECYCLE row calls plan/apply and **never** `masterStatusService` (C11). At zero stock on eBay the coordinate enqueues `verify` and renders pending.
## 7. Surfaces
`StudioFrame.tsx:11-17` forbids a band above the grid, and `GridSheet` measures the document. So: a `Pill tone="danger"` in `StudioHeader`'s existing meta row (`:57-83`) reading `Sale stopped`, zero height cost; the full `Banner tone="danger"` on the presence surface and on **Needs attention** (both sheet-free tab bodies); one line in the sheet's footer strip. The verb lives on the presence surface's row menu and its own mounted `BulkActionBar` (C7), confirmed by `ActionConfirm`. **The sheet toolbar is untouched, so CH.1 needs no amendment for this family.** No new page: `/fulfillment/stock/recalls` gains the proposal and a link in — D9 holds, because presence is state, the sheet is content, and the recall list is inventory.
## 8. What I could not establish
Etsy's lifecycle reach (and now, per C8, its schema reach). Whether `restoreToDraft` works — zero web callers, `SNAPSHOT_FIELDS` excludes `externalListingId`; no relist promise may cite it until it is exercised against a captured request, reading the body. The Amazon reopen's price: `reopenMarketOffers` replays the snapshot verbatim with no margin, floor or promo check, and the `control` block it stores is read nowhere — any stop-sale that closes an Amazon offer inherits that, and must show the price and re-run the price guard. Every pixel claim: the four browser gates drive `localhost:3000`, which the brief forbids, so the banner and pill placements are reasoned from source, not measured.

Repairs

#### One operational-impact service: coordinate-scoped, honest about what it could not check, wired into every studio lifecycle verb

Extract the five queries inside `GET /products/hard-delete-preflight` into one `operational-impact.service.ts`, add stock holds, ads and a three-source FBA posture, and give it a coordinate-shaped second consumer (`POST /api/products/operational-impact`) that `delete-variant`, `offer-toggle` and `presence/plan` all call. The reuse argument survives review. Five things in the original do not: the PD-2 filter correction is described as harmless when it is a producer/consumer change that must land on both sites and only after the adapter preconditions; the FBA check invents a third definition of "is this FBA" and prints an unpopulated table as zero; `spendCents` is not daily spend; adding two checks to an all-or-nothing GET raises the rate at which a permanent delete runs with no preflight at all, because the caller does not block on a preflight error; and `unavailable` refuses the whole verb, so an advisory check must never set it.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- ActionImpact.findings[].severity is 'info' | 'warn' | 'error' (registry.ts:102) with no way to say a check could not run. ADD 'unknown' to the union plus a .nds-grid-confirm-unknown rule beside grid.css:1038-1039. Without it a failed FBA read renders byte-identically to zero FBA units.
- ActionImpact.findings[] has no blocking flag, so the DS cannot tell an advisory unknown from a blocking one — and impact.unavailable refuses the WHOLE verb (runAction.ts:74-76). ADD blocking?: boolean. Without it, a failed ads read (a check that never blocks) would stop a delete.
- ActionImpact.findings[] carries no as-of, so a fact read from a local mirror (FbaInventoryDetail.lastSyncedAt, AdProductAd.lastSyncedAt, StockLevel.lastSyncedAt) asserts a currency it does not have. ADD optional asOf?: string, rendered as a trailing relative time in ActionConfirm.tsx:110-121. Precedent: MasterDataTab.tsx:983-997 relTime.
- validateImpact(impact) receives only the impact (registry.ts:264) and cannot enforce a rule that depends on the verb's reach. ADD validateAction(action, impact) beside it, refusing a reach 'local-destructive'|'channel' action carrying a blocking 'unknown' finding without impact.unavailable set. It catches the lane that forgot; the single refusal path stays rule 4.
- GridAction has no reach field (only danger?: boolean at registry.ts:184, measured inert — 0 occurrences of `danger` in menuAdapters.tsx). This spec DEPENDS on the state model's reach: 'local' | 'local-destructive' | 'channel' to decide which checks run and which findings block. It is a precondition, not a follow-up. Note DX-5 ("ActionImpact cannot express reversibility") is REFUTED — do not cite it as the justification.
- MEASURED with the repo's own contrast script from apps/web/src/design-system: --nds-warning 3.76:1 light / 4.05:1 dark and --nds-danger 3.91:1 light / 3.89:1 dark, both used as BODY TEXT at grid.css:1038-1039 — under AA in both themes. The new -unknown rule must use --nds-warning-text (8.87 / 8.30) and those two existing lines must be repointed to --nds-warning-text / --nds-danger-text (7.36 / 7.14) in the same edit. The 2px border-left use of --nds-warning at .nds-grid-confirm-aside is a non-text use and stays.

##### API
- UNCHANGED WIRE — GET /api/products/hard-delete-preflight?ids=… keeps its path, method, comma-separated ids param, the 200 cap and both 400 bodies verbatim (products-catalog.routes.ts:1670, :1673), and all four response keys with every existing element field name and type intact (including marketplace: l.marketplace ?? l.region at :1735, role: 'master'|'component', and condition on fbaInventory). Element ADDITIONS are permitted (the caller renders counts, BulkActionBar.tsx:1212-1276); removals and renames are not.
- CHANGED FAILURE SEMANTICS, and this is required, not optional — the four legacy keys keep all-or-nothing (one try/catch, 500), but the two NEW checks may NOT 500 them: their failure surfaces as status:'unavailable' inside a new top-level `checks` key. Reason: BulkActionBar renders preflightError as an amber note (:1205-1209) and arms on typed === 'DELETE' alone (:1128), so a 500 does not block the delete. Two new failure modes on the old semantics would raise the rate at which a permanent delete runs with no preflight at all.
- CALLER FIX, same wave — BulkActionBar must not arm while preflightError is set. Without it the previous item is cosmetic.
- ADDITIVE to that GET — two new top-level keys, stockHolds[] and advertising[], plus the `checks` key. Inert in the existing caller: PreflightWarnings (BulkActionBar.tsx:1062-1067) names four keys and res.json() is cast, so extras are inert at runtime and at compile time.
- CORRECTED, and it is a PRODUCER+CONSUMER change in ONE write, sequenced after P1/P4/P7 — the listingStatus IN (ACTIVE,INACTIVE) AND externalListingId NOT NULL filter is replaced by the shared identityHeld predicate at BOTH sites: the preflight at products-catalog.routes.ts:1678-1684 AND the delist enqueue inside the delete transaction at :1854-1860. Correcting one alone makes the preview promise a delist that never enqueues. Requires the Owner's approval (see openQuestions).
- ALSO REQUIRED BY PD-2 — bulk-hard-delete must refuse the purge when a live external id exists and channelAction === 'none'. A silent orphan must not be reachable by a default that the emptiest surface chose.
- NEW — POST /api/products/operational-impact, body { verb: string; targets: ImpactTarget[] }, ImpactTarget = { productId, channel?, marketplace?, channelConnectionId?, aliasKey?, offerScope? } where offerScope maps to the FulfillmentMethod enum (FBA|FBM) or 'all'. Read-only, writes nothing, 200-target cap from the same shared constant as the GET. Returns per check { status: 'ok'|'unavailable'; scope: 'product'|'coordinate'; asOf?; provenance[]; blocking; rows[] } for channelListings, openOrders, activeBundles, fbaPosture, stockHolds, advertising. A body and not a query string because a five-level coordinate does not survive one.
- QUERY SHAPE, binding — one query per check, selected by productId IN (ids) and partitioned to coordinates in memory. Only ChannelListing may filter the coordinate in SQL (it has the exact composite index, schema.prisma:1786). No 200-way OR against Order, FbaInventoryDetail or AdProductAd, which have single-column indexes only.
- CHANNEL MAPPING — ChannelListing.channel is a free String; Order.channel is the OrderChannel enum (schema.prisma:5384-5391). Map explicitly; an unmapped value returns status:'unavailable' for that target and never reaches Prisma, which would throw a validation error and 500 the whole report.
- NEW SERVICE — apps/api/src/services/operational-impact.service.ts exporting operationalImpact(targets, opts). Holds the five queries MOVED VERBATIM from products-catalog.routes.ts:1676-1732 plus stockHolds and advertising. Deliberately NOT named *-preflight: listing-preflight.service.ts owns content validation and has five non-test importers (amazon-flat-file.routes.ts, amazon-cockpit-publish.routes.ts, preflight-report.service.ts, pim/readiness.service.ts, ebay-variation-preflight.ts).
- NEW SHARED HELPER — fba-posture, returning { isFba, via: ('fulfillmentMethod'|'stockLevel'|'inventoryDetail')[], units?, sellable?, notSellable?, pooled?, pooledAcross?, asOf? }. Three sources: Product.fulfillmentMethod, StockLevel at location code AMAZON-EU-FBA, and FbaInventoryDetail (matched on productId OR sku ∈ {Product.sku} ∪ {Offer.sku} OR asin, tagged matchedBy). If the first two say FBA and the third is silent → status 'unavailable', NEVER units 0: FbaInventoryDetail is a daily-cron snapshot (schema.prisma:9515-9521) and findFbaDeleteRows (flat-file.service.ts:2615-2646) does not read it at all. Pan-EU (Marketplace.fbaProgram === 'PAN_EU') returns pooled units and OMITS the per-market number. Wave 1 does not repoint the flat-file caller (untouchable); a parity test asserts agreement.
- ROUTE RATCHET — extracting the five prisma base accesses (products-catalog.routes.ts:1678, :1692, :1712, :1716, :1720) is EXPECTED to drop that file from its baseline 102 to 97 in scripts/route-prisma-baseline.json; measure with --census, do not assert. MEASURED TODAY: the gate is already red on assets.routes.ts 62→64, brand-story.routes.ts 32→33 and catalog-transfer.routes.ts 0→1, none caused here. product-studio.routes.ts has 0 database accesses and is not in the baseline, so it is held at ZERO — any studio-namespaced route must call the service.
- MANIFEST — apps/api/src/lib/auth/permissions-manifest.ts (NOT lib/permissions-manifest.ts). Add an explicit entry for /api/products/operational-impact mapped to products.view for BOTH methods, ordered BEFORE RW(F.productsView, F.productsEdit, pfx('/api/products')) at :439 — otherwise a read-only POST resolves to products.edit. First-match-wins has already produced one unreachable rule here (ruling #10); the shadowing note is at :430-438 and the resolution loop at :486 (the ':443' in that ruling is stale). Extend permissions-manifest-order.vitest.test.ts.
- AUTHORISATION — an explicit in-file preHandler on the new POST naming the required permission. The global rbacHook defaults to SHADOW, so a manifest entry alone is a log line.
- CALLER — POST /products/:id/presence/plan calls operationalImpact() in-process and folds the result into its ActionImpact. plan must never re-implement a check. How the preflightToken then binds facts it may not cache is unresolved (see openQuestions).
- SERVER MIRROR — assertCanDeleteRelationshipProduct (product-relationship.service.ts:123-129) gains open-order and un-released HARD-reservation clauses so catalog.routes.ts:1837 refuses what the preflight refused. Note this guard ALREADY implements the identityHeld predicate (externalListingId not null, notIn [''] OR isPublished) — it is the reference, not a defective site. Owner decision (see openQuestions).
- SHARED PREDICATE (P9) — isLiveOnChannel moves to packages/shared UNCHANGED as identityHeld. It currently imports the web's ListingRow (listingRisk.ts:19), so the shared signature must be structural: (row: { externalListingId?: string | null }) => boolean. listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:15-31 pins the current behaviour and must pass byte-identically after the move.
- REJECTED as endpoints — GET /api/products/:id/delete-impact (INV-3) and GET /api/products/:id/fulfilment-posture (INV-4). Both are subsets of the POST. INV-4's one-definition requirement is NOT rejected: it is honoured as the shared fba-posture helper above.

##### Schema
- NONE — verified field by field, and correctly labelled. ChannelListing (channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, aliasKey, externalListingId, isPublished, listingStatus default "DRAFT" at schema.prisma:1617; composite unique at :1786). Order.channel is the OrderChannel enum at :5384-5391, marketplace String? at :5415, channelConnectionId at :5507. OrderItem.productId SetNull at :5876-5877. Bundle.name/isActive/productId; BundleComponent.productId (scalar, @@index) with NO product relation at :12185-12200. FbaInventoryDetail.productId (SetNull) / sku / asin / marketplaceId / fulfillmentCenterId / condition / quantity / lastSyncedAt, unique on (workspaceId, sku, marketplaceId, fulfillmentCenterId, condition) at :9522-9550. Marketplace.marketplaceId / code / fbaProgram at :1947,:1966. StockLevel (:9105-9144, cascades from Product, carries variationId). StockReservation.kind/releasedAt/consumedAt/orderId (:9149-9180). AdProductAd.status (CampaignStatus enum) / asin / sku / spendCents / lastSyncedAt (:3312-3330). Offer.sku / fulfillmentMethod (FulfillmentMethod enum = FBA | FBM, schema.prisma:10-13), unique on (workspaceId, channelListingId, fulfillmentMethod) at :2322. Product.fulfillmentMethod (read by flat-file.service.ts:2626-2628).
- NOT PROPOSED — a persisted impact-report table. The report is derived on read and must never be cached: a stale operational fact behind a destructive verb is worse than no fact. See openQuestions on how the preflightToken then binds it — that mechanism is unresolved, and 'no storage' is only coherent with a stateless signed digest.
- DEPENDENCY, not a change here — the state model's ChannelListing.presenceIntent and channelFact* columns are ADDITIVE and land with that spec. This specification reads neither and can ship before them.

##### Open for you
- THE PD-2 PREDICATE CORRECTION. It widens what both the warning AND the delist cascade see, by a measured 20 rows on one eBay family alone, and it changes what the /products bulk delete acts on. That is an Owner decision about blast radius, not a bug fix. Approve it as one write across products-catalog.routes.ts:1678-1684 and :1854-1860, sequenced after P1/P4/P7 — or hold the writer's filter and accept that the preview and the action disagree by design, stated on screen.
- THE SERVER MIRROR ON assertCanDeleteRelationshipProduct. Adding open-order and HARD-reservation clauses means DELETE /products/:parentId/children/:childId starts refusing deletions it accepts today. Anyone with a stale order line cannot delete a child until the order closes. Is that the right default, or should the server warn and let the typed confirmation carry it?
- THE LITERAL WORD 'DELETE'. PD-5 is confirmed: BulkActionBar arms on typed === 'DELETE' (:1128), which the DS's own rule forbids (registry.ts:118, 'A SKU, never DELETE'). I have NOT changed it — it is a legacy Tailwind surface on a different page and outside this spec's scope. Fix it here as a one-line honesty change, or leave it to whatever replaces that dialog?
- THE AT-REST FBA BANNER. INV-4 asks for a persistent warning Banner in the channel scope. CS-1 is confirmed at 213px of chrome above the first data row. Should the banner live inside the scope body (conditional on fbaUnits > 0 or posture === FBA, dismissible per session), or is any new persistent band in the studio refused until CS-1 is resolved?
- THE FLAT-FILE PARITY. INV-4 asked for ONE definition of 'is this FBA'. The shipped one is inside findFbaDeleteRows (flat-file.service.ts:2615-2646), and the flat-file editors are untouchable. I propose a new shared helper plus a parity TEST, and repointing the flat-file caller later under its own approval — accepting two definitions in the tree meanwhile. Ratify, or authorise the repoint now as an exempted compile-equivalent change?
- THE preflightToken MECHANISM. Stateless HMAC over the plan digest, or a persisted plan row? This spec asserts no storage and no caching; a token binding operational facts needs one of the two, and 'in memory' fails the moment the API runs more than one instance.
- ADS AS A BLOCKING CHECK. I have it report-only and non-blocking, on the house no-pause rule. Should ENABLED ads with recorded spend instead BLOCK a permanent delete until the operator suppresses the bids, given that after the delete the campaign keeps spending and the guard built to catch it reports 'ok'?
- THE THREE PRE-EXISTING GATE REDS. assets.routes.ts 62→64, brand-story.routes.ts 32→33 and catalog-transfer.routes.ts 0→1 are red now, on other lanes' work. Should this work be held until they are resolved or re-baselined by their owners, or should it proceed against a captured census as its baseline?

##### Risks
- The PD-2 predicate correction is a producer/consumer change, not a preview tweak. Landing it on the GET alone makes the preview promise a delist the transaction never enqueues (products-catalog.routes.ts:1854-1860 holds its own copy). Landing it on both without P4 and P7 first sends UNPUBLISH/DELETE for ~20 measured DRAFT/ENDED rows holding real ItemIDs through an adapter that escalates Amazon UNPUBLISH to DELETE (channel-delist.service.ts:155-158) and reads an eBay ownership refusal as success (:227,231,290-293). Either order is worse than today. Sequencing step (0) is the mitigation and it is not optional.
- Adding two checks to an all-or-nothing GET raises the 500 rate on an endpoint whose failure does NOT block the delete: BulkActionBar renders preflightError as an amber note (:1205-1209) and arms on typed === 'DELETE' alone (:1128). Without both mitigations in §5 this specification makes permanent deletes measurably less safe than before it shipped.
- The FBA fact now has three sources with different populations. If the helper's precedence is wrong, or the parity test against findFbaDeleteRows is not run per coordinate, the studio and the flat-file preflight will disagree about the same SKU — the exact two-validator failure readiness.service.ts:1-10 records having already happened here once.
- impact.unavailable refuses the entire verb (runAction.ts:74-76) and validateImpact refuses first if level !== 'none' (registry.ts:270-272). A lane that sets unavailable alongside a confirmation level shows the operator a developer sentence instead of a reason — the shape DX-4 already confirmed. The 'set level:none with unavailable' rule needs a test, not a convention.
- Two hundred five-level targets over six checks is the load nobody has measured. Only ChannelListing has the coordinate as an index (schema.prisma:1786). The in-memory partition keeps every query on productId, but the response size at 200 targets is untested and the route has no pagination.
- Order.channel is an enum and ChannelListing.channel is a free String. An unmapped value passed to Prisma is a validation throw, so a coordinate on an unrecognised channel would 500 the whole report rather than return nothing for that target.
- The preflightToken this spec feeds has no store: §Schema says the report is never persisted and never cached. A token that binds facts must therefore be a stateless HMAC over the plan digest with an expiry, or the plan must be persisted. Neither is specified anywhere yet, and a memory-resident token does not survive a second API instance.
- The service lands beside code that does not compile (bulk-action.service.ts:1591, TS2339) and in a repo whose route-prisma gate is already red on three files. A green run after this work will not distinguish 'my change is clean' from 'the pre-existing reds moved'. Capture the census before starting.
- Claiming ADP-2 as closed would be a lie: the report is honest but the retail-readiness guard stays disarmed (null-productId ads skipped at :74-75, missing product counted in stock at :60-61, zero linked products hard-coded 'ok' at :82-83). If the Owner reads the finding as closed, nobody fixes the guard.

##### The specification
# CORRECTIONS
Everything below was re-derived from the working tree with `/usr/bin/grep`, `sed` and the repo's own scripts. Fourteen corrections; the reuse decision and the endpoint shape survive.
1. **The gate baseline is wrong, and I measured it.** `node scripts/check-route-prisma-ratchet.mjs --check` is red on **three** files, not one: `assets.routes.ts 62 → 64`, `brand-story.routes.ts 32 → 33`, `catalog-transfer.routes.ts 0 → 1` (a zero-held file that gained one — the case the gate's own message calls out). `products-catalog.routes.ts` is not among them, so **102 holds**. A lane cannot use "the gate is green" as its signal here; it must diff the census, not the exit code.
2. **The rest of the tree is red too.** `api-tsc`: `bulk-action.service.ts(1591,184): TS2339 Property 'userId' does not exist on type 'CreateJobInput'`. Studio vitest: 6 failures (`layers.vitest.test.ts:55` unmapped `channelSnapshot`; `masterWrite.vitest.test.ts` ×5). A new service lands beside a service that does not compile.
3. **The contrast numbers were half right.** Run from `apps/web/src/design-system`, the repo's own script gives `--nds-warning` **3.76:1 light / 4.05:1 dark** and `--nds-danger` **3.91:1 light / 3.89:1 dark`. The proposal applied the warning range to both. The conclusion holds — both fail AA as body text — and the replacements pass comfortably: `--nds-warning-text` 8.87/8.30, `--nds-danger-text` 7.36/7.14.
4. **The PD-2 filter correction is not a preview-only change and is not harmless.** The identical filter exists **twice**: the preflight at `products-catalog.routes.ts:1678-1684` and the delist enqueue inside the delete transaction at `:1854-1860`. Correcting only the GET manufactures exactly the divergence the proposal invokes `readiness.service.ts:1-10` against — the operator reads "23 listings will be unpublished" and 20 of them are never enqueued. Correcting both widens what the destructive cascade reaches, through adapters that escalate (`channel-delist.service.ts:155-158` UNPUBLISH→DELETE) and that record refusals as success (`:227,231,290-293`). **Producer and consumer in one write, and sequenced after P4/P7/P1.**
5. **PD-2's second half is missing.** Its fix also requires a server-side refusal when a live external id exists and `channelAction === 'none'`. The proposal mirrors only `assertCanDeleteRelationshipProduct`; the silent orphan stays reachable by default on `/products`.
6. **`assertCanDeleteRelationshipProduct` already implements the right predicate.** `product-relationship.service.ts:123-129` matches `externalListingId: { not: null, notIn: [''] } OR isPublished: true`. It is not the defective site — it is the reference implementation. P9 repoints the two route filters at it, not the other way round.
7. **`delete-variant` has no `Promise.all` and already refuses on a live listing.** Its preflight is one awaited `ops.listings(row.id)` in a try/catch (`familyActions.ts:439-452`), and `:453` returns `level:'none'` + `unavailable` whenever any listing is live or `isPublished`. So the new checks render only on rows with no live listing — which is precisely the ADP-2 / INV-4 population (ended coordinates still holding FBA units and ENABLED ads). That strengthens the case; it also means the wiring instruction as written does not describe the code.
8. **BundleComponent does have a `productId`.** `schema.prisma:12185-12200` declares `productId String` with `@@index([productId])`; what is absent is the `product` *relation* (route comment, `products-catalog.routes.ts:739-743`). "No product field at all" would tell an implementer the shipped query is impossible.
9. **The FBA check invents a third definition and prints an unpopulated table as zero.** INV-4 asked for one definition. The shipped one is `findFbaDeleteRows` (`flat-file.service.ts:2615-2646`): `Product.fulfillmentMethod === 'FBA'` **or** positive `StockLevel` at location code `AMAZON-EU-FBA`. It never reads `FbaInventoryDetail`, which is a daily-cron snapshot (`schema.prisma:9515-9521`). A `FbaInventoryDetail`-only check returns `[]` for an FBA product the cron has not covered, and `[]` renders as "no FBA exposure". Three sources, with provenance, and `unknown` when the posture says FBA and the detail table is silent.
10. **`spendCents` is not daily spend.** `AdProductAd` (`schema.prisma:3312-3330`) carries cumulative `impressions/clicks/spendCents/salesCents` plus `lastSyncedAt`. "39.80 EUR/day" needs the campaign's daily budget, not this column. Also `status` is the `CampaignStatus` enum, and `suppressCampaignBids` lives in `ads-bid-suppression.service.ts` (imported at `ads-retail-readiness.service.ts:16`, called at `:119`) — not at `:106`.
11. **`unavailable` refuses the entire verb, and refuses it twice.** `runAction.ts:73-75` rule 4 returns `refused`; and `validateImpact` (`registry.ts:270-272`) already pushes a problem when `unavailable` is set with `level !== 'none'`, so rule 3 fires first with a developer sentence. Two consequences: a lane must set `level:'none'` whenever it sets `unavailable`, and **only a check whose found rows would block may escalate to `unavailable`**. An ads read that failed must not stop a delete that ads never blocks.
12. **Adding two checks to an all-or-nothing GET makes deletes *less* safe.** `BulkActionBar` renders `preflightError` as an amber note (`:1205-1209`) and arms on `typed === 'DELETE'` alone (`:1128`). A 500 does not block the delete. Two more failure modes therefore raise the rate at which a permanent delete runs with no preflight at all.
13. **200 five-level targets must not become a 200-way `OR`.** Only `ChannelListing` has the coordinate as an index (`@@unique([workspaceId, productId, channel, marketplace, channelConnectionId, aliasKey])`, `schema.prisma:1786`). `Order`, `FbaInventoryDetail` and `AdProductAd` have single-column indexes only.
14. **Smaller factual fixes.** `Order.channel` is the `OrderChannel` **enum** (`schema.prisma:5384-5391`) while `ChannelListing.channel` is a free String — an unmapped value is a Prisma validation throw, i.e. a 500, not an empty result. The manifest is at `apps/api/src/lib/auth/permissions-manifest.ts`, entry at `:439`, shadowing note `:430-438`, resolution loop `:486` (ruling #10's ":443" is stale). `FbaInventoryDetail`'s unique includes `workspaceId`, and it carries `asin` as a third match key. `listingStatus` defaults to `"DRAFT"` at `:1617`. `isLiveOnChannel` imports the web's `ListingRow` (`listingRisk.ts:19`), so the shared copy must take a structural `{ externalListingId?: string | null }`. **DX-5 is REFUTED** — the `reach`/`reversal` DS additions are a state-model dependency, not a closure of DX-5.
---
# 1. The reuse decision (unchanged)
One service, two routes, three consumers. `apps/api/src/services/operational-impact.service.ts` holds the five existing queries moved verbatim plus the new ones. `GET /products/hard-delete-preflight` becomes a thin adapter projecting back to its legacy four-key shape. `POST /api/products/operational-impact` takes five-level targets in a body. `POST /presence/plan` calls the service in-process. `delete-impact` (INV-3) and `fulfilment-posture` (INV-4) are rejected as endpoints — both are subsets — but **INV-4's shared-helper requirement is not rejected** (see §3).
The name deliberately avoids "preflight": `listing-preflight.service.ts` owns content validation and has five non-test importers (`amazon-flat-file.routes.ts`, `amazon-cockpit-publish.routes.ts`, `preflight-report.service.ts`, `pim/readiness.service.ts`, `ebay-variation-preflight.ts`).
# 2. Query shape
Per check: `{ status: 'ok' | 'unavailable'; scope: 'product' | 'coordinate'; asOf?: string; provenance: string[]; rows: [...] }`. `scope` is on the wire so no surface can present a product-wide fact as coordinate-scoped.
**One query per check, partitioned in memory.** Every check selects by `productId IN (ids)` — indexed on all five tables — and is partitioned to coordinates in application code. `ChannelListing` may filter the coordinate in SQL because it has the exact composite index; nothing else may.
**channelListings — coordinate, 5/5.** Predicate = the shared `identityHeld` (§4). Reports more rows than today, never fewer.
**openOrders — coordinate, 3/5.** `Order.channel` (enum), `marketplace String?` (`:5415`), `channelConnectionId` (`:5507`). No alias dimension: `aliasScoped: false`. Null `marketplace` rows are always included, flagged `marketplaceUnknown: true`. Channel strings are mapped to `OrderChannel` through an explicit table; an unmapped value returns `status:'unavailable'` for that target, never a 500. Do not widen to `OrderItem.sku`.
**activeBundles — product-level, coordinate-relevant.** Add `bundleName` and, per bundle, the master's `ChannelListing` coordinates. A coordinate verb leaves a kit selling with an unsellable component; a product delete leaves a `BundleComponent` row pointing at a dead id, because there is no relation to clean it up.
**fbaPosture — Amazon only, three sources, one helper.** A new `fba-posture` helper returns `{ isFba, via: ('fulfillmentMethod'|'stockLevel'|'inventoryDetail')[], units?, detail?, asOf?, pooled? }`, derived from (a) `Product.fulfillmentMethod`, (b) `StockLevel` at `AMAZON-EU-FBA`, (c) `FbaInventoryDetail` split into `sellable` / `notSellable` by `condition`. Rules: if (a) or (b) says FBA and (c) has no rows → `status: 'unavailable'`, never `units: 0`. If any Amazon market in the target set has `Marketplace.fbaProgram === 'PAN_EU'` → `{ pooled: true, pooledAcross: [codes], units }` and **omit** the per-market number. Match (c) on `productId` **or** `sku ∈ {Product.sku} ∪ {Offer.sku}` **or** `asin`, tagging `matchedBy` — `productId` is `SetNull`, so a prior delete is exactly the population it cannot otherwise see. **Wave 1 does not refactor `findFbaDeleteRows`' caller** (the flat-file path is untouchable); a parity test asserts the helper agrees with it on the same input, and the caller is repointed later under its own approval.
**stockHolds — product-level, local-destructive verbs only.** `StockLevel` (cascades from Product, `:9126`) and un-released, un-consumed `HARD` `StockReservation` rows with `orderId` (`:9155-9180`). `StockLevel.variationId` rows are included and labelled, never silently dropped. Not emitted for a coordinate verb, which destroys no ledger.
**advertising — coordinate-scoped via ASIN, report only.** `AdProductAd` by `productId` **or** `asin`, `status: 'ENABLED'`, returning `{ adGroupId, campaignId, asin, sku, spendCents, lastSyncedAt }`. The sentence says *cumulative recorded spend, as of <lastSyncedAt>* — never "/day" unless a daily budget is actually read. The next step named is `suppressCampaignBids`, the house no-pause path; this endpoint writes nothing.
# 3. Blocking, unknown, and the two DS rules
Each check declares `blocking: boolean` for the verb's reach. **Only a blocking check may escalate to `impact.unavailable`, and when it does the lane sets `level: 'none'` and an operator sentence** ("We could not check open orders for SKU-123, so the delete is refused. Try again shortly."). A non-blocking check that failed becomes a finding at `severity: 'unknown'`.
DS additions, in `design-system/grid/actions/registry.ts`:
- `findings[].severity` gains `'unknown'` (union at `:102`), plus `blocking?: boolean` and `asOf?: string`.
- `.nds-grid-confirm-unknown` beside `grid.css:1038-1039`, using `--nds-warning-text`; **the two existing lines are repointed to `--nds-warning-text` / `--nds-danger-text` in the same edit** (figures in correction 3).
- `validateAction(action, impact)` beside `validateImpact` (`registry.ts:264-274`), refusing a `reach: 'local-destructive' | 'channel'` action that carries a `blocking` `'unknown'` finding **without** `unavailable` set. It catches the lane that forgot; the single refusal path stays rule 4.
`ActionConfirm.tsx:110-121` renders `asOf` as a trailing relative time (precedent: `MasterDataTab.tsx:983-997`). No new component; the at-rest FBA notice uses `design-system/components/Banner.tsx` with `tone="warning"` (`Tone` includes `'warning'`, `primitives/tone.ts:2`).
# 4. Wiring
**`delete-variant`** (`familyActions.ts:415-477`, `danger: true`, reach `local-destructive`). Its single `ops.listings` await becomes a `Promise.all` of `ops.listings` and one `POST /operational-impact`; the existing catch, which already refuses rather than softening, covers both. On the path where the listing gate passes: a non-terminal open-order line or an un-released `HARD` reservation escalates `level` from `'confirm'` to `'type-to-confirm'` with `confirmPhrase: row.sku` (`registry.ts:118` already demands a real SKU); a component-role bundle is a `'error'` finding naming the bundle and where it sells; FBA units emit the Seller Central sentence from `amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:805-809` **verbatim, as one exported constant**; ads emit a `'warn'` finding.
**Server mirror.** `assertCanDeleteRelationshipProduct` gains open-order and HARD-reservation clauses so `catalog.routes.ts:1837` refuses what the preflight refused, and `bulk-hard-delete` gains PD-2's refusal on `channelAction === 'none'` with a live external id.
**`offer-toggle`** (reach `local`) gains FBA and ads as non-blocking findings only. **Presence verbs** inherit the mapping through `presence/plan`.
# 5. Legacy contract
Path, method, `ids`, the 200 cap, both 400 bodies, the four keys, `marketplace: l.marketplace ?? l.region` (`:1735`), `role`, `condition`: unchanged. Element *additions* are permitted (the caller counts); removals and renames are not. `stockHolds` and `advertising` are new top-level keys, inert in `PreflightWarnings` (`BulkActionBar.tsx:1062-1067`).
**Two changes to failure behaviour, both required.** (a) The four legacy keys keep all-or-nothing; the two new checks may not 500 them — their failure surfaces in a new `checks` key. (b) `BulkActionBar` must not arm while `preflightError` is set (`:1128`). Without (b), correction 12 stands and this specification makes deletes worse.
# 6. Sequencing
(0) Land P1, P4, P7 — the delist queue survives the transaction, eBay resolves the owning connection and stops reading a refusal as success, and no adapter escalates. (1) Characterisation test pinning the legacy body; extract the service; re-run the census (expect 102 → 97, measured, not asserted). (2) The two new checks + the caller's arming fix. (3) The POST + a manifest entry mapped to `products.view` for both methods, ordered before `:439`, extending `permissions-manifest-order.vitest.test.ts`, plus an in-file `preHandler` (the global hook is shadow-mode). (4) DS types + the CSS repoint. (5) The PD-2 predicate on **both** filters in one write, with the server refusal. (6) `delete-variant`. (7) Presence verbs.
Tests are node-environment, each with a positive control in the same run — a filter that matches nothing and a filter that is wrong both return `[]`. Explicitly: a Pan-EU test asserting the per-market number is **absent, not zero**; an FBA test where `fulfillmentMethod = 'FBA'` and `FbaInventoryDetail` is empty asserting `status: 'unavailable'`; and a test that an advisory check's failure does **not** set `impact.unavailable`.

#### Repair the delist path before any lifecycle UI is wired — corrected: two callers already run it live, and D5 must land last of all

The reviewed proposal is largely right and I confirmed eleven of its twelve defects line by line. Five things are wrong. Two cited file paths do not exist as written. The typecheck baseline is cleaner than claimed (exit 0, zero diagnostics). Its governing conclusion — "nothing downstream has ever run" — is false: two of three `dispatchChannelDelist` callers are in-process and live today through an endpoint with no authorisation at all. Its D9 fix removes the only backstop that runs when Redis is down. And, most seriously, its own sequencing lands D5 while `delistEbay` still ignores its action argument, which would make the pre-selected "Unpublish (recommended)" default irreversibly end live eBay listings for the first time. Corrected order, corrected paths, three new preconditions.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- No DS gap is created or closed. Every change is in apps/api and packages/shared; no control is added.
- packages/shared has no listing-liveness module. It DOES already ship packages/shared/listing-readiness.ts (types only: state 'ready'|'warn'|'blocked'|'absent', plus accountId/aliasKey), so the five-level coordinate already has a shared vocabulary — but readiness is not liveness and must not be overloaded. Add packages/shared/listing-liveness.ts exporting isLiveOnChannel/classifyListings, and note that packages/shared/package.json uses an explicit per-module `exports` map over ./dist — a new file needs an exports entry and a build, or every import fails at runtime and not at typecheck.
- Standing gap, untouched and made more urgent by this wave: .claude/DS-GAPS.md:330-348 — MenuItemDef carries no tone and no .nds-menu danger rule exists in any stylesheet. Once a delist verb is reachable from a menu it will render identically to Reload. Owner's ruling CH.1 governs; not this wave's to close.

##### API
- POST /api/products/bulk-hard-delete — response gains `channelCascadeDispatched` (an AFTER-COMMIT count of delist rows that actually survived), `channelCascadePartial`, and NEW per correction C10: `channelSkipped: [{ channel, marketplace, externalListingId }]` naming every listing that will stay live because no adapter exists (Etsy today; WooCommerce under containment). `channelCascadeEnqueued` is retained for one release then removed — it is a pre-commit create count of rows that no longer exist.
- POST /api/products/bulk-hard-delete — the delist rows change shape: productId and channelListingId become null; channel, marketplace, region, aliasKey, channelConnectionId, externalListingId, externalParentId, sellerSku, fulfillmentMethod, isVariationChild and sharedItemIdRefs move onto `payload`; holdUntil set to +5 minutes. No request-body change.
- NEW, and a precondition of the holdUntil claim (correction C13) — POST /api/products/delist-cascade/cancel { queueIds: string[] } sets syncStatus:'CANCELLED' on rows still PENDING and inside their hold window. The worker already honours CANCELLED at bullmq-sync.worker.ts:117-125. Without this route the five minutes is a delay, not a grace window, and no Undo may be offered.
- GET /api/products/hard-delete-preflight — the channelListings selector drops the `listingStatus IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE')` clause (products-catalog.routes.ts:1680-1682), and each row gains `channelRemovable: boolean`. More rows return for the same product; BulkActionBar.tsx:1117 defaults channelAction from this list's emptiness and must be re-read in the same change, and BulkActionBar.tsx:1129 (arms on the literal word DELETE) must be re-read too, because after D5 that dialog reaches live marketplaces for the first time.
- POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove — per-target result gains `delistError: string | null`; `delisted` becomes meaningful (today it is false with no text because amazon/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:82 is a bare `catch {}`). Order inverts: no local row is deleted until the remote call succeeded. Clients reading only `channelListingsRemoved` keep working.
- Internal contract — ChannelDelistJob gains `sellerSku: string | null` and `channelConnectionId: string | null`. All three callers update in the same change: bullmq-sync.worker.ts:142, amazon/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:71, ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:143-160 (buildDelistJob).
