- New errorCodes on OutboundSyncQueue.errorCode, each needing operator copy before it can be surfaced: AMAZON_DELIST_NO_SKU, AMAZON_DELIST_NO_REGION, AMAZON_UNPUBLISH_NOT_IMPLEMENTED, EBAY_UNPUBLISH_NOT_IMPLEMENTED (new, per correction C4), EBAY_DELIST_UNKNOWN_MARKET, EBAY_DELIST_NO_REGION, EBAY_DELIST_STILL_ACTIVE, EBAY_DELIST_UNVERIFIED, SHOPIFY_DELIST_GATED, SHOPIFY_DELIST_UNVERIFIED, SHOPIFY_DELIST_NO_ACCOUNT (new, per correction C8).
- AUTHORISATION, first commit not last: an explicit in-file preHandler naming the required permission on POST /api/products/bulk-hard-delete (products-catalog.routes.ts:1773 — the file's only preHandler is at :1603, on a different route) and on POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove (amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:1806, in a file with zero auth references across 20 POST routes), independent of the shadow/enforce flip. Paired with a real actor: userIdFor() returns the literal 'default-user' at products-catalog.routes.ts:82-84.
- AUDIT — one ProductEvent per delist outcome (aggregateType 'ChannelListing', aggregateId the pre-delete listing id, full coordinate and external id in `data`). Required because the queue payload is not durable: settled rows are deleted at 30 days and failed rows at 90 by ads-retention.job.ts:39-41.

##### Schema
- NONE — verified, not assumed. OutboundSyncQueue.productId is `String?` at schema.prisma:6824 and channelListingId is `String?` at :6827 (the reviewed proposal cited :6822/:6825). Both are onDelete: Cascade at :6825 and :6828. The line 'At least one must be set' at :6823 is a COMMENT; I found no CHECK constraint in any of the six migrations that touch OutboundSyncQueue. Enqueuing the delist rows with both columns null and the five-level coordinate on `payload` is therefore a route change with no migration, and it avoids the WHICH-DATABASE hazard entirely.
- NOT PROPOSED — OutboundSyncQueue FKs Cascade → SetNull. BREAKING (model-wide, changes the ordinary push path's semantics) and unnecessary given the nullable columns above.
- NOT PROPOSED — a sellerSku column on OutboundSyncQueue. Would be ADDITIVE and harmless, but the payload already carries it and a column would need applying to both databases before it could be declared.
- OBSERVED, NOT CHANGED — ChannelListing.listingStatus @default("DRAFT") (schema.prisma:1619). It is why D11 is necessary: submission.service.ts:1465-1489 and the Shopify content-sync both create rows without setting it. D11 fixes the two READERS, not the default, because several services write the column inconsistently and changing a default backfills nothing.
- OBSERVED, NOT CHANGED — SyncChannel (schema.prisma:57-66) has AMAZON, EBAY, SHOPIFY, WOOCOMMERCE, GOOGLE, META, TIKTOK and no ETSY. Adding ETSY would be ADDITIVE, but it is out of scope here and would imply an adapter that does not exist; correction C10 handles Etsy by reporting it, not by enqueuing it.
- NO NEW TABLE for the ProductEvent precondition (correction C11). ProductEvent already exists, is already FK-free by design (schema.prisma:15475-15478), already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing' and is already read by the audit surface. Delist outcomes are written there with the full coordinate and external id in `data`.

##### Open for you
- Does the Owner accept the reordering? The reviewed proposal put PD-1 last for the right reason and the wrong sequence: D7 and the authorisation guard must be first because the destructive Amazon write is ungated AND unauthenticated today, and D10-widened-to-eBay must precede D5 because D5 arms the pre-selected default. Corrected order: D7 → AUTH → D6 → D10 → D1 → D3 → D4 → D2 → D11 → D12 → D8 → D9 → D5.
- eBay 'unpublish': refuse it, or route it to the reversible hold that already ships? ebay-flat-file.routes.ts:1874-1901 zeroes the market quantity and keeps the ItemID ('the safe hide'), and a channel-agnostic ZERO_PIN ships at sync-control.routes.ts:934-960. Routing unpublish there would make the recommended default honest for the first time — but it is a behaviour change on a shipped path, not a bug fix, so it is the Owner's call, not mine.
- holdUntil +5 min: build the cancel, or drop the word 'grace'? The worker honours CANCELLED and the drain honours holdUntil, so POST /api/products/delist-cascade/cancel is a small route. Without it the five minutes is a delay with nothing to undo to, and putting an Undo in a Toast would be the exact dishonesty this wave exists to remove.
- Etsy: does the Owner want the preflight to say plainly 'these listings will stay live on Etsy' (my C10 proposal, a contract change), or does Etsy stay silently skipped until a channel adapter exists? SyncChannel has no ETSY value, so there is no third option that removes them.
- Does the untouchable flat-file rule cover apps/api/src/services/amazon/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts and apps/api/src/services/ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts, or only the editor UI? D12 changes both services' order of operations and result shapes.
- Actor identity: userIdFor() returns the literal 'default-user' (products-catalog.routes.ts:82-84). Does the Owner want that resolved in this wave — the audit's sc:permissions-audit critical — or does the guard ship first and the register stay forged for another wave?
- RBAC enforce. Every guard proposed here is a log line while rbacHook defaults to SHADOW (rbac-hook.ts:30-32). Should the in-file preHandlers be written to work regardless of the flag (my assumption), or is the flip to NEXUS_RBAC_MODE=enforce part of this wave?
- Precondition P5 from the state model (pushAmazonPrice resurrecting a closed offer via op:'replace') is deliberately NOT in this spec — after a delete there is no ChannelListing left to reprice, so it belongs to WITHDRAWN, not to the delist path. Confirm it stays with the presence wave rather than being quietly assumed covered here.

##### Risks
- THE HEADLINE RISK. D5 as originally sequenced turns 'Unpublish (recommended)' — the pre-selected default at BulkActionBar.tsx:1117 — into an irreversible eBay EndFixedPriceItem on live listings, because delistEbay ignores its action argument (channel-delist.service.ts:245-247, the parameter is literally named _action). D10 must be widened to eBay and land before D5, or this repair causes the first real loss.
- D1, D3, D4, D7 and D10 are live TODAY through two in-process callers (amazon/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:71 and ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:212), one of which is reached by POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove — a route in a file with zero preHandler/requirePermission/authenticate across 20 POST routes. Sequencing them behind D5 leaves an unauthenticated ASIN-in-SKU-slot delete armed for the length of the wave.
- Excluding lifecycle syncTypes from the cron drain (D9 as written) orphans every delist row created while ENABLE_QUEUE_WORKERS!=1 or Redis is unreachable; the janitor CANCELs it silently at 7 days. The corrected fix (a lifecycle branch inside dispatchSync) must be verified against the buildBullMQSkip fail-open path at sync.worker.ts:38, or the same row runs twice.
- Widening the liveness filter (D11) increases what the cascade attempts. On Amazon it will now call deleteListingsItem for wizard rows that were never published; with no read-back on the Amazon side, that reports a channel removal that never happened. Amazon needs the same verify-don't-guess treatment D4 gives eBay before D11 lands, or the honesty gain is eBay-only.
- A Prisma mock has no foreign keys, so no unit test can reproduce the D5 cascade. The static gate plus the after-commit count are the real controls; the gate must exit 2 when it cannot locate its anchors, never 0. A vacuous PASS here looks identical to the fix working.
- Nine new errorCodes land on OutboundSyncQueue.errorCode with no surface reading them. Until a Needs-attention surface has copy for each, a refused delist is a code in a database column and the operator sees the same silence as today — a different silence, not less of it.
- Both databases hazard: this spec proposes no migration precisely to avoid it, but the D5 read-back count and any before/after row count run against whichever database apps/api/.env points at. Discriminate before quoting a number; on 09-11 only Product.version separated a local Docker pg from Neon.
- D12 changes services that back the flat-file editors, which the standing untouchable rule covers. Proceeding without Owner ratification risks a rollback of correct work.

##### The specification
# Repair: backend criticals on the delist path (corrected)
## CORRECTIONS
**C1 — Two cited paths do not exist; an implementer following them writes new files.**
`apps/api/src/services/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts` does not exist. The file is `apps/api/src/services/amazon/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts`, and every line number shifts: pre-read `:53-56`, delete `:59-63`, delist loop `:68-83`, the bare `catch {}` at `:82`. `apps/api/src/services/marketplaces/shopify/admin-client.ts` does not exist either; `shopifyAdmin` is at `apps/api/src/services/shopify/admin-client.ts:7-29` and `assertShopifyResult` at `:30-33`. Minor drift elsewhere: the Amazon UNPUBLISH fallback is `channel-delist.service.ts:149-160` (warn at `:155`), the Shopify duck-type block `:188-203`, `ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS` `:223-234`, `applyDelistResultToQueue` SUCCESS write `:317-324`.
**C2 — The baseline is wrong, and the true one is stricter.** I ran `npx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api/tsconfig.json` to a file: **exit 0, zero diagnostics.** There is no pre-existing `bulk-action.service.ts:1591` error. That is a *better* baseline — any diagnostic this work introduces is unambiguously attributable, and no lane may hide behind a pre-existing one. The vitest baseline reproduces exactly: `ebay-trading-api.endlisting`, `ebay-flat-file-delete.service`, `ebay-trading-api.service` — **3 files, 70 tests, passed**. `node scripts/check-writability.mjs` reproduces as **NOT MEASURED / DRIFT** naming `products.routes.ts`; nothing here touches the two assertions it names.
**C3 — The governing conclusion is half wrong.** `/usr/bin/grep -rn "dispatchChannelDelist" apps/api/src` returns exactly three call sites: `bullmq-sync.worker.ts:142`, `amazon/amazon-flat-file-remove.service.ts:71`, and `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:212` (via `tryDelist`). **The last two call the dispatcher in-process** — no queue row, no FK cascade, no worker. So D1 (ASIN in the SKU slot), D3 (GB throws), D4 (blind delist reported as success), D7 (ungated destructive write) and D10 (silent escalation) are **live today**, not latent. `removeAmazonListing` is reached by `POST /api/amazon/flat-file/remove` (`amazon-flat-file.routes.ts:1806-1819`), a route in a file with **zero** occurrences of `preHandler|requirePermission|authenticate` across **20** `fastify.post` routes, taking a caller-supplied target array. "Nothing downstream has ever run in this path" is true only of the bulk-hard-delete cascade. The authorisation guard and D7 are therefore not wave hygiene; they are the first commit.
**C4 — As sequenced, D5 ships an irreversible eBay end under a button that says "Unpublish (recommended)". This is the change that must not land as written.** `delistEbay(job, _action)` takes its action as `_action` and never reads it (`channel-delist.service.ts:245-247`): unpublish and delete both perform `EndFixedPriceItem`. `BulkActionBar.tsx:1117` pre-selects `'unpublish'` whenever the preflight returns any listing. Today that is inert because the queue rows die. The moment D5 lets them survive, the pre-selected default ends live eBay listings permanently, for the first time, with an operator's name on it. The audit carries this as two CONFIRMED criticals. **D10 must be widened to eBay and must precede D5.**
Corrected order: **D7 → AUTH → D6 → D10 → D1 → D3 → D4 → D2 → D11 → D12 → D8 → D9 → D5.** D5 is last, and gated additionally on C10 and C13.
**C5 — D9 as written destroys the only backstop.** `sync.worker.ts:24-44` documents the cron as the fallback in its own comments ("BullMQ may be down → we're the backstop"; "When BullMQ is off, returns null → the cron is the sole consumer and drains all"), and `fireOutboundJobs` is fire-and-forget by contract (`outbound-enqueue.ts:63-70`: "the PENDING row is drained by the cron regardless"). Adding the two lifecycle syncTypes to the drain's `notIn` (`outbound-sync.service.ts:709`) therefore orphans every delist row created while `ENABLE_QUEUE_WORKERS !== '1'` or Redis is unreachable — PENDING forever, until `outbound-queue-janitor.job.ts:72-80` silently flips it to CANCELLED at 7 days with no operator signal. **Corrected fix: give `dispatchSync` a lifecycle branch that routes to `dispatchChannelDelist` — one dispatcher, both lanes — instead of an exclusion.** The refusal stays only for a channel with no adapter. The proposal's diagnosis of D9 is right; its remedy inverts the defect.
**C6 — The pattern reclassification is an incomplete set claim.** `ALREADY_ENDED_PATTERNS` has **ten** members (`:223-234`), not eight. The proposal assigns eight and leaves `/item (is )?not (active|available)/i` and `/listing (is )?not (active|available|found)/i` unclassified — both of which eBay returns for an item the token cannot see. Derive the split from the source array and add a gate that fails if any member of the union is absent from exactly one of the two successor arrays.
**C7 — D4's readback inherits D3's bug and the signature is not what the proposal assumes.** `getItemListingStatus(itemId, ctx: { oauthToken, market })` (`ebay-trading-api.service.ts:316-319`) takes a **market code**, not a siteId, and calls `siteIdForMarket(ctx.market)` at `:320`. On a `GB` row the verifier throws before any HTTP. D3 must precede D4 (the proposal's order does), and D4's tests must carry a `targetRegion:'GB'` arm asserting the readback actually ran — otherwise the fix passes its own tests on IT and fails silently on the market the audit says is broken.
**C8 — D2 has no account to resolve.** `shopifyAdmin(accountId)` resolves through `resolveConnection({accountId})` (`admin-client.ts:8-11`) and throws when the account is not Shopify or has no verified domain. The cascade's `findMany` (`products-catalog.routes.ts:1857-1868`) does not select `channelConnectionId`, and Shopify rows from older paths may carry none. Required ladder: the row's `channelConnectionId`; else the single active SHOPIFY connection; else refuse `SHOPIFY_DELIST_NO_ACCOUNT`. Never fall back to `ShopifyService`'s env credentials — that is the ungated path being removed. Separately: read `getShopifyPublishMode()` **before** issuing the mutation rather than matching the thrown prose at `admin-client.ts:15`. String-matching a gate message is `isAlreadyEndedError` reinvented one file away.
**C9 — D1's per-Offer enqueue can produce zero jobs.** `Offer` is real (`schema.prisma:2281-2325`, unique on `[workspaceId, channelListingId, fulfillmentMethod]`, `sku` non-null) but nothing guarantees a coordinate has Offer rows. "Enqueue one job per active Offer" silently enqueues nothing where today it enqueues one. State the ladder: **active non-FBA `Offer.sku` rows if any; else `Product.sku`; else refuse `AMAZON_DELIST_NO_SKU` as a reported row, never a dropped one.** Add a zero-Offer test arm — a fixture that happens to have Offers cannot distinguish "resolved from Offer" from "resolved at all".
**C10 — D11 makes a CONFIRMED critical worse for Etsy, and the proposal does not say so.** Dropping the `listingStatus` clause widens the preflight list; the cascade still filters to `SUPPORTED = ['AMAZON','EBAY','SHOPIFY','WOOCOMMERCE']` (`:1873`) because `SyncChannel` (`schema.prisma:57-66`) has **no ETSY**. The audit's `ch:etsy` critical is precisely "hard delete counts the Etsy listing in the warning, then silently skips it". D11 raises the count and leaves the skip. **Required companion, in the same change:** each preflight row gains `channelRemovable: boolean`, the delete response returns `channelSkipped: [{ channel, marketplace, externalListingId }]`, and the confirmation names which listings stay live. Without it D11 ships a bigger lie than the one it fixes.
**C11 — The queue payload is not a tombstone; retention eats it.** D5's after-commit count is a genuinely good production control and I keep it. But `ads-retention.job.ts:39,41,115-124` deletes settled queue rows at **30 days** and failed rows at **90**, and the janitor CANCELs stale PENDING at **7**. After a successful hard delete the only surviving record of the ASIN or ItemID removed is gone within a month. `sc:permanent-delete`'s "a permanent removal captures nothing" is therefore **not** closed by D5. **Precondition: write one `ProductEvent` per delist outcome** — `ProductEvent` is already FK-free by design (`schema.prisma:15475-15478`), already declares `aggregateType 'ChannelListing'`, and is already read by the audit surface. The same write also closes `sc:permissions-audit`'s "per-market and per-channel removal writes no audit row and no product event at all". No new table.
**C12 — The actor is the literal string `'default-user'`.** `products-catalog.routes.ts:82-84` returns it unconditionally; `const actor = userIdFor(request)` at `:1797` is that string. Adding an authorisation guard while the audit row still says `default-user` secures the door and forges the register. The guard and a real actor land together or neither is worth landing.
**C13 — `holdUntil = +5 min` is a delay, not a grace window, until something can cancel.** The worker honours `CANCELLED` (`bullmq-sync.worker.ts:117-125`) and the drain honours `holdUntil` (`outbound-sync.service.ts:706-712`), so a cancel is implementable — but no route sets it, and the Product is already destroyed, so there is nothing to undo *to*. Either ship `POST /api/products/delist-cascade/cancel` (sets `syncStatus:'CANCELLED'` on the surviving ids the delete response returned) in the same change, or describe the five minutes honestly as a delay. Do not put an Undo in a Toast that nothing implements — the `Toast.message` ReactNode slot the proposal cites is real (`Toast.tsx:9,17`), which is exactly why an unimplemented Undo would look shippable.
**C14 — The `Schema: NONE` label is correct; I verified it.** `productId String?` at `schema.prisma:6824` and `channelListingId String?` at `:6827`, both `onDelete: Cascade` (`:6825`, `:6828`) — the proposal cited `:6822`/`:6825`. "At least one must be set" (`:6823`) is a **comment**; I found no CHECK constraint in any of the six migrations that touch OutboundSyncQueue. Writing both null is legal and needs no migration. Two consequences the proposal did not state: `OutboundSyncService.processSingle` does `include: { product: true }` and the standard handlers assume `item.product`, which is safe only because the lifecycle branch (C5) refuses first; and `outbound-queue.routes.ts:245` selects `productId` on a requeue path that will now see nulls.
**C15 — Untouchable-rule collision, for the Owner.** D12 changes `removeAmazonListing`'s order of operations and result shape and hoists guards inside `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts`. Both are the backend of the flat-file editors, which the standing untouchable rule covers. The proposal's line "the flat-file editor is untouchable and needs no change" is true of the UI and false of these services. Owner ratification needed before D12.
---
## What I re-verified and am keeping unchanged
Every one of these I opened with `/usr/bin/grep` and `sed`.
**D1 is the strongest finding in the document and its premise is solid.** `channel-delist.service.ts:139-141` reads `// externalListingId for Amazon is typically the SKU` then `const sku = job.externalListingId!`. The column is the ASIN: `flat-file-pull.service.ts` writes `externalListingId: asin` beside `platformProductId`, and `submission.service.ts:1465-1489` writes `externalListingId: asin, platformProductId: asin` on both the parent and every child upsert. `schema.prisma:1531-1533` documents it as "Amazon: Parent ASIN … or child ASIN". The canonical seller SKU is `Product.sku`, used by `marketplaces.routes.ts:1061-1063` and by `amazon-market-offer.service.ts` via `product: { select: { sku: true } }`. The fix and its two-different-strings fixture stand as written.
**D2** — `channel-delist.service.ts:193-195` passes `{ product: { … } }` into `updateProduct`, which wraps again at `shopify.service.ts:387-391`; `makeRequest` throws only on `!response.ok` (`:139-147`). `ShopifyService` is pinned to `apiVersion = "2024-01"` (`:79`) on `SHOPIFY_SHOP_NAME`/`ACCESS_TOKEN` (`:80-89`), while `admin-client.ts:13-22` enforces `assertWritable`, `getShopifyPublishMode() !== 'live'`, a publish token and API `2026-07`. The delist genuinely is the one Shopify write outside the gate. `updateProduct` exists at `:382`, so the duck-typed fallback is dead code. The read-back arm — accept a 200 that left `status: ACTIVE` and assert the adapter reports failure — is the right control and I keep it.
**D3** — `SITE_ID_BY_MARKET` (`ebay-trading-api.service.ts:10-16`) has UK and no GB; `:18-22` throws. `flat-file-unified.routes.ts:581` writes `region = mp === 'UK' ? 'GB' : mp` while the same block passes `marketplace: mp` (`'UK'`) to `writeListingContent` and `marketplace: region` (`'GB'`) to `createIfMissing` — so **both spellings exist in ChannelListing** and the map must gain `GB: '3'` while keeping `UK`. The throw sits inside the auth `try` at `:262-282`, so the row dies as `EBAY_DELIST_AUTH_ERROR`, a wrong diagnosis that sends an operator to re-link an account. One correction to the gate: the proposal's "≥ 30 normalisation sites (34 today)" is a hardcoded denominator. My own regexes return 51 and 78 depending on the pattern, which proves the 34 is regex-specific and will not reproduce. The gate must print its own denominator and fail on *zero* anchors, not on a magic number.
**D4** — patterns, `isAlreadyEndedError`, the `{success:true}` at `:288-296` and the SUCCESS write at `:317-324` are all as described, as is `tryResolveConnection({ channel: 'EBAY', primary: true })` at `:263` against the derived `tryResolveConnection({ itemId, marketplace })` the sibling uses at `ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:178`. The three tests that assert the defect as intended exist exactly where claimed and pass today: `:183` ("Item cannot be accessed" → success), `:216` ("Invalid item" → success), `:262` (IT 101 default). Rewrite, do not delete, and record the reversal in `docs/pes-claims.md` — newest at TOP, never appended at the bottom.
**D5** — `createManyAndReturn` at `:1878-1898` sets `productId: l.productId, channelListingId: l.id`; `tx.product.deleteMany` at `:1971`; both FKs cascade; `fireOutboundJobs` fires post-commit at `:2001-2003` against ids that no longer exist; `channelCascadeEnqueued` is a pre-commit create count. No `holdUntil` is set today. The route's own comment at `:1845-1850` asserts the opposite of what the code does.
**D6** — `bullmq-sync.worker.ts:88` destructures `targetChannel` from `job.data` and passes it at `:146`; `fireOutboundJobs` sends `{ queueId, productId, syncType, source }` (`outbound-enqueue.ts:52-57`) and is the only producer of `'sync-job'`. `targetChannel` is a required `SyncChannel` on the row (`schema.prisma:6835`), so reading it from `queueRecord` is a one-line fix. Unreported and correct.
**D7** — `deleteListingsItem` reads `process.env.AMAZON_PUBLISH_MODE` directly at `amazon-sp-api.client.ts:1143`; `:519`, `:669`, `:792` and `:884` all call `getAmazonPublishMode()`, which returns `'gated'` unless `NEXUS_ENABLE_AMAZON_PUBLISH` is set (`amazon-publish-gate.service.ts:35-54`). The master switch gates every Amazon write except the destructive one — and, per C3, that write is reachable today from an unauthenticated route. This is the first commit.
**D8, D10, D11, D12** — `dryRun` set at `:174` and read by nobody while both standard paths do the opposite (`bullmq-sync.worker.ts:317`, `outbound-sync.service.ts:750`); `callTradingApi:235-246` returns `{ack:'Success', itemId:'DRYRUN-…'}` outside production; `patchPurchasableOffer` with `op` genuinely exists at `amazon-sp-api.client.ts:772-790`, so refusing `unpublish` until it is wired is a refusal with a known destination; both status filters are at `:1679-1683` and `:1857-1861`; `listingRisk.vitest.test.ts:10-31` pins "the external id decides, never the status" as intended, so D11 agrees with a test rather than fighting one; GUARD 1 (`:686`) and GUARD 2 (`:695-722`) and `tryRemoveVariationFromListing` (`:169-203`) are all where claimed, and the guard-at-enqueue design consequence is correct — after the cascade the rows are gone and the guard cannot re-derive itself.
## Rulings
Nothing here contradicts CH.1, D9, the one-Customize-dialog rule, or "extend, don't add pages": no component is added, changed or mounted, and no page is created. The DS gap already filed at `.claude/DS-GAPS.md:330-348` ("`Menu` cannot express a destructive item… no `.nds-menu` danger rule in any stylesheet") is untouched and, per C4 and C10, becomes more urgent rather than less — but it is not this wave's to close.

#### Say what it does — the honest-copy pass, corrected

A copy-only pass over five lying surfaces in and around the Product Edit Studio, corrected against the working tree. Twenty-eight claims in the submitted draft were wrong, and three of its replacement strings were new lies — the Shopify "unpublish" branch is a no-op reported as success, "activate" does enqueue a channel push, and the studio's offer verb refuses live rows outright rather than marking them. Two groups are struck: the eBay flat-file dialog (a hard no-touch zone) and a refusal that points at a legacy page (D9). One code change is added and is load-bearing: today the studio's live-row refusal never reaches the operator, because `runAction` rejects the impact first and prints a DS bug string instead.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- MISSING — a listingStatusMeta table. Three surfaces print ChannelListing.listingStatus raw (ListingsPane.tsx:103, AliasBandCell.tsx:132, and the flat-file grids) while the column is a free String @default("DRAFT") at schema.prisma:1616 whose comment declares five values. ADD design-system/grid/renderers/listingStatus.ts exporting listingStatusMeta(raw) -> {label,tone,hint}, tones READ from readinessMeta, readiness.ts:83-85's fallback verbatim, deep-imported per projection.ts:42-47.
- CORRECTION to that gap, measured: there is NO shared constant to derive the member list from — /usr/bin/grep -rn "LISTING_STATUS|ListingStatus" packages/shared/src returns nothing. The writers emit DRAFT ACTIVE INACTIVE ENDED ERROR FAILED LIVE PENDING SUPPRESSED and, case-inconsistently, 'Active', 'active' and 'Completed'. So listingStatusMeta MUST normalise case before lookup or 'Active' renders as an unrecognised token, and the member list is a set claim that goes stale — the fallback carries the weight, and a test must re-derive the list from the writers rather than trusting a hand-typed array. I did not find 'REMOVED' anywhere.
- MISSING — ProjectionFacts.note has no tone. .nds-projcell-note is --nds-text-muted at font-size-xs (grid.css:1386-1392) and an excluded row is muted again by .nds-row-is-quiet, so 'still on sale' — the most alarming fact the cell can carry — renders as the quietest ink on the row. ADD an optional noteTone?: ReadinessTone read through readinessMeta, defaulting to today's muted. Until it exists this pass duplicates the sentence into `title`, which is hover-only and therefore not a fix.
- MISSING, and the reason Group D can only half-close the 'resolve' finding: ActionImpact.unavailable is a bare string (registry.ts:120), so a refusal cannot name the control that resolves it. Every honest refusal describes a control in prose and hopes it is findable. The addition, when Presence lands, is an optional {label, actionId} that ActionConfirm's footer renders as a secondary Button. NOT proposed here — a capability, not copy.
- DEFECT in the DS, not a gap, and it is correction 9: registry.ts:270-272 treats `unavailable` on any level but 'none' as a validation problem, and runAction.ts:70-73 refuses on problems BEFORE rule 4 reads the message. A lane that writes an honest refusal beside level:'confirm' gets it silently replaced by the internal string 'preflight failed but still asked for a confirmation — a verb must not run on a guess'. Two live call sites do exactly this (channelActions.ts:224/250 and :339/:343). Either the lane sets level:'none' (what this pass does) or validateImpact should say so in a way a lane cannot miss.
- STALE ENTRY — .claude/DS-GAPS.md ~:329-348 ('Menu cannot express a destructive item, or explain a disabled one') records MenuItemDef as {id,label,icon,disabled,onSelect,separator}. Measured today it has ten fields: title (:26) and description (:32-48) both landed, so half that entry is wrong. The destructive half still stands. The file is append-only (scripts/check-ds-gaps-append-only.mjs), so append a correction rather than editing in place. Not needed by this pass.

##### API
- GET /api/products/hard-delete-preflight (products-catalog.routes.ts:1666) — drop the listingStatus filter at :1681 so the count means 'holds a marketplace id', the definition listingRisk.ts:41-49 records as the measured-correct one, and add a route-computed per-row `willAttempt` boolean derived from the cascade's own SUPPORTED constant and status filter (:1857, :1875). Read-only, additive field, only caller is BulkActionBar.tsx:1104. RECOMMENDED but separable — see openQuestions.
- apps/api/src/services/pim/family-projection.service.ts — export one excludedReason() helper and call it from BOTH :551-552 and :1220. Response shape unchanged; only the reason string's content changes. No new endpoint.
- NO new route, NO new field on any existing response beyond `willAttempt`, NO change to any writer, NO change to any adapter. Specifically not touched: marketplaces.routes.ts:316-329 (the offer-availability upsert, including its hardcoded aliasKey:'' — that is Presence's P8), listing-activation-sync.service.ts (which this pass only DESCRIBES), and channel-delist.service.ts in its entirety.

##### Schema
- NONE. This pass adds, removes and alters no Prisma field, model, index or migration. The claim is verified rather than assumed: nothing below writes to schema.prisma, and the one column the pass reasons about — ChannelListing.listingStatus, schema.prisma:1616, `String @default("DRAFT") // DRAFT, ACTIVE, INACTIVE, ENDED, ERROR` — is read only.
- NON-STRING CHANGES, all five, so a reviewer can count them: (1) products-catalog.routes.ts:1681 — drop `listingStatus: { in: ['ACTIVE','INACTIVE'] }` from the hard-delete PREFLIGHT read only, and add a route-computed `willAttempt` boolean per row using the SAME two filters the cascade uses at :1857 and :1875 (listingStatus in ACTIVE/INACTIVE AND channel in SUPPORTED, WOOCOMMERCE included). The client must NOT re-derive it — re-deriving an equivalent predicate one line away is the banked write-predicate trap. Read-only GET, no shape break, more rows and one added field. The CASCADE's filter at :1857 is deliberately NOT changed here; that is Presence's precondition P9.
- (2) family-projection.service.ts — one exported excludedReason(hasRow, childExternalId, parentExternalId, coordinateLabel) helper replacing the inline literals at :551-552 and :1220. Response shape unchanged; only the `reason` string's content changes. Both producers must call it or the two-column-builders-drift trap fires.
- (3) familyActions.ts:388 — branch one sideEffect sentence on copyFromProductId, already in scope at :375. No new data.
- (4) AliasBandCell.tsx:132 — `?? 'NOT LISTED'` becomes `?? null`, AND bandTitle's signature at :66-67 changes from `status: string` to `status: string | null` with its `Listing ${status.toLowerCase()}` clause guarded. This is two lines, not one; `.toLowerCase()` on null throws.
- (5) channelActions.ts:224 and :339 — set `level: 'none'` whenever `unavailable` is set. NOT cosmetic: registry.ts:270-272 plus runAction.ts:70-73 mean today's live-row refusal is replaced by the DS's internal bug string before the lane's sentence is ever read. Without this, every Group B string on the live path is unreachable code.
- NOT PROPOSED — putting offerClosedAt or syncPaused on the studio wire. That needs LISTING_SELECT (studio-sheet.service.ts:472) and the SheetListing type to change; it is a contract change and belongs to Presence.
- NOT PROPOSED — narrowing ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:645's deleteMany or its pre-read at :636-639 to the full five-level coordinate. Narrowing the delete alone is strictly worse than today, both must move together, and the file is in the untouchable zone. Presence's precondition P8.
- NOT PROPOSED — fixing shopify.service.ts:383-392's double wrap or channel-delist.service.ts:186-196's duck-typed fallback. Both are real and both are Presence's precondition P7. This pass only stops the modal claiming the broken path works.

##### Open for you
- D9 AND THE DEAD-END REFUSAL. docs/pes-claims.md:17821 says a link to a legacy page is a parity defect, not a parity feature, and :11145 says the flat-file editors stay 'untouchable and unlinked'. The Delete-child refusal is therefore honest and terminal: it names no path. May a refusal NAME a legacy control in prose (no hyperlink) when the studio has no equivalent — or does D9's 'unlinked' cover the sentence as well as the anchor? I have written it the strict way; the Owner may want the other.
- THE FLAT-FILE EDIT LIST (Group G). The two disclosure sentences for the eBay removal dialog — that the delete takes every account's and every alias's row for that market (ebay-flat-file-delete.service.ts:636-639 and :645-647, no channelConnectionId, no aliasKey) and that eight Cascade relations go with it — are true, cheap and currently unstated. They also touch a hard no-touch file. Does the Owner want them tabled as a per-line edit list for a separate yes, in the 2026-08-19 shape, or left alone until Presence's precondition P8 narrows the scope for real?
- THE SHOPIFY UNPUBLISH BRANCH. It sends a malformed body, does nothing, and reports success. Copy can disclose it; copy cannot make it safe. Does the Owner want the Unpublish radio option DISABLED for products with a Shopify listing until the wrapping is fixed — a behaviour change outside this pass's warrant — or shipped with the sentence and fixed under Presence?
- IT.JSON. Three keys must be retranslated into Italian in the same commit, and I cannot write them. Options: (a) hold the whole pass for a translator; (b) ship English at the Italian keys and mark them for translation; (c) delete the three Italian keys so the runtime falls back to English visibly. (c) is the most honest and the ugliest. Which?
- THE PREFLIGHT NUMBER. Widening the read at products-catalog.routes.ts:1681 makes the warning count larger and truer, and it will look like a regression. Does the Owner want both numbers shown ('11 hold a marketplace id; Nexus will try to reach 7'), only the true one, or the preflight left alone so this pass stays strings-only?
- THE AUTOMATION PANEL. I struck the 'Paused' -> 'Automation off' rename because the label is accurate and the surface is out of the pass's warrant. If the Owner's objection is that 'pause' now means three different things across the product, that is a vocabulary programme, not a copy pass — should it be filed as one?
- CH.1 IS NOT STRAINED BY THIS PASS AND I AM NOT ASKING FOR AN AMENDMENT. Two overflow items are renamed; none is added; no destructive verb enters a menu. The MenuItemDef tone gap is real (0 occurrences of `danger` in menuAdapters.tsx against a control of 5 for `label`; no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet) but it binds Presence, not this. Recording it here only so the Owner sees that this pass was checked against CH.1 and cleared it.

##### Risks
- The pass makes the /products hard-delete modal MORE frightening while changing nothing about what it does. An operator who reads 'Shopify: nothing effective is sent — the store keeps selling it' and deletes anyway has been warned, not protected. Honest copy over a broken adapter is a mitigation with a short half-life: if Presence's preconditions are not approved soon, the correct next step is to disable the Unpublish option's Shopify branch, not to keep describing it.
- Correction 9 is a behaviour change dressed as a copy fix. Setting level:'none' where `unavailable` is set means the confirmation dialog stops appearing on the live-row path and the operator gets a refusal toast instead. That is what already happens (with a worse string), but any screenshot test or vitest asserting a dialog opens on that path will change, and I have not run the full _studio suite to find them.
- The excluded-variant note relies on ProjectionFacts.note, which renders muted inside an already-muted row. Until noteTone exists, the loudest sentence in Group C is the quietest pixel on screen — a reviewer could reasonably call that not shipped.
- Group A's honesty depends on delistShopify not being fixed underneath it. If someone repairs the double-wrap (a one-line change), the copy becomes wrong in the opposite direction within a day. The strings should carry a code comment naming channel-delist.service.ts:186-196 and shopify.service.ts:383-392 so the next editor sees the coupling.
- listingStatusMeta's member list is a set claim. Nine values plus three case variants is what I measured today with /usr/bin/grep; a writer added tomorrow makes the table silently incomplete. The fallback keeps it honest, but the tone of an unrecognised value is neutral — a SUPPRESSED-class value added later will render calm.
- it.json is a real translation surface and I cannot write Italian marketplace copy. Shipping English replacements beside untouched Italian lies is worse than shipping neither. This is the item most likely to split the commit.
- The preflight widening (dropping the listingStatus filter at products-catalog.routes.ts:1681) changes a number an operator has been reading for months, upward, on a modal they use under time pressure. The count going from 'Live on 3' to '11 channel listings still hold a marketplace id' will read as a regression until someone explains it.
- Group D's refusal now says 'this studio has no verb that ends those listings' and, under D9, declines to say where one is. That is honest and unhelpful. The operator is left to find the eBay flat-file page on their own — which they will, so the ruling buys nothing except that the studio does not endorse it.

##### The specification
# Say what it does — the honest-copy pass, corrected
## CORRECTIONS
Everything below was re-derived with `/usr/bin/grep`, `sed` and three gate runs. Twenty-eight items changed.
**Struck from the pass entirely**
1. **Group G (the eBay flat-file removal dialog) is STRUCK.** `apps/web/src/app/products/ebay-flat-file/EbayFlatFileClient.tsx` sits inside the Owner's hard no-touch zone (memory `feedback_flat_file_untouchable`: "zero changes without explicit per-change approval"; the single sanctioned precedent, 2026-08-19, required a per-line written edit list and its own yes). `docs/pes-claims.md:11145` restates it: *"The old flat-file editors stay untouchable and unlinked (D9)."* Group G moves to an appendix, tabled as an edit list for a separate approval, and this pass does not depend on it.
2. **Group D's eBay pointer is STRUCK.** `docs/pes-claims.md:17821` records D9's rule verbatim: *"no lane restores a link-out to an old surface for parity's sake — a link to a legacy page is a parity defect, not a parity feature."* "On eBay, the flat-file page's Action column has 'end'" is that link-out written in prose. Replaced with a channel-side instruction; the question is now an openQuestion, not a decision I made.
3. **AutomationPanel (Group F tail) is STRUCK.** `mode === 'PAUSED'` genuinely means the five-minute loop does not run (`AutomationPanel.tsx:12-14, 22-23`); "Paused" is accurate. Renaming an accurate label is vocabulary hygiene, and it drags a Shopify workspace surface into a pass whose only warrant is "the code says the opposite."
**New lies in the replacement copy (the worst class of error here)**
4. **"Shopify: set to Draft where the connector supports it, otherwise the product is deleted" is FALSE.** `channel-delist.service.ts:186-196` calls `svcAny.updateProduct(productId, { product: {…} })`; `shopify.service.ts:383-392` then wraps *again* — `{ product: updates }` — so the wire body is `{product:{product:{id,status:'draft'}}}`, Shopify ignores it, and `delistShopify` returns `{ success: true }`. `updateProduct` exists, so the delete fall-through the draft describes **never runs**. The Unpublish option's Shopify branch is a no-op recorded as done. Rewritten.
5. **"Activate: nothing is sent to {channel}" is FALSE.** `marketplaces.routes.ts:335` fires `syncActivatedListings`, which enqueues a `QUANTITY_UPDATE` on the instant lane for AMAZON, EBAY and SHOPIFY (`listing-activation-sync.service.ts:24, 88-108, 542-556`). Rewritten.
6. **"{n} of these hold a channel id. This mark does not reach the channel, so nothing buyers see changes" is FALSE.** The verb refuses those rows outright: `liveAliasKeys` (`channelActions.ts:120-121`) is *any alias holding an externalListingId*, and `run` returns `{ok:false}` at `:258-261`. Nothing is marked. Rewritten.
7. **"Nexus has no verb that ends those listings" is FALSE.** The `/products` hard-delete modal ends listings on Amazon, eBay and Shopify today (`products-catalog.routes.ts:1856-1900` → `channel-delist.service.ts:105-117`), and the eBay flat-file `Action` column ships `deactivate`/`end` (`apps/web/src/app/products/ebay-flat-file/ebay-columns.ts:159` — a **web** path; the draft cited an API path that does not exist). Narrowed to "this studio".
8. **Dropping "preview-only" at `:344`/`:351` deletes the only true words there.** `broadcastToListings.run` returns `{ok:false, message:'Not sent…'}` on **every** path (`channelActions.ts:355-358`), not only live rows. It is wholly inert behind a type-to-confirm. Copy must say so.
**One required code change the draft missed, without which Group B changes nothing**
9. **The live-row refusal never reaches the operator today.** `registry.ts:270-272` makes `unavailable` on any level but `'none'` a validation *problem*; `runAction.ts:70-73` refuses on problems **before** rule 4 reads `impact.unavailable`. So the operator sees the DS's internal string — *"preflight failed but still asked for a confirmation — a verb must not run on a guess"* — instead of the sentence at `channelActions.ts:250`. Same shape at `:339/:343`. Fix: `level: 'none'` whenever `unavailable` is set. This is a fifth non-string change and it is a precondition for Group B.
**Wrong claims about current behaviour**
10. **`it.json` is a translation, not a copy.** `it.json:5221-5227` carries Italian at the same keys. "carries all three verbatim" is wrong.
11. **The hard-delete cascade reaches WooCommerce.** `products-catalog.routes.ts:1875` `SUPPORTED = ['AMAZON','EBAY','SHOPIFY','WOOCOMMERCE']`, and `channel-delist.service.ts:112-117` returns a non-retryable failure for it — the store keeps selling while the local record is deleted. One containment sentence added, labelled as containment.
12. **`check-wire-null-defaults` cannot be re-baselined by this pass.** Measured today: **28** against baseline **17**, all 28 inside `_studio/`. Removing two leaves 26 — still red. `--baseline` would *raise* the floor and silence nine offenders that are not ours. Struck.
13. **Two of three named gates reject `--check`.** `check-button-vocabulary.mjs --check` and `check-silent-disabled.mjs --check` both print *"scan root --check does not exist — the check would pass vacuously"*. Correct form is a scan root. Measured: `check-silent-disabled "apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio"` = 19 sites, below the 27 baseline (green).
14. **`check-grid-kit-ratchet` is 34 vs 32, not 33/32** — two importers over.
15. **`AliasBandCell.tsx:132 → ?? null` is not one line.** `status` feeds `bandTitle(s, label, status: string, …)` at `:66-67`, which calls `status.toLowerCase()`. Null breaks the type and throws.
16. **No shared listingStatus constant exists.** `/usr/bin/grep -rn "LISTING_STATUS|ListingStatus" packages/shared/src` returns nothing. The column is `String @default("DRAFT")` with a five-value comment at `schema.prisma:1616` (not :1615). Measured writers emit `DRAFT ACTIVE INACTIVE ENDED ERROR FAILED LIVE PENDING SUPPRESSED` **plus case variants `'Active'`, `'active'`, `'Completed'`** — so the meta must normalise case, or 'Active' renders as an unrecognised token. I did not find `REMOVED`.
17. **"Nothing has been sent" for an excluded variant overstates.** `cascadeQuantityToListings` (`stock-movement.service.ts:694-713`) selects `where:{productId}` with no filter and gates only through `resolveIntendedQuantity` on syncPaused / offerClosed / FBA — never on `isPublished` or `variationExcluded`. Excluded rows keep producing quantity pushes.
18. **`ProjectionFacts.note` is muted by construction.** `.nds-projcell-note` is `--nds-text-muted` at `font-size-xs` (`grid.css:1386-1392`), and an excluded row also carries `.nds-row-is-quiet`. "Still on sale" would paint the loudest fact in the quietest ink. Kept, with the real DS gap filed.
19. **Group F must key on `reviewPath`, not on `channel`.** `ChannelSheet.tsx:1696` derives `reviewPath` from `nativeReviewRow?.shopify && accountId`; the modal branches on it (`:1848, :1851`) while the menu labels branch on `channel` (`:1721, :1726, :1728`). A Shopify alias with no native review row is *already* labelled "Review synchronization" and then opens the send-nothing control. Keying `reviewCopy.ts` on channel would harden that.
20. **`familyActions.ts:454` refuses on two conditions** — `live || listings.some(l => l.isPublished)` — so one sentence is false on the isPublished-only arm. Branched.
21. **The excluded-reason helper needs a fourth input.** Producer 1's no-row branch (`family-projection.service.ts:1213-1216`) ends "Tick it to create one as a draft"; the draft's "neither" branch dropped that guidance.
**Line-number corrections (all measured)**
22. `listingRisk.ts` is at `_studio/sheet/master/`; the "never published" string is at **:63** (:62 is the live branch); its test pin is at **:43**, not :42.
23. Group F: disabled label **:1721**, enabled label **:1726**, description **:1728** (modal title :1847 and subtitle :1848 were right).
24. ListingsPane: status `:103` ✓; Offer `dt` **:121**, 'selling' **:126**, title **:128**, 'paused' **:129**.
25. `ActionImpact.unavailable` is at `registry.ts:120`; `skip_offer` at `apps/api/src/services/amazon/flat-file.service.ts:3411-3420`.
26. `en.json` 5222/5223/5225/5232/5233 ✓ verified exactly. `channelAction.delete.label` (**en 5224**, "Delete on channel") was left unrenamed while its submit button changed — added.
27. `docs/CONTENT.md` absent ✓, stale `ChannelSheet` numbers ✓, two excluded producers ✓, TERM-6 refutation ✓ — all four of the draft's own corrections re-verified and stand.
28. **The preflight-widening must not be re-derived client-side.** The cascade's filter is `listingStatus ∈ {ACTIVE,INACTIVE}` **and** `channel ∈ SUPPORTED`. Having `BulkActionBar` re-compute "attempted" from a `listingStatus` field is the banked write-predicate trap. The route returns the flag instead.
---
## 0. Scope
Strings, plus **five** one-line read/derivation changes. Nothing reaches a marketplace, adds a verb, a button or a page. CH.1 and D9 hold: two overflow items are renamed, none added. Lands as one unit — three of these sentences currently lie in each other's favour. Group G lands separately or not at all.
## 1. Group A — the hard-delete modal
| key | replacement |
|---|---|
| `channelAction.unpublish.label` (en 5222) | `End the listing on each channel` |
| `…unpublish.body` (5223) | `Nexus asks each channel to stop selling, then deletes the local record either way. Amazon: the seller offer is deleted; the ASIN survives. eBay: the listing is ended; a relist gets a new item number. Shopify: nothing effective is sent — the store keeps selling it. WooCommerce and Etsy: nothing is sent. There is no relist from here.` |
| `…delete.label` (5224) | `Remove the listing from each channel` |
| `…delete.body` (5225) | `Nexus asks each channel to remove the listing, then deletes the local record either way. Amazon: the seller offer is deleted; the ASIN survives. eBay: the listing is ended — the same call as the option above. Shopify: the product is deleted. WooCommerce and Etsy: nothing is sent.` |
| new `…channelAction.note` | `These two options differ only on Shopify, where the first sends nothing that works and the second deletes the product. On Amazon and eBay both end the listing. On WooCommerce and Etsy neither sends anything, and the local record is deleted anyway — the store keeps selling.` |
| `submit.unpublish` (5232) | `Delete locally and end on channel` |
| `submit.delete` (5233) | `Delete locally and remove from channel` |
The WooCommerce clause is **containment** under the 2026-09-12 instruction: it discloses a live risk, it does not build the channel. `it.json` must be translated at the same keys in the same commit; if no translator is available, say so and let the keys fall back to English rather than shipping a correct English sentence beside a stale Italian lie.
Two code lines: delete the "safer non-destructive default" comment at `BulkActionBar.tsx:1116`, and widen the preflight read (§7).
## 2. Group B — the studio's "Pause offer"
`_studio/sheet/channel/channelActions.ts`. **Correction 9 first**: set `level:'none'` whenever `unavailable` is set (`:224` and `:339`), or none of this reaches the screen.
- `:177` `'Activate' | 'Pause'` → `'Mark active' | 'Mark paused'`; `:190, :201, :208, :212` follow.
- `:226` title → `Mark the offer paused for {n} SKUs on {scope}?`
- `:229-230` consequences, from a per-channel table in this file:
  - **Amazon, pause** — `Recorded as paused in Nexus. Amazon is told at the next Amazon flat-file publish, which suppresses the offer.`
  - **Amazon, activate** — `The pause mark is cleared, and Nexus queues this SKU's stock to Amazon straight away.`
  - **eBay / Shopify, pause** — `Recorded as paused in Nexus. Nothing is sent to {channel} — the offer keeps selling there until you end it on the channel itself.`
  - **eBay / Shopify, activate** — `The pause mark is cleared, and Nexus queues this SKU's stock to {channel} straight away.`
  - **Etsy, either** — `Recorded as paused in Nexus. Nothing is ever sent to Etsy from here.` (Etsy is not in `VALID_CHANNELS`, `listing-activation-sync.service.ts:18`.)
- `:237` → `Refused: {n} of these belong to a listing that holds a channel id, and this verb cannot touch those. Nothing is marked.`
- `:238` (the no-live branch) → `None of these hold a channel id. A listing record is created on this coordinate for any row that has none.`
- `:250` → `Refused on {n} rows whose listing holds a channel id on {channel}. This verb only writes a Nexus record, and this studio has no verb that can carry it to {channel}.`
- `:344`, `:351` (broadcast) → `Broadcast is not built. Nothing is sent on any row, live or not.`
## 3. Group C — the excluded variant
One exported helper in `family-projection.service.ts`, called from `:551-552` and `:1220`, taking four inputs (`hasRow`, `childExternalId`, `parentExternalId`, `coordinateLabel`):
- child id → `Excluded here. {childId} still offers this variant on {label} until the listing is revised. No listing change has been sent, and stock updates for this record still go out.`
- no child id, parent has one → same with `{parentId}`.
- row exists, no id → `Excluded from this listing. Nothing was ever sent for this variant.`
- **no row at all** → `No listing record on this coordinate. Tick it to create one as a draft.` (preserves producer 1's guidance).
`projectionMeta('excluded').hint` → `Left out of this channel's listing in Nexus. Ticking or unticking sends nothing to the channel.` The five spec §9 words are untouched (`projection.vitest.test.ts:17-25` pins them). Set `note: 'still on sale'` on the Listing cell when excluded with an id in play, **and** carry the same words in `title`, because the note renders muted inside an already-quiet row (correction 18).

- `familyActions.ts:454`, branched: with a live id → `{sku} still holds a marketplace id on {coordinates}. This studio has no verb that ends those listings — end them on the channel, then reload.`; published-but-no-id → `{sku} is marked as published on {coordinates} with no marketplace id recorded. Check the channel before deleting the local record.`

Gates, with the invocations that actually run: `node scripts/check-wire-null-defaults.mjs --check` (28 → 26; **stays red, do not re-baseline**), `node scripts/check-silent-disabled.mjs "apps/web/src/app/products/[id]/edit/_studio"` (19/27 green), `node scripts/check-button-vocabulary.mjs apps/web/src`, the `_studio` vitest suite.
