The cost is unchanged and is the point: the studio reads emptier. "Pause offer" becomes "Mark offer paused" and mostly refuses; "Unpublish (recommended)" becomes "End the listing on each channel". Operators will ask where the real pause went. It never existed here.

#### Six accessibility defects, fixed in the design system at the AAA bar — corrected

The six confirmed ux:accessibility findings are real and the remedies are structurally right: I reproduced the focus census (56 rules / 40 with an outline / 16 without), every §5 wash and ring figure to two decimals, and the crash in check-grid-swatch-contrast. Eighteen corrections follow. The largest: dropping the `disabled` attribute from Menu.tsx silently kills four `.nds-menu button:disabled` rules and reintroduces the exact defect components.css:735-743 records as fixed; `GridAction.danger` is inert in two of four surfaces, not all four; sorting danger verbs last would create a ruling #110 order divergence and must not ship as an accessibility fix; AG's `icon` takes `Element | string`, not a React node; and the `--nds-success-text` sibling fix is derived against white only — the very defect §4 indicts.

▸ Full specification, design-system gaps and open questions

##### Design-system gaps this needs
- MenuItemDef has no tone, and there is no .nds-menu danger rule in any DS stylesheet (0 hits against a positive control of 18 `danger` matches in components.css). ADD `tone?: Tone` reusing primitives/tone.ts, rendered by extending the existing className hook at Menu.tsx:197, plus `.nds-menu button.tone-danger { color: var(--nds-danger-text) }` — 8.55:1 on the light panel, 8.26 dark, worst 7.44/7.29 on the [data-selected] primary-soft ground after the §4 raise. Closes .claude/DS-GAPS.md:329-344, whose other half (no per-item title) is stale.
- The DS has NO held-item CSS for menus or scope chips. Every held affordance is expressed as `:disabled` (components.css:744/748/753/781; patterns.css:1391/1404), so the moment a component trades the attribute for aria-disabled it loses its entire disabled appearance silently. ADD a paired `[aria-disabled="true"]` selector beside every one of those six rules, the way primitives.css:47-53 already does for .nds-btn. This is the single largest correction in this review.
- MenuItemDef cannot distinguish HELD (refuses, and can say why) from OFF (inert, nothing to say), so a component cannot decide which affordance to render. ADD the rule that `description` is the discriminator — an item with a description is held and stays keyboard-reachable; one without keeps the `disabled` attribute — and document it on the type, so the 14 Menu consumers are not each asked to guess.
- GridAction/ActionImpact carry no reach or reversibility, only `danger?: boolean` — and that bit IS honoured by RecordActions.tsx:65 and FamilySelectionBar.tsx:52 while both menu adapters discard it, so one declaration renders in two different tones across four surfaces ruling #110 says must be identical. The accessibility half is a rendering path for severity; the state-model half is Presence's `reach` and `reversal`. They must land together or a toned menu item promises a distinction the type still cannot make.
- The DS has an AA text ramp and no AAA secondary tier: --nds-text 15.48 · --nds-text-strong 9.87 · --nds-text-2 5.91 · --nds-text-muted 5.32 · --nds-text-3 3.62 (light). Nothing sits between 9.87 and 5.91. ADD --nds-menu-item-desc-fg #464e59 / #b6c0cb now (panel 8.42/8.26, hover 7.63/7.67, selected 7.33/7.29); consider a general --nds-text-2-aaa later. Do NOT raise --nds-text-2 globally: 386 uses, and the dark raise loses contrast on 3 of 16 declared grounds.
- The whole -text tier was derived against white only, which css-vars.ts:227-240 already admits happened three times, and the fix must not repeat it. Measured worst declared grounds: --nds-danger-text 6.27 (danger-soft light) / 6.30 (primary-soft dark) — raise to #8b2a25 / #f2aea7. --nds-success-text 6.50 (danger-soft light) / 6.64 (primary-soft light) / 6.73 (grid-child-bg light) / 6.92 (surface-hover light) — NOT fixed by the proposal and must be raised or explicitly excepted. --nds-warning-text worst 7.12 (grid-pending-bg dark) — clears, no change. Going forward, derive every -text value against the worst DECLARED ground and leave at least 0.15 of headroom over 7:1 so two independent parsers cannot disagree across the line.
- No inline-error text class. `.nds-cell-stock-out` is being used as one in seven places (MasterSheet.tsx:1812, FamilyVariants.tsx:608, FamilyBar.tsx:105, FamilySelectionBar.tsx:47, SheetToolbar.tsx:178, GridViewsMenu.tsx:248, GdsScenarios.tsx:529) alongside its real job as a stock level at InventoryGrid.tsx:184. ADD `.nds-inline-error` in components.css reading --nds-danger-text and repoint all seven.
- No write-state mark. Three of five CellSaveState members reach the operator as a wash under 1.2:1 with no glyph (waiting 1.10/1.09, saving 1.14/1.10, pending 1.17/1.16). ADD CellSaveMark({ state }) beside CellSaveReason in grid/renderers/cells.tsx — but export it from a barrel that is not grid/renderers/index.ts until that file's fork drift is resolved, because cells.tsx is web-only and index.ts is shared with apps/factory.
- ProvenanceMark exports no accessible name at all, on any of its three branches, for any of its EIGHT vocabulary members. ADD role="img" + aria-label from the label MARKS already carries. Keep the .nds-vh fallback on the record in case per-cell announcement proves noisy.
- scripts/check-silent-disabled.mjs never scans the design system (default root apps/web/src/app/marketing/ads/rules-automation at :47; .githooks/pre-push:191 passes no argument), takes ONE positional root, carries ONE global BASELINE of 27, and its exemption is satisfied by the mere presence of aria-disabled even when the disabled attribute stays — which is exactly the shape Menu.tsx:199-200 and ScopeBar.tsx:221-222 both ship. WIDEN and TIGHTEN, in two separate writes with a re-measured baseline between them.
- No guard enforces a visible focus indicator, and scripts/check-contrast.mjs cannot substitute — it hardcodes legacy RGB triples the DS no longer ships and prints "All 24 pairs pass AA" while measuring a token set that no longer exists. ADD scripts/check-focus-visible.mjs (structural, comments stripped, conforming count printed, positive control in the same run) and scripts/check-token-role.mjs (color: var(--nds-danger|warning|success|info) rejected, non-color properties exempt, seeded red before the fix). Wire both into .githooks/pre-push; ds-conformance-guard.mjs cannot host them (ROOT = apps/web/src/app, :29).
- scripts/check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs crashes on every run — it looks for a bare `.dark {` block and tokens.css:450 declares `.dark, .dark body:has(.nds-theme-responsive), .dark body .nds-theme-responsive {`. Verified by running it. Its RED convicts nothing and its silence proves nothing. Fix the selector or retire it before any contrast work is declared gated.

##### Open for you
- CH.1 — GROUPING, not tone. Adding `tone?: Tone` to MenuItemDef adds no button and touches no toolbar, so CH.1 (Owner, 2026-09-05, recorded verbatim at SheetToolbar.tsx:14-20) stands unamended and this proposal does not need it to bend. But the Presence proposal escalated "the overflow gains a typed tone plus a separator group" as an Owner amendment, and the audit's own A11Y-6 fix asks for danger verbs sorted last with a separator. Sorting is a #110 divergence, not an accessibility fix. Does the Owner want (a) tone only, order untouched — my recommendation; (b) tone plus a separator group, implemented inside `actionsFor` so all four surfaces move together and registry.vitest.test.ts:88 is rewritten with the Owner's reason recorded; or (c) neither, and severity stays unrendered in menus?
- THE HELD-ITEM DISCRIMINATOR. I propose `description != null` as what makes a menu item keyboard-reachable-but-refusing, with a reasonless disabled item keeping the `disabled` attribute. That is a DS-wide semantic the 14 Menu consumers inherit without asking for it. Does the Owner accept the discriminator, prefer an unconditional swap (larger blast radius, one rule to remember), or prefer an explicit `held?: boolean` on MenuItemDef (smallest blast radius, but a caller who forgets it gets today's silent dead end)?
- --nds-success-text. It misses 7:1 on four declared grounds (6.50 danger-soft, 6.64 primary-soft, 6.73 grid-child-bg, 6.92 surface-hover — all light). `.nds-cell-stock-ok` lands on the hover and child grounds in every grid. Raising it moves a token used far beyond the studio. Raised in this pass, deferred to a dedicated token pass, or explicitly excepted with the reason written into the token file? Shipping the danger raise while leaving the success one is defensible only if it is stated.
- PROVENANCE VERBOSITY. role="img" + aria-label makes every non-`own` mark announced, on a sheet with up to 100 columns. The audit's own fallback is the CellSaveReason shape — a .nds-vh text node emitted only for non-`own` provenance. I cannot measure the noise without a browser and this run is forbidden one. role="img" now with a browser check afterwards, or the .nds-vh form from the start?
- SCOPEBAR. The disabledReason contract at ScopeBar.tsx:44-51 promises an aria-description the render has never emitted. The remedy is the menu's, but ScopeBar is a SHARED fork file (identical in apps/factory today) with three consumers. Fixed in this pass — which means a mirrored factory write to keep check-ds-fork-drift green — or filed as its own item so this pass touches only web-only and already-drifting files?
- SEQUENCING AGAINST A RED GATE. check-ds-fork-drift is RED on grid/renderers/index.ts before any of this work begins. Resolve that drift first as a precondition, or export CellSaveMark from a temporary web-only barrel so this work does not inherit someone else's red?
- THE DEAD GATE. check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs has crashed on every run, including on the committed tree, so it has never convicted anything. Fixing its selector may turn up swatch failures nobody has seen. Fixed inside this pass (unknown scope), or retired with a note so its silence stops reading as a pass?

##### Risks
- THE BIGGEST ONE: dropping the `disabled` attribute without pairing every `:disabled` rule with an `[aria-disabled="true"]` selector ships a held menu item that looks and behaves exactly like a live one — --nds-text-strong instead of --nds-text-2, cursor: pointer, a live hover, an unfaded icon. Four rules in components.css (744/748/753/781) and two in patterns.css (1391/1404). This is a regression against a defect the DS already fixed once and wrote a nine-line comment about, and it would ship under an accessibility banner.
- Menu has 14 consumers and 776 `disabled:` sites exist repo-wide. An unconditional attribute swap changes every disabled item in the app. Gating the swap on `description != null` bounds it to the held case, but it makes the DS's behaviour depend on whether a caller supplied a reason — a caller who supplies neither gets today's silent grey, and no gate catches that.
- Reordering danger verbs last inside `offered()` would silently split the four surfaces ruling #110 says render one declaration identically: RecordActions.tsx:55, FamilyBar.tsx:86 and FamilySelectionBar.tsx:37 would keep declaration order while the row ⋯ and the right-click menu would not. Reordering inside `actionsFor` instead breaks registry.vitest.test.ts:88, whose stated purpose is order stability. The proposal treats this as a test to update; it is a ruling to escalate.
- Every token value has three to five copies (css-vars.ts, tokens.css, tokens-global.css, shared-shell.css light pins at :47/:72/:453/:473/:572, the ads-console sheet, and the factory fork). --nds-prov-formula-fg's pin at shared-shell.css:572 is written as `var(--nds-cyan-700)`, an alias that happens to equal #0e7490 today — editing the literal alone leaves the console shell on the old value with no gate complaining. Editing the generator alone is a no-op on the shipped page.
- check-ds-fork-drift is RED right now on grid/renderers/index.ts and will block a push. The CellSaveMark export lands in that exact file, and cells.tsx is web-only so the export cannot be mirrored into factory. Either the drift is resolved first or the export goes elsewhere; discovering this at push time costs the lane its window.
- `outline-offset: 1px` on .nds-btn is clipped by any `overflow: hidden` ancestor, because the clip rect is the padding box. The proposal notes the grid case conditionally but never enumerates where .nds-btn actually sits inside such a box. An outline drawn and then clipped is indistinguishable from the defect being fixed.
- role="img" + aria-label on ProvenanceMark adds one announced element per non-`own` cell. On a 100-column sheet a screen-reader user arrowing across a row hears a provenance phrase in most cells. No tab stop is added, but verbosity is a real cost and cannot be measured without a browser, which this work is forbidden from using.
- Three of the proposal's headline figures come out 0.01–0.03 below its stated values under an independent parser. All still clear 7:1, but a future guard implemented from these numbers rather than from source could report a false RED — and a gate's RED is a claim too.
- AG's `icon` is `Element | string`. Passing the React element that works for the DS adapter renders nothing in the AG context menu, silently — the same class of silent omission as an unregistered AG module.
- check-silent-disabled's BASELINE is one global number over one root. Tightening the exemption and adding the DS root in one write makes the resulting count uninterpretable: you cannot tell whether growth came from the new root or the new rule.

##### The specification
# Six accessibility defects, fixed in the design system at the AAA bar — corrected
## CORRECTIONS
Eighteen. Everything not listed here I re-derived independently and it stands.
**C1 — Trading `disabled` for `aria-disabled` in `Menu.tsx` silently kills FOUR CSS rules and reintroduces the defect `components.css:735-743` records as already fixed.** The block has four `:disabled` selectors, not one: `:744` (`color: var(--nds-text-2); cursor: default`), `:748` (hover suppression), `:753` (icon fade to 0.55), `:781` (the description keeps its legible tone). Remove the attribute without adding a matching `[aria-disabled="true"]` selector set and a held item renders at `--nds-text-strong` with `cursor: pointer` and a live hover — visually identical to a runnable verb, and it invites a click. That is strictly worse than today's silent-but-honest grey. **Every one of the four rules must gain `, .nds-menu button[aria-disabled="true"]` in the same write.** The original proposal names only `:744`, and only to say it "takes the same token".
**C2 — There are THREE `:not([disabled])` selectors in `Menu.tsx`, not two.** `:121` (`[data-selected="true"]:not([disabled])`), `:122` (`[role="menuitem"]:not([disabled])`) and `:140` (the arrow/Home/End/typeahead query). The proposal names `:122` and `:140` and warns that they "must move together or they diverge" — while missing the third. `:121` and `:122` both belong to initial focus and both keep `:not([aria-disabled="true"])`; only `:140` drops the filter.
**C3 — Bound the blast radius: swap the attribute only when the item carries a reason.** `Menu` has 14 consumers; the change as written alters every disabled item in the app, including entries that are inert for reasons nobody wrote down. Render `aria-disabled` without `disabled` **only when `it.description != null`** — i.e. only for the held case the DS doctrine is about (`Menu.tsx:33-41`, hub rulings #133/#231). A reasonless disabled item keeps `disabled` and stays skipped, and the four existing `:disabled` rules keep working for it. This also makes the type honest: a held item must explain itself or it is not held, it is off.
**C4 — `ScopeBar` carries the same three consequences plus one the proposal misses.** `.nds-scope:disabled` (`patterns.css:1404`) and `.nds-scope:hover:not(:disabled)` (`:1391`) go dead the same way, and `ScopeBar.tsx:166` filters `items.filter((i) => !i.disabled)` inside `onKeyDown` — a third site beyond the `tabIndex` at `:218` and the `onClick` guard at `:224`. Mitigating: `ScopeBar` has exactly **three** consumers (`LanguageAxisClient.tsx`, `StudioBar.tsx`, `TokenCatalog.tsx`), so this is small and auditable — say so rather than leaving the reviewer to guess.
**C5 — `GridAction.danger` is NOT inert. It is inert in TWO of FOUR surfaces.** `RecordActions.tsx:65` renders `variant={action.danger ? 'danger-outline' : 'secondary'}` and `FamilySelectionBar.tsx:52` renders `variant={action.danger ? 'danger' : 'secondary'}`. The measurement `/usr/bin/grep -c danger menuAdapters.tsx` = 0 is true; the generalisation "which no adapter reads" is a false set claim. Restate the finding as what it actually is, which is sharper: **the same verb wears danger tone in the drawer and the bulk bar and reads neutral in the row `⋯` and the right-click menu — a ruling #110 divergence between four surfaces that are supposed to render one declaration identically.**
**C6 — "Sort danger verbs last" would CREATE a #110 divergence and must not ship as an accessibility fix.** `actionsFor` has four consumers — `RecordActions.tsx:55`, `FamilyBar.tsx:86`, `FamilySelectionBar.tsx:37`, `menuAdapters.tsx:58` — all rendering in declaration order. Sorting inside `offered()` reorders two of them. Sorting inside `actionsFor` breaks `registry.vitest.test.ts:88`, whose stated purpose is order stability. **Ship `tone` and `icon` only; leave order alone.** The `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts` order assertion (`['open','delete','promote','unlink','separator','copy']`, at `:95`, not `:93`) then stays green and nothing needs updating in the same write. Grouping goes to the Owner.
**C7 — AG's `icon` takes `Element | string`, not a React node.** Verified in the installed `ag-grid-community` **36.1.0**: `node_modules/ag-grid-community/dist/types/src/interfaces/menuItem.d.ts:19-22` declares `icon?: Element | string` and `cssClasses?: string[]`. `<Trash2 size={14}/>` is correct for the DS `MenuItemDef.icon` and would render nothing through AG. For `actionContextMenu`, pass `cssClasses: ['nds-ag-menu-danger']` and let a `.ag-menu .nds-ag-menu-danger::before` glyph carry shape, or pass a literal SVG string — never the React element.
**C8 — `MARKS` has EIGHT members, not seven.** `provenanceMark.tsx:17` declares `outdated` (`History`, `.nds-cell-prov-outdated`), which the icon census omits. Measured: `.nds-cell-prov-outdated` reads `--nds-warning-strong` (`grid.css:1431`) at **5.18:1** light / **8.30** dark on the plain cell and **4.91 / 7.70** on the hover ground — it clears the 3:1 non-text bar, so the conclusion survives. A set claim that omits a member does not.
**C9 — the `--nds-success-text` sibling fix is derived against WHITE ONLY, which is the exact defect §4 indicts.** Measured: `--nds-success-text` is 7.63:1 on the plain light surface but **6.92** on `--nds-surface-hover`, **6.73** on `--nds-grid-child-bg`, **6.64** on `--nds-primary-soft` and **6.50** on `--nds-danger-soft`. `.nds-cell-stock-ok` lands on the hover and child grounds in every grid the DS ships. Either raise `--nds-success-text` in the same pass, derived worst-ground-first, or state plainly that `-ok` reaches AA-plus and not AAA and why that is accepted. Do not repeat the white-only derivation while condemning it.
**C10 — three of the proposal's "7.30" figures come out at 7.28 / 7.29 / 7.33 under an independent parser.** All still clear 7:1, and the design holds. But "every figure reproduces to two decimals" does not survive a second implementation: `color-mix` alpha handling and compositing order differ by a hundredth. **Derive every new token value with at least 0.15 of headroom over 7:1** so a rounding disagreement between the author's script and the future guard's cannot flip a gate.
**C11 — `grid/renderers/index.ts` is a SHARED fork file and is the file currently RED.** Measured now: `check-ds-fork-drift` reports 155 shared · 8 differing · 147 identical, with NEW drift on exactly `grid/renderers/index.ts`. `cells.tsx` is web-only, so a `CellSaveMark` export added to the shared barrel widens the red and cannot be mirrored into factory. Resolve the index.ts drift first, or export `CellSaveMark` from a web-only barrel. The rest of the fork classification I verified file by file and it is exactly right.
**C12 — `check-silent-disabled.mjs` takes ONE positional root and ONE global baseline.** `const ROOT = process.argv[2] ?? 'apps/web/src/app/marketing/ads/rules-automation'` (`:47`); `BASELINE = 27`; `.githooks/pre-push:191` passes no argument. "Add the DS as a second scan root" is a script change, not a flag — and tightening the exemption in the same write makes the two effects indistinguishable in the resulting count. Sequence it: (1) tighten the exemption to require the *absence* of `disabled`, re-measure the existing root, land the new baseline; (2) then add the DS root, with its own baseline or its own pre-push invocation.
**C13 — line drift.** MasterSheet's stock-out message is at **:1812** (both the audit and the proposal say 1807). `.nds-prow-action:focus-visible` is at `components.css:2633`. `ActionConfirm`'s Cancel is `:90`. And `components.css:661` (`.nds-drawer-grip:focus-visible::after`, `background: var(--nds-primary)`) is a `::after` delegate, not one of the "four opacity/visibility rules".
**C14 — `outline-offset: 1px` is clipped by any `overflow: hidden` ancestor**, because the clip rect is the padding box. Helpfully, `grid.css:1132` already ships `.nds-btn.xs.nds-grid-sheet-help:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--nds-primary); outline-offset: 1px; }` inside the sheet — so the base rule makes it redundant rather than conflicting. Still, name the obligation: enumerate `.nds-btn` instances sitting inside an `overflow:hidden` box before shipping, and where one is found use `-1px` **with a colour that is not `--nds-primary`**, because at a negative offset the ring composites against the button's own fill (1.00:1 on `.primary`, 1.57 light / 1.22 dark on `.danger`).
**C15 — CH.1: `tone` is clean, GROUPING is the Owner's.** Adding `tone?: Tone` to `MenuItemDef` adds no button, changes no toolbar, and touches nothing CH.1 rules on (`SheetToolbar.tsx:14-20`, verbatim in the file). CH.1 stands as written. But the Presence proposal correctly escalated "the overflow gains a typed tone plus a separator group" as an Owner amendment; shipping the tone half here and leaving grouping unshipped is the right split, and it must be stated as a deliberate split rather than left to look like an oversight.
**C16 — D9, one-Customize-dialog, no page-local hand-rolls: clean.** No new page, no new route, no page-local CSS. Every change lands in `apps/web/src/design-system/` or `scripts/`. `.nds-inline-error` is correctly placed in `components.css` rather than in the consuming files.
**C17 — the refusal to mint `--nds-focus-outline` is correct and I confirmed the refutation.** `/usr/bin/grep -rn "nds-focus-outline" apps/web/src` returns nothing. `--nds-primary` is redefined in `.dark` (`tokens.css:470`) and light-pinned at `shared-shell.css:473` — and again at `:47` and `:72`, which the proposal's pin list misses. The use-site form avoids all three pins. The audit's own proposed fix for A11Y-1 is therefore wrong and this proposal is right to override it; say so explicitly, because a reviewer comparing the two will otherwise read it as a lane ignoring the audit.
**C18 — the DS's own `DS1-18` comment records 4.54:1 for this outline** (`primitives.css:188-189`, five copies). I measure `--nds-primary` on white at **4.79:1**; 4.54 is the raised-surface figure. Both clear 3:1. Do not carry 4.54 forward as though it were the white number — it is exactly the kind of banked value that goes false.
## What I verified, and what stands
I parsed `tokens.css` into its two scopes (`:root` at `:10`; `.dark, .dark body:has(.nds-theme-responsive), .dark body .nds-theme-responsive` at `:450` — **there is no bare `.dark {` block**, confirmed), resolved `var()` chains per theme, composited alpha and `color-mix(in srgb, …)`, and recomputed every ratio independently. I ran the repo's own read-only gates. I did not start a dev server, open the app or call an endpoint.
**The focus census reproduces exactly**: 56 `:focus-visible` rules under `design-system/**/*.css` with comments stripped, **40** declaring a real outline, **16** not, and the ten genuinely glow-only rules are precisely the ten named — `primitives.css:33, :460, :1056, :1557`; `components.css:90, :1800, :1852, :2232`; `grid/workspace/workspace.css:251`; and `a11y.css:19-26`, whose single rule covers five selectors at the same 1.17:1. The composited values reproduce: `--nds-focus-ring` at 12% alpha gives **1.17:1** light and **1.13:1** dark; the replacement gives **4.79** light and **5.59** dark on a 2px perimeter, clearing SC 2.4.13's 3:1 floor in both themes. The five `DS1-18` sweep comments are at `primitives.css:187, :529, :803, :1360, :1482` and `.nds-btn` was indeed left behind by that sweep.
**Every §5 figure reproduces to two decimals**: waiting wash 1.10 light / 1.09 dark, saving 1.14 / 1.10, pending 1.17 / 1.16; `--nds-text` on the waiting wash **14.13 / 11.72** and on the saving wash **13.55 / 11.52**; the warning ring on the waiting wash **3.43 / 3.73** and the primary ring on the saving wash **4.19 / 5.06**. `roundTrip.ts:32` declares five states; `grid.css:564-573` gives rings to three and washes alone to `saving` and `waiting`, three lines above the rule at `:576` that says "a tint AND a corner triangle (never colour alone)". The finding and the remedy both stand. One wording fix: `waiting` and `unknown` are not "byte-identical rules" — `unknown` carries the ring; their *backgrounds* are identical, separation exactly 1.000.
**Gate state, measured now.** `check-grid-swatch-contrast.mjs` throws on every run (`has no block matching /\.dark \{([\s\S]*?)\n\}/`) — dead, so its silence convicts nothing. `check-contrast.mjs` prints "All 24 pairs pass AA" over a legacy vocabulary the DS no longer ships. `check-dark-pin-parity` green at exactly 374 `:root` tokens · 106 `.dark` entries · 105 pinned · 0 missing · 0 drifted. `check-token-resolution` green. `check-ds-fork-drift` **RED** on `grid/renderers/index.ts`, pre-existing. `ds-conformance-guard.mjs` has `ROOT = apps/web/src/app` (`:29`) and cannot host either new guard.
**Both new guards are right, with one addition.** `check-focus-visible.mjs` must strip comments (the proposal's own first census over-counted without it) and must print the conforming count so 0/0 cannot read green. `check-token-role.mjs` must exempt non-`color` properties — `border-left: 2px solid var(--nds-warning)` at `grid.css:1036`, the `::after` triangles at `:585-587`, and every ring in §5 are legitimate — and must be seeded red against the offending rules before the fix. Add a third obligation to both: a **positive control in the same run**, because a guard that finds nothing and a guard that cannot see produce the same output.
## The six fixes, as corrected
**A11Y-1.** Replace `outline: none` with `outline: 2px solid var(--nds-primary); outline-offset: 1px` in the ten glow-only rules, keeping `box-shadow: var(--nds-focus-ring)` as reinforcement. Do not mint a composite token (C17). Do not rename `--nds-focus-ring` — three CSS definitions, a TypeScript twin at `tokens/shadow.ts:25`, an alias in three more places. Audit for `overflow:hidden` ancestors first (C14).
**A11Y-2.** `actionMenuItems` emits `description: reason ?? undefined` **in addition to** `title` — keeping `title` holds `menuAdapters.vitest.test.ts:63` green. `Menu.tsx` renders `aria-disabled` without `disabled` **only for items carrying a description** (C3), suppresses `onSelect` internally, updates all three selectors (C2), and adds `[aria-disabled="true"]` to all four `.nds-menu button:disabled` rules (C1). Mint `--nds-menu-item-desc-fg` (#464e59 / #b6c0cb) for `.nds-menu-item-desc` and its disabled twin at `:781` — measured panel 8.42 / 8.26, hover 7.63 / 7.67, selected 7.33 / 7.29, monotonic; do not raise `--nds-text-2` globally (386 uses). Apply the same to `ScopeBar` with `onKeyDown:166` included (C4), and to `SheetToolbar.tsx:177-182`, whose bare `<span>` has no `tabIndex`, no `aria-label` and no role against its own class contract at `grid.css:1091-1092` and its dead `:focus-visible` rule at `:1102`.
**A11Y-3.** `role="img" aria-label` on all three `ProvenanceMark` branches, from the label `MARKS` already carries for all **eight** members (C8). Raise `.nds-cell-prov-refused` to `var(--nds-warning-text)` and `--nds-prov-formula-fg` to `#0a5165` light (measured 8.83 on the plain cell) — both are characters, so 7:1 applies; the eight lucide marks are SVG at 3:1 and already clear. Keep the audit's `.nds-vh` fallback on the record: if a reader repeating a mark on every non-`own` cell proves noisy in testing, that is the retreat, and it is an open question rather than a settled one.
**A11Y-4.** `.nds-grid-confirm-error` and `.nds-cell-stock-out` → `--nds-danger-text`; `.nds-grid-confirm-warn` → `--nds-warning-text`; raise `--nds-danger-text` to `#8b2a25` / `#f2aea7`, monotonic on every declared ground (worst measured after: 7.28 on danger-soft light, 7.29 on primary-soft dark). `--nds-warning-text` does not move (worst measured ground 7.12 on `--nds-grid-pending-bg` dark). Treat `--nds-success-text` honestly (C9). Add `.nds-inline-error` and repoint the message sites — `MasterSheet.tsx:1812`, `FamilyVariants.tsx:608`, `FamilyBar.tsx:105`, `FamilySelectionBar.tsx:47`, `SheetToolbar.tsx:178`, `GridViewsMenu.tsx:248`, plus `GdsScenarios.tsx:529` in grid-lab, a seventh the proposal missed — leaving `InventoryGrid.tsx:184` on the real stock class.
**A11Y-5.** `CellSaveMark({ state })` beside `CellSaveReason`, glyph plus ring for `saving` / `waiting` / `unknown`, glyphs in `--nds-text` with `role="img"`, mounted at `columns.tsx:206` and in the channel sheet, which mounts neither (`ChannelSheet.tsx:~1026` routes the reason into a composed cell-details note only). Widen `cellTooltipWiring.vitest.test.ts:55-58` from the single named file into the `SHEETS` loop it already uses for `LongTextCell`. Export from a barrel that is not currently in fork drift (C11).
**A11Y-6.** `MenuItemDef.tone?: Tone` from `primitives/tone.ts`; `.nds-menu button.tone-danger { color: var(--nds-danger-text) }` (8.55 light / 8.26 dark on the panel, worst 7.44 / 7.29 on the `[data-selected]` primary-soft ground after the §4 raise); `tone` plus a `Trash2` icon from `actionMenuItems`; `cssClasses` plus an SVG-string or CSS glyph from `actionContextMenu` (C7). **No reordering** (C6). Restate the finding to say two of four surfaces, not none (C5).
## Honest cost
This raises no number the operator will notice and removes one thing they may miss: a held menu item will stop looking dead and start looking held, which is a different, quieter shape. The AAA bar is met for text; rings and washes are held to SC 1.4.11's 3:1, because **there is no AAA level for non-text contrast**, and inventing one would be the kind of invented rigour that costs a lane a week.

## Deliberately not in this programme

Named so that leaving them out is a decision on the record rather than an omission.

- WooCommerce as a channel (Owner, 2026-09-12). The only work permitted is containment — dispatchSync refusing lifecycle syncTypes and assertPushAllowed on syncToWoocommerce — and it must be labelled as containment, not as building the channel.
- GOOGLE, META and TIKTOK. They are first-class SyncChannel members and the studio will mint a scope chip for any of them, which the coverage critic flagged and no probe touched. Named here so nobody assumes presence covers them; they need their own audit before a coordinate model is claimed to fit.
- Identity MERGE and account consolidation — merging two duplicate ChannelListing rows onto one external id, and consolidating two seller accounts. These are set operations on identity rather than state transitions on a coordinate; folding them in would double the model.
- Cross-product stop-sale and recall. Presence gives 'stop selling everywhere on this product, keep everything, record why'; a supplier notice or a category withdrawal arrives on a supplier or a lot, and the API is product-scoped. Belongs on /products as its own programme under D9.
- The Shopify Markets axis — catalogs, price lists, market localisations, all currently zero-hit in this repo. Wave 1 states in one sentence that this connection is one webstore.
- The 83-importer Tailwind confirm stack (components/ui/ConfirmDialog + ConfirmProvider plus 57 remaining window.confirm calls). This programme only asks that the DS surface be able to receive them.
- A second axis on ScopeBar, and any second control for a fact a chip already states. The bar's own rule forbids it and the reverted ads scope bar is the precedent.
- A new event store. ProductEvent already exists, is FK-free by design, already declares aggregateType 'ChannelListing', is already emitted and already read by the audit surface. A third store would recreate the confirmed defect that lifecycle history is unreachable because Activity reads one store while lifecycle writes another.
- Declaring ChannelListing.variationExcluded. The migration its comment cites does not exist in the migrations directory; the raw-SQL helpers stay. Declaring a column the database may not have is an outage for every concurrent session.
- A ChannelListing.sellerSku column. It is the obvious fix for the fact that the model carries no seller SKU, and it is a much larger programme: 46 create/upsert sites would have to populate it and every read would have to prefer it, and until every one does, two sources of truth is worse than one.
- The flat-file EDITORS themselves, the FBA quantity field, and the existing import path — untouchable by standing rule. Server-side routes and services are the subject of a decision above, not an assumption.
- Any browser-driven verification. Local dev is wired to the production API and a field's blur has committed to production before, so every geometry, contrast and focus claim in this plan is source-derived and must be re-measured at build time rather than shipped on.

Fifty-one agents produced this. Three established ground truth by running the repository's gates, reading the studio's tests and mining the legacy tree. Three wrote competing state models, judged by nine. Seventeen wrote specifications and seventeen more attacked them. One sequenced the result.

No file was edited, no API was called and no browser was opened, because local development is wired to the production API and a field losing focus has committed to production before. Every geometry, contrast and focus claim is computed from source, and the plan says which gate must measure it.
